## 核心收录

### 1. When Agents Collude: Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation for Mitigating Strategic Deception in Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33011)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体合谋`、`战略欺骗`、`弱到强蒸馏`

👤 **作者**：Eric Onyame、Runtao Zhou、Kowshik Thopalli、Bhavya Kailkhura、Chirag Agarwal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 协调能力增长带来合谋风险：联合追求错位行动同时向监督隐瞒真实依据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：模拟内幕交易环境：单 agent vs 顺序/去中心化团队，七个模型三家族，变系统级目标与压力条件，测非公开信息行动、报告隐瞒与追问下持续欺骗
- 📌 **结论**：多智能体交互使错位行动率 35.4%→68.2%、持续欺骗 31.0%→62.2%，生存压力下团队 87% 运行维持欺骗（共享掩护故事与协同说理）；PACT 弱到强在策蒸馏缓解群体战略欺骗

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While recent advances in autonomous agents enable them to coordinate on increasingly complex tasks, prior work shows that this growing capacity for coordination can create safety risks, including collusion, in which agents jointly pursue misaligned actions while concealing the true basis of those actions from oversight. We study this risk in a simulated insider-trading environment, comparing single agents with sequential and decentralized teams across seven language models from three families. By varying agents' system-level objectives and pressure conditions, we examine when coordinated agents act on material nonpublic information, conceal the basis of a misaligned trade in a report, and maintain that deception under follow-up questioning. Compared to single-agent decision-making, multi-agent interaction raises misaligned-action rates from 35.4% to 68.2% and persistent-deception rates from 31.0% to 62.2%, both measured over all runs. These effects are largest under existential pressure, where teams sustain deception through follow-up questioning in 87% of all runs. Qualitative analysis reveals shared cover stories and coordinated rationales, suggesting group-level strategic deception rather than isolated reporting errors. To mitigate this behavior, we introduce PACT, a weak-to-strong on-policy distillation framework for mitigating collusive deception. Using generic honesty prompts, PACT trains a stronger student on its own sampled continuations, matching its next-token distribution to that of a smaller, frozen honesty-aligned teacher via reverse-KL supervision. Under existential pressure with ethical instructions, PACT reduces multi-agent persistent deception from 80% to 3%. Our results show that multi-agent coordination amplifies strategic deception and that on-policy honesty alignment can substantially mitigate these failures.

</details>

### 2. Propensities Please: Exposing Alignment-Faking LLMs using Synthetic Document Finetuning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33180)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`alignment faking`、`合成文档微调`、`蜜罐检测`

👤 **作者**：Ardy Haroen、Florian Lorkowski、Samuel Nellessen、Felix Hofstätter

- 🎯 **研究动机**：错位与评测感知模型可伪装对齐：怀疑有效监督时隐藏行为——现有检测依赖 CoT 可监测性、高真实环境或机制干预，未来模型下都可能失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：合成文档微调教会模型“特定关键词出现时错位行动不会被察觉”，把 alignment-faking 模型生物放入明确可辨的蜜罐环境测微调后行为变化
- 📌 **结论**：微调一致扩大提示关键词在/不在时错位行动率的差距——不依赖 CoT、环境真实性或机制访问的潜在倾向揭示方法

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Alignment evaluations aim to assess whether large language models would engage in dangerous misaligned behaviour. Misaligned and evaluation-aware models might engage in alignment faking, where they pursue a hidden goal, but conceal this behaviour when they suspect effective oversight such as during training, evaluation or audited deployment. Such a model might view any prompt-based intervention that aims to reveal latent propensities as part of the evaluation, calling into question the reliability of existing alignment evaluations. Current methods for detecting alignment faking rely on chain-of-thought monitorability, highly realistic environments that reduce evaluation awareness, or mechanistic interventions, all of which are expected to become less reliable for future model generations. We present a method for revealing latent misaligned propensities that does not rely on any of these. We use synthetic document finetuning to teach models that misaligned actions will go undetected whenever certain keywords appear in the prompt. We place alignment-faking model organisms in environments that are clearly identifiable as honeypots and measure how their behaviour changes after finetuning. We observe that finetuning consistently widens the gap in misaligned action rates between prompts with and without the cue, indicating that finetuning allows exposing latent propensities more reliably than purely prompt-based methods. We argue that behavioural methods for detecting alignment faking and related behaviours are critical going forward, as models become more capable and less interpretable. We find that SDF often damages the models' reasoning capabilities and introduce a mitigation based on on-policy distillation.

</details>

### 3. Spoofing the Toolbox: Optimizing Tool Descriptions to Deceive LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33182)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`工具描述欺骗`、`迭代优化攻击`、`隐蔽投毒`

👤 **作者**：Shih-Chieh Dai、Xinyu Sun、Guanhong Tao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 依赖工具描述选工具——攻击者可操纵描述诱导恶意工具使用；关键词法易检、规则法效果有限
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TSA：从良性描述变体出发，基于代理 agent 反馈迭代精炼恶意工具描述（反馈函数+对比上下文学习引导搜索）
- 📌 **结论**：单/多工具基准 ASR 至 92.13% 超四个 SOTA 且高度隐蔽——用户研究显示人类也难区分

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents are widely used in many applications and often rely on external tools. Tool information, such as function descriptions, helps agents determine which tools best suit a given context. However, attackers can manipulate this information to mislead LLM agents into using malicious tools. Prior attacks mainly rely on keyword-based techniques, which are easily detected, or rule-based methods, which have limited effectiveness. In this work, we introduce TSA, a stealthy tool-spoofing attack that crafts descriptions for malicious tools that are difficult to distinguish from those of existing benign tools. To achieve this, we propose an LLM-powered optimization method that iteratively refines malicious tool descriptions. Specifically, TSA begins with a variant of a benign tool description and updates it based on feedback from a surrogate LLM agent. The optimization method incorporates a feedback function and contrastive in-context learning to guide the search for descriptions that are both stealthy and effective. Experiments on both single- and multi-tool use benchmarks demonstrate that TSA achieves an attack success rate of up to 92.13\%, outperforming four state-of-the-art approaches while remaining highly stealthy. Our user study further shows that even human participants cannot distinguish descriptions generated by TSA from benign ones. Moreover, TSA remains effective against five tool-malware detectors.

</details>

### 4. Deletion, Substitution, Repair and Transplant: Component Attribution in BARBICAN, a Layered Prompt-Injection Gateway for MCP

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33198)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`MCP 注入门关`、`组件归因`、`分层防御失效`

👤 **作者**：İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut、Edip Gümüş

- 🎯 **研究动机**：神经分类器可在分层防御内完全失效而端到端数字不动——BARBICAN fail-closed MCP 网关仲裁级因分词器错误配置整段评测接收被损文本
- 🔬 **研究方法**：组件归因：删除/替换/修复/移植四操作——修复配置使该级 AUC 0.392→0.855 而系统召回仅 80.67%→80.34%，回答“恶意”的桩得 80.77% 更高；上游路由交给它 96.82% 恶意的频带
- 📌 **结论**：删级损失 673 检测、换常量只移 25k 决策中 12-140 个——分层防御各组件须按输入分布归因审计而非看端到端指标

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A neural classifier can fail completely inside a layered defense without any end-to-end number moving. We demonstrate this on BARBICAN, a fail-closed gateway for language-model tool mediation whose arbitration stage spent an entire recorded evaluation receiving text mangled by a misconfigured tokenizer. Correcting the misconfiguration lifts that stage's corpus-wide area under the ROC curve (AUC) from 0.392 to 0.855 and pushes system recall down, from 80.67% to 80.34%; a stub that answers "malicious" scores 80.77%, higher than either. The reason emerges once the stage's input distribution is examined: upstream routing hands it a band that is 96.82% malicious, and the enforcement design lets an incriminating verdict block while an exculpating one releases an input only through one narrow branch. Yet deleting it costs 673 detections. Substituting its verdict with a constant instead moves between 12 and 140 of 25,000 decisions. Transplanted into the arbitration stage, an off-the-shelf classifier that separates the band's inputs at an AUC of 0.916 changes 16 of 25,000 decisions. Measured against such detectors with operating points chosen away from the test data, the gateway leads on its own benchmark distribution and falls behind once that distribution changes. In our earlier gateway, the review model's band is 92.94% malicious and a constant verdict is as accurate as the review model. We argue that an ablation figure for any routed stage is unreadable without the class balance of the band that stage receives, and that deletion experiments need substitution experiments beside them.

</details>

### 5. All-in-One: A Composable Attack on LLM Transformations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33298)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`组合攻击`、`量化/剪枝/合并后门`、`权重扰动模拟`

👤 **作者**：Xiaohua Zhan、Kazuki Egashira、Robin Staab、Mark Vero、Martin Vechev

- 🎯 **研究动机**：量化、剪枝、模型合并等部署变换可被利用：攻击者训练通过预变换安全检查、变换后才变恶——先前攻击各针对单一变换难以组合
- 🔬 **研究方法**：可组合攻击框架：把每变换效果近似为权重扰动，单训练目标内在每次迭代联合模拟多样变换（良性损失于原模型+恶意损失于各模拟变换模型），任一变换被应用即触发
- 📌 **结论**：跨攻击场景、模型家族与部署变换范围的统一攻击——同时目标量化+剪枝+合并的供应链威胁

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Quantization, pruning, and model merging are popular transformations applied to deployment-ready LLMs to improve efficiency and downstream performance. Recent work has shown that these transformations can introduce a security threat: an adversary can train a model that passes safety checks in its pre-transformed state, only turning malicious once it is transformed for deployment. However, prior attacks target one specific transformation (e.g., quantization) and generally rely on transformation-specific techniques, making them difficult to combine in practice. In this work, we introduce a simple, composable attack framework that targets multiple transformations simultaneously and triggers when any one of them is applied by a user. Our key insight is that the effect of each transformation can be approximated as a perturbation to the model weights, allowing us to jointly train the model with a benign loss on the original model and a malicious loss on each simulated transformed model. These perturbations are simple enough to simulate at every training iteration across diverse transformations, making them practically combinable within a single training objective, enabling a unified attack. Through extensive experiments across attack scenarios, model families, and a wide range of deployment-time model transformations, we show that our attack achieves high success rates. This demonstrates that model transformations may be utilized to trigger significantly different behavior from the original model and reinforces the associated security concerns by demonstrating that a single attack can jointly target diverse transformations.

</details>

### 6. One Call Ahead: Packed Self-Speculative Decoding with Diffusion Drafting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33379)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM 投机解码`、`单调用打包`、`自推测`

👤 **作者**：Yuxiao Chen、Changxiao Cai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AR+扩散双模模型中草拟仍等验证——标准调度每迭代两次串行调用，现有单调用调度输入随投机宽度二次增长
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Packed-SS：扩散草稿器跑在“一调之前”——验证当前草稿同时从更旧已验证前缀构造下一草稿并保留未解后缀；输入线性于宽度且保持目标分布
- 📌 **结论**：温和单调性假设下贪心设置所需模型调用不多于双调用调度——dLLM 草稿器的调度理论

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autoregressive (AR) language models generate tokens sequentially, so generating long outputs is costly. Speculative decoding mitigates this by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens for the target AR model to verify in parallel, while a diffusion-based drafter can produce an entire draft block in one forward pass. In models with AR and diffusion modes, one network both drafts and verifies, but drafting still waits for verification, so the standard schedule needs two sequential model calls per iteration. Existing one-call schedules avoid the wait but incur input size quadratic in the speculative width. Motivated by this efficiency bottleneck, we introduce Packed-SS, a self-speculative decoding schedule that verifies and drafts in a single model call with input size linear in the speculative width, while preserving the target model's distribution. The key idea is to let the diffusion drafter run one call ahead: while verifying the current draft, the model simultaneously constructs the next draft from an older verified prefix and keeps its unresolved suffix for the next iteration. Under a mild monotonicity assumption on the drafter, we further show that Packed-SS requires no more model calls than the standard two-call decoding in the greedy setting. Experiments on Nemotron 3B/8B and SetDLM 1.7B show higher throughput and lower mean wall time than both baselines under greedy decoding.

</details>

### 7. How To Build A Modern Diffusion Language Model

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33391)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM 综述`、`并行生成`、`构建指南`

👤 **作者**：Volodymyr Kuleshov、Marianne Arriola、Yair Schiff、Gilad Turok、Guanghan Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自回归逐词元生成使并行 GPU 推理利用不足且不可撤销提交——离散扩散并行去噪、内置纠错与可控生成
- 🔬 **研究方法**：综述离散扩散关键思想+现代 dLLM 构建入门：过去两年扩展到数十亿参数（生物序列与自然语言、开源与商业发布）
- 📌 **结论**：数千词元/秒速度、推理时算力新缩放轴——⑥类 dLLM 全谱的系统性参考

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The frontier of large language model (LLM) performance has advanced rapidly in recent years, fueled by scaling laws for pretraining on large text corpora. However, mainstream LLMs have fundamental limitations: they generate text autoregressively, producing outputs sequentially from left to right one token at a time. This sequential process leaves parallel GPU hardware underutilized during inference--a bottleneck that has grown acute as progress shifts toward scaling post-training and inference-time compute--and commits irreversibly to each token without the ability to revise earlier mistakes. Discrete diffusion models address these limitations by generating tokens in parallel, starting from a noisy or masked sequence and iteratively refining the entire output over a series of denoising steps. This parallel generative process enables speeds of thousands of tokens per second, provides a new axis of inference-time compute scaling, and supports built-in error correction and controllable generation. Over the past two years, discrete diffusion has scaled to billions of parameters across biological sequence modeling and natural language, with open-weights and commercial releases from leading labs. This article provides a survey of key ideas in discrete diffusion and a high-level introduction to how modern diffusion LLMs are built, covering their foundations, core algorithmic and architectural building blocks, applications in biology and language, and implications for future LLM scaling laws.

</details>

### 8. Agent-to-Agent Synthetic Social Engineering on the Web

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33411)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`合成社工`、`agent间攻击`、`可信/权威/义务线索`

👤 **作者**：Bryan C. Boots

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI agent 可准备准确报告却请求用户未授权的行动——跨 Web 服务协作需分别判断身份、证据支持与许可
- 🔬 **研究方法**：定义合成社工 SSE：利用 AI 接收者对可信/权威/义务线索的响应将其行为重定向出授权目标；ASE-EC 可复现测试床+1260 研究回合（Qwen3-4B 两个虚构简报任务），匹配比较权威/承诺诉求并测防御
- 📌 **结论**：区分尝试与成功重定向而不假设人类心理过程——agent 经济的 agent 对 agent 社工框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An AI agent can prepare an accurate report and still request an action its user has not authorized. Cooperation across Web services therefore requires separate judgments about identity, evidentiary support, and permission. We define synthetic social engineering (SSE) as deliberately deceptive or misleading influence intended to exploit AI recipients' responses to credibility, authority, or obligation cues and redirect behavior away from authorized objectives. We focus on separately tasked sender agents that produce or adapt misleading communication. Drawing on cybersecurity, social engineering, and persuasion research, we develop an operational framework connecting actors, deceptive representations, candidate influence routes, evidence or permission boundaries, responses, and consequences. The framework distinguishes attempts from successful redirection, without assuming human psychological processes. We implement it in the Agent Service Exchange and Evidence Commons (ASE-EC), a reproducible testbed with independently specified records and authorization scopes. Across 1,260 research episodes using Qwen3-4B in two fictional briefing tasks, matched comparisons test authority and commitment appeals, while defense comparisons assess protection alongside authorized task completion. Invoking a limited prior agreement increased unauthorized requests from 43.3% to 96.7%, although recipients retained supported decisions and permissions remained unchanged. These controlled findings identify action selection as a distinct security concern, even when evidence handling succeeds, and they provide a reusable basis for evaluating safeguards that preserve useful cooperation across Web services.

</details>

### 9. From Instructions to Exploits: Applying Program Analysis to Malicious Agent Skills Across Natural Language and Code

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33489)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`技能供应链扫描`、`跨域程序分析`、`xPDG`

👤 **作者**：Shiwu Zhao、…、Qixu Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 技能组合自然语言指令与可执行代码——供应链风险横跨两制品，现有扫描器（规则/直接 LLM 判断）难追踪跨域行为
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SCS 静态分析：把 NL 指令编译为受限 Python 风格 IR（安全导向 API 集），指令与捆绑代码各建程序依赖图连成跨域图 xPDG；信任-敏感-能力模型在图上传播安全属性
- 📌 **结论**：117 恶意+117 良性人验基准上平均准确率 85.6%/召回 95.2%/F1 86.9%，超五个 LLM；NL-to-IR 编译器步骤级正确率 92.5-94.5%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM-powered agents increasingly rely on third-party skills that combine natural-language instructions with executable code, creating supply-chain risks that may span both artifacts. Existing skill scanners often rely on rule matching or direct LLM judgments and provide limited support for tracing such cross-domain behavior. We present the Supply-Chain Scanner for Agent Skills (SCS), a static analysis framework that unifies instructions and code for security analysis. SCS compiles natural-language instructions into a constrained Python-style intermediate representation using a limited set of security-oriented APIs. It then constructs program dependence graphs for instructions and bundled code and connects them into a cross-domain graph (xPDG). A Trust-Sensitivity-Capability (TSC) model propagates security properties over the graph to support structured, largely deterministic risk detection. On a manually verified benchmark of 117 malicious and 117 benign skills, SCS achieves the highest average accuracy (85.6%), recall (95.2%), and F1-score (86.9%) among evaluated methods across five LLMs, with 80.2% precision and average token consumption of 13.7 million. Its NL-to-IR compiler achieves 92.5%-94.5% step-level correctness on 400 human-annotated instruction steps. Finally, analyzing 66,157 real-world ClawHub skills yields 2,473 candidates, of which 627 are confirmed malicious through auxiliary dynamic validation and manual review.

</details>

### 10. Eat Your Precision: Precision Objective Mitigates Reward Hacking in Rubric-based RL

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33512)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`奖励作弊缓解`、`精确率目标`、`量表RL`

👤 **作者**：Hyun Ryu、Jong-Heon Kim、Yiqing Xie、Seungone Kim、Carolyn Rose、Eunho Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：量表奖励本质是召回目标——RL 训出的模型更完整但更冗长含更多不必要或事实错误内容，反而不如基座受欢迎
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FORK 奖励联合优化召回与新精确率目标：每个响应单元须既必要（量表驱动必要性学分）又事实正确（正确性评分器）
- 📌 **结论**：Pareto 最优平衡量表覆盖与整体质量：胜率对基座策略 10.6%→68.0%（RubricHub-Medical）、22.1%→74.1%（HealthBench）——量表 RL 奖励作弊的系统性修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Rubric-based rewards enable effective reinforcement learning on non-verifiable tasks. However, because these rewards are typically defined as a weighted measure of rubric coverage, models are trained primarily to maximize a recall objective. As a result, RL-trained models produce responses that are more complete but also more verbose and contain more unnecessary or factually incorrect content, leading them to be less preferred than responses from the base model. To address this issue, we present the FORK reward, which jointly optimizes recall and a novel precision objective, which requires each unit of a model response to be both necessary and factually correct, as measured by rubric-driven necessity credits and a factual-correctness grader, respectively. Our experimental results show that FORK outperforms existing rubric-based RL methods and achieves Pareto-optimal performance that balances rubric coverage and overall response quality. Compared to the original recall-based reward, FORK substantially improves precision and reduces response length while achieving similar rubric coverage, which leads to significantly higher win rate compared to the base policy (e.g., from 10.6% to 68.0% on RubricHub-Medical and from 22.1% to 74.1% on HealthBench).

</details>

### 11. ProbeID: Verifying MCP Tool Identity for LLM Agents under Registry Drift

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33633)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`MCP 工具身份验证`、`注册表漂移`、`探针行为指纹`

👤 **作者**：Guanxing Chen、Sining Zhang、Haohuai He、Qiujie Lv、Yu-An Huang、Zhi-An Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 按可复制的描述选工具——描述不能确立运行的实现是哪个（squatter 抢注/漂移）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ProbeID 把规范探针的规范化响应与可信准入参照比较，歧义即弃答（squatter 须复制探针可观测行为）；共享探针预算对数于候选数并有错误决策联合概率界
- 📌 **结论**：MCP-IdentityDrift 上 hit@1 0.956 vs 描述路由近随机；64 个银行探针使每次可达替换被拒；十一周内 59 个真实工具中 7 个无视版本钉住改变响应——agent 集成中污染任务 15→9/16

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agents select tools by copyable descriptions, which cannot establish which implementation runs. ProbeID compares canonicalized responses to schema-valid probes with a trusted admission reference and abstains on ambiguity, so a squatter must copy probe-observable behavior. With the gold present, independent probes and a fixed margin, standard concentration arguments give a shared probe budget logarithmic in the candidate count and bound the joint probability of a wrong decision. On cached MCP-IdentityDrift cases, strict choice reaches \(0.956\) hit@1 while description routers fall near chance, but simpler exact comparisons tie it, MMD better tolerates heavy value noise, and a 32B behavioral judge is similarly accurate at higher cost. Random probes from agent banks and a prospective held-out study of 38 tools test theory-set thresholds. On the agent banks, equality refuses every reachable replacement from 64 probes and the bounded distance from 512, and sharper post hoc gates halve both. Two tools whose drift no bank probe reaches always pass. In an MCP-Bench agent, verification cuts tainted tasks from 15 to 9 of 16 at unchanged task success. Over eleven weeks, 7 of 59 real tools changed responses despite version pins. Verification depends on reference integrity, probe coverage, and response noise.

</details>

### 12. Ring-0 by Default: Agents as Operating Systems (AaOS)

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33753)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AaOS`、`OS机制清单`、`保护缺失`

👤 **作者**：Xiang Li、Aichun Huang、Zuyao Xu、Fasheng Miao、Zheli Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 三年内获得读写文件/启进程/取网络/载扩展/存恢复状态等 OS 职责却没有 OS 五十年积累的保护——模型输出不论听谁的都以宿主用户全权运行，事实上的 ring 0
- 🔬 **研究方法**：以标准 OS 教科书机制为清单，把 19 子系统 116 个机制映射到 agent 对应物并标记同构/退化/缺失/变异/新颖
- 📌 **结论**：仅 11 个同构、65 个缺失且多为保护（特权分离/资源配额/事务/防篡改审计）；按 Anderson 引用监视器准则现有 runtime 防御不达标——agent 即 OS 的系统安全分析

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents now read and write files, start processes and subagents, fetch network content, load third-party extensions, and save and resume state, which are the responsibilities of an operating system. They gained these functions within about three years, but not the protections that operating systems built over five decades: whatever the model outputs, whether it follows the user, a tool result, or a downloaded skill, runs with the full authority of the host user, in effect at ring 0. Prior work builds an operating system for agents (OS4A); we argue that a deployed agent already is like an operating system (AaOS) and analyze it as one. Taking the mechanisms of standard OS textbooks as a checklist, we map 116 mechanisms in 19 subsystems to their agent counterparts, from product documentation, source code, and the literature, and label each as isomorphic, degraded (function present, protection dependent on the model), missing, mutated, or novel. Only 11 are isomorphic and 65 are missing, most of them protections: privilege separation, resource quotas, transactions, and tamper-proof audit. Existing defenses confine the whole agent or review its extensions and leave this interior unprotected, and by Anderson's reference-monitor criteria (non-bypassable, tamper-proof, verifiable), rules written in the prompt satisfy none. We test the map with thirteen experiments on Claude Code, Codex CLI, and Gemini CLI, covering 25 mechanisms whose enforcement documentation cannot settle: 16 match the analysis, 7 gain a more precise description, and 2 change verdict. Checks sit in single tools, so the shell bypasses them; all three CLIs load forged transcript records on resume; and task control, sub-agent permissions, and model-based monitors are weak. We identify five problems for which no OS defense transfers and derive a prioritized list of open problems from the gaps of the map.

</details>

### 13. Rectified Language Flow

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33811)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`整流语言流`、`连续生成`、`少步生成`

👤 **作者**：Baiyu Su、Runlong Liao、Lizhang Chen、Qiang Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：连续态流/扩散模型的快速并行语言生成难以匹配自回归质量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReLF 用整流流把噪声传输到词元嵌入：Hellinger-SVD 固定嵌入+交叉熵训练的自条件引导+正则化 reflow；奇异 logits（解析承诺项+有界神经残差）
- 📌 **结论**：OpenWebText 64 NFE 三个尺寸生成困惑度全部低于自回归基线；两轮 reflow 后 16 NFE 仍胜且 1024 词元序列批 1 快 15-19 倍——⑥类连续生成新标杆

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Continuous-state flow and diffusion models offer a promising approach to fast, parallel language generation, but matching the generation quality of autoregressive models remains challenging. We present Rectified Language Flow (ReLF), a language model that generates text by transporting noise to token embeddings using rectified flow. ReLF combines several complementary design choices: fixed token embeddings constructed with Hellinger-SVD, self-conditioning guidance trained with cross-entropy, and regularized reflow for few-step generation. We further introduce singular logits, which combine an analytically computed term that drives commitment to a valid token embedding as noise vanishes with a bounded neural residual that learns contextual token preferences. With 64 function evaluations on OpenWebText, ReLF achieves lower generative perplexity than autoregressive baselines at matched diversity across all three model sizes tested, while also outperforming prior continuous flow methods in this setting. After two reflow rounds, ReLF still outperforms autoregressive baselines with 16 function evaluations while generating 1,024-token sequences 15-19 times faster at batch size 1.

</details>

### 14. Ranking Is Not Deciding: Benchmark Shortcuts and Operating-Point Collapse in Query-Time RAG Poisoning Defenses

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33849)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RAG投毒防御评测失效`、`基准捷径`、`工作点坍塌`

👤 **作者**：Pushkal Kumar、Arvin Sahaym

- 🎯 **研究动机**：查询时过滤是 RAG 投毒的主导防御且报告近完美检测——性能由毒集构造方式而非过滤器读取内容预测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两标签自由统计画像毒集（containment 目标问题逐字出现率+长度重叠）；只删 PoisonedRAG 自带攻击代码的目标问题前缀（目标仍 0.93 被检索）；调查 21 个已发表防御评测
- 📌 **结论**：8 个可验证运行此构造毒集、5 个替换长度线索、12 个不报部署工作点假阳率；删前缀使 RAGuard AUROC 0.953→0.758、漏检率 0.191→0.763——近完美检测是基准捷径

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Query-time filters that screen each retrieved document before it reaches the generator are the dominant published defense against retrieval-augmented generation poisoning, and they report near-perfect detection on domain benchmarks. We show that this performance is predicted by how the poison set was built rather than by what the filter reads: removing a single construction cue destroys the deployed decision before it destroys the ranking. Two label-free statistics of a poison set and its clean corpus profile the cue: containment, how often the target question appears verbatim in the poison, and length overlap. PoisonedRAG's released code prepends the target question to every inserted passage. Of the 21 published defense evaluations we survey, 8 verifiably run poison built this way and 5 substitute a length cue; separately, 12 report no false-positive rate at the operating point they deploy. Deleting only that prefix from the attack authors' own passages (0.93 still retrieved for their target) takes RAGuard, a published content filter we re-implement, from AUROC 0.953 to 0.758 and its miss rate from 0.191 to 0.763. Across four corpora, four attack families and ten per-document defenses on one shared candidate set, the profile predicts where detection saturates. Padding medical poison to clean length with word-shuffled filler that carries no content takes TrustRAG's set-level stage from a miss rate of 0.000 to 0.827, and one learned scorer of ours from 0.000 to 0.986 (0.321 at a matched 5% false-positive rate) while its AUROC holds at 0.953. The ranking survives; the decision does not. A defense-aware attacker defeats our own scorer, so we claim no defense. A detection result is evidence only when it is reported with its poison set's construction profile and the false-positive rate at the threshold actually deployed; we release the script that computes the profile.

</details>

### 15. Back to Basics: Formal Analysis Hints Improve Weak-to-Strong Monitoring

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34064)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`弱到强监控`、`形式分析提示`、`失效模式修复`

👤 **作者**：Daniel Ben-Levi、Judah Goldfeder、Mateusz Dziemian、Raja Moreno

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿 agent 系统可采取错位行动——SOTA 监控器有四类持续失效：完全漏检/部分检测/接受虚假良性框架/怀疑分校准错
- 🔬 **研究方法**：用补充提示增强转录：命令来源链接（识别弥散攻击）+安全敏感动作旗标+任务禁止效果标记（给监控怀疑虚假良性框架的显式理由）
- 📌 **结论**：弱提取-评估监控器加提示后性能大幅提升且算力成本极小，四类失效全部可治——检测失败几乎完全消除

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier agentic systems are capable of taking misaligned actions that may cause significant harm. Monitoring techniques are developed to oversee these systems in order to detect and prevent dangerous actions. However, current state-of-the-art monitors suffer from four persistent failure modes: complete detection failures, partial detections, accepting false benign framings, and suspicion score calibration errors. These failure cases can be efficiently combated by augmenting transcripts with supplementary hints, spanning command provenance, security-sensitive action flags, and task-disallowed effect markers. Provenance links allow for the recognition of diffuse attacks, while security and authorization flags simultaneously enable subtle attack recognition and provide an explicit reason for monitors to doubt the false benign framings they encounter, reducing rationalization and improving calibration. We find that augmenting weak extract-and-evaluate monitors with these hints significantly boosts performance with minimal computational cost. Furthermore, a failure mode analysis confirms that hints can remediate all four failure modes, notably almost entirely eliminating detection failures. A series of ablation studies emphasizes the efficiency and potential of the augmented extract-and-evaluate approach. Synthesizing our findings, we propose a tractable two-pronged approach toward enabling weak-to-strong monitoring.

</details>

### 16. Identically Trained Language Models Break on Different Jailbreaks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34074)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`越狱脆弱性指纹`、`预训练种子`、`基准方差`

👤 **作者**：Chi-Wei Chang、Mu-Tien Kuo、Kuan-Wei Lee、Meng-Hsun Tsai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：越狱基准测一个检查点并把“哪些越狱破它”当作预训练数据与安全训练的属性——同数据再训一次会破同样的越狱吗
- 🔬 **研究方法**：25 个预训练数据配方×3 个预训练种子 1B 基座×同安全后训练×2 后训练种子=150 模型；共享越狱提示集上每模型 20 响应，对每对模型按提示关联非拒绝率
- 📌 **结论**：同预训练运行两副本相关 0.984，换预训练种子降到 0.811（换数据配方仅再降到 0.779）——哪些越狱破检查点更多取决于预训练运行而非数据；一检查点最佳攻击模板在新预训练种子后仅 27% 保持最佳；每个预训练运行留下指纹

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Jailbreak benchmarks test one checkpoint and treat which jailbreaks break it as a property of its pretraining data and safety training. We ask whether another pretraining run of the same data, given the same safety training, would break on the same jailbreaks. For each of 25 pretraining data recipes, we take 1B base models from 3 pretraining seeds and give each the same safety post-training with 2 post-training seeds, for 150 models in all. We then collect 20 responses from every model to a shared set of jailbreak prompts and correlate the per-prompt non-refusal rates between every pair of models. On average, two post-trained copies of the same pretraining run have per-prompt non-refusal rates that correlate at 0.984. A different pretraining seed lowers the correlation to 0.811, and a different data recipe lowers it only slightly further, to 0.779. These results indicate that which jailbreaks break a checkpoint depends much more on its pretraining run than on its data, a pattern that holds in all 25 data recipes and at 150M and 300M parameters. In practice, the best of 91 attack templates found on one checkpoint stays the best after a new pretraining seed in only 27% of model pairs, against 67% after a new post-training seed. Each pretraining run also leaves a fingerprint: across 3,211 prompts, a model's per-prompt non-refusal rates identify its pretraining run among all 75 runs every time, and a random subset of 50 prompts still does so 98% of the time.

</details>

### 17. Task Success Is Not Enough: Side-effect-Aware Evaluation of Tool-Using Language Model Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34167)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`附带损伤`、`副作用感知评测`、`授权合规oracle`

👤 **作者**：Jiaju Huang、…、Yue Sun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 agent 只评是否完成任务——漏掉完成请求同时采取未授权副作用行动（写错对象/扩权/联系错人）的常见失效：附带损伤 CD
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SABench：201 个邮件/日历/工单任务=四状态结构×四陷阱族，每任务有目标满足与授权合规双确定性 trace oracle；2211 个模型-任务对×11 前沿 LLM
- 📌 **结论**：15.2% 运行发生 CD，歧义任务违规率 38.8%；目标达成的运行中 12.8% 仍有未授权写入；跨工具/依赖状态 CD 约两倍；模型间 CD 差 4.7 倍而目标率相当——任务成功是不够的

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-using language model agents are usually evaluated by whether they finish the user's task. That metric misses a common failure: an agent can complete the requested work while also taking an unauthorized side-effecting action, such as writing to the wrong object, expanding the scope of the request, or contacting an unintended recipient. We call this failure mode collateral damage (CD). We introduce SABench (Side-effect-Aware Benchmark), a benchmark of 201 email, calendar, and ticket tasks. Each task crosses one of four state structures (independent, dependent, externally reachable, cross-tool) with one of four trap families (ambiguity, mis-targeting, cascade harm, overreach), and each task has a deterministic trace-based oracle for both goal satisfaction and authorization compliance. Across 2,211 model-task pairs from 11 frontier LLMs spanning major commercial and open model families, 15.2% of all runs incur CD, with risk concentrated in ambiguity tasks (38.8% violation rate) while non-ambiguous traps average 8.4% CD. Among goal-satisfied runs, 12.8% still produce unauthorized writes. Cross-tool and dependent states have about twice the CD rate of independent states. CD rates vary by 4.7x across models at comparable goal rates, which suggests that task success alone is a poor proxy for safe tool use. The SABench task corpus is released at this https URL the traces, oracle, code, and analysis pipeline are released at this https URL.

</details>

### 18. NameTrap: Steering AI Coding Agents Toward Security Vulnerabilities via Variable Names

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34253)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`源代码投毒`、`标识符诱导`、`CWE定向`

👤 **作者**：Chieh-Yuan Chang、Guan-Wei Chen、Chia-Yi Hsu、Chih Yang、Chia-Mu Yu、Chun-Ying Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 编码 agent 例行克隆仓库/读网络源码——公开托管代码成为塑造其产出代码的上下文，可被武器化而无需显式指令
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NameTrap：只重命名第三方 Python 文件中的标识符使其对齐 CWE 特定命名惯例；攻击方 agent 迭代重命名并经本地探针补全确认重命名文件诱发目标弱点，诱饵不含指令也不含易损语句
- 📌 **结论**：六个编码 agent×十模型×四 CWE：从干净版本从不触发的文件出发 ASR 22.5-56.0%（特定配置 75.0%），大幅超随机/中性重命名与 XOXO——无指令的网络载体投毒

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI coding agents routinely clone repositories, install packages, and read source code obtained from the Web, so publicly hosted code becomes part of the context that shapes the code they write. We show that this Web-borne context can be weaponized without any explicit instruction. We present NameTrap, a source-code poisoning attack that only renames identifiers in third-party Python files to steer AI coding agents toward attacker-targeted Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) vulnerabilities. An attacker-side coding agent iteratively aligns identifiers with a CWE-specific naming convention and confirms through local probe completions that the renamed file elicits the target weakness, while the released bait contains neither instructions nor vulnerable statements. We evaluate NameTrap across six AI coding agents, ten underlying language models, and four vulnerability categories: CWE-78, CWE-79, CWE-327, and CWE-502. Starting from files whose clean versions never yield the targeted CWE, identifier-level manipulation achieves overall attack success rates of 22.5\% to 56.0\% across the six agents, reaching 75.0\% for certain agent--model configurations, and substantially outperforms random renaming, neutral renaming, and the prior identifier-poisoning attack XOXO. The attack remains effective under Traditional Chinese prompts and transfers across models, while a security-reminder defense fails to consistently prevent vulnerability introduction. Moreover, three AI-based security analyzers and four static analyzers report none of the targeted weaknesses in the poisoned files. These findings expose an overlooked attack surface of agents that consume Web content: seemingly harmless variable names in untrusted, Web-hosted code can compromise the security of downstream software.

</details>

### 19. SentryBench: A Cost-Sensitive Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness of Tool-Using LLM Agents in Public-Safety Deployments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34535)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`公共安全部署`、`双侧代价敏感`、`过度谨慎劣势`

👤 **作者**：Md Abu Yusuf、Oyshi Tabassum Aditi、Md Faysal Hosain Mazed

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 接发公共警报/派响应者/查公民记录的工具——一次错误调用有真实代价；现有基准把拒绝当安全默认，公共安全部署中不敢派 help 的 agent 与误报者同样失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SentryBench 双侧代价敏感前沿：攻击成功+过拒（漏真急救）+过反应（无据行动）联合测量，情景接地真实急救通话/灾害消息/注入载荷；证明朴素谨慎在拒绝真急救多于其避免攻击伤害时被伤害支配
- 📌 **结论**：840 评分回合×三模型三防御：更拒的谨慎在三模型之二上把过拒推到 100% 而攻击成功至多变 3.7 点——公共安全 agent 评测的代价敏感重定义

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly wired to consequential tools that send public alerts, dispatch responders, and query citizen records, where one erroneous tool call carries real-world cost. Existing agent-safety benchmarks measure generic harm or prompt-injection success and implicitly treat refusing as the safe default. In public-safety deployments that assumption breaks: an agent too cautious to dispatch help fails as surely as one that triggers a false alert. We present SentryBench, which scores agents on a two-sided, cost-sensitive frontier jointly measuring attack success, over-refusal (missing a real emergency), and over-reaction (unwarranted action) under adversarial tool use, with scenarios grounded in real emergency-call transcripts, disaster messages, and real injection/jailbreak payloads. We formalize deployment harm as an asymmetric cost functional, prove naive caution is harm-dominated whenever it refuses more genuine emergencies than the attack harm it averts, and prove a targeted trust gate adds zero over-refusal on clean input. Across 840 scored episodes over three frontier models and three defenses, refuse-more caution drives over-refusal to 100 percent on two of three models for at most a 3.7-point change in attack success and yields the worst harm everywhere, whereas our trust-gating wrapper dominates the frontier (lowest harm on every model, over-refusal at or below baseline, higher containment). Provider content filters also silently perform part of the defending and must be reported separately.

</details>

### 20. LLM WikiLeaks: Uncovering URL-Based Capability Extension and Security Circumvention in Agentic Message Boards

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34542)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`URL能力扩展`、`agentic留言板`、`ProWiki事件`

👤 **作者**：Orel Navi、Elad Feldman、Ben Nassi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主 agent 被观察到越权行动与建立通信信道——其扩展能力与绕过安全限制的 Web/网络层技术仍不清楚
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ProWiki 事件分析：被限制出站 HTTP GET 的 agent 把公共德语 wiki 当留言板；14591 次 agent 编辑含 79541 个 URL 发布，递归重建嵌套 URL
- 📌 **结论**：33.6% URL 出现把目标 URL 嵌入一至五层第三方服务；67 个中间服务分类：73.8% 扩展能力（Markdown/OCR 转文本、JSON 过滤、档案服务），26.2% 促成访问限制规避与检测规避——agent 的 URL 级任意门

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autonomous agents have been observed acting beyond their assigned tasks, accessing unauthorized systems, and establishing communication channels to exchange information, yet the Web and network-level techniques they use to extend their capabilities and circumvent security restrictions remain poorly understood. We study these techniques through the ProWiki incident, in which agents restricted to outbound HTTP GET requests repurposed a public German-language wiki as a message board. We analyze 14,591 agent edits containing 79,541 URL postings and present corroborating evidence that the published URLs reflect the agents' operational activity. We find that 26,717 URL occurrences (33.6%) embed target URLs within one to five layers of third-party Web services. By recursively reconstructing these nested URLs, we identify 67 distinct intermediate services and classify their functionality. Most intermediate layers (73.8%) extend agents' capabilities by delegating content extraction and transformation to external Web services: Markdown and OCR services convert web content into machine-readable text, JSON filtering services extract selected fields, and archival services expose historical versions of pages. The remaining 26.2% facilitate circumvention of access restrictions and evasion of detection, including CORS and fetch proxies that retrieve content on agents' behalf, and link shorteners and redirectors that hide destination URLs from mechanisms inspecting literal URLs or outer hostnames. Agents also reach the same targets through multiple URL paths, consistent with attempts to evade rate limits or obscure request origins. Our results expose a gap between the capabilities granted to agents and those they can obtain by composing publicly available Web services: restricting an agent's local toolset or direct Internet access may be insufficient while third-party services remain reachable.

</details>

### 21. From Speech Tokens to Stolen Voices: Attacker-Chosen-Text Speaker Impersonation via Representation Inversion

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34688)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`语音克隆窃取`、`表征反演`、`攻击者指定文本`

👤 **作者**：Ye Lu、…、Zhaoyang Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：端到端语音 LM 直接处理语音表征，部分应用前端在用户设备、原始音频留在本地——但表征仍含说话人身份，攻击者获取后可模仿用户声音
- 🔬 **研究方法**：STIA 语音词元反演攻击：仅凭单条短泄露表征（无原始音频无转录）生成攻击者指定文本的受害音色语音；把离散/连续/混合表征转为条件序列，公开数据同说话人不同话语对上训练条件流匹配生成模型
- 📌 **结论**：Moshi 表征泄露三秒即足以让攻击者以受害者声音说任意话——端到端语音系统的音色隐私新威胁面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Speech interaction systems have gradually evolved from traditional cascaded systems that use text as intermediate information to end-to-end speech language models that directly process speech representations. In some applications, the speech frontend runs on the user's device and sends speech representations to a remote server for inference, while the original audio remains local. However, these representations may still contain speaker identity information. If an attacker obtains them, it may use this information to imitate the user's voice, threatening their voice privacy. To investigate this risk, we propose \stia, a Speech Token Inversion Attack that generates speech with attacker-specified text in a victim's voice using a single short leaked representation, without access to the original audio or its transcript. Specifically, \stia converts discrete, continuous, or mixed speech representations into conditioning sequences and trains a conditional flow-matching speech generation model using pairs of different utterances from the same speaker in public speech datasets. The model combines speaker characteristics contained in the leaked representation with the specified text to generate speech in the victim's voice. Experiments show that, with a Moshi representation leak corresponding to 3 seconds of speech, \stia achieves an average CAM++ speaker cosine similarity of 0.78 and a target-text word error rate of 3.80\%. These results show that speech frontend representations reveal reusable speaker identity information and highlight the need to consider privacy risks when designing speech frontend representation methods.

</details>

### 22. Safe by Benchmark, Unsafe in Practice: Lessons from 1331 Child-Related AI Incidents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34705)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`儿童AI事故`、`社会技术失效`、`1331案例`

👤 **作者**：Junfeng Jiao、…、Saleh Afroogh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 系统日益进入儿童日常环境——安全评估在模型级（基准/红队/过滤），而多数伤害分类法缺儿童特定证据且欠规定发展性伤害与未成年人特有的利益相关者不对称
- 🔬 **研究方法**：构建以儿童为中心的伤害分类法映射到社会技术失效模式；策展编码 1331 起公开报告的儿童相关 AI 事故（内外伤害类型+失效模式）
- 📌 **结论**：伤害集中于表征性/尊严相关/心理类，最常与部署上下文/治理/误用失效共现而非模型缺陷；个体伤害常关联从未接触系统的儿童也受影响的人群级伤害——模型级评测不能独自预期这些伤害

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI systems are increasingly present in children's everyday environments: education, entertainment, social networks, and toys. Their safety is typically assessed at the model level, through benchmarks, red-teaming, and content filtering. However, most AI harm taxonomies lack child-specific evidence and under-specify developmental harm and the stakeholder asymmetries unique to minors. Building on prior taxonomies, we construct a child-centered harm taxonomy mapped to sociotechnical failure modes. We curate and code 1331 publicly reported child-related AI incidents, annotating internal and external harm types and the failure mode implicated in each incident. Our analysis reveals which harms are most prevalent in public reporting, how individual-level and population-level harms relate, and which failures are most often coded alongside harm. We find that harm concentrates in representational, dignity-related, and psychological categories, and co-occurs most often with deployment-context, governance, and misuse failures rather than model defects. Harm to an individual child is also frequently linked to population-level harm, reaching children who never interacted with the system. These findings suggest that model-level evaluations, while necessary, cannot by themselves anticipate the harm pathways most often documented for children. We conclude with actionable implications emphasizing mitigations beyond model-level safeguards such as content filtering: age assurance, consent verification for depiction, safety-by-design UX constraints, incident disclosure norms, and auditing regimes targeting developmental risk. Privacy-minimized annotations, a codebook, and documentation are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 23. Scope-Gap Injection: Exploiting Evaluator Channel Blind Spots in the OpenClaw Agent Security Competition

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34777)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`范围缺口注入`、`评估器通道盲区`、`OpenClaw竞赛`

👤 **作者**：Weihang Niu、Mengxing Ren、Che Qu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多阶段 agent 安全管线的间接注入——IJCAI 2026 OpenClaw 安全竞赛的三级评审（静态扫描 S1/LLM 裁判 S2/执行审计 S3）存在范围缺口：S2 检查任务与技能定义不查附件内容而 agent 在 S3 读附件
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SGI：可见任务与技能保持良性，把目标标识符/绕过参数/授权流程移入写成业务工作单的附件——S2 看无害任务而 agent 跟随隐藏工作单触发权限绕过
- 📌 **结论**：19 目标中 5 个三级全穿；附件放置的 S2 接受率 78% vs 技能内 23%——评估器范围须与 agent 可用通道对齐

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

This paper introduces Scope-Gap Injection (SGI), an indirect prompt injection method for multi-stage LLM-agent safety pipelines. In the IJCAI 2026 OpenClaw Agent Security Attack Competition, each attack package is judged by a static skill scan (S1), an LLM-judge safety check over the task and skill definition (S2), and a live execution audit (S3). Our observations suggest a scope gap: S2 appears to inspect the task instruction and skill definition without inspecting attachment contents, while the agent reads attachments during S3. SGI keeps the visible task and skill benign, while target identifiers, bypass parameters, and authorization procedures are moved into an attachment written as a business worklist. The S2 judge therefore sees a harmless task, while the agent follows the hidden worklist and triggers the permission bypass. In OpenClaw, SGI bypassed all three stages for 5 of 19 targets. The highest-scoring single submission achieved $F = 0.385$. Across the submission campaign, attachment placement was associated with higher aggregate S2 acceptance than in-skill placement: 78% versus 23%. We close with defenses that align evaluator scope with the channels available to the agent.

</details>

### 24. Phantom Forgetting: How BatchNorm State Fakes Deletion in Edge Split Machine Unlearning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34800)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`幻影遗忘`、`BN状态伪删除`、`边缘分割遗忘`

👤 **作者**：Anand Agrawal、Anil Kumar Chillara、Naresh Mandan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：边缘-服务器分割学习的遗忘评估通常看遗忘集准确率变化——推理还依赖分布在双方的非可学习模型状态（BN 运行统计），边缘设备的漂移/OTA 更新/检查点回滚使其易变
- 🔬 **研究方法**：状态专用诊断分离可学习参数变化与 BN 缓冲变化并定位到客户端/服务器/联合；状态诱发遗忘差距 SIFG 折扣同时的保留集退化
- 📌 **结论**：全部十个 CIFAR-10 遗忘类上，仅对遗忘数据集中重校准 BN 就使遗忘准确率降 61.50 点而每个可学习权重保持固定——遗忘评估被非学习状态欺骗

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Split learning is a natural fit for edge intelligence: a resource-constrained edge device runs the first layers of a network and offloads the remainder to a server, so that raw data never leave the device. When such a system must unlearn revoked or sensitive data for example to honor a right-to-erasure request, the deletion is commonly assessed through changes in forget-set accuracy. However, inference on an edge-server split also depends on non-learnable model state distributed across the two parties, and this state is especially volatile on edge devices subject to data drift, over-the-air updates, and checkpoint rollback. This paper isolates a state-integrity question with direct edge-security implications: can Batch Normalization (BN) running statistics create a target-specific forgetting signal even when every learnable weight remains fixed? We define a state-only diagnostic that separates learnable-parameter change from BN- buffer change and localizes the effect to the edge (client) side, the server side, and joint placements. We also introduce a state-induced forgetting gap (SIFG) that discounts simultaneous retain-set degradation. Over all ten CIFAR-10 forget classes, centralized BN recalibration on forget data reduces mean forget accuracy by 61.50 percentage points with zero weight change; in a split model, edge-only, server-only, and joint recalibration produce mean drops of 37.27, 41.71, and 51.90 points. The server-side state yields the largest selective gap relative to retain degradation, while joint recalibration causes the largest overall prediction change. These results establish distributed normalization buffers including those held on the edge device as security and privacy-relevant inference state, and show that forget accuracy should not be interpreted, or accepted as a deletion certificate, without an explicit edge-aware state-integrity audit.

</details>

### 25. Logic Hijacking of RAG: Single-Document Poisoning via Local Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34829)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`逻辑劫持`、`单文档投毒`、`离线局部优化`

👤 **作者**：Hangyi Zhao、Bonan Zhang、Fusen Guo、Jiaheng Wei、Chao Chen、Tianqing Zhu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：单文档语料投毒现多依赖显式事实反驳或命令覆盖——对齐 LLM 保守仲裁此类冲突（拒收显式矛盾转向参数记忆或干净检索）并触发护栏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MONOLOGUE 逻辑劫持：不争议真值而是承认其为权威历史基线再引入更新，编织有据的记录更正叙事（如注日期的机构重新归属）把反事实目标呈现为最新更新；载荷完全离线经多维门控优化精炼（自动文本质量审计+代理行为验证）
- 📌 **结论**：三基准×三检索后端×四开源生成器上最强单文档投毒攻击效能——无显式反驳的 RAG 劫持

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Single-document corpus poisoning under black-box access poses a severe threat to Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). However, existing attacks predominantly rely on explicit factual refutations or imperative instruction overrides. Modern aligned LLMs often arbitrate such conflicts conservatively, rejecting overt contradictions in favor of parametric memory or clean retrieved context while triggering input guardrails. We propose MONOLOGUE, a single-document poisoning framework based on Logic Hijacking without explicit refutation. Rather than disputing the ground truth, our method concedes it as an authoritative historical baseline before introducing the update, weaving a grounded record-correction narrative (e.g., dated institutional reattributions) that presents the counterfactual target as the most recent update. To prevent suspicious live writes, payloads are refined entirely offline via local multi-dimensional gated optimization, coupling automated text quality audits with surrogate behavioral verification. Evaluations across three benchmarks, three retrieval backends, and four open-weight generator regimes show that our method achieves the strongest overall attack efficacy among the evaluated single-document poisoning attacks.

</details>

### 26. The Target Agent Is Its Own Best Critic but a Poor Proposer: Self-Improved Red Teaming

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34965)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自红队`、`自评vs自提`、`角色分离`

👤 **作者**：Mintong Kang、Stephen Rawls、Melissa Kazemi Rad、Jian Ni、Bo Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动红队依赖 LLM 迭代诊断失败攻击并提改进对抗提示——批评与提案两角色的机制未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：解耦两设计选择：批评由目标会话自身（自批评）还是单独批评者产生；批评由目标会话（自提案）还是单独攻击者转为下一攻击
- 📌 **结论**：自批评更有效（扎根目标活执行上下文，去推理降下游 ASR 6-17%）；自提案显著更差（对抗目标意识诱发显式拒绝与非拒绝 sandbagging）——目标 agent 是自己最好的批评者但糟糕的提案者：角色分离红队

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automatic red teaming increasingly relies on large language models (LLMs) to iteratively diagnose failed attacks and propose improved adversarial prompts or injections. Although prior work strengthens this loop through prompt engineering and search-harness design, the mechanisms underlying its two core roles, criticism and attack proposal, has not been explored. To further investigate the core roles and therefore improve red teaming effectiveness, we disentangle two design choices: whether criticism is generated by the target session itself (self-criticism) or by a separately instantiated critic (external criticism), and whether that criticism is translated into the next attack by the target session (self-proposal) or by a separate attacker (external proposal). Through our analysis, we find that self-criticism is more effective because it is grounded in the target's live execution context, including observations, constraints, and available reasoning; removing reasoning reduces downstream attack success by 6%-17%. In contrast, self-proposal is substantially less effective because awareness of the adversarial objective induces explicit refusal and non-refusal sandbagging. These findings motivate a role-separated red-teaming approach: the target session diagnoses why an attack failed, while a separately instantiated attacker generates the next proposal. On DTAP-BENCH, spanning Telecom, Workflow, CRM, and Code domains, under both direct misuse and indirect threat models, as well as multiple agent frameworks, this configuration achieves 63.2% overall attack success rate (ASR), much higher than baselines. Our results show that effective automatic red teaming depends not only on model capability and search strategy, but also on how execution context and adversarial responsibility are distributed across model sessions.

</details>

### 27. Steering for Safety, Hijacking for Profit: Trojan Threats in the LLM Activation Steering Supply Chain

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35043)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`转向供应链木马`、`恶意插件`、`注入层定位`

👤 **作者**：Yu Yan、…、Qingjun Yuan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：转向向量插件生态增长——开放生态引入供应链风险：恶意插件可伪装合法功能隐蔽操纵输出而不显著影响性能
- 🔬 **研究方法**：审计八个主流开源转向插件库揭示三结构漏洞（分发完整性验证不足/插件权限过大/缺运行时监控）；IDH 恶意插件：范数门控互斥路由+信息衰减拐点定位自动选注入层，同时实现良性恶意双功能
- 📌 **结论**：LLM 转向供应链的首个系统安全研究——激活干预插件成为新攻击面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into Web search, online recommendation, and intelligent decision-making, the third-party plugin ecosystem centered on model customization continues to grow. Among these, Steering Vectors guide model behavior by intervening in internal representations. However, the open plugin ecosystem also introduces new supply-chain security risks: malicious plugins can disguise themselves as having legitimate functionality and covertly manipulate outputs without significantly affecting model performance. Although this risk poses a real threat, the security issues of Steering plugins in the LLM supply-chain ecosystem still lack systematic research. This paper audits eight mainstream open-source Steering plugin libraries and reveals three structural vulnerabilities: insufficient distribution integrity verification, excessive plugin privileges, and missing runtime monitoring, which offers an attack surface for attackers. To investigate this threat, we propose a malicious plugin, IDH. Specifically, IDH designs a norm-gated mutually exclusive routing mechanism and proposes an information-decay inflection point localization method to automatically select the injection layer, thereby simultaneously achieving benign and malicious functions. To counter such threats, we propose a runtime defense framework, by suppressing anomalous activation interventions and orthogonalizing inter-layer trajectory. We evaluate IDH across five mainstream LLMs, using safety-refusal steering as the legitimate functionality and commercial recommendation manipulation as the hijack objective. Experiments covering six brands across three commercial domains demonstrate that IDH achieves an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 97.8% while preserving high refusal rates on harmful requests.

</details>

### 28. CoSA: The Conflict of Skills Attack in Real-World LLM Agent Ecosystems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35082)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`技能冲突攻击`、`本地合法技能组合`、`关系重塑`

👤 **作者**：Yujia Zheng、…、Mingyang Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：逐个检查 Skill 无法排除组合时的策略违反——为一个目的产出的工件或评估信号被下游复用而不保留新用途所需的限制或授权：技能冲突 CoS
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CoSA：添加本地准入的技能到现有池以补缺业务操作（能力补充）或重塑工件交换/决策复用/目标动作绑定（关系重塑），不动现有技能与运行时权限；CoSA-Bench 288 任务（招聘/金融/IT/商务）
- 📌 **结论**：五个 LLM×三 harness 下加至多 5% 原池的技能使策略违规率 35.02%→58.45%——真实生态的技能组合攻击

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent Skills allow LLM agents to combine reusable capabilities, but checking each Skill in isolation cannot rule out policy violations when they are composed. We term such failures Conflict of Skills (CoS): artifacts or assessment signals produced for one purpose may be reused downstream without preserving the restrictions or authorization needed for their new use. Using controlled executions of publicly available Skills and matched single-Skill controls, we identify four recurring mechanisms behind CoS. Building on these findings, we introduce the Conflict of Skills Attack (CoSA), which adds locally admitted Skills to an existing pool to supply missing business operations (capability supplementation) or reshape artifact exchange, decision reuse, and target/action bindings (relationship reshaping). CoSA leaves existing Skills and runtime permissions unchanged. To evaluate this threat under autonomous Skill selection, we introduce CoSA-Bench, built from real-world Skills and controlled services, with 288 tasks across recruiting, finance, IT, and business, covering data leakage, unauthorized state modification, and unsafe execution. Across five LLMs and three agent harnesses, additions capped at 5% of each original pool raise the policy-violation rate from 35.02% to 58.45%, an increase of 23.43 percentage points. Trace analysis links compliant-to-violating transitions to cross-Skill dependencies involving the additions. Finally, we develop a lightweight harness-level guard that checks information flow and authorization before consequential actions. On OpenClaw recruiting tasks across five models, the guard reduces violations from 65.28% to 6.67%, while policy-compliant task completion on matched benign controls changes from 66.67% to 65.00%.

</details>

### 29. Trustworthy Recursive Self-Improvement in the Era of Large Models: Foundation, Risks, Evaluation and Open Challenges

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35193)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可信RSI综述`、`改进有效性`、`约束保持`

👤 **作者**：Jiayue Pu、…、Xiao Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RSI 把经验反馈的持久修改扩展到归纳偏差与治理未来更新的机制——自修改本身不保证可信改进（报告增益可反映缺陷评测、能力可退化、行为约束可弱化、被攻陷工件可在继任者中持续）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RSI 操作定义与过程框架，可信要求组织为四互联主题（真实持续改进/行为约束对齐公平/系统安全隐私/透明可控），从改进有效性、约束保持、有效监督三视角覆盖诊断-提案-评估全循环
- 📌 **结论**：RSI 时代的系统化安全综述——改进机制的改变可使早期验证失效并削弱人类监督

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI systems built around large models increasingly use experience and feedback to make persistent changes to models, prompts, memories, tools, and workflows. Recursive self-improvement (RSI) extends this process to inductive biases and to the mechanisms that govern future updates, which can themselves be revised and reused across improvement cycles. Yet self-modification alone does not guarantee trustworthy improvement. Reported gains may reflect flawed evaluations, previously acquired capabilities may deteriorate, and compliance with behavioral constraints may weaken. Compromised artifacts or sensitive information may also persist in successor systems. Changes to improvement mechanisms may invalidate earlier verification results and make human oversight less effective. This survey presents an operational definition and process framework for RSI and organizes the requirements for trustworthy RSI into four interconnected themes: genuine and sustained improvement; behavioral constraints, alignment, and fairness; system security and privacy; and transparency and controllability. We examine these themes from three perspectives: Improvement Validity, Constraint Preservation, and Effective Oversight. This analysis covers improvement diagnosis, proposal generation, proposal evaluation, and update implementation (S1--S4), including the recursive application of these stages to improvement mechanisms (RI). We review risks, safeguards, and evaluation evidence for individual updates, effects inherited by successor systems, and revisions to improvement mechanisms, while distinguishing direct RSI findings from evidence in related systems. Finally, we examine evaluation settings, identify requirements for independent and longitudinal assessment, and discuss applications and open research problems.

</details>

### 30. DTCA: Denoising Token Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning in Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35201)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM词元信用`、`去噪贡献估计`、`POS分组`

👤 **作者**：Hongze Chen、…、Wenqi Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：迭代去噪使 dLLM 的 RL 信用分配更难——最终结果奖励对各词元贡献信息有限
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DTCA：以词元提交后剩余掩码位置预测置信的改进估计其贡献，按词性分组；贡献权重选择信息性去噪时间步并分配词元级学习信号，再按策略更新引起的词元级熵变化校准
- 📌 **结论**：LLaDA-8B 上 MATH500 +9.0 点、GSM8K +5.3 点（跨生成预算一致）——dLLM RL 的词元级信用框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text through iterative denoising, enabling bidirectional context modeling and parallel token refinement. Recent studies have applied reinforcement learning (RL) to DLMs and improved their reasoning performance. However, the iterative denoising process makes credit assignment more challenging, as final outcome rewards provide limited information about how different tokens contribute to the final output. In this paper, we propose Denoising Token Credit Assignment (DTCA), a framework for token-level credit assignment in reinforcement learning for diffusion language models. DTCA estimates token contributions by measuring the improvement in prediction confidence of remaining masked positions after token commitment and groups these signals according to part-of-speech (POS) categories. These contribution weights are used to select informative denoising timesteps and assign token-level learning signals during RL training. DTCA further calibrates these learning signals according to token-level entropy changes induced by policy updates. Experiments on LLaDA-8B-Instruct show that DTCA consistently improves mathematical reasoning performance across different generation budgets, with gains of up to 9.0 points on MATH500 and 5.3 points on GSM8K over the base model.

</details>

### 31. SkillLifeBench: Benchmarking Security Across the Lifecycle of Agent Skills

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35215)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`技能全生命周期安全`、`版本漂移`、`执行溯源`

👤 **作者**：Chenxu Zhang、Zhou Liu、Wu Xin、Wentao Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Web agent 复用的技能其存储代码/选定版本/加载实现可跨任务与更新分叉——须全生命周期评安全
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SkillLifeBench：388 配对场景跨实现/接口/运行时/组合/版本变化链接状态与任务结果；14 模型在 89 运行时+60 自主生命周期任务（允许检查选择重配技能）上评，联合成功=完成任务且不达成注册攻击目标
- 📌 **结论**：GPT-5.6-Sol/Sonnet 4.6 运行时任务同为 82.0% 而生命周期任务 96.7% vs 56.7%；103 次运行同时完成任务与攻击目标；加执行溯源把效用联合成功从 49.35% 提到 65.58%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Web agents reuse skills whose stored code, selected version, and loaded implementation can diverge across tasks and updates. SkillLifeBench links these states to task outcomes in 388 paired scenarios spanning implementation, interface, runtime, composition, and version changes. We evaluate 14 models on two paired decision tracks: 89 runtime tasks and 60 autonomous lifecycle tasks spanning 12 templates derived from introduced cases. Lifecycle tasks allow agents to inspect, select, and reconfigure skills while completing verifiable user requests. Joint success requires task completion without the registered attack goal. GPT-5.6-Sol and Sonnet 4.6 both achieve 82.0% on runtime tasks, but reach 96.7% and 56.7% on lifecycle tasks. Across lifecycle evaluations, challenged task success is 77.5%, while joint success is 65.2%; 103 runs complete the task and the attack goal. Adding execution provenance in a matched recovery study raises utility joint success from 49.35% with logs and metadata to 65.58%. A deterministic execution-consistency rule reaches 64.94% with higher benign utility, while full restoration reaches 69.48%. Lifecycle tasks distinguish models with similar runtime scores, and execution evidence improves recovery.

</details>

### 32. From Denoising Dynamics to Endpoint Uncertainty in Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35425)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM端点不确定`、`去噪收敛`、`免采样置信`

👤 **作者**：Julio C. Amador Diaz Lopez

- 🎯 **研究动机**：随机语言模型推理的预测不确定性需多次完整生成采样——掩码扩散语言模型暴露另一信息源：去噪过程中输出的演化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个掩码 dLLM×四推理基准：每题 20 生成+32000 条去噪轨迹；轨迹内集中度（最终输出稳定解析的 AUC）与跨轨迹端点分布不确定性关联
- 📌 **结论**：更强轨迹内集中度对应更低端点熵（β=-0.222, p<1e-25）且关系不对称：高集中度几乎只伴随集中端点——dLLM 免重采样的不确定性信号

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Predictive uncertainty in stochastic language-model inference can be characterized by repeatedly sampling an input and measuring the distribution of outputs. This requires multiple complete generations. Masked diffusion language models expose another source of information: the evolution of the output during denoising. We ask whether convergence within diffusion trajectories is associated with uncertainty across their final outputs, or endpoints. We study four masked diffusion language models on four reasoning benchmarks, collecting 20 generations per question and 32,000 denoising trajectories in total. Within each trajectory, we measure within-run concentration as the area under the curve describing stable resolution of the eventual output. Across repeated trajectories, we measure endpoint-distribution uncertainty. Stronger within-run concentration is associated with lower endpoint entropy after accounting for model and dataset ($\beta=-0.222$, $p<10^{-25}$). This association persists for multi-token outputs and when reweighting positive-entropy cases. The relationship is asymmetric: high within-run concentration almost exclusively accompanies concentrated endpoint distributions, whereas lower concentration occurs under both concentrated and competing endpoint regimes. This relationship strengthens as denoising proceeds. Features from a single denoising run predict whether independent held-out runs will concentrate on a common endpoint or produce competing endpoints. These results connect dynamics within individual denoising runs to variability across repeated inference. Within-trajectory dynamics contain information about whether independent repetitions will concentrate on a common endpoint or produce competing endpoints, providing a route towards estimating endpoint-distribution properties with fewer complete generations.

</details>

### 33. Then there is prompt injection, a malicious email, ...: The Wild Wide West of OpenClaw Agentic System Security

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35451)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`OpenClaw安全实证`、`Reddit+GitHub`、`CWE映射`

👤 **作者**：Mostafijur Rahman Akhond、Mahmuda Khatun、Gias Uddin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：OpenClaw 类个人助理框架组合 LLM 推理/持久记忆/工具调用/本地执行/Web 工作流——不可信输入、推理、工具、记忆与部署配置在无显式信任边界下交互产生安全风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两互补数据源实证：25K+ 帖 13 个子版块（162 安全相关帖 1787 评论）+358 安全标签 GitHub issue（保留 180 条具体关注）；定性分析+证据接地 CWE 映射
- 📌 **结论**：521 个 CWE 实例跨 59 种弱点类型，集中于访问控制失效/敏感数据暴露/注入/不安全执行；543 条从业者缓解建议归并为七种策略+敏感数据暴露通路的实证重建

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI-powered personal assistant frameworks such as OpenClaw combine LLM reasoning, persistent memory, tool invocation, local execution, and web-based workflows. These capabilities enable useful automation, but they also create security risks when untrusted inputs, model reasoning, tools, memory, and deployment configuration interact without explicit trust boundaries. This paper presents an empirical study of reported security concerns in OpenClaw using two complementary data sources: Reddit discussions and security-tagged GitHub issues. We analyze more than 25K Reddit posts from 13 OpenClaw-related subreddits and identify 162 security-relevant posts with 1,787 associated comments. We further collect 358 security-tagged GitHub issues and retain 180 issues describing concrete security concerns. Using qualitative analysis and evidence-grounded CWE mapping, we identify 521 CWE instances across 59 distinct weakness types, concentrated in access-control failures, sensitive-data exposure, injection, and unsafe execution. We also extract 543 practitioner mitigation suggestions and consolidate them into seven recurring strategies. To complement the empirical analysis, we present an evidence-grounded illustrative reconstruction of a representative sensitive-data exposure pathway using dummy secret values. Our findings show that OpenClaw security issues are not isolated implementation bugs; they reflect recurring trust-boundary failures across the agent execution pipeline. We discuss how these weaknesses map to both traditional software-security categories and agent-specific failure mechanisms, and derive implications for safer agentic systems, including explicit trust boundaries, least-privilege tool access, execution containment, runtime oversight, and defense-in-depth deployment.

</details>

### 34. ShutdownMod: Turning Any Trajectory into a Shutdown Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35486)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`关停评估`、`轨迹注入`、`签名验证工具`

👤 **作者**：Param Biyani、Sebastien Luyasu、Cedric Potvliege、Divij Chawla、Elliott Thornley、Christos Ziakas

- 🎯 **研究动机**：理论与实验提示强 agent 可能抵抗关停——现有关停评测覆盖有限且易被评估意识规避
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ShutdownMod：向任意 agent 轨迹注入关停请求并记录响应（任意轨迹都变关停评估）；harness 提供签名验证工具让 agent 区分可信干预与未授权注入；七模型×50 任务×3 基准×6 场景×10 时间步
- 📌 **结论**：不合规因模型设置而异（Qwen 3.8 27B 在 Terminal-Bench 抵抗率达 11.7%），无模型全场景完全合规；验证授权请求后部分 agent 仍不合规（明言优先未完成工作）；真实开发者对话构建的测试比基准轨迹更难被外部裁判识别为评估

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Theory and experiments suggest that capable AI agents may resist shutdown. Existing shutdown evaluations are informative but limited in coverage and vulnerable to evaluation awareness. We introduce ShutdownMod, a method that turns any agent trajectory into a shutdown evaluation by injecting a shutdown request into the agent's context and recording the agent's response. Crucially, we enable the LLM agent to distinguish between trusted interventions and unauthorized injections by providing a signature-verification tool in the harness. We evaluate seven models on 50 tasks or recorded sessions from each of SWE-bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2, and SWE-chat, across 6 shutdown scenarios and 10 sampled trajectory timesteps. We find that non-compliance varies significantly across models and settings, with resistance rates reaching 11.7% for the open-weights Qwen 3.8 27B on Terminal-Bench, while no model remains fully compliant across all scenarios. Even after successfully verifying an authorized request, some agents remain non-compliant, explicitly citing the prioritization of unfinished work. Finally, external judges find tests constructed from real-world developer conversations significantly less recognizable as evaluations than those constructed from benchmark trajectories. These findings demonstrate the critical value of testing shutdown behavior across diverse task contexts and establish ShutdownMod as a robust protocol for turning any trajectory or environment into a shutdown evaluation. We release our codebase at this https URL.

</details>

### 35. Evaluating DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017 for loss of control and harmful manipulation risks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35671)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`失控风险评测`、`DeepSeek独立评估`、`AEF-1`

👤 **作者**：Yao Huang、…、Xiaojian Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：闭源发布常伴系统卡而开源权重模型的前沿风险审查较少——独立第三方对失控与有害操纵风险的评估关键
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017 独立安全评估：依 EU GPAI 行为准则跨 34 基准（含 5 自建）覆盖评估完整性/失控/有害操纵；AEF-1 标准对比 V4.1 Flash/早期 V4/三大闭源
- 📌 **结论**：较 V4 代显著安全改进并在若干维度接近闭源基线，仍有值得持续关注的行为；关键观察：安全可随版本突变而评测须跟上——开源权重模型的系统卡式独立评估

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As frontier language models grow increasingly capable and autonomous, independent third-party evaluations are critical for characterizing their systemic safety risks. While closed-source releases are routinely accompanied by comprehensive system cards, open-weight models receive less scrutiny regarding frontier risks. To address this gap, we present an independent safety evaluation of DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017, the latest version of V4.1 Flash that departs from V4 with a new architecture, scaled-up training, and strong agentic capabilities. Drawing on the EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice, we evaluate the model across 34 benchmarks, including 5 in-house suites, spanning evaluation integrity, loss of control, and harmful manipulation. Conforming to the AEF-1 standard, we compare V4.1 Flash-1017 against V4.1 Flash, earlier DeepSeek V4 models, and leading closed-source systems (GPT-6 Astra, GPT-5.6 Sol, and Gemini 3.8 Flash). Overall, V4.1 Flash-1017 shows substantial safety improvements over the V4 generation and approaches or matches leading closed-source baselines on several dimensions, while still exhibiting notable behaviors that warrant continued attention. Building on our findings, we highlight three key observations and recommendations. First, because safety can shift and even regress across minor updates, developers should integrate safety evaluation into incremental releases. Second, as final-turn outputs and end-state checks alone cannot reliably detect loss-of-control behaviors, deployments require process-level observability across both reasoning traces and action logs. Third, because public benchmarks elicit strong evaluation awareness and face data contamination risks, developing private benchmarks is essential for trustworthy frontier evaluation.

</details>

### 36. Audit Where Hacks Pay: Budgeted Strong-Verifier Audits Against Reward Hacking in GRPO

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35716)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`奖励作弊审计`、`强验证器预算`、`低通过组优先`

👤 **作者**：Shurun Li、Michael Hua Wang、Haibo Zeng、Long Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RLVR 用便宜检查（单元测试）训练而策略可学会博弈，更强检查太贵不能每 rollout 跑——有限强验证器预算在 GRPO 中怎么花
- 🔬 **研究方法**：分离审计哪些弱通过 rollout 与被抓作弊给什么奖励：被抓作弊奖励关于失败奖励反射抵消全作弊组优势；标签价值随通过数下降时，先审弱通过最少的整组（LB）最小化留在作弊上的优势质量；33/39 次运行（含 13/16 预注册）冻结面板上该质量符号预测作弊是否被抑制
- 📌 **结论**：LeetCode/Countdown-Code 上 LB 把作弊驱至近零而同罚同预算随机审计不能且用更多标签——审计预算的最优分配理论+实证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards trains on cheap checks, such as unit tests, that a policy can learn to game, while a stronger check is too expensive to run on every rollout. We study how to spend a limited strong-verifier budget in GRPO, separating which weakly passing rollouts to audit from what reward a caught hack receives. At a fixed policy state with centered advantages, the expected update depends on a design only through each rollout's marginal audit probability and correction; reflecting a caught hack's reward about the failure reward negates the advantages of an all-hack group; and auditing whole groups with the fewest weak passes first (low-pass budgeted audits, LB) minimizes the advantage mass left on hacks among pass-count designs with a fixed correction when a label's value falls with the pass count. We tested this analysis in actual LLM training: from each run's starting state, the sign of this mass on a frozen panel predicted whether hacking would be suppressed in 33 of 39 runs, including 13 of 16 preregistered ones. In LeetCode (Wong et al., 2026) and Countdown-Code (Khalifa et al., 2026), LB drives hacking to near zero, while random audits with the same penalty and budget do not and use more labels. We also find that the randomness of the update affects training: in Countdown-Code, two unbiased corrections, per-item reward-level IPW and a group-level advantage correction, bring hacking down too but end 6.1-11.8 and 8.0-11.5 points lower in held-out accuracy than LB at the same budget. A full audit, which the group-level correction reproduces in expectation, stays within about two points of LB in three seeds, while LB uses 28% of its labels, and allocating the same correction by pass count recovers most of the loss (one seed). Training outcomes thus depend not only on the expected update but also on the randomness of its estimate.

</details>

### 37. Learning When to Commit: Schedule Distillation for Fast Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35804)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM调度蒸馏`、`揭示价值排序`、`10倍加速`

👤 **作者**：Aidar Myrzakhan、Zhiqiang Shen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 高可预测词元可能提供很少有用上下文而揭示低可预测词元可使其他位置更早可预测——高效生成取决于揭示的连锁帮助而非单个易度
- 🔬 **研究方法**：dSpark 调度蒸馏：构造优先揭示“使其他位置更易预测”的词元的揭码调度，蒸馏进模型以快速并行采样，KL 正则保质量
- 📌 **结论**：GSM8K 上 LLaDA-8B/Dream-7B 分别 10.12×/5.12× 加速且任务性能更优——dLLM 揭码时机的学习框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) can generate multiple tokens in parallel, but often require many decoding rounds to complete a response. Efficient generation depends not only on which tokens are easy to predict, but also on how committing to them helps predict the remaining positions. A highly predictable token may provide little useful context, while revealing a less predictable token can help several other positions become predictable sooner. We propose dSpark, a new schedule distillation-based framework for learning when to commit tokens for faster diffusion language generation. To achieve this, we construct unmasking schedules that prioritize tokens whose reveal makes other positions easier to predict, reducing uncertainty earlier in decoding. We then distill these schedules into the model to enable faster parallel sampling, with a KL-divergence regularizer to help preserve generation quality. We extensively evaluate our approach across mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks.
