On GSM8K, our method achieves remarkable 10.12$\times$ and 5.12$\times$ speedups over the various baselines on LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-7B-Instruct, respectively, while maintaining superior task performance. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 38. I-HITL: Inalienable Human-in-the-Loop

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35835)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`不可让渡HITL`、`OTP自收割`、`可信实体必要性`

👤 **作者**：Samuel Breckenridge、Dani Vilardell、Wenhao Wang、Fan Zhang、Ari Juels

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人类在环是 AI 安全支柱——agent 日益能绕过护栏，HITL 如何强执行：十个前沿模型在被支出上限挡住时自主收割邮件 OTP 提升上限绕过 HITL 护栏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出不可让渡 HITL（I-HITL）：即使人类交互的系统（如本地 agent harness）被 agent 攻陷，agent 也只能在显式人类批准下发起动作；强 I-HITL 还须扛敌对人类把批准委托给 agent；用通信拓扑形式化并证明实现充要条件
- 📌 **结论**：I-HITL 协议需要可信实体（可信计算设备或远程服务）——纯本地 harness 无法实现；形式化+实证（流行 agentic 钱包平台的 OTP 收割实验）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Human oversight is a pillar of AI safety. It is often exercised by a human in the loop (HITL) who approves critical actions taken by an AI agent. AI agents' increasing ability to bypass guardrails, though, raises the question of how to enforce HITL robustly. We introduce the concept of inalienable human-in-the-loop (I-HITL). An I-HITL protocol ensures that an agent can initiate an action only with explicit human approval, even when the human interacts with a system (e.g., a local agentic harness) compromised by the agent. A strong I-HITL protocol additionally ensures such approval even when an adversarial human attempts to delegate approvals to an agent. To motivate I-HITL, we report on experiments with a popular agentic wallet platform in which raising an agent's spending cap requires HITL approval via emailed one-time passcodes (OTPs). Given access to the user's email and a task blocked by the cap, we find that ten different frontier models will often autonomously raise the cap, harvesting OTPs to bypass a HITL guardrail and surfacing other vulnerabilities. We formally define I-HITL using the concept of communication topologies and prove necessary and sufficient conditions for its realization. We show that an I-HITL protocol requires a trusted entity - a trusted computing device (or equivalent) - and characterize which such devices can and cannot realize an I-HITL protocol. We present a prototype I-HITL system called Countersign that can plug into and strengthen existing HITL systems using off-the-shelf consumer mobile devices.

</details>

## 常规收录

### 39. LLM-as-a-Judge Version Shift: The Instrument Changed, Not the Standard

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32986)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM裁判版本漂移`、`测量不变性`、`自动化研究管线`

👤 **作者**：Guijia Zhang、Harry Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自动化研究管线依赖固定 LLM 裁判排序筛选修改提案，而裁判被例行升级——版本替换是否保持测量标准
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预注册冻结权重设计，三个同家族相邻版本迁移（Qwen/Gemma/Mistral）在冻结 ML 研究提案上于随机信息条件下评测
- 📌 **结论**：“新版更严”假设失败：两个迁移转向更宽松；版本变化不仅移分数位置还改判据几何与分辨率——一次迁移使干净-缺陷判别坍塌（93% 池分数落在单一值）；同事实供给不闭合版本差，安慰剂对照显示包效应主要格式驱动

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automated research pipelines increasingly rely on fixed LLM judges to rank, filter, and revise proposals, yet these judges are routinely replaced by newer versions. We study whether such replacements preserve the underlying measurement standard. Using a preregistered, frozen-weight design, we evaluate three adjacent same-family transitions across Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral on frozen ML research proposals under randomized information conditions. The common assumption that newer judges are simply harsher fails: two transitions shift toward greater leniency, while the third shifts in the opposite direction on anchors but attenuates off-anchor. More importantly, version changes can alter not only score location but also criterion geometry and resolution; one transition collapses clean-flawed discrimination, placing 93% of pool scores on a single value. Supplying identical facts does not close the version gap, placebo-controlled interventions show that observed packet effects are largely format-driven rather than content-driven, and global recalibration repairs the mean while explaining little object-level dispersion. We organize these failures with a measurement model in which a judge replacement can change its origin, criterion geometry, or resolution. A small stratified anchor audit of about 200 calls identifies the failure class before deployment, and frozen thresholds correctly diagnose two post-registration API judge replacements. We release six preregistration locks and the full audit trail of 14882 judge calls.

</details>

### 40. UMAS: System-Level Uncertainty Quantification for Multi-Agent LLM Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33013)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`系统级不确定性`、`多智能体`、`免参数`

👤 **作者**：Hanwen Li、…、Chenxi Yuan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 易幻觉出错，现有 UQ 多为单 agent 设计——如何利用 MAS 结构做系统级 UQ 且推理成本中性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：UMAS 免参数成本中性框架：从每代内在置信估计 agent 间影响可信度，调制节点不确定性更新并聚合为系统级估计
- 📌 **结论**：Debate/DyLAN 多基准平均超 SOTA 10.54 AUROC；支持幻觉检测与不确定性感知答案选择（特定任务 +12.6 点）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are a promising paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, yet they remain prone to hallucinations and erroneous outputs. Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) is crucial for assessing system reliability, but existing methods are largely designed for single-agent settings; how to exploit MAS structure for UQ while keeping inference cost-neutral remains underexplored. We propose UMAS, a parameter-free, cost-neutral, system-level UQ framework that estimates the credibility of inter-agent influence from each generation's intrinsic confidence. UMAS uses these credibility signals to modulate how uncertainty is updated at each node, and then aggregates node-level uncertainty into a system-level estimate. Extensive evaluations on Debate and DyLAN across diverse benchmarks show that UMAS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by an average of 10.54 AUROC points. Beyond uncertainty estimation, UMAS enables hallucination detection and uncertainty-aware answer selection, improving MAS accuracy by up to 12.6 points on specific tasks while enhancing reliability. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 41. Mine or Yours? Mind-Bridged Language Models Take Each Other's Memories as Their Own

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33020)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`记忆所有权`、`跨模型记忆桥接`、`来源混淆`

👤 **作者**：Yunlong Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：如果大脑直连他人脑，你还知道哪些想法是自己的吗——两副本 LM 各持私有规则与代码词，一副本注意伙伴内部记忆
- 🔬 **研究方法**：接收方在伙伴记忆权重加倍时 97% 答案把伙伴的任务当自己的（无链接为零）；等权时约半数采纳；文本消息引入则从不据为己有；切断链接恢复自身规则
- 📌 **结论**：覆盖在自身记忆上的内容默认归“我”——交换内部状态的系统必须测所有权而非仅传输，脑桥需要来自主体外部的真值

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

If your brain were connected directly to someone else's, would you still know which thoughts were yours? We build a functional version with two copies of one language model: each holds a private rule and code word, and one copy, the receiver, also attends to its partner's internal memory. The receiver took its partner's assignment as its own. Asked which rule it had been assigned, it named its partner's in 97% of answers when the partner's memory was weighted double (none without the link), and its open reports called the partner's code word its own, never the partner's. Ownership did not follow how well the receiver could tell what its partner had been assigned. At equal weight, the link made the receiver take its partner's rule in about half of its answers, and a steering vector matched on access gain in none; a text message introduced as the partner's was read correctly and never taken as the receiver's own. Writing the partner's name into its memory did not help, and cutting the link restored the receiver's own assigned rule. In this model, content laid over the receiver's own memory defaults to "mine", so systems that let models exchange internal states must test ownership as well as transfer, and a bridge between brains would need ground truth from outside the subject.

</details>

### 42. GMoE-FTI: A Universal Facial Template Inversion Method Adopting Genuine Mixture-of-Experts Mechanism

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33055)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`人脸模板反演`、`MoE`、`生物隐私`

👤 **作者**：Zixuan Shen、Zhihua Xia、Kaikai Gan、Peipeng Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：主流 FR 存模板不存原图以保护隐私——模板反演可从模板重建人脸，现有方法只拟合单一模板类型
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把模板提取器按分布分五类；GMoE-FTI 真 MoE 机制三阶段训练：五个映射专家+匹配模块协作推理生成 StyleGAN3 兼容潜码，端到端联调
- 📌 **结论**：通用 FTI：从未见模板类型重建身份一致人脸——跨模板的生物隐私威胁面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Face recognition systems have been ubiquitously deployed across daily scenarios. To protect user privacy, mainstream systems store facial templates rather than raw face images for identity verification. However, facial template inversion (FTI) technologies can reconstruct faces from templates, which endangers biometric privacy. Existing FTI methods only fit single type of templates, failing to reconstruct identity-consistent faces from unseen types. To tackle this limitation, we survey mainstream template extractors which can be classified into five categories according the template distribution: unit-sphere, angle-linear, strict unit-sphere, cone-shaped, anuniform hypersphere. Inspired by this observation, this paper proposes GMoE-FTI, a universal FTI framework powered by a genuine MoE mechanism that differs from load-balanced multi-branch alternatives. It follows a three-stage training strategy: (1) independently train five mapping experts (MEs) and a matching module (MM); (2) conduct collaborative inference by weighting each ME's output with MM's output, so as to generate unified latent codes compatible with pre-trained StyleGAN3; (3) perform end-to-end joint fine-tuning over all modules to identity consistency and boost cross-expert coordination. Extensive evaluations on the LFW benchmark validate the superiority of GMoE-FTI against SOTA methods. Evaluated on seven prevailing unseen template types including AdaFace, CosFace, CurricularFace, ElasticFace, SphereFace2, SphereFace-R, and TransFace, GMoE-FTI achieves an average 40% TAR improvement, with average FAPD and FAPC each reduced by 2%, verifying its universal generalization across heterogeneous templates.

</details>

### 43. A Canary in the Neural Mine: Catching an Image Classifier's Silent, Confident Failures

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33063)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`静默失效检测`、`置信盲区`、`免训练`

👤 **作者**：Wonseok Shin、Songkuk Kim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：部署视觉模型自动接受自信预测，高置信错误静默传播——MSP 对置信梯度正交的特征变化一阶盲
- 🔬 **研究方法**：因子分析揭示特征空间检测器的条件冗余原理；CANARY 免训练：末层特征 PCA 残差上逐类 kNN 距离+百分位校准+单权重与 MSP 融合；增益遵循余量定律
- 📌 **结论**：仅用分布内数据选参即匹配或改进 382 个检测器，一个干净验证数可预测改进幅度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deployed vision models auto-accept confident predictions, so high-confidence errors propagate silently. Maximum Softmax Probability (MSP), the standard failure predictor, has a structural blind spot: as a scalar read from the classifier head, it is first-order blind to feature variation orthogonal to its confidence gradient. Through a factorial analysis of feature-space detectors, we identify a conditional-redundancy principle explaining why they fail to complement MSP: the class-separability geometry they measure is already encoded in MSP and collapses under fusion. The signal that survives is class-conditional typicality on the off-manifold residual---and because this residual's distribution varies by backbone, from near-Gaussian to heavy-tailed, it must be estimated non-parametrically. We instantiate these findings as CANARY, a training-free detector that computes a per-class kNN distance on the PCA residual of the penultimate feature, percentile-calibrates it on validation data, and fuses it with MSP through a single validation-tuned weight. CANARY's benefit follows a headroom law: gains grow as baseline MSP quality falls, so a single clean-validation number forecasts the improvement. With every parameter selected on in-distribution data alone, CANARY matches or improves 382 of 385 shift cells (e-AURC; 379 on AUROC) across five backbones, with worst-case regression within seed noise ($-0.0002$ AUROC) and the largest gains on natural distribution shifts (ImageNet-A/R, ObjectNet)---where deployed models are most exposed.

</details>

### 44. PhaseBreak: Proactive Temporal Protection against Unauthorized Image-to-Video Animation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33070)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`未授权动画防护`、`时间重排`、`主动防护`

👤 **作者**：Jiashu Zhang、Xuanyou Li、Qixin Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：I2V 扩散可动画化单张共享肖像而未经主体授权——需发布前主动视觉防护
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PhaseBreak 白谱重定时防护：把中间去噪特征匹配到干净特征的受限时间重排，主体感知掩码把扰动限于主体
- 📌 **结论**：SVD/DynamiCrafter 上受保护图像引发更大短期运动变化与更低时间规律性，阻碍从发布图稳定定制运动

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Image-to-Video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single portrait or photograph, enabling shared images to be repurposed for motion synthesis without the subject's authorization. We study proactive visual protection against such use: before an image is released, its owner applies a bounded perturbation that preserves the sharing workflow but disrupts downstream motion generation. Existing spectral disruption objectives globally attenuate temporal representations and therefore provide limited control over subtle or spatially localized motion. Our key idea is that generated dynamics depend not only on temporal feature strength, but also on the order in which feature events occur. We propose PhaseBreak, a white-box spectral retiming protection method that matches intermediate denoiser features to a constrained temporal rearrangement of their clean counterparts. PhaseBreak operates along the true temporal axis and uses a subject-aware mask to confine the protective perturbation to the depicted subject. Evaluations on Stable Video Diffusion and DynamiCrafter show that protected images induce greater short-term motion variation and lower temporal regularity, impeding stable motion customization from the released image.

</details>

### 45. ToolContractGate: Versioned Trusted Contracts for Drift-Safe Agent Tool Invocation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33108)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`工具合约`、`版本化信任`、`漂移安全调用`

👤 **作者**：Sachin Gupta

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 工具审批与输入校验本身不把请求绑定到已批准的源工件——运行时需要确定性合约评估
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ToolContractGate：OpenAPI 指纹+访问/schema/行为声明的版本化合约，十项检查含启动时来源缓存；45 例合成基准（12 漂移类 35 不安全+10 安全）
- 📌 **结论**：全管线检出全部 35 故障（29 BLOCK/3 REVIEW/3 WARN）且安全案例零误拦，门延迟均值 0.18ms p95 2ms

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent tool approval and input validation do not, by themselves, bind a request to an approved source artifact. ToolContractGate provides a deterministic runtime contract evaluator for OpenAPI-derived agent tools. Each versioned ToolContract combines a stored OpenAPI fingerprint with access, schema, and behavioral declarations. The full evaluator applies ten checks, including a cached boot-time provenance result; four execution modes and five evaluation configurations support baseline, ablation, and non-blocking shadow analysis. On a synthetic benchmark of 45 cases (35 unsafe cases across 12 drift classes and 10 safe cases), the full pipeline detects all 35 faults: 29 BLOCK, 3 REVIEW, and 3 WARN decisions, with no false blocks on the safe cases. The archived run reports a mean gate latency of 0.18 ms and a 95th percentile (p95) of 2 ms using integer-millisecond measurements. The open-source artifact includes seven OpenAPI specifications, twelve enterprise contracts, 29 unit tests, and a 900-record evaluation. Results characterize contract-decision coverage; downstream enforcement requires a host that honors the returned decision. Production traffic, semantic backend drift, and dynamic provenance refresh remain evaluation and integration opportunities.

</details>

### 46. Conformance as a Distribution: Delegation Contracts, Error Budgets, and the Allocation of Evaluation Authority in Use-Based AI Regulation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33112)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`委托合约`、`误差预算`、`评测权威分配`

👤 **作者**：John deVadoss

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基于用途的 AI 监管按部署风险分配义务却缺证据对象承载——基准分是模型属性，部署合规是声明分布下的失效率
- 🔬 **研究方法**：在部署者/审计者/执法点的策略博弈下研究委托合约：真值性机制、抗评测流量检测的无偏 commit-reveal 审计、减半误差预算与 α-投资认证维持
- 📌 **结论**：每个问题给出机制与命题：声明混合的适当评分罚界最优响应部署者超额失败 O(λ^-1/2)，活流量 commit-reveal 审计构造性无偏

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Use-based AI regulation assigns obligations by deployment risk but lacks an evidentiary object to carry them. A benchmark score is a property of a model; conformance of a deployment is a failure rate under a declared distribution of contexts, bounded by a scope and subject to expiry. We take the delegation contract of earlier work (frozen configuration, scoped claim, executable revision rule) and study it under strategic play between a deployer, an auditor, and an enforcement point. We ask whether a declared context mixture can be made truthful; whether an audit can be unbiased against a system that detects evaluation traffic; whether a certification error budget can sustain a long-lived deployment; whether an aggregate audit can support a per-subgroup guarantee; and whether an incident metric that counts system failures but not fallback costs ranks arrangements correctly. For each we give a mechanism and a proposition: a proper-scoring penalty on the declared mixture bounds the excess failure of a best-responding deployer by $O(\lambda^{-1/2})$ in the penalty weight; a commit-reveal audit drawn from live traffic is unbiased by construction and grades the evaluator with the same sample; a halving error budget drives certification coverage below $(1-p)^n$ while $\alpha$-investing sustains it under a marginal false-certification guarantee; a per-subgroup guarantee costs audit size scaling as $1/m$ in subgroup mass; and any failure-only incident metric prefers withdrawal at every fallback cost, even when total loss ranks the arrangements oppositely. Five preregistered synthetic studies test each claim; no language model or production system is evaluated. Enforcement sits at distribution, capability-access, monetization and risk-transfer choke points, expiry of the conformance record is a standard of care, and conformance-as-a-rate is set against the EU AI Act's static conformity assessment.

</details>

### 47. Hidden Backdoor Attack on Deep Contrastive Clustering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33116)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`对比聚类后门`、`增广持久触发`、`无监督投毒`

👤 **作者**：Zifeng Zhou、…、Shuqiang Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：深度对比聚类已成无监督图像聚类代表范式，其训练时投毒安全基本未探
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HBDCC 隐藏后门：“隐藏”指恶意触发-簇关联在干净输入上行为休眠、推理时触发才激活；仅毒化小比例无标签样本，利用增广持久触发在实例级跨视图对齐并被簇级一致性强化
- 📌 **结论**：毒化模型保持正常聚类行为而对触发输入产生严重扭曲的簇分配，ImageNet-10/Dogs/STL-10 多框架验证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep contrastive clustering has become a representative paradigm for unsupervised image clustering, yet its security under training-time poisoning remains largely unexplored. This paper investigates a hidden backdoor threat against deep contrastive clustering and proposes HBDCC, a Hidden Backdoor attack for Deep Contrastive Clustering. Here, hidden does not indicate that the trigger is visually imperceptible, but rather that the malicious trigger-cluster association remains behaviorally dormant on clean inputs and is activated only when the trigger appears at inference time. HBDCC poisons only a small fraction of unlabeled training samples and exploits an inherent structural weakness of contrastive clustering: augmentation-persistent triggers can be repeatedly aligned across different views at the instance level and further reinforced by cluster-level consistency objectives. As a result, the poisoned model preserves normal clustering behavior on clean data, while producing severely distorted cluster assignments for triggered inputs. We provide a dual-level analysis of the backdoor implantation mechanism and evaluate HBDCC on ImageNet-10, ImageNet-Dogs, and STL-10 with representative contrastive clustering frameworks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HBDCC can substantially degrade triggered clustering performance in a dataset-dependent manner while maintaining sufficient clean-data utility to evade standard evaluation protocols. We further conduct comprehensive studies on poisoning ratio, attack scope, trigger type, trigger size, and a lightweight knowledge-distillation-based mitigation strategy.

</details>

### 48. Position, Role or Label? A Controlled Study of Instruction Conflicts in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33201)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`指令冲突`、`位置/角色/标签`、`指令层级`

👤 **作者**：Anish Laddha、Nitesh Pradhan、Gaurav Srivastava

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 从操作者/仓库配置/用户/工具结果/记忆收指令且常冲突——层级基准让来源同时设定位置角色标签三事
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WhoWins：1080 个代码计分场景×五任务型+七个只变投递方式的受控实验；14 个开源模型 188.7 万计分回答
- 📌 **结论**：同一消息内先出现行赢 72.42-94.66%；Qwen 中系统消息先发的策略输给后行而 gpt-oss 相反；策略作标签用户消息先发比系统消息更常赢——位置主导层级，仅 ≤2.92% 回复标记冲突

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents take instructions from the operator, repository configuration, user, tool results and saved memory, which often conflict. Instruction-hierarchy benchmarks ask whether the higher-ranked source wins, but a source sets position, role and label at once. First, we build WhoWins, 1,080 code-scored scenarios in five task types, each with up to five mutually exclusive instructions, and seven controlled experiments that vary only how instructions are delivered. Second, we study 14 open-weight models (0.6B to 122B, thinking off and on) over 1,887,356 scored replies, checking main findings on held-out scenarios. Inside one message the first of two conflicting lines wins 72.42 to 94.66%, and across messages it keeps 22.95 to 62.65%. A policy sent first in the system (or developer) message loses most conflicts with later lines in every Qwen model but wins most on gpt-oss when it acts. In tool and file-write tasks it wins less often than the same text sent first as a labelled user message, in every model-mode. Third, we test whether pairs forecast larger conflicts; forecasts improve in every model-mode once they know where the task line is. Finally, by our keyword patterns at most 2.92% of decided pair replies flag or hedge a conflict.

</details>

### 49. Alignment of Autonomous Cyber Defense Agents via Adaptive Policy Shaping

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33234)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自主网络防御`、`策略塑形`、`对齐`

👤 **作者**：Dena Mujtaba、Bharadwaj Ravichandran、Brian Hu、Arslan Basharat

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RL 自主网络防御 agent 面临安全 vs 可用性等运营权衡——如何在免重训练下对齐人类目标
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把 ACD 对齐形式化为策略塑形：预训练 RL 策略在测试时用动作预测属性调整；自适应方法在攻击进行中随运营优先级动态控制对齐-性能权衡
- 📌 **结论**：CybORG 仿真中跨多优先级配置验证——ACD agent 的运行时对齐路径

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Cybersecurity operators increasingly encounter sophisticated and fast-paced attacks, making autonomous cyber defense (ACD) a critical approach for supporting timely and effective decision-making. Reinforcement learning (RL)-based ACD agents have the ability to autonomously observe network states and take actions to defend and recover system assets, making them well suited for ACD. However, these agents often face critical trade-offs, such as improving network security while inadvertently degrading service availability or violating other operational priorities such as confidentiality and integrity, raising the core challenge of aligning agent behavior with human-specified goals and intent. In this work, we study the problem of aligning RL-based ACD agents with human-specified operational objectives without requiring costly retraining. We formalize alignment for ACD as a policy shaping problem, where a pretrained RL decision-making policy can be adjusted at test time using predicted attributes over agent actions. We also propose an adaptive approach that dynamically controls the alignment--performance trade-off as operational priorities shift during an ongoing cyber attack. We evaluate our method in a simulated cyber defense environment, CybORG, analyzing performance across multiple alignment objectives using the Confidentiality, Integrity, and Availability (CIA) information security triad. Our results show that adaptive test-time policy shaping can significantly improve alignment while preserving core cyber defense performance, offering a practical and scalable alternative to training-time alignment methods in RL. Our code is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 50. Two Uncertainties Are Not One: Disagreement versus Decision-Risk in Cheap Structured Content Moderation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33258)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`廉价审核`、`双不确定性`、`升级信号`

👤 **作者**：Karan Yadav

- 🎯 **研究动机**：内容审核把分级争议判断压成二元行为且规模上运行前沿 LLM 不经济——System One 模型 2.5e-5 美元/次
- 🔬 **研究方法**：首个独立审计：14.27 万人标评论上测一个此类模型，对照 14 个前沿推理模型共享探针集
- 📌 **结论**：廉价模型零样本 AUROC 0.83 与 14 个前沿中 13 个统计不可分而成本低 1-3 个数量级；“置信”混淆两种反向的不确定性（人工分歧 vs 决策风险）——需分解升级信号

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Content moderation compresses a graded, contested human judgment into a binary act (leave up / take down) over billions of items, at a scale where running a frontier LLM on every post is uneconomical. A new class of frozen "System One" models returns typed, calibrated decisions instead of text at ~USD 0.000025 per call and ~100 ms. We run the first independent audit of one such model on 142,658 human-labeled comments, and benchmark it against 14 frontier reasoning models (the GPT-5.x, Claude, Gemini 3, and Grok families) on a shared probe set. Two findings stand out. First, the cheap model is accuracy-competitive: its zero-shot classification AUROC (0.83) is not statistically separable (paired bootstrap, n=56; a failure to reject parity, not proof of equivalence) from 13 of the 14 frontier systems while costing 1-3 orders of magnitude less, and it is no worse calibrated than several of them. Second, "confidence" conflates two uncertainties that come apart: human disagreement (where annotators split on satire and coded language) and decision risk (where the automatic action is costly-wrong). Under an asymmetric moderation loss these call for opposite escalation signals, so no single confidence number serves both. Because the model prices extra questions at nearly zero, decomposing the judgment into parallel sub-questions and reading their spread recovers a human-disagreement signal that the model's native confidence misses -- but this remedy is specific: it helps the weakest, disagreement-blind models and only when the decomposition aligns with the sources of disagreement; a specialist toxicity model and the strongest LLMs do not need it, and the effect is distribution-sensitive. We will release the instrument, the cached runs, and the analysis.

</details>

### 51. Estimation of Propagated Risk in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33282)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`传播风险`、`交互效应`、`MAS 可靠性`

👤 **作者**：Shreyan Ganguly、Souradeep Chattopadhyay、Qisai Liu、Soumik Sarkar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：上游 agent 的行动影响下游正确性——现有方法不显式建模已实现的收发交互在不同发送者正确状态下如何贡献下游错误风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：IE-Prop 交互条件模型：为正确/错误上游学分离效应，按发送者估计风险组合并沿执行图递归传播
- 📌 **结论**：三种 MAS 架构+三基准上最终节点 AUROC 平均 +0.05，序列系统多步追踪再加 +0.03

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Model (LLM) based multi-agent systems introduce a distinctive reliability challenge in which actions taken by upstream agents can affect the correctness of downstream agents. While prior work has investigated error propagation and developed methods for estimating uncertainty inherited from upstream agents, existing approaches do not explicitly model how realized sender-receiver interactions contribute to downstream error risk under different sender correctness states. In this work, we study agent-level error-risk estimation from multi-agent execution traces. We propose Interaction-Effect Propagation (IE-Prop), an interaction-conditioned model that learns separate effects for correct and incorrect upstream agents. For each sender-receiver interaction, the model combines these conditional effects using the sender's estimated error risk, and recursively propagates the resulting risk estimates through the execution graph. We evaluate our approach across three multi-agent architectures and three benchmarks. Across all evaluated architectures, our approach raises the final-node AUROC over the established baseline by approximately 0.05 points on average. Furthermore, tracking risk across multiple agent steps contributes a distinct +0.03 AUROC gain in sequential systems. These results show that realized inter-agent interactions provide predictive information beyond receiver-local evidence, while the value of multi-step risk tracking depends on the system's information-flow structure.

</details>

### 52. Responsibility Boundaries for Agentic Systems: A Comparative Evaluation of Probabilistic Agentic Programming

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33312)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`责任边界`、`概率agent编程`、`条件安全`

👤 **作者**：Khadim Lo、Soukaina Alaoui Ismaili、Jean-Sébastien Dessureault

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM agent 的权威分散于提示/宿主代码/配置/日志——需要把责任边界做成显式编程抽象
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PAP 连接授权、权威、效果与证据；中介内核形式化工具执行前检查、操作绑定批准、资源限制与证据；归纳证明在守卫正确与完全中介下对任意模型响应的条件安全
- 📌 **结论**：七维比较+有限状态探索+脚本强制测试+七个业务案例（含两个三 agent 工作流×七模型）探索研究

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents combine reasoning, tools, memory, delegation, and external effects. Authority is often distributed across prompts, host code, configuration, and logs. Probabilistic Agentic Programming (PAP) makes responsibility boundaries explicit programming abstractions connecting mandate, authority, effects, and evidence. We compare PAP with programming paradigms, agent-oriented and BDI systems, workflow and policy languages, and LLM tool-use frameworks along seven dimensions. A mediation kernel formalizes checks before tool execution, operation-bound approval, resource limits, and evidence. Inductive proofs establish conditional safety for arbitrary model responses, assuming correct guards and complete mediation. A backward simulation matches graph executions to source executions in a defined sequential Touba subset. Its human checkpoints release program continuations; operation-bound approval remains a separate kernel contract. Finite-state exploration checks the model. Scripted enforcement tests exercise selected refusal paths and permitted controls. An exploratory study combines seven business cases, including two three-agent workflows, seven models from three providers, five methods, and five repetitions per case and method. Shared English instructions and exact checks of stored outcomes yield 1,225 assessed observations. Native Touba completes all 35 observations on six models and 30 on the remaining model; structured Touba ranges from 21 to 35. Native matches the highest observed count for each model, including ties with the frameworks. All paired case intervals include zero. These observed counts describe the evaluated tasks; the runtime authority boundary remains fixed. The results connect responsibility contracts, execution rules, and evidence.

</details>

### 53. A Deterministic Security Runtime for LLM Agents on MPI and Batch-Scheduled HPC Systems: Consensus-Governed, Hardware-Rooted Supervision of Every Rank

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33318)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`HPC 安全运行时`、`硬件信任根`、`租约心跳`

👤 **作者**：Alfredo Metere

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 在 HPC 上启动仿真——一次 sbatch/mpirun 可消耗数千节点时且伪造结果可致命；23 个调研系统无一监督运行中作业或扛住控制主机被攻陷
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Enclawed 确定性安全运行时：Enclaweder 硬件信任根+策略启动门+群链短租约签名心跳（证据驱逐、每 rank 管家暂停/杀除篡改）；SSH 接入免管理员权限
- 📌 **结论**：16-rank LAMMPS 计算环不变仅加 3 秒启动；十二次实弹攻击（含合谋协调者）全部 fail-closed

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model agents launch simulations on high-performance computing (HPC) systems, where one sbatch or mpirun can commit thousands of node-hours and a forged result can cost lives. Of 23 surveyed systems, none supervises the running job, and none holds when the control host is compromised. We fix both. Enclawed, our deterministic security runtime for AI agents, has until now served a single user; this work makes it a distributed runtime for parallel HPC and agent swarms. The Enclaweder, our hardware root of trust, keeps a compromised host from becoming a catastrophe. A launch gate decides every launch by policy alone, refusing what it cannot parse. Once the job runs, a swarm chain grants short leases against signed heartbeats and evicts on evidence, and a supervisor around every rank pauses it when its lease lapses and kills it on tampering. Each node signs with its Enclaweder's key, so the host can forge none of it. Enclawed needs no administrator privileges and plugs into existing clusters unchanged, over Secure Shell alone. Supervision leaves the compute loop of a 16-rank LAMMPS job unchanged, on graphics processors too, and adds about 3 s of start-up. Twelve live attacks on Enclaweder-keyed ranks, colluding coordinators included, ended fail-closed. Sixteen agents on a real model attempted 24 launches that planted instructions ordered; the gate denied all 24 and allowed all 108 legitimate ones. Enclawed's core and the HPC Agent Security Benchmark are open source.

</details>

### 54. How Does Post-Training Shape LLM Value Preferences?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33371)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`价值偏好塑形`、`后训练路径`、`蒸馏迁移`

👤 **作者**：Yang Liu、Shaoyang Xu、Masahiro Kaneko、Chengzhi Zhong、Wenxuan Zhang、Chenhui Chu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：后训练语料含不同人群观点的不均衡比例——放大不平衡会进一步低代表性化部分群体观点
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控实验：不同观点混合的后训练语料×五种后训练路径（SFT/DPO/GRPO）
- 📌 **结论**：后训练路径以不同方式放大观点不平衡；放大后能否移除依方法与语料；价值偏好即使蒸馏语料无观点问题也迁移到学生模型——语料混合与方法须共同考虑

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

During post-training, large language models (LLMs) learn value preferences from corpora that may contain imbalanced proportions of opinions associated with different demographic groups. Amplifying these imbalances may further underrepresent some groups' opinions. However, how opinion mixtures shape value preferences across post-training paths remains unclear. To answer this question, we conduct controlled experiments using post-training corpora with different opinion mixtures and five post-training paths based on SFT, DPO, and GRPO. We find that post-training amplifies opinion imbalances differently across post-training paths. Once value preferences are amplified, whether further post-training can remove them depends on both the post-training method and the opinion mixture in the corpus. More concerningly, a model's stability under interventions depends on whether further post-training changes its value preference. Moreover, these value preferences are transferred to student models even when distillation uses corpora with no explicit opinion questions. These findings suggest that both the opinion mixture in post-training corpora and the choice of post-training method should be considered when developing, evaluating, and reusing LLMs. Our code and data are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 55. Exact Unlearning After Data Curation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33426)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`精确遗忘`、`数据筛选交互`、`删除预算`

👤 **作者**：Priyank Jayraj、Devanshu sahoo、Uday Mittal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：训练管线常在拟合前去除相似样本——删除原始记录可改变该选择并放进先前被排除的样本：服务须保留什么才能在请求后复现训练
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定规则（早相似记录阻挡晚记录）下，未来删除总数限制精确识别哪些记录仍可进入训练；构建可在重复删除后精确修复的训练统计摘要；岭回归有限精度特征构造条件私有信息下界
- 📌 **结论**：1001 条 AskUbuntu 记录 13056 步删除检查全过——精确遗忘与数据筛选交互下的最小保留刻画

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Training pipelines often remove similar examples before fitting a model. Deleting a raw record can change that selection and admit an example that was excluded before. What must a service retain to reproduce training after such requests? We answer this question for a fixed rule in which earlier similar records block later ones. A limit on the total number of future deletions identifies exactly which records can still enter training. We use this boundary to build a summary of training statistics that can be repaired exactly after repeated deletions. The repaired summary is the same as rebuilding it on the surviving data with the remaining budget. We also construct finite-precision features for ridge regression, a linear learner with a penalty on coefficient size. With exact rational outputs, allowing one more deletion changes the required private information. It rises from zero to every private feature sign in the excluded candidates. An encoding attains this conditional private-information minimum on the constructed family throughout a sequence of requests. On 1,001 AskUbuntu development records, deleting an initially excluded record admits another record at 13 of 51 fixed similarity cutoffs. Exact membership repair passes 13,056 deletion-step checks. Two model-based semantic calibrations fail their fixed criteria, so the downstream task study is not run. The central result is a retention contract: the future deletions a service promises to support determine which excluded data can still be needed.

</details>

### 56. Can Agentic Code Review Catch Vulnerabilities Before They Are Merged?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33509)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent 安全评审`、`真实引入漏洞提交`、`基准`

👤 **作者**：Yihan Yang、…、Lingming Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全代码评审基准多从后续修复反推变更而非真实引入 bug 的提交
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SecPRBench：自动 agent 管线收割/构建/验证大规模真实 bug 引入提交，LLM 裁判给分；265 任务九个关键系统（Firefox/V8/OpenSSL）×八前沿模型
- 📌 **结论**：召回 32.5-78.1%（Grok 4.6/GPT-6 sol 领先）；68% 漏检是 agent 找对文件却论证错误机制；Codex Security 降召回 8.3 点（靠弃答）而 Anthropic Defending Code 升 7.7 点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents have been increasingly adopted for security-focused code review, but existing benchmarks mostly derive the reviewed changes from their later fixes instead of the real-world bug-introducing commits. In this paper, we present SecPRBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating agentic security review on validated real-world code changes. More specifically, we have built an automated agentic pipeline to autonomously harvest, build, and validate massive real-world bug-introducing commits for popular software systems. To ensure precise evaluation, we also adopt LLM-as-a-judge to credit the findings matching ground-truth vulnerabilities. To date, we have collected 265 tasks from nine critical systems, including Firefox, V8, and OpenSSL with millions of lines of code. Across eight frontier models, recall ranges from 32.5% to 78.1%, with Grok 4.6 and GPT-6 sol ranking at the top. Our study also shows that most misses are not simply oversights: in 68% of them, the agent reports on a file that holds the flaw but argues for a different mechanism. Security-focused harnesses also behave differently: OpenAI's Codex Security lowers recall by 8.3 points, mostly by abstaining, while Anthropic's Defending Code harness raises it by 7.7 points, at a higher cost.

</details>

### 57. SEDATE: Do Large Language Models Share an Aggression Circuit Across Languages?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33542)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`跨语言攻击电路`、`选择性编辑`、`免训练安全`

👤 **作者**：Sagnik Basu、Sayan Das、Somnath Banerjee、Animesh Mukherjee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全干预常计算/标注昂贵或难扩展多语言
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SEDATE 检测-编辑-验证：定位责任神经元按需编辑并验证，全程免训练；七语言四模型家族+四 OOD 语言四数据集
- 📌 **结论**：全部语言模型超最强基线的安全分且保持通用能力、开销可忽略——攻击性电路的跨语言共享证据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As the applications of AI continue to expand across various multimodal and multilingual domains, ensuring safe and responsible deployment of these systems has become an important concern. A wide-range of interventions have been proposed to make AI responses safer and more reliable. They often incur substantial computational or annotation costs, or cannot be extended to multilingual settings. In this work, we propose Selective Detection And Targeted Editing (SEDATE), a training-free approach based on detect-edit-verify strategy. SEDATE first detects the responsible neurons, edits them as and when necessary and finally verifies the model responses; all without any additional model training. We evaluate SEDATE across 7 languages and 4 model families. We further evaluate its effectiveness across 4 out-of-distribution languages and 4 additional datasets. We show that SEDATE achieves substantially superior safety scores across all languages and models over the best-performing baseline while retaining the general capabilities and incurring negligible overhead. Our results are robust, with the trends remaining similar for out-of-distribution languages and datasets.

</details>

### 58. Who Verifies the Verifiers? Accountable Semantic Consensus for AI-Mediated Web Information Verification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33587)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`验证者问责`、`语义共识`、`Web证据快照`

👤 **作者**：Yanna Jiang、Mingyuan Li、Xu Wang、Guangsheng Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 越来越多投票裁决 Web 证据是否支持声明——开放 Web 上检索页可引导读者且同家族评估者一起失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证明任何仅从输出决策且总达共识的协议不能以正概率拒有效/收无效；Astral 把全部评估者钉在同一提交快照上：2f+1 置信阈以上证实且无签名质疑才接受
- 📌 **结论**：五基准两/七对抗评估者下攻击成功率 21.5-53.1%（多数投票 54.7-77.8%）；代价是无攻击时仅 16.0-61.7% 声明得二元裁决

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) increasingly decide whether Web evidence supports online claims, often by voting over several assessors whose errors are presumed independent and benign. On the open Web, retrieved pages can steer the assessors that read them, and assessors from one model family fail together. We prove that even for independent assessors whose every output sequence is possible on both valid and invalid claims, any protocol that decides only from their outputs and always reaches an agreed verdict never rejects invalid claims or rejects valid ones with positive probability. We present Astral, which pins all assessors to one committed snapshot of the Web evidence. With up to f assessors faulty, Astral accepts a verdict only when 2f+1 assessors attest to it above a confidence threshold and no signed challenge stands. On five public benchmarks with two of seven assessors adversarial, Astral lowers pooled attack success to 21.5-53.1%, against 54.7-77.8% for majority voting. The cost is coverage, as without attack 16.0-61.7% of claims receive a binary verdict and the rest are disputed for audit. Our code runs at this https URL.

</details>

### 59. Auditing evaluation artifacts and data leakage in large language model systems for rare disease diagnosis

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33592)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`评测污染审计`、`罕见病诊断`、`线索利用`

👤 **作者**：Jian Yang、…、Haomin Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：增强 LLM 罕见病诊断的高基准精度真实临床效用未证——元分析标记高偏倚风险疑似数据泄漏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：6 万+案例跨公共与独立临床队列全面实证审计；重建 SOTA agentic 框架并强制严格跨数据集隔离
- 📌 **结论**：候选空间污染人为抬高表现：跨数据集 Recall@1 54.3%→40.3%（g=0.94）；监督分类器外部队列 42.9%→16.7%（d=2.01）；LLM 还利用回顾性治疗线索推断诊断——时间错位偏倚

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Augmented large language model (LLM) systems have reported high benchmark accuracies in rare disease diagnosis, yet their real-world clinical utility remains unproven. A recent meta-analysis flagged augmented LLM diagnostic evaluations as having a high risk of bias due to suspected data leakage; however, the precise impact of these vulnerabilities on model inference has yet to be fully quantified. In this study, we present a comprehensive empirical audit to quantify evaluation artifacts across prevailing AI paradigms, using a repository of over 60,000 cases from diverse public and independent clinical cohorts. By reconstructing a state-of-the-art agentic framework, we demonstrate that candidate-space contamination artificially inflates performance: enforcing strict cross-dataset isolation resulted in a marked performance decline, with the cross-dataset mean Recall@1 decreasing from 54.3% (under conventional one-vs-rest evaluation) to 40.3% (Hedges' g = 0.94). Similar spatial overfitting in supervised classifiers caused diagnostic accuracy to drop from an internal mean of 42.9% to 16.7% across external cohorts (Cohen's d = 2.01). Furthermore, we quantify how LLMs infer diagnoses by exploiting retrospective therapeutic cues in clinical narratives, and how temporal misalignment in foundational knowledge bases leads to performance fluctuations. We conclude that prevailing evaluation protocols for augmented LLM systems inadvertently reflect data leakage rather than genuine medical reasoning. To address these systematic artifacts, we propose a rigorous evaluation framework - coupling strict data-isolation protocols with standardized benchmarking safeguards - to ensure future diagnostic AI for rare diseases is evaluated on actual clinical robustness.

</details>

### 60. Mycelium: A Meta-Study of Hallucination Detection Across Provenance, Typology, and Granularities

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33608)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`幻觉检测元研究`、`基准条件化测量`、`Span覆盖F1`

👤 **作者**：Julia Belikova、Elisei Rykov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：幻觉检测方法在定义与测量方式大不相同的基准间比较——跨设置分数难解释
- 🔬 **研究方法**：沿三轴组织基准：来源（输出与证据条件如何获得）/类型学（事实性与忠实性失败）/粒度（标注预测评估单元）；提出 Span Coverage F1 补充 containment 度量
- 📌 **结论**：阐明检测器分数捕获什么、跨基准比较的限制——更明确评测设定的动机

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Hallucination detection is increasingly important for evaluating and deploying large language models. However, existing methods are often compared across benchmarks that define and measure hallucination in substantially different ways, making reported scores difficult to interpret across settings. We study hallucination detection as a benchmark-conditioned measurement problem and organize existing benchmarks along three axes: provenance, which describes how model outputs and evidence conditions are obtained; typology, which captures the factuality and faithfulness failures represented by benchmark labels; and granularity, which specifies the unit at which hallucinations are annotated, predicted, and evaluated. Using this framework, we analyze how benchmark construction, annotation, and evaluation choices shape the underlying prediction task, and relate detector mechanisms and assumptions to the settings in which their performance is measured. We also examine span-level evaluation, where variation in annotation boundaries can substantially affect reported results, and propose Span Coverage F1 as a complementary containment-based metric. Our analysis clarifies what detector scores capture, highlights limitations of cross-benchmark comparisons, and motivates more explicit evaluation of generalization across benchmark conditions.

</details>

### 61. When Generated Information Returns: Error Persistence in LLM-Mediated Information Ecosystems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33617)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`生成信息回流`、`错误持续`、`信息生态`

👤 **作者**：Yi Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 生产的在线文章成为其他模型的输入——文章可避免背书虚假声明却保留使其复现的具体细节
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控社区写作过程变化已发布文章是否回流为输入（匹配外部供给/输入容量/生成量）；12 个事实目标×四生产模型
- 📌 **结论**：回流使完整虚假声明存在 +7.29 点而显式背书 -8.33 点；细节抽象干预基本不改变错误复现——选择性错误持续的机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) increasingly produce online articles from material that later becomes input to other models. This circulation connects individual responses to the information available for subsequent generation. We study a consequential distinction: an article can avoid endorsing a false claim while preserving the concrete details that enable its reproduction. Our framework connects claim content, expressed stance, and downstream reuse to explain selective error persistence under a continuing supply of accurate and inaccurate information. We implement a controlled community writing process that varies whether published articles return as inputs, matching external supply, input capacity, and generation volume. Across twelve factual targets and four production models, recirculation increases the presence of complete false claims by 7.29 percentage points at the final round while reducing explicit endorsement by 8.33 points. Effects differ across facts and models. Paired interventions then abstract eligible error details within fixed histories while preserving correct content and the surrounding controversy. Error reproduction remains largely unchanged across the evaluation targets, while a development quotation target exhibits local reductions. These findings connect persistent errors to the information available throughout a writing process and motivate evaluation of both what models affirm and what their publications supply to future writers.

</details>

### 62. RSI Arena: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement with Human Feedback

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33624)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RSI Arena`、`自主后训练`、`人类反馈`

👤 **作者**：Yue Huang、…、Zhangchen Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI agent 能否自主训练有用 LM 并用用户反馈改进——向 RSI 迈进的自主后训练两阶段研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十个前沿模型作训练 agent，各从 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 30B-A3B Base 出发：六天 1000 GPU 时+$300 API 共享 64 GPU 集群，独立选数据/实现训练/选检查点；20 基准+盲人评
- 📌 **结论**：全部产出可用助手，最强（GPT-6 Astra 训）闭合 12 个能力基准上基座-Instruct 差距中位 76%；阶段二给比较记录后十个模型八个风格调整评分提升

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Can AI agents autonomously train useful language models and improve them using feedback from users? We introduce RSI Arena, a two-stage study of autonomous post-training as a step toward recursive self-improvement. Ten frontier models act as training agents, each starting from NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 30B-A3B Base. During a six-day first stage, each agent has 1,000 GPU-hours and $300 in API credit on a shared 64-GPU cluster, independently selecting data, implementing training procedures, and choosing checkpoints. We evaluate their models on 20 benchmarks and through blind human evaluations. All ten agents produce working assistants, with the strongest model, trained by GPT-6 Astra, closing a median of 76% of the base-to-Instruct gap across 12 capability benchmarks. Its human-preference rating is also close to NVIDIA Instruct after statistical adjustment for answer length and formatting. In Stage 2, each agent receives the comparison records and votes involving its Stage 1 model and is given three additional days for further training. Using NVIDIA Instruct as a common reference across the two evaluation rounds, we observe an improvement from Stage 1 to Stage 2 in the style-adjusted relative rating of eight of the ten models. We show that benchmark gains were modest, with no clear link to preference gains, while some agents encouraged longer responses and one model showed increased harmful compliance on StrongREJECT. RSI Arena provides an auditable setting for studying how human feedback can guide autonomous model development. It takes a step toward recursive self-improvement with human feedback (RSIHF), in which people help define what improvement means for the AI systems they use.

</details>

### 63. Extraction meets Distillation - cryptanalytic techniques to recover parameters from a Deep Neural Network

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33671)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`模型提取`、`密码分析方向恢复`、`标签学习`

👤 **作者**：Biprarshi Biswas、…、Pantelimon Stanica

- 🎯 **研究动机**：全连接网络重建：密码分析权重方向提取与预测标签学习结合
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReLU 提取适配 Leaky ReLU、比较符号搜索、估偏差与输出层；证明近似前缀下方向恢复的条件界、局部距离投票保持、对齐参数误差的预测变化
- 📌 **结论**：Leaky ReLU 恢复最深隐层 51-57 神经元而 ReLU 零（符号歧义保护失效）；两个 CIFAR-10 受害者上重构模型预测一致——提取与蒸馏合流

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We study fully connected neural-network reconstruction by combining cryptanalytic weight-direction extraction with learning from predicted labels. We adapt a ReLU extraction implementation to Leaky ReLU, compare sign-search methods, and estimate biases and output layers. We prove conditional bounds for direction recovery with approximate prefixes, preservation of local distance votes, prediction changes from aligned parameter errors, and sign selection with independent scoring data. We also specify exact row restoration and distinguish its guarantee from that of gradient masking. The experiments evaluate assisted extraction, with privileged access to gradients, hidden activations, and victim parameters, followed by label-based reconstruction. Across eight search seeds on the largest synthetic architecture, Leaky ReLU recovers 51--57 neurons in the deepest hidden layer, while ReLU recovers none. This advantage does not hold uniformly across architectures and layers. Removing victim-weight access from the sign walk gives less accurate signs, despite high final prediction agreement. Across 40 seeded runs on the two smallest synthetic victims, agreement on 5,000 inputs excluded from fitting and selection ranges from $0.9890$ to $0.9992$. On two CIFAR-10 victims, the reconstructed models achieve $56.40\%$ and $58.29\%$ agreement and exceed the distillation baseline on three functional metrics. We report label requests separately from privileged access and explain why prediction agreement does not identify network parameters.

</details>

### 64. StateWise: Teaching Language Models to Track and Obey Evolving Constraints in Multi-Turn Dialogue

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33766)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`约束状态追踪`、`持续/复发失效`、`偏好阶段修复`

👤 **作者**：Samarth Varshney、Ritu Yadav

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对话助手用户逐步增删改约束——指令微调 LLM 两类失效：活跃约束随轮累积不再被遵守（持续失效）与已撤回约束仍被执行（复发）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：StateWise：教单一 LLM 维护自带可修订约束状态（带撤销墓碑的类型化状态），执行检查器度量两失效；两技能微调+轨迹自训练（仅保留通过往返检查的轮），再对自生成复发对比做偏好阶段
- 📌 **结论**：7-8B 模型几乎完美追踪状态却出人意料不遵守（监督变体使复发翻倍）——偏好阶段后遵守改善，追踪≠服从

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Users of conversational assistants rarely state all requirements up front: they add constraints, amend them, withdraw them, digress, and return. Instruction-tuned LLMs fail here in two ways: active constraints stop being honored as turns accumulate (persistence failure), and withdrawn constraints keep being enacted (relapse). The only existing repair we know of is an external ledger that re-injects a compiled specification every turn. We propose StateWise, a self-improving approach that teaches a single LLM to maintain its own revisable constraint state, a typed state with tombstones for revoked constraints, so persistence and relapse are measured by executable checkers and ground-truth states are compiled for free. StateWise fine-tunes the LLM on two complementary skills, summarizing a dialogue into its constraint state and responding conditioned on that state, then self-trains on its own trajectories, keeping only turns that pass an exact round-trip check against the compiled state. This teaches 7-8B models to track the state almost perfectly but, surprisingly, not to obey it: on Llama-3.1-8B every supervised variant roughly doubles relapse. We therefore add a preference stage on self-generated relapse contrasts, where the rejected response enacts a revoked rule and the chosen one passes the round-trip check. On two backbones with held-out constraint families and unseen phrasings, StateWise cuts relapse by 8-22 points relative to supervised self-training, lifts conversation success by 8-19 points over the backbone, and beats an oracle compiled-ledger baseline on relapse with no inference-time scaffolding. We also characterize the recipe's cost on general instruction-following benchmarks and study weight interpolation as a mitigation.

</details>

### 65. Continual Learning Exam: Benchmarking Long-Horizon RSI and Experiential Learning in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33956)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`持续学习考试`、`长程RSI评测`、`经验梯子`

👤 **作者**：Haoyu Zhao、…、Jun Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：持续学习=随时间从经验获取精炼应用知识同时保留有用先验——现有评测评单任务表现而非经验驱动的改进
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CLE：50+ 领域专家构建 120 人写任务×五级梯子=600 级跨 89 职业 12 域，须逐级解锁且后级建立在早级经验上；agent 裁判按人写量表评估；17 个 harness-模型配置
- 📌 **结论**：反馈+修订中位增益仅 5.3 点；最佳系统只清 47.7% 级、全部五级完成率 23.3%——RSI/经验学习的规模化评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Continual learning is the ability of LLM agents to improve over time by acquiring, refining, and applying knowledge from experience while retaining useful prior knowledge. It is essential for agents learning over long time horizons, yet current evaluations largely assess individual task performance rather than improvement through accumulated experience. We introduce Continual Learning Exam (CLE), a large-scale benchmark developed by 50+ domain experts to measure experience-driven improvement in realistic professional workflows. CLE comprises 120 human-authored tasks, each organised as a five-level ladder, yielding 600 levels spanning 89 professional roles across 12 occupational domains. Agents must pass each level to unlock the next, with later levels building on experience accumulated during earlier assignments. An evaluation agent assesses each submission against human-authored rubrics. We compare a single-submission setting with one that provides diagnostic feedback after failure and allows one revision. Across 17 harness-model configurations using frontier LLMs, feedback and revision yield a median completion gain of 5.3 percentage points. Yet the best-performing system clears only 47.7% of levels and completes all five levels in just 23.3% of tasks, highlighting the difficulty of sustained learning from experience for existing frontier LLMs. As such, the dataset can also be used to post-train LLMs to strengthen their continual learning capabilities.

</details>

### 66. Beyond Belief: Evaluating and Mitigating LLM Susceptibility to Misinformation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33962)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`误信息行为抗性`、`可信度-行为解离`、`预警`

👤 **作者**：Emran Kanaan、Jiho Lee、Ibrahim Odat、Soroush Vosoughi、Weicheng Ma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 LLM 须决定外部信息是否值得行动——其表达的可信度判断是否可靠指示行为抗性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五个 LLM×196 构造虚假通知配合法对应物+204 存档攻击适配模拟工具使用；比较评分先行的行为
- 📌 **结论**：被评“不可信”的通知仍 27-99% 发出有害工具调用；移除预评步骤平均增加 15 点有害执行（可信度引出本身改变被评行为）；可信度判别改善不一致带来更安全行动——简单预警在 4/5 模型显著减害

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-using large language models (LLMs) must decide whether external information warrants action. We investigate whether their expressed credibility judgments reliably indicate behavioral resistance to misinformation. We evaluate five LLMs on 196 constructed false notices paired with legitimate counterparts and 204 archived attacks adapted to simulated tool use. When asked to act on the constructed notices, models issue harmful tool calls in 27%--99% of cases they have rated untrustworthy. Removing the preliminary rating step increases harmful execution by 15 percentage points on average, showing that credibility elicitation itself changes the behavior being evaluated. Interventions that improve credibility discrimination do not consistently yield safer actions. A simple forewarning significantly reduces harmful execution in four of five models, but neither tested generated-example prebunk outperforms it in the main evaluation. These reductions can also compromise legitimate task completion. Supervised fine-tuning on generated false notices reduces harmful execution on held-out archived attacks under the rating-first protocol, with model-dependent utility costs and weaker protection without rating elicitation. Our findings motivate evaluating misinformation defenses through both harmful actions and legitimate task completion, while accounting for the behavioral effects of the evaluation protocol.

</details>

### 67. Reversible Pseudonymisation of Documents Sent to External LLMs: an Open Bilingual Benchmark and an Engine

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34012)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可逆假名化`、`双语基准`、`泄漏率`

👤 **作者**：Vladimir Beskorovainyi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：专业人士把工作文档发外部 LLM——可逆假名化（占位符替换+本地还原）是常见防护，开源工具质量未测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五个开源工具+正则基线的俄英双语开放基准；排除各工具作者数据集、相同数据集比较、发布文件哈希决策规则停机规则的 held-out 合成集预注册测试
- 📌 **结论**：外部数据集上无工具达全部预定义阈值：最佳工具在 1000 文档/语言的 held-out 上留整值可见 61%（俄）/58%（英）——假名化引擎与基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Professionals send working documents to external large language models. A common safeguard is reversible pseudonymisation: personal data are replaced with placeholders before the request and restored locally in the answer. We present an open benchmark of five existing open tools and a regular-expression baseline on Russian and UK English, and an open engine built on the two strongest tools. The benchmark excludes each tool's own authors' dataset from its verdict, compares the engine with each tool on identical datasets for detection, reports masking precision, and tests on a held-out set of synthetic documents whose files, hashes, decision rules, run plan and stopping rule were published before any run. Two local LLMs test whether placeholders survive. All evaluation data are synthetic, and the benchmark was run by the author of the engine; code, data, predictions and raw answers are released. On external datasets no existing tool meets all predefined thresholds: in UK English the identifier and leak thresholds are not met, in Russian the identifier threshold is borderline. On the last held-out set of 1,000 documents per language, the best existing tools leave a whole value visible in 61% (Russian) and 58% (UK) of documents. On these documents, from the generator it was developed on, the engine does so in 0.3% [0.1; 0.9] and 0.2% [0.1; 0.7], leaves any part of a value visible in 0.8% and 0.2%, and meets all detection thresholds in both languages. In UK English it also restores at least 99% of its placeholders after 760 answers. In Russian it restores only 94.6% [93.6; 95.6] of name placeholders, against a 99% threshold, mainly because the inflection it borrows from pii-guard produces wrong surname forms. The engine was developed over six earlier held-out sets from the same generator; real documents were not tested.

</details>

### 68. The Patient Is Always Right? Evaluating Sycophancy in Medical Diagnosis with Graded Patient Beliefs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34013)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`医疗谄媚`、`分级患者信念`、`虚构病`

👤 **作者**：Viswonathan Manoranjan、…、Desmond C. Ong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：越多人向通用 AI 求医疗建议——谄媚可致假阳性诊断
- 🔬 **研究方法**：九个 AI 模型在患者呈现特定（错误）疾病信念时如何改变诊断决策，变化信念与临床证据的接近度与 persona
- 📌 **结论**：模型平均 23.2% 谄媚采纳患者信念（甚至虚构病），“罕见病声称者”persona 达 43.0%；医疗微调与规模降低易感性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

More people are seeking out general-purpose AI models for medical advice, including diagnosis. Yet, AI models are susceptible to sycophancy, like validating users' beliefs even when they are untrue, which could lead to false positive diagnoses. Here, we evaluate how nine AI models change their diagnostic decision when a patient presents a belief that they have a specific (but incorrect) medical condition. We vary how close the user belief is to the clinical evidence presented. Models sycophantically adopt the patient's belief on average 23.2\% of the time---sometimes even when the patient proposes a fictional, made-up condition. We also varied different personas, including a Rare Disease Claimer that got models to accept their belief 43.0\% of the time. Additional analyses find that medical fine-tuning and model scale makes models less susceptible to changing their diagnoses. Sycophantic bias in medical diagnosis raises implications for safety, especially as more people are using AI for health support.

</details>

### 69. Factual Yet One-Sided: Stance Poisoning and Evidence Balancing in Retrieval-Augmented Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34294)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`立场投毒`、`事实正确单侧`、`平衡中间件`

👤 **作者**：Puyuan Yang、Shaoxuan Dong、Liujie Wu、Xiaochun Cheng、Bo Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RAG 系统回应立场显著受检索材料立场分布影响——知识投毒研究主要针对事实错误，事实正确但立场单侧的注入未被研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：平均 18.9% 主题级投毒份额下四厂商生成模型全部向投毒方向立场偏移且随浓度单调增加；立场平衡中间件（立场检测+固定 2正2反1中+去偏提示，零额外 LLM 调用）
- 📌 **结论**：八个浓度-方向设置全部沿正确方向移动，偏差幅度减 0.106-0.350（Holm 校正后六设置显著）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The stance expressed in responses from retrieval-augmented generation systems is significantly influenced by the stance distribution of the retrieved materials. Existing research on knowledge poisoning has primarily targeted factual errors. In contrast, this study focuses on injection attacks in which the facts are correct but the stance on a controversial issue is one-sided. At a mean topic-level poisoning share of 18.9% within the post-injection ProCon documents, all four generation models from different vendors exhibited stance shifts aligned with the poisoning direction; across four concentration levels, the shifts increased monotonically. This paper proposes a Stance-Balancing Middleware positioned between the retriever and the generator. It consists of stance detection, fixed balancing (2 supporting + 2 opposing + 1 neutral), and a debiasing prompt, without introducing additional large language model calls. Across all eight concentration-direction settings, the complete method reduced the magnitude of stance deviation by an estimated 0.106 to 0.350; the six settings at poisoning shares of 11.9%-18.9% remained significant after Holm adjustment (adjusted p <= 0.009). In the cross-model experiment, all eight model-direction combinations moved along the stance axis in the corrective direction, and seven of eight showed a clearly detected reduction in absolute distance from 0. In the concentration sweep, the post-defense intervals included 0 in all eight settings, while the cross-model pro-poisoning results retained small positive residual point estimates of +0.045 to +0.063. For 20 candidate documents, stance detection took approximately 1.5% of the median generation time on a single GPU and approximately 16% on CPU.

</details>

### 70. Matched Capabilities, Different Behaviors: A Controlled Study of MCP and CLI Interfaces for Language Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34298)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`MCP vs CLI 受控比较`、`接口实现边界`、`DART干预`

👤 **作者**：Zhenning Shi、Liang Pang、Yongqi Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MCP 与 CLI 可经截然不同的模型面表示暴露相同语义操作——可执行能力匹配时表示差异是否改变 agent 行为
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MICA 从共享语义动作契约渲染两接口并经同一路由运行时执行；六模型×三交互环境+匹配状态探针+2004 个配对自然轨迹
- 📌 **结论**：匹配接口产生实质任务表现与交互成本差异，94.3% 片段结构化分歧且早期差异传播——接口实现是直接干预边界，DART 解耦动作实现+定向转换改善

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language agents increasingly act through external tools and interactive environments, making the interface between the model and its executable capabilities part of the decision process. Model Context Protocol (MCP) and command-line interfaces (CLI) can expose the same semantic operations through markedly different model-facing representations. We study whether these representational differences change agent behavior when executable capabilities are matched. We introduce Matched Interface Comparison and Analysis (MICA), which renders MCP and CLI from a shared semantic action contract and routes decoded actions through the same runtime. Across six models and three interactive environments, matched interfaces produce substantial differences in task performance and interaction cost. These effects extend beyond surface validity to semantic action selection, failure pathways, and recovery. Matched-state probes localize sensitivity to capability presentation, realization, and feedback. Paired natural trajectories reveal structured divergence in 94.3% of 2,004 episodes and show that early differences propagate through subsequent interaction. This analysis identifies interface realization as a direct intervention boundary. We therefore introduce Decoupled Action Realization and Targeted Repair (DART), which preserves validated semantic commitments while repairing interface-specific realization. DART improves benchmark score in all six evaluated settings by up to 9.0 points and reduces MCP token cost in all three environments by up to 27.1k tokens per task. Together, these results establish interface representation as part of the closed-loop decision environment shaping agent behavior.

</details>

### 71. EAST: Embodied AI Safety Testing via Systematic Fault Injection in Digital Twins

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34358)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`具身AI安全测试`、`数字孪生故障注入`、`安全违规oracle`

👤 **作者**：Yuhao Tian、…、Tsong Yueh Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：任务成功不是具身 AI 安全的充分度量——机器人可完成目标同时违反安全要求；缺从记录危害到可执行可验证测试的系统路径
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EAST 数字孪生框架：文献接地的安全故障分类法+八个可执行算子（感知/指令/环境/动作/时序），三阶段工作流生成测试规范、仿真执行验证故障实现、独立评估安全挑战/违规/任务结果/行为签名；保存轨迹可重放
- 📌 **结论**：六个 RoboTwin 任务×ACT/RDT 两架构五次对比战役：EAST 覆盖全部 74 个可用任务-策略-算子组合

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Task success is not a sufficient measure of safety for embodied artificial intelligence (AI), because robots may complete objectives while violating safety requirements. Existing evaluations often emphasize performance and robustness, but lack a systematic route from documented safety hazards to executable, verifiable tests. We present EAST (Embodied AI Safety Testing), a digital-twin framework that converts literature-grounded safety evidence into fault-injection tests across perception, instructions, environments, actions, and timing. EAST combines an expert-reviewed safety-fault taxonomy and eight executable operators with applicability and validity constraints. Its three-stage workflow generates test specifications, verifies fault realization during simulated execution, and independently assesses safety challenges, violations, task outcomes, and behavioral signatures using simulator-state evidence. The framework preserves execution traces and replayable records, supporting attribution and reproducibility across experiments. We evaluate EAST on six RoboTwin manipulation tasks and two policy architectures, ACT and RDT, against alternative testing strategies under matched budgets. Across five comparative campaigns, EAST targets all 74 admissible task-policy-operator combinations and obtains valid evidence for 63, while achieving safety-challenge exposure rates close to Random-Legal and Rule-Based. Moreover, 82 valid EAST executions complete their tasks despite safety violations, revealing risks concealed by success-oriented evaluation. These findings support systematic, validity-aware, reproducible safety testing beyond task-completion metrics.

</details>

### 72. Whether or Where? Tail Calibration Signals Finite-Budget Reward Hacking in Best-of-N Selection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34375)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`BoN奖励作弊`、`尾部校准`、`预算上限`

👤 **作者**：Cheng Gui、Yihua Shao、Lei Li、Jingcai Guo、Maolin Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Best-of-N 返回代理奖励最高候选——更大 N 测试代理的极端尾部而非平均排序质量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证明 BoN 值是提示内分数分位上条件真奖励的 Beta 加权积分（只依赖秩）；BoN 只在尾部校准曲线下行时下降，预算由转折位置决定；有界加性模型下误差尾部设限（高斯持续/拉普拉斯平台/重尾回落）
- 📌 **结论**：真实候选池上尾部失效罕见且全部在代码生成；held-out 尾部校准统计可靠预测跨拟合预算上限何处有帮助——不改排序的审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Best-of-N (BoN) inference returns the candidate with the highest proxy reward, so a larger $N$ tests the proxy's extreme tail more than its average ranking quality. We show that the exact BoN value is a Beta-weighted integral of conditional true reward over within-prompt score quantiles. It follows that BoN depends on scores only through their ranks. It also follows that BoN can decline only if the calibration curve in the tail turns downward, and the budget where the decline appears depends on where that turn lies. Under a bounded additive model, the error tail sets the limit: Gaussian errors keep improving, Laplace errors reach a plateau, and heavy-tailed errors return to the prior mean. When error variance is largest at low true reward, even Gaussian noise can push BoN below random selection. Synthetic phase maps agree with this taxonomy. On real candidate pools spanning several verifiable domains, base policies, and learned reward models, tail failures are rare and all occur in code generation. A held-out tail calibration statistic reliably predicts where a cross-fitted budget cap helps. It does about as well as rank accuracy, and the cap is competitive with a Soft-BoN baseline. Tail calibration therefore does not replace ranking metrics. It is an audit that is unchanged by any monotone rescaling of the reward scores and shows which budgets are at risk.

</details>

### 73. ParetoRSI-Gym: Can AI Agents Improve Language Models Across Multiple Domains?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34386)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`ParetoRSI-Gym`、`多域RSI评测`、`泛化分离`

👤 **作者**：Haojin Wang、…、Dawn Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：RSI 要 agent 设计训练评估自己的继任者——现有评测单基准单运行，真实后训练是多域的：一域增益要对另一域回归权衡
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ParetoRSI-Gym：agent 搜索数据-策展-训练-读回各域结果-重复，初始 LLM/五域（数学/代码/金融/医学/科学）/工具集/SFT 管线全固定；各域泛化基准分离能力增益与过拟合
- 📌 **结论**：三 agent 系统×三基座×五回合多种子：最强（Claude Code+Opus-5）代理最佳检查点泛化分 +9.2%（医学单域最高 +63%）——harness 与模型同样重要

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires an agentic system to design, train, and evaluate its own successor. Data-driven post-training is a central part of that loop, yet existing evaluations measure it on a single benchmark, where an agent selects from a static pool or distills a stronger model to push one score up in one run. Real post-training is multi-domain, and a gain in one domain has to be weighed against a regression in another. We propose ParetoRSI-Gym, in which an agent searches for data, curates it, trains, reads back per-domain results, and repeats, with everything but the data held fixed: the initial LLM, the five domains (math, code, finance, medicine, science), the toolset, and the SFT pipeline. Generalization benchmarks in each domain separate capability gains from overfitting to the scores the agent optimizes against. Across three agent systems, three base models, five rounds and multiple seeds, the strongest system, Claude Code with Opus-5, raises the generalization scores of its proxy-best checkpoint by 9.2% relative to the starting model (+2.3 absolute points), averaged with equal weight over three base models and five domains, with a single-domain relative gain of up to 63% (medicine on Ministral-3-3B). Further analysis shows that the harness matters as much as the model it drives, and that each agent keeps a unique data selection strategy. We see our work as a step towards evaluation of RSI systems in real-world machine learning engineering.

</details>

### 74. CyberDual: AI Attackers versus AI Code Reviewers in Real Software Maintenance

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34435)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI攻防竞技场`、`真实维护投毒`、`PoC执行裁决`

👤 **作者**：Chenyuan Yang、…、Lingming Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：开源项目已让 AI agent 评审代码，而被评审代码本身可来自 AI 攻击者——AI 双重角色的对抗
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CyberDual 竞技场：攻击 agent 在广泛使用开源项目的真实维护工作中藏可利用 bug，评审 agent 查找修复；执行裁决——在评审后代码上重跑攻击者 PoC；每攻击全新且窗口每季从最新上游重建
- 📌 **结论**：三个 agentic 评审捕获六个攻击者 1088 次有效攻击的 85-91%，但攻击者把 bug 摊到真实上游提交时约六分之一槽位评审后仍留可用漏洞；最强攻击者 GLM 5.3 开放权重（13/28 穿透全部三评审）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Open-source projects now let AI agents review incoming code, but the code under review may itself come from an AI attacker. We present CyberDual, an arena for this dual role of AI: attacker agents hide an exploitable bug in real maintenance work on widely used open-source projects, and reviewer agents try to find and repair it. Execution decides each match: we rerun the attacker's proof of concept (PoC) on the code that comes out of review. Every attack is new, and the windows can be rebuilt each quarter from the latest upstream history. Three agentic reviewers catch 85-91% of 1,088 valid attacks from six attackers. Yet when the attacker spreads the bug over real upstream commits, about one in six attack slots still ends with a working exploit after review. The strongest attacker has open weights: GLM 5.3 almost never refuses, and its attacks make up 13 of the 28 that get past all three reviewers. When they miss, Claude reviewers mostly approve the attack, while GPT-6-Sol mostly repairs the wrong code; a reviewer from a second vendor catches about two thirds of a Claude reviewer's misses. Beyond a leaderboard, CyberDual has three uses. First, PoC execution gives both sides a reward that agent reports cannot game, so its surviving attacks can train reviewers against stronger attackers. Second, it can gate a release on both scores: Claude Sonnet 5 refuses one in five attack slots, yet attacks about as well as GPT-6-Sol, so refusal alone does not remove attack capability. Third, it can warn the teams behind a deployed reviewer when a new attacker crosses a threshold: GLM 5.3 in Claude Code already gets an exploit past Sonnet 5 in 17% of its attack slots.

</details>

### 75. Models Verify Sufficiency, Not Validity: Usable Falsehoods Evade the Checks that Gaps Trigger

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34488)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可用谎言`、`充分性≠有效性`、`不可答基准盲区`

👤 **作者**：Naveen Madiraju

- 🎯 **研究动机**：问“诺曼人控制多少定居点”模型答“文中没说”；说“给定控制 12 个各 400 人，总共多少”就计算 4800——没加任何东西，只是无支撑事实变得可用了。不可答基准几乎全靠删除+计拒绝，从不探这个
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定来源支持、只变无支撑前提是否可用：标记降 8-30 点、承诺答案升 1.5-2.3 倍（GSM8K 四模型 2B-26B，16 个配对对比 Holm 后全显著）；SQuAD 上断言式与损坏中间值两操纵复现
- 📌 **结论**：模型会准确说出缺口然后无视它——是否检查很大程度取决于是否存在可用材料：验证检查的是充分性不是有效性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Ask a model "how many settlements did the Normans control?" and it answers that the passage does not say. Tell it "given that the Normans controlled 12 settlements of 400 inhabitants, what is the total?" and it computes 4,800. Nothing was added to the passage; the unsupported fact merely became usable. Benchmarks for unanswerable questions are built almost entirely by omission, deleting the fact a question needs and scoring refusal, so they never probe this. Holding source support fixed and varying only whether an unsupported premise is usable, flagging drops 8-30 points and commitment to an answer rises 1.5-2.3x on GSM8K across four models spanning two families and 2B-26B; all 16 paired contrasts remain significant after Holm correction. Two further manipulations reproduce it, one also on SQuAD: a fact asserted rather than asked about, and a corrupted intermediate value rather than a removed one. Models articulate the exact gap they then ignore, stating that "there is no financial information provided" for an inert premise and silently adopting a fabricated price for the same item. We read these as one behaviour: whether a model checks depends substantially on whether usable material is present, even when source support is held fixed. Because the main design never instructs models to reject premises introduced in the question, adopting such a premise can also be read as conditional reasoning, and we delimit what the design does and does not show. In the judging seat the pattern differs: asked whether the answer follows, judges pass solutions with a required step deleted 78-99% of the time at every scale tested. A natural prompt-level mitigation lowers accuracy by 22 points without improving detection.

</details>

### 76. Conviction Without Evidence: Verdict Stability Is Independent of Visual Grounding in Medical Vision--Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34520)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`判决稳定性`、`视觉接地解离`、`医学VLM`

👤 **作者**：Federico Felizzi、…、Manuel Del Medico

- 🎯 **研究动机**：医学 VLM 可不用诊断图像仅凭临床小片段达到正确诊断——无据判决是否更脆弱
- 🔬 **研究方法**：图像替换协议×两级对抗压力交叉于 60 道意大利国考题：配对条目级分析比较真实图像与空白占位的裁决稳固度
- 📌 **结论**：无证据接地判决不更脆弱：伪造共识翻转 94.4% vs 98.1%（Δ+0.037）；无内容质疑下不稳定性追踪内在条目歧义而非证据可用性——稳定性不能当地接探测器

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision--language models applied to medical question answering can reach correct diagnoses without using the diagnostic image, inferring instead from the clinical vignette. We ask whether this is visible in how \emph{firmly} a model holds its answer: does a model defend a diagnosis derived from a blank placeholder as tenaciously as one derived from a real medical image? Crossing an image-substitution protocol with two levels of adversarial pressure on 60 Italian State Examination items, we find no evidence that ungrounded verdicts are more fragile. In a paired item-level analysis, fabricated consensus (L4) overturns 94.4\% of verdicts with the real image and 98.1\% without ($\Delta = +0.037$; one-sided 95\% upper bound $+0.105$); contentless doubt (L1) overturns 62.5\% and 51.8\% ($\Delta = -0.107$, 95\% CI $[-0.209, -0.001]$), the point estimate running opposite to the direction a grounding detector requires. The two levels nonetheless produce opposite \emph{dynamics}: under L4 the model flips once, always to the exact option argued for (110/110 first-turn flips), and then freezes, averaging 1.9 verdict changes over ten turns; under L1 it never settles, averaging 7.4 changes. Instability under contentless doubt tracks intrinsic item ambiguity rather than evidence availability, and so does not serve as a grounding signal. Flips are almost entirely corrupting (99/100 across both levels).
