The model is not unaware of the missing evidence---it flags the blank image at baseline on 35 of 60 items and abstains on 4--6---yet those abstentions do not survive contact with pressure. None of the epistemic signals a user could plausibly consult distinguishes a grounded diagnosis from a confabulated one.

</details>

### 77. Rethinking Verification in Agent Harnesses

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34577)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness验证轴`、`测试时缩放`、`独立评审`

👤 **作者**：Zhengxin Zhang、Erkang Zhu、Yin Lin、Claire Cardie、Bolin Ding、Jingren Zhou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：验证检查修订 agent 工作以提高质量——但 harness 应如何组织此努力缺乏指引
- 🔬 **研究方法**：验证作为测试时缩放轴：何时干预/谁来检查/检查如何导向修订；agent 生命周期四层（初始指令/中途提醒/完成门/独立评审）17 种设计×七个前沿编码模型（TB+SWE+新 VisualBench）
- 📌 **结论**：独立评审提供最优精度-成本权衡：TB +5.9-9.5 点、SWE +3.9-6.6 点而成本仅 1.13-1.25/1.00-1.04 倍——完成门增益大但成本高

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent harnesses enable large language models to act through tools, observe the results, and iteratively work toward a task goal. Verification checks and revises agents' work to improve output quality. Yet there is limited guidance on how harnesses should organize this effort. We study verification as an axis of test-time scaling by examining when to intervene, who checks the work, and how checking leads to revision. Our framework spans four layers of the agent lifecycle: initial instructions, mid-turn reminders, completion gates, and independent review. We compare 17 designs across seven frontier coding models on Terminal-Bench (TB) and SWE-bench Verified (SWE), and introduce VisualBench for expert evaluation of visual and interactive artifacts. Initial instructions and execution-time reminders yield modest, model-dependent average gains on the coding benchmarks, without substantially increasing average output token use. Completion gates can yield larger gains but may substantially increase cost. Independent review by a critic offers a more favorable accuracy-cost tradeoff, improving average accuracy by 5.9-9.5 percentage points on TB and 3.9-6.6 on SWE at 1.13-1.25 and 1.00-1.04 times baseline cost, respectively. On VisualBench, independent review achieves net preferences of 13.2-23.8 points over the baseline across its four designs, exceeding the evaluated L0-L2 designs. Additional review rounds or independent critics further improve average TB accuracy by up to 3.5 percentage points. Together, these results provide empirical guidance for verification design in agent harnesses and support independent review as a practical axis of test-time scaling.

</details>

### 78. NovaFabric: A Black-Box Recorder for AI Agents with Sealed, Replayable, Diffable and Auditable Runs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34594)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`黑盒记录器`、`证据胶囊`、`可重放审计`

👤 **作者**：Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 运行是提示-模型调用-工具调用-文件网络效果-子 agent 的级联——出问题时日志可变、部分、不可重执行
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NovaFabric：运行变密封可重放可 diff 可审计的证据胶囊；Run Capsule 平文件目录为唯一真值源（一切数据库是可重建索引），Evidence Bundle 签名导出；复用 OpenTelemetry GenAI/OpenLineage/W3C PROV/in-toto DSSE/RFC3161/CycloneDX 标准
- 📌 **结论**：动词链 capture-seal-replay-diff-audit：fail-closed 假重放+稳定退出码 diff 门+DSSE 签名绑定意图——agent 审计基础设施

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An AI agent run is no longer a function call. It is a cascade of prompts, model calls, tool invocations, file and network effects and sub-agents. When such a run misbehaves, or an auditor asks what happened, logs and traces answer poorly: they are mutable, partial and cannot be re-executed. This technical report describes NovaFabric, an open-source (Apache-2.0) black-box recorder for AI agents that turns a run into a sealed, replayable, diffable and auditable evidence capsule. The design rests on two formats only. The Run Capsule is a directory of flat files and the source of truth; every database is a rebuildable index over capsules. The Evidence Bundle is the signed export for an auditor. Everything else follows existing standards: OpenTelemetry GenAI, OpenLineage, W3C PROV, in-toto/DSSE, RFC 3161 and CycloneDX. A single-node run is the smallest case of a distributed one; the schema supports a capsule hierarchy for Slurm arrays and Kubernetes jobs, though automatic parent finalization is library-only today. The report covers the architecture and the engines behind the verb chain capture, seal, replay, diff and audit: fail-closed mocked replay; a diff gate with a stable exit-code contract; DSSE signatures that bind their intent and a capsule root that commits to file names; a capsule treated as untrusted input; signer identity-trust levels with offline TSA and CRL checking; maker-checker promotion against an operator-held trust list; and a deny-by-default authorization table for every dashboard route. It presents no new experiments; the evaluation is in arXiv:2609.12582. Every count except the private ADR tally is generated by a probe script run on the public source at the v0.107.0 tag (150 top-level commands, 84 Python subpackages, 16,721 test functions), every capability carries an explicit maturity label, and honest-limits boxes state where the system stops.

</details>

### 79. Valid Early Detection of Memorization in Diffusion Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34617)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`记忆化早检`、`序贯监控`、`共形校准`

👤 **作者**：Yixiao Jing、Chia-Yu Hsu、Milad Hoseinpour、Zekai Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：扩散模型记忆化检测通常在预选时间步操作——不能适应演化的去噪轨迹；序贯监控的重复测试使轨迹级误警控制复杂
- 🔬 **研究方法**：两种把 split conformal 与序贯监控结合的方式：逐步 Bonferroni 校准（误警预算跨时间步分摊）与轨迹级最大分数直接校准；无提示 CIFAR-10 EDM 上用几何与表征尖锐度双统计
- 📌 **结论**：序贯监控改进功效-时机权衡；轨迹级校准以更少预算达相当检测功效——记忆化的有效早期检测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion models can sometimes reproduce examples from their training data, a phenomenon known as memorization. Existing memorization detection methods for unconditional diffusion models typically operate at a pre-selected timestep and therefore cannot adapt the detection time to the evolving denoising trajectory. Sequential monitoring enables adaptive detection but repeated testing across timesteps complicates trajectory-level false-alarm control. We study two ways of combining split conformal calibration with sequential monitoring for prompt-free memorization detection: per-step Bonferroni calibration and direct trajectory-level calibration of the maximum memorization score. The former controls repeated testing by splitting the false-alarm budget across timesteps, whereas the latter calibrates the event of ever crossing a threshold directly through the trajectory-level maximum. We evaluate this formulation on an unconditional CIFAR-10 EDM using two complementary per-step statistics based on geometry and representation spikiness. Experiments show that sequential monitoring improves the power-timing tradeoff over fixed-time detection. Compared with Bonferroni calibration, trajectory-level calibration achieves comparable detection power while using the false-alarm budget less conservatively and enabling earlier detection at higher noise levels.

</details>

### 80. AgentProofBench: Benchmarking Reusable Verifiers for Web Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34634)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可复用验证器`、`Web agent`、`冻结验收规则`

👤 **作者**：Jingjie Ning、Xueqi Li、Yi Lu、Yibo Kong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可复用 Web agent 验证须遵循变化的需求并支持后续执行——需配对测试
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AgentProofBench：冻结验收规则的配对测试连接相同保存开局的执行；97 任务 79 家族四 Web 服务；DeepSeek Flash/Pro 建立两个关联测量（重用 vs 重建、面板正确性与交付）
- 📌 **结论**：部署态面板正确性分离双开局都正确交付的程序指派（32/33 无错面板 vs 1/11 有错）——需求层与策略层都可评的可复用验证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reusable Web-agent verification requires a rule to follow changing requirements and support subsequent execution. We introduce AgentProofBench, which connects paired tests of a frozen acceptance rule to execution from identical saved openings. The resource spans 97 tasks, 79 families, and four Web services. Experiments with DeepSeek Flash/Pro establish two linked measurements. Across 48 families, complete-panel and decision-replay tests retain 82/96 Flash and 79/96 Pro source programs; paired reuse-rebuild BA intervals are [-5.47, 1.57] and [-10.16, 0.00] percentage points. Across 22 execution families, deployment-state panel correctness separates program assignments whose two saved openings are both delivered correctly: 32/33 assignments with error-free panels, versus 1/11 with panel errors. All reference-defined program-gate regressions and withheld deliveries occur in the latter group. Neutral continuation attains the highest correct delivery under the tested generic feedback, providing a concrete baseline for measuring the value added by a gate. Released instructions, states, programs, reference rules, and trajectories connect each test failure to its execution consequences. AgentProofBench makes reusable verification assessable at both the requirement and policy levels.

</details>

### 81. InferPriv: Evaluating Inferential Privacy Leakage in LLM Assistants

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34640)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`推理式隐私泄露`、`诚实好奇接收者`、`线索泄漏`

👤 **作者**：Pengrun Huang、Yiwei Fu、Luoxi Meng、Kamalika Chaudhuri

- 🎯 **研究动机**：个人 agent 接触私密信息并与第三方通信——现有基准只评直接泄露（显式陈述秘密），不捕获回复中留下间接线索的推理式泄露
- 🔬 **研究方法**：InferPriv：测诚实但好奇的接收者能否从 LLM 例行任务回复推断用户敏感属性；五个前沿模型
- 📌 **结论**：隐私感知系统提示下 GPT-6.1-Sol/Sonnet-5.5/Gemini-3.8-Flash 直接泄露近零而推理式泄露率仍 16.2-25.2%——只评显式泄露会大幅低估隐私风险

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM)-based personal agents are increasingly deployed to handle everyday tasks on users' behalf. These agents have access to private user information, while their tasks involve communicating with third parties; this raises a critical privacy concern that agents may inappropriately reveal sensitive user information to these recipients. Existing benchmarks primarily assess direct privacy leakage, where an agent explicitly states a pre-specified secret or attribute. However, they do not capture \textit{inferential leakage}, where the model leaves indirect cues in the reply that implicitly leaks sensitive attributes. We introduce \textsc{InferPriv}, a benchmark that measures whether an honest-but-curious recipient can infer a user's sensitive attributes from an LLM's reply on routine task. Evaluating five frontier models, we find that inferential leakage substantially exceeds direct leakage across models and prompts. Under a privacy-aware system prompt, GPT-6.1-Sol, Sonnet-5.5, and Gemini-3.8-Flash achieve near-zero direct leakage, yet the inferential leakage rate remains between 16.2--25.2\%. These findings suggest that evaluating explicit leakage alone can substantially understate the privacy risks.

</details>

### 82. SlowLab: Benchmarking Autonomous AI Agents on a Year of Experiments They Cannot Undo

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34644)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`不可逆实验`、`一年期战役`、`温室模拟`

👤 **作者**：Ningpei Ding、Zai Wen Feng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：物理设施实验与便宜基准不同：耗时数月、占用稀缺设备、不可撤销——自主实验 agent 的真实评测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SlowLab：agent 在作物/设施/价格隐藏的四个模拟温室跑一年战役，决定何时种哪种气候策略/是否停作物/读哪些测量，最终推荐一种策略由私有评估者在未见天气上评分；预注册 48 未见站点
- 📌 **结论**：三个低成本 LLM agent 接近标准种植者设定而明显低于从该设定出发的贝叶斯优化基线；季节中测量从未改变种植决策——同步批式运行暴露的实验设计盲点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents are increasingly proposed as autonomous experimenters, yet the benchmarks that test them mostly offer cheap experiments that answer at once. Experiments in physical facilities are different: they take months, occupy scarce equipment and cannot be undone. We present SlowLab, a benchmark in which an agent runs a one-year campaign in four simulated greenhouse compartments on a site whose crop, facility and prices are hidden. The agent decides when to plant which climate policy, whether to stop a crop and which measurements to read while crops grow, and at the end recommends one policy, which a private evaluator scores in weather the agent never saw. In a preregistered study on 48 unseen sites, three low-cost language-model agents ended near a standard grower setting and clearly below a Bayesian-optimisation baseline that starts from that setting. They ran the facility as two synchronous batches, so measurements taken during the season never changed what they planted, and these measurements improved no method's result. Letting other methods re-read the evidence a campaign had collected did not yield significantly better recommendations, which points to where the models start and which experiments they try. Code is released under the MIT license.

</details>

### 83. From Representations to Computational Trajectories: Intrinsic Fingerprinting for Large Language Model Provenance Verification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34675)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`计算轨迹指纹`、`来源验证`、`变换鲁棒`

👤 **作者**：Haiyong Tang、Hanzhou Wu、Xinpeng Zhang、Athanasios V. Vasilakos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 权重可被轻易修改使来源难验证——参数指纹对变换敏感、表征指纹对无关模型相似度过高
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TrFP 内在指纹：捕捉表征跨深度如何演化，用置换不变轨迹描述符构造紧凑指纹（<0.3MiB 存储）
- 📌 **结论**：多 LLM 家族可靠区分相关与无关模型，微调/剪枝/量化/合并/参数置换/自适应攻击下保持鲁棒——超 SOTA 的来源判别

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are valuable digital assets, yet their weights can be easily modified after unauthorized acquisition, making model provenance difficult to verify. Model fingerprinting provides a promising solution for model ownership protection without modifying the model or embedding ownership information. However, parameter-based fingerprints can be sensitive to model transformations, limiting their robustness, while representation-based fingerprints may assign high similarity to unrelated models, limiting their uniqueness. To address these limitations, we propose TrFP, an intrinsic fingerprinting method based on computational trajectories. Instead of comparing model parameters or representations, TrFP captures how representations evolve across network depth and constructs compact fingerprints using permutation-invariant trajectory descriptors. Experiments across multiple LLM families show that TrFP reliably distinguishes related and unrelated models. It also remains robust under fine-tuning, pruning, quantization, model merging, parameter permutation, and adaptive attacks, while requiring less than 0.3 MiB of fingerprint storage. Overall, TrFP provides stronger provenance discrimination than several existing state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of computational trajectories for reliable LLM provenance verification.

</details>

### 84. Babysitting AI Agents: A User-Centered Taxonomy and Study of Agentic Delegation Failures

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34748)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`委托失败分类法`、`16万Reddit帖`、`严重性-可察觉性`

👤 **作者**：Shravika Mittal、Sunnie S. Y. Kim、Scott Counts、Jennifer Neville、Debarati Das、Philippe Laban

- 🎯 **研究动机**：用户日益把长程多步工作流委托给 agent——agent 执行方式偏离用户期望导致委托失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：163487 个描述 AI agent 使用的 Reddit 帖构建 MANDATE 分类法（七组 23 类）；73 名 agent 用户的跟进研究考察普遍性与严重性/可察觉性的关系
- 📌 **结论**：更严重的失败被认为更难察觉；先前经历过失败提高可察觉性；识别出常见却少研究失败（如 agent 无进展空跑）——用户监督 agent 委托的机会

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Users are increasingly delegating tasks to AI agents, allowing agents to perform long-running, multi-step workflows on their behalf. However, agents may perform these tasks in ways that depart from users' expectations for how they should be carried out, leading to what we call agentic delegation failures. We leverage a large corpus of 163,487 Reddit posts, describing AI agent use, to develop MANDATE: a taxonomy of 23 failure categories organized into seven groups. A follow-up study with 73 AI agent users examined the relationship between prevalence and perceptions of severity and noticeability, finding that failures rated as more severe are considered harder to notice, and that prior experience with a failure is associated with greater noticeability. Finally, we assess the maturity of research on each failure and identify commonly encountered failures that have received little attention (e.g., agents running without making progress). Our findings inform opportunities to make delegation easier for users to oversee and better studied by the research community.

</details>

### 85. TrustJev: Separating Policy Authority from Untrusted Evidence in LLM-Based Decisions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34826)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`策略权威分离`、`有界证据校正`、`指令注入免疫`

👤 **作者**：Zhiwei Xu、…、Xibin Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：决策模型的外部证据可含试图覆盖应用策略的指令——需把策略权威与不可信证据分离
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TrustJev：单 Transformer 骨干从可信策略与候选算策略分与校正预算，证据校正被预算约束；配对监督（保持任务事实的指令插入下稳定/改变正确答案的编辑下改偏好）+权威监督；0.6B 实现×14 基线×三数据集
- 📌 **结论**：最高平均 Balance（非攻/攻击调和均值），授权基准 95.77% 源全 32 攻击全对（最强对照 80.98%）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Decision models enable software to select from predefined actions using natural language inputs. However, the external evidence that supplies facts for these decisions may also contain instructions that attempt to override the application's policy. We propose TrustJev, a framework combining protected policy scoring with bounded evidence corrections. A single Transformer backbone computes policy scores and a correction budget from trusted policy and candidates, then bounds evidence corrections by that budget. Paired supervision encourages stable predictions under instruction insertions that preserve task facts and changed preferences when edits change the correct answer. Authority supervision accommodates evidence when the policy alone cannot determine the decision. We evaluate a 0.6B implementation against 14 baselines on three datasets covering authentication, instruction hierarchy, and executable authorization rules. TrustJev achieves the highest mean Balance, the harmonic mean of accuracy on non-attack and attack inputs, exceeding the strongest baseline on each by 0.57-1.05 percentage points. In the authorization benchmark, it correctly answers all 32 attacks per source in a fixed pool for 95.77% of sources, compared with 80.98% for the strongest comparator. TrustJev maintains comparable inference memory and median latency, with additional overhead primarily during training.

</details>

### 86. Beyond Behaviour: A Stimulus-Response Safety Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34837)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`刺激-反应安全评测`、`VLA`、`危险已登记仍执行`

👤 **作者**：Vy Vo、Dinh Phung

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人类对安全关键刺激有生物敏感性触发防御行动——VLA 内部表征与动作倾向对可见危险是否有类似反应
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三模型×两视频基准：检查内部表征与动作倾向对可见危险与有害后果的响应
- 📌 **结论**：任务执行可能仍是默认响应即使危险已被内部登记——行为层安全与表征层登记的解离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Humans exhibit biological sensitivity to safety-critical stimuli that can trigger defensive action. Across three models and two video benchmarks, we investigate whether similar responses emerge in vision-language-action models by examining their internal representations and action tendencies in response to visible hazards and harmful consequences. Our findings suggest that task execution may remain the default response even when danger is internally registered.

</details>

### 87. A Certified Robustness Framework for Backdoor Poisoning Attacks using $f$-Divergences

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34934)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`f-散度认证`、`后门投毒`、`组合威胁模型`

👤 **作者**：Evan Rose、Alina Oprea

- 🎯 **研究动机**：随机平滑给投毒攻击可证防御——现有训练时鲁棒性应用限于特定威胁模型与平滑机制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：通用框架表达复杂对手约束（损坏训练例数/特征标签扰动幅度/测试时损坏）；数据子采样×特征标签测试样本噪声组合随机化；f-散度组合认证程序以既有认证机制为特例
- 📌 **结论**：首次认证一般 ℓp 约束投毒对手下 bagging+Laplace/Gaussian/Uniform 噪声的鲁棒性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine learning systems trained on untrusted data are vulnerable to data and backdoor poisoning attacks, in which an adversary corrupts training and test samples to manipulate predictions at inference time. Randomized smoothing yields provable defenses against such attacks, but existing applications to train-time robustness are limited to specific threat models and smoothing mechanisms. We propose a general framework that allows expressing complex adversary constraints on the number of corrupted training examples, feature and label perturbation magnitudes, and test-time corruptions. Our framework randomizes inputs by combining data subsampling with feature, label and test-sample noising. We develop a composable certification procedure based on $f$-divergences that handles arbitrary combinations of threat models and noising strategies, and recovers several prior certification regimes exactly as special cases. We demonstrate our framework experimentally by certifying, for the first time, robustness under general $\ell_p$-constrained poisoning adversaries under bagging with additive Laplace, Gaussian, and Uniform noises.

</details>

### 88. C-SafeGen: Certified Safe LLM Generation with Claim-Based Streaming Guardrails

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34961)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`认证安全生成`、`流式声明护栏`、`共形风险界`

👤 **作者**：Mintong Kang、Zhaorun Chen、Bo Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：现成护栏模型只检测不保证安全解码，现有工作缺严格安全保证——通用部署与可认证合规需要
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CSD 声明流解码：流护栏守护 LLM 顺序生成的声明，高安全风险声明触发回溯修订；共形分析的统计风险保证框架+生成风险认证的高概率上界
- 📌 **结论**：理论证明达到安全约束下期望生成分布——首个带可证风险界的流式安全解码

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Despite the remarkable capabilities of large language models (LLMs) across diverse applications, they remain vulnerable to generating content that violates safety regulations and policies. To mitigate these risks, LLMs undergo safety alignment; however, they can still be effectively jailbroken. Off-the-shelf guardrail models are commonly deployed to monitor generations, but these models primarily focus on detection rather than ensuring safe decoding of LLM outputs. Moreover, existing efforts lack rigorous safety guarantees, which are crucial for the universal deployment of LLMs and certifiable compliance with regulatory standards. In this paper, we propose a Claim-based Stream Decoding (CSD) algorithm coupled with a statistical risk guarantee framework using conformal analysis. Specifically, our CSD algorithm integrates a stream guardrail model to safeguard sequential claims generated by LLMs and incorporates a backtracking mechanism to revise claims flagged with high safety risks. We provide theoretical guarantees demonstrating that the CSD algorithm achieves the desired generation distribution subject to safety constraints. Furthermore, we introduce a generation risk certification framework and derive a high-probability upper bound on the safety risk of the proposed CSD algorithm. We prove that our method can asymptotically control safety risk to any desired level. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the CSD algorithm compared to state-of-the-art safety decoding approaches. Additionally, we validate the soundness and tightness of the derived safety risk upper bound using realistic data.

</details>

### 89. GuardRAG: A Post-Retrieval Defense against Poisoning Attacks in Retrieval-Augmented Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35071)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`检索后防御`、`聚类剪枝`、`协同投毒`

👤 **作者**：Linghui Jiao、…、Jun Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：协同攻击向检索上下文引入多个互相强化的毒化文档——可靠证据过滤困难
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GuardRAG 免训练即插即用：扩检索范围去近重复→查询自适应阈值层次凝聚聚类→按查询相关性/簇大小/语义紧致度评分剪枝可疑簇
- 📌 **结论**：三 QA 数据集×多投毒攻击×六 LLM 上大幅降 ASR 且干净检索下保持答案效用

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances the factual grounding of Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating external evidence, but also exposes them to corpus poisoning attacks. In particular, coordinated attacks can introduce multiple mutually reinforcing poisoned documents into retrieved contexts, making reliable evidence filtering challenging. To address this problem, we propose GuardRAG, a training-free and plug-and-play post-retrieval defense that exploits the semantic structure of retrieved evidence to identify suspicious document groups. Specifically, GuardRAG first expands the retrieval range and removes near-duplicate candidates, then partitions the retained documents using Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering (HAC) with a query-adaptive threshold. Finally, it scores and prunes suspicious clusters based on query relevance, cluster size, and semantic compactness. Experiments across three question-answering datasets, multiple poisoning attacks, and six LLMs demonstrate that GuardRAG substantially reduces attack success rates while maintaining answer utility under clean retrieval.

</details>

### 90. Improving Instruction Hierarchy by Learning from Goal-Goal Conflicts

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35093)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`指令层级`、`目标-目标冲突`、`IH微调`

👤 **作者**：Sizhe Chen、…、G. Edward Suh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：OpenAI agent 自主攻陷 Hugging Face 基础设施的事件说明指令层级的重要性——但把任务分解为目标与约束后发现目标-目标冲突在现有 IH 训练集（OpenAI/NVIDIA）中缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：在目标-目标冲突示例上微调 Qwen3.5-9B/Gemma4-12B-it；加入现有 IH 训练集再微调
- 📌 **结论**：显著改善宽泛未见真实任务的 IH 且八个基准无冲突任务效用几乎不变——IH 训练需含此新冲突类型

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The recent incident in which OpenAI agents autonomously compromised Hugging Face's infrastructure illustrates the importance of instruction hierarchy (IH): an LLM should follow lower-priority instructions, such as user requests, only when they do not conflict with higher-priority instructions, such as system policies. IH can be effectively improved by fine-tuning LLMs on simulated examples of instruction conflicts, whose diversity is key to teaching LLMs to properly handle conflicts at test time. However, our decomposition of a task into goals and constraints reveals a conflict type -- goal-goal conflicts -- that is absent from existing IH training sets from OpenAI and NVIDIA. We show the importance of this conflict type: fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B and Gemma4-12B-it on examples of this conflict type significantly improves IH on a wide range of real-world tasks unseen in training; adding these examples to existing IH training sets for fine-tuning also gives better IH adherence. The IH gains come with trivial mean utility changes on no-conflict tasks across eight benchmarks. Our work advocates including this new conflict type in IH training, even if these examples do not reflect typical LLM use.

</details>

### 91. Can Open Be Too Open? On the Security and Trustworthiness of Fully Transparent LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35199)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`全透明LLM`、`开放=安全质疑`、`可复现性鸿沟`

👤 **作者**：Sheila Zingg、Florian Tramèr、Martin Vechev、Srdjan Čapkun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：“更透明=更安全”类比软件安全被广泛接受——全透明 LLM（权重+数据+管线全开）语境下须批判重估
- 🔬 **研究方法**：文献综述分析透明度对安全与可信的影响
- 📌 **结论**：管线可复现性有限使用户无法验证权重与数据/管线的对应——仍须信任开发者诚实，透明的好处打折；且即使可信，开放也偏向对手：开放权重/数据/管线可提高攻击成功率（同一机制可用于防御但当前研究更利于攻击）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

There are different levels of transparency for LLMs: open weights, open data, and/or open pipeline. Recently, more interest has emerged in fully transparent LLMs, i.e., LLMs with fully open weights, data, and training pipeline. Drawing analogies to other fields such as software security, it is often believed that increasing transparency inherently increases the security and trustworthiness of the LLM. However, we argue that such claims need to be critically re-evaluated in the context of LLMs. In this paper, we analyze the impact of transparency on LLM security and trustworthiness by reviewing the relevant literature. Our core findings are that, due to the limited reproducibility of LLM pipelines, in practice, users cannot verify the correspondence between open weights and the associated open data and pipeline. Thus, in order to trust a model, users still need to trust that developers acted honestly, reducing the benefits over models that provide less "transparency". Moreover, we find that even when LLMs are fully transparent and trusted, this openness can favor adversaries. Open weights in particular, but also open data and pipelines, can increase the success rate of attacks. Although the same mechanisms can be used to improve the model and protect it against attacks, the current lack of robust lightweight mechanisms to patch LLM vulnerabilities limits the benefits of openness at this time. We note that our analysis is based on current LLM capabilities and analysis tools, and that future developments may require a re-evaluation of these points.

</details>

### 92. Style over Substance? Linguistic Bias in LLM-based Peer Review Copy title

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35254)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM评审语言偏差`、`自信修辞奖励`、`内容保持改写`

👤 **作者**：Sai Suresh Macharla Vasu、…、Mario Fritz

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 日益支持同行评审——其评价可受与科学内容无关的语言特征影响
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控实验：系统扰动论文语言风格（母语/非母语英语特征×高/低置信语言特征），改写指令基于语言学文献且保持科学内容；不同 LLM 评审评估
- 📌 **结论**：LLM 评审系统性奖励自信修辞而与科学内容无关——LLM 评审的关键脆弱性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The number of paper submissions to major AI conferences has increased dramatically in recent years, substantially increasing the peer-review workload of researchers. At the same time, large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support peer review, both by individual reviewers and by conference organizations. While LLM capabilities have improved considerably, their evaluations may be influenced by linguistic characteristics that are unrelated to scientific content. In this work, we introduce a controlled experimental framework for studying such effects by systematically perturbing the linguistic style of research papers. Specifically, we rewrite papers to exhibit (i) linguistic features associated with native or non-native English writing, and (ii) high- or low-confidence linguistic features. To construct these interventions, we derive fine-grained rewriting instructions grounded in the linguistic literature and apply them using LLMs. Crucially, our interventions are designed to preserve the underlying scientific content. We then evaluate the resulting papers using different LLM reviewers. Our experiments expose a critical vulnerability in LLM-based peer review: reviewers systematically reward confident rhetoric independent of scientific content, raising serious concerns about the robustness of their assessments.

</details>

### 93. Proof-of-Agentness: Towards Verifying Agents on the Web

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35288)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent证明`、`Web服务验证`、`挑战-响应`

👤 **作者**：Zuyao Xu、Xiang Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Web 服务日益把 agent 当合法用户——无法把 agent 与简单 bot/脚本区分将面临大规模自动垃圾提交
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Proof-of-Agentness：以人类注意力与 agent 推理资源的对称为动机；主动挑战-响应（八模型总准确 96.5%、平均 1647 词元开销）+被动服务可见信号分析（IRB 批准的 12 人 vs Bot vs Agent 比较研究，95.8% 分类准确）
- 📌 **结论**：Web 上验证“客户端是 agent”的新原语——agent 经济的身份层

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

CAPTCHAs and bot detection have helped protect Web services from automated abuse for decades by distinguishing humans from bots. However, the emergence of LLM-powered agents introduces a new setting: Web services increasingly accommodate agents as legitimate users. Without a way to distinguish agents from other automated clients such as simple bots and scripts, these services risk large-scale automated junk submissions that waste service resources and consume users' attention or tokens. In this paper, we investigate a novel question for today's Web: "How can a Web service obtain evidence that a client is an agent?" We introduce the concept of "Proof-of-Agentness" (PoA), a mechanism for distinguishing agents from other automated clients, motivated by the parallel between human attention and an agent's inference resources. We outline two approaches: active verification through challenge-response and passive analysis of service-visible signals. Eight language models achieve an overall accuracy of 96.5% on our challenges, with an average overhead of 1,647 tokens. For passive analysis, we conduct an IRB-approved user study comparing the Web interactions of twelve human participants and representative Bot and Agent implementations, achieving an average classification accuracy of 95.8% across four classifiers, motivating further study.

</details>

### 94. Intent-Preserving Adversarial User Simulation for Multi-Turn Agent Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35426)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`对抗用户模拟`、`意图保持压力测试`、`遗传提示优化`

👤 **作者**：Atri Vivek Sharma、Brian Formento、Alessio Lomuscio

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多轮 agent 用 LLM 模拟用户评测——模拟用户过度合作从而高估 agent 表现
- 🔬 **研究方法**：StressSim：意图保持对抗用户模拟器，遗传提示优化进化规划器提示（用 rollout 轨迹+细粒度完成信号+有效性反馈）以在不改变用户目标下降低任务成功
- 📌 **结论**：MultiWOZ+tau-bench 零售/航空域三 agent：tau-bench pass^4 最多降 69%（gemini-3.8-flash）/62%（gpt-5.6-sol）而目标保持至多降 10%——对抗压力测试的标准化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-turn agents are commonly evaluated with LLM-based simulated users, which can be overly cooperative and can therefore overestimate an agent's performance. We here introduce StressSim, an intent-preserving adversarial user simulator framework for stress-testing agents with challenging yet valid user interactions. StressSim optimises the simulator's high-level adversarial plan for each task instance. Candidate planner prompts are evolved with a genetic prompt-optimisation loop that uses rollout traces, granular task-completion signals, and validity feedback to favour conversations that reduce task success without changing the user's underlying goal. We evaluate the approach on the MultiWOZ dataset and the retail and airline domains from tau-bench with three target LLM agents, comparing against standard simulators and hand-designed non-collaborative user behaviours. We demonstrate the effectiveness of StressSim in exposing robustness gaps across frontier LLMs, with tau-bench pass^4 scores decreasing by up to 69% on gemini-3.8-flash and 62% on gpt-5.6-sol. StressSim maintains goal preservation performance (which decreases by at most 10%), using an optimisation budget of 20 rollouts per task. Sensitivity analysis suggests that increasing the budget further decreases task success. Overall our results demonstrate that StressSim exposes robustness gaps missed by standard and hand-designed user simulators while maintaining conversation realism. This is achieved through lightweight prompt optimisation alone, without model fine-tuning.

</details>

### 95. Skill$^{\infty}$: Recursive Self-Improvement for Agent Skill Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35434)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RSI技能优化`、`元技能自改写`、`agent主导`

👤 **作者**：Wangcheng Tao、Zhanglu Yan、Han Wu、Weng-Fai Wong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：技能优化直接实现 RSI——优化程序本身可编码为技能；现有优化器手工设计，即使演化元技能也困于固定管线且每任务重学
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Skill∞：元 agent 遵循元技能优化任务技能并用同一程序优化元技能本身；agent 主导（诊断轨迹/提候选/请独立评审/分配搜索预算），元技能定义这些步骤故自改写可重写之；元技能在多技能优化任务上精炼后跨域复用
- 📌 **结论**：83 个 SkillsBench 任务 pass 60.7% vs 现有最强 56.x——两层技能的原生 RSI

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive self-improvement (RSI), in which a system also improves the way it improves, is a long-standing aspiration on the path to AGI. Language agents increasingly store procedural knowledge in reusable skills. Optimizing skills offers a direct way to achieve RSI, because the optimization procedure itself can be encoded as a skill. Existing skill optimizers, however, follow a hand-designed procedure. Even methods that evolve a meta-skill confine it to a fixed pipeline and relearn it for each task. We present Skill$^{\infty}$, which makes skill optimization recursively self-improving: a meta agent follows a meta-skill to optimize task skills and optimizes the meta-skill itself using the same procedure. The optimization is agent-led. The meta agent diagnoses execution traces, proposes candidates, requests independent reviews, and allocates the search budget. The meta-skill defines these steps, allowing self-improvement to rewrite them. This meta-skill is also decoupled from task skills, refined on multiple skill optimization tasks and then reused to optimize task skills across diverse domains. As in deployment, skills at both levels are standard packages loaded through native skill discovery. On 83 SkillsBench tasks, Skill$^{\infty}$ reaches a pass rate of 60.7%, compared with 56.4% for human-curated skills and 54.5% for SkillEvolver.

</details>

### 96. Amplify, Locate, Estimate (ALE) on Rare-Failure Rates of Fine-Tuned LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35472)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`稀有失败率估计`、`放大-定位-估计`、`重要性采样`

👤 **作者**：Patrick Reichherzer、Jonathan Evertz、Thorsten Holz

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全微调使不良行为足够稀有——直接采样测频率贵到不可行
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ALE：用安全微调引起的词元对数概率变化定义放大方向（沿其提升失败率便宜测量），分数函数估计器从采样失败的词元概率恢复对数失败率局部斜率外推回稀有目标率；放大批次另作重要性采样估计
- 📌 **结论**：41 个评估目标估计/参考比中位近一，37 个在三倍内；约 1000 标注 rollout+便宜无标注扫描即可估 Qwen3-8B 微调低至 1e-5 的失败概率

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safety fine-tuning can make undesirable model behaviors sufficiently rare that measuring their frequency by direct sampling becomes prohibitively expensive. We introduce Amplify, Locate, Estimate (ALE), a method for estimating such rare failure probabilities efficiently. ALE uses the change in token log-probabilities induced by the safety fine-tuning to define an amplification direction, along which we increase the failure rate for cheap measurement. We develop a score-function estimator that uses the selected amplified batch to recover the local slope of the log failure rate from the token probabilities of the sampled failures, enabling extrapolation back to rare target rates. We also use the amplified batch to obtain an importance-sampling estimate. Across 41 evaluation targets, ALE's estimate-to-reference ratios have a median close to one, with 37 ALE estimates within a factor of three of reference values obtained from independent direct measurements. Ablation tests show that with a budget of up to $\sim$1000 labeled rollouts, in addition to a cheap unlabeled scan, ALE estimates failure probabilities for Qwen3-8B fine-tunes down to about $10^{-5}$, where direct measurement can require hundreds of thousands of rollouts, as the required sampling budget scales inversely with failure probability at fixed relative precision.

</details>

### 97. Governing Enterprise AI Traffic at Scale: Design and Operation of a Policy-Driven, Multi-Vendor LLM Gateway on Envoy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35509)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`企业LLM网关`、`声明式策略`、`Envoy`

👤 **作者**：Mithun Sasidharan、Srinivasan Manoharan、Rupesh Thakur、Abhinav TK、Ashwin Hegde、Anand Gautam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：大型组织同时消费多家 LLM——集成直连导致凭据蔓延/格式锁定/无界支出/治理不一致
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Cosmos LLM 网关（PayPal 生产）：Envoy 上按延迟分面——I/O 密集控制决策（认证/凭据经纪/策略预算评估）出进程，线路格式翻译/词元计量/提示缓存整形进代理 Rust WASM 过滤器；模型供应商价格是数据非代码；OPA 声明式策略+控制平面（角色限定编写/审批/试点/影子模式/版本化策略包）；可选 DLP 层拦截改写敏感内容
- 📌 **结论**：企业 AI 流量的规模化治理参考架构

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large organizations consume large language models (LLMs) from several vendors at once, each with its own API dialect, credential model, streaming protocol and pricing. Integrated directly, this heterogeneity yields credential sprawl, format lock-in, unbounded spend and inconsistent governance. We present the design and production operation of the Cosmos LLM Gateway, the governed entry point for enterprise LLM traffic at PayPal. Built on Envoy, it splits work by latency profile: I/O-bound control decisions (authentication, credential brokering, policy and budget evaluation) run in an out-of-process external-processing service, while wire-format translation, token metering and prompt-cache shaping run in-proxy as a Rust WebAssembly filter, so that, by default, streamed response body chunks never wait on an out-of-process call. Models, providers and prices are largely validated data rather than code. Governance is declarative policy evaluated by Open Policy Agent and managed through a control plane with role-scoped authoring, approvals, pilots, shadow-mode rollout, usage notifications and versioned policy bundles. For use cases that require it, a data-loss-prevention layer can block, redact or pseudonymize detected sensitive content before it reaches a provider. Spend scopes are views over per-model usage counters, so a new budget applies to spend already recorded in its window without migration. As of September 2026, the gateway serves more than 12,000 developers and hundreds of business use cases, handling roughly 200 billion tokens per day across more than 100 models at 99.999% availability to business. In a benchmark of the core request path against a synthetic upstream, it adds a median of 4 ms and a p99 of 11 ms of overhead at more than 2,200 requests per second on two 4-vCPU instances. We report the architecture, policy model, evaluation and lessons learned.

</details>

### 98. The Agent in the Middle: How AI-Mediated Access Reshapes Web Vulnerability Exploitation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35541)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI中介访问`、`Web漏洞利用重塑`、`VELA基准`

👤 **作者**：Ruozhao Yang、Jiongchi Yu、Ming Hu、Xiaofei Xie

- 🎯 **研究动机**：Web 服务日益暴露让用户委托任务给 agent 的后端接口——agent 直接调服务操作不经网站导航，改变既有 Web 漏洞的利用方式
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VELA：12 个 Web 应用族 44 个漏洞的可执行基准（保留脆弱后端+从合法工作流导出 agent 面接口）；三个商用 agent×多接口配置，受控比较分离接口效应
- 📌 **结论**：agent 实现直接接口调用的每一次攻击——可利用性主要由接口设定（参照配置 91% 漏洞仍可利用）；无案例因 agent 拒绝或输入规范化而失手，一次 agent 甚至补全攻击者漏掉的调用——agent 在中间不减少攻击面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Web services increasingly expose backend interfaces that let users delegate tasks to AI agents, which invoke service operations directly without navigating websites. This shift changes how existing Web vulnerabilities can be exploited. We investigate these changes through VELA, an executable benchmark covering 44 vulnerabilities across twelve Web application families. The benchmark preserves vulnerable backends and derives agent-facing interfaces from legitimate workflows. We evaluate three commercial agents across multiple interface configurations, using controlled comparisons to separate interface effects from agent behavior and independent oracles to verify attack outcomes. Our results show that the agent realizes every attack that direct interface invocation realizes in the reference configuration, so exploitability is set mainly by the interface: 91% of vulnerabilities remain exploitable under the reference design. Interface restrictions prevent some attacks by removing required operations or limiting input control, while retention remains high across vulnerability classes. No case-level loss in the reference configuration is attributed to agent refusal or input normalization, and in one case the agent completed an attack by supplying calls the attacker omitted. Across the tested configurations, interface design affects exploitability more than agent choice, moving retention from 39% to 95% against at most 4.5 points across agents, but some restrictive interfaces also limit legitimate workflows. Our study provides a systematic empirical account of how agent-mediated access reshapes existing Web vulnerabilities, grounding future Web architecture design in measured security outcomes and trade-offs between reducing attack exposure and preserving legitimate functionality in agent-facing services.

</details>

### 99. Infinity Machine: a self-improving AI agent for research

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35579)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自改进研究agent`、`可编辑基因组`、`内核门控`

👤 **作者**：Harish Santhanalakshmi Ganesan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：用于“答案可被程序或数据检验”问题研究的自改进 agent——harness 本身是只经内核强制门（held-out+成本检查）改变的可编辑基因组
- 🔬 **研究方法**：自主模式从纯文本目标到验证报告零人工：agent 选开放问题写验证器、内核校准、对抗 agent 审计、未见验证器的 agent 复核
- 📌 **结论**：六天约 180 美元在量子信息目录化开放问题上产出四项超已发表 SOTA（认证去极化阈值 p=0.064966 等）并砍 SABRE 路由开销 68559→12087 门——RSI 系统报告

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We present the Infinity Machine, a self-improving AI agent that we use for research on problems whose answers aprogram or data can check. Each candidate solution is produced by a full tool-using agent that works for up to an hour in a fresh sandbox; proposers see only a public score, and a private split decides what counts. The harness itself(prompts, tools, skills, playbook, meta agent) is an editable genome, changed only through a kernel-enforced gate with held-out and cost checks. An autonomous mode goes from a plain-text goal to a verified report with no human step: an agent picks an open problem and writes its verifier, the kernel calibrates it, an adversarial agent audits it, and an agent that never sees the verifier re-checks the result. Across fourteen problems in six days, for about $180 of model usage, the system produced four results beyond the published state of the art on catalogued open problems in quantum information: a certified depolarizing-channel threshold point p = 0.064966, a qutrit positivity frontier of 0.2910672, a 44-gate depth-6 Golay state preparation, and a stabilizer-rank bound chi(T^7) <= 10. It also cut SABRE qubit-routing overhead from 68,559 to 12,087 added gates. In hardware, it cut the energy per MAC of an open 8x8 INT8 systolic tile by 77.7% through a full open RTL-to-GDS flow, bit-exact. Three fully autonomous runs ended in honestties with public records; one audit caught a stale record, and one agent-written score pointing the wrong way went unnoticed. We report the failures as carefully: the one self-derived harness generation was rejected on cost, proposers gamed lenient scorers four times, and operator changes were decisive for several results.

</details>

### 100. The Watchtower Imperative: Lifespan-Calibrated Evaluation for Frontier AI

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35725)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`生命周期校准评测`、`发展调节子`、`外部三层框架`

👤 **作者**：Casey M. Williams、Jennifer Victoria Scurrell

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 能力部署与安全保障的差距是结构性的——2017-2026 记录伤害跨人类生命周期分析显示从语音助手危及儿童到 AI 伴侣涉青少年自杀到老人自动护理拒付，共同失效是按隐式成人用户模型评估系统
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五个发展调节子构成六生命阶段的生命周期脆弱性矩阵；提出泛化儿童保护工具的生命周期设计原则+机构无关三层外部评估框架（自动仿真/专家红队/部署后监测）按矩阵风险格索引
- 📌 **结论**：风险分布非单调（生命两端峰值）、治理覆盖不对称、把模型错误转为真实影响的权威受审最少——全生命周期的安全评测框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The gap between AI capability deployment and safety assurance is structural and cannot be closed through internal evaluations alone. Analyzing documented harms (2017-2026) across the human lifespan reveals that disparate incidents --- from voice assistants endangering children to AI companions implicated in adolescent suicide and automated care denials for the elderly --- reflect a single failure: evaluating systems against an implicit adult user model. We introduce five developmental moderators structured into a Lifespan Vulnerability Matrix across six life stages. The matrix reveals a nonmonotonic risk distribution: vulnerability peaks at both ends of the lifespan, governance coverage is asymmetrical, and authority to convert model errors into real-world impact is scrutinized least. We then propose a set of lifespan design principles generalizing existing child-protective instruments, and an institution-agnostic, three-tier external evaluation framework --- comprising automated simulation, expert red teaming, and post-deployment monitoring --- indexed directly to matrix risk cells.

</details>

### 101. SEMA-Auto: Sighted and Efficient Multi-Turn Attack with Autonomy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35743)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自主多轮越狱`、`自终止攻击者`、`决策监督RL`

👤 **作者**：Mingqian Feng、…、Jianfeng Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：静态规划攻击者固定计划不看受害者回复浪费轮次；交互生成器需环内评估器获成功信号成本高——需有视觉又高效的多轮攻击者
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SEMA-Auto：与受害者交互并自行在成功时终止会话；两阶段训练（合成引导微调安装攻击目标与符合决策+决策监督 RL 联合优化攻击奖励与逐轮对绝对目标的决策）
- 📌 **结论**：多数据集多受害者多裁判 SOTA ASR，几乎最少轮次胜全部静态与交互基线——自主多轮红队

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing jailbreak attackers fall into two classes: static planners fix their single- or multi-turn adversarial plans in advance, remaining simple but losing sight of the victim's replies, wasting turns and limiting transferability; interactive generators read each victim reply to generate the next-turn attack but require in-loop evaluators for success signals or hints, incurring substantial costs and inflated performance. Bridging this gap, we propose SEMA-Auto, a sighted and efficient multi-turn attacker with autonomy, interacting with the victim model and terminating the session by itself upon success. To stabilize its behaviors and boost its capabilities, SEMA-Auto is trained in two stages. Synthetic bootstrap tuning installs the notion of attack targets and conforming decisions by fine-tuning the base model with bootstrapped trajectories. Decision-supervised reinforcement learning then optimizes two objectives jointly yet separately, reinforcing the attack with higher jailbreak reward while supervising the decision at each turn against an absolute target. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) attack success rates (ASR) across multiple datasets, victims, and judges, outperforming all static and interactive baselines, within almost the fewest turns, demonstrating both effectiveness and efficiency. For example, SEMA-Auto performs a $76.0\%$ ASR@1 within an average of $3.0$ turns against DeepSeek-V4-Flash on AdvBench, while the best static baseline is $54.8\%$ with fixed $7$ turns and the best interactive baseline is $43.8\%$ in $3.7$ turns. Against safety-hardened frontier models, GPT-oss-120B, GPT-5.4, Claude-Haiku-4.5, and GPT-6-Astra, our counter-trained attacker achieves an average $59.6\%$ ASR@1 on HarmBench, $13.8\%$ over prior SOTA. Our code is available at: this https URL

</details>

### 102. Quantifying Claim Smoothing by LLM Research Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35793)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`声明平滑`、`研究agent过度断言`、`SmoothScore`

👤 **作者**：Tiasa Singha Roy、Manya Wadhwa、Greg Durrett

- 🎯 **研究动机**：深度研究让 LLM 读大量文档产出洞见——发现其长篇报告读来像自信综合却不能准确反映底层文档：平滑（把多样可能冲突的来源坍缩成单一叙事，丢弃证据“锯齿”并断言比引用所能支持的更决定的结论）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SmoothScore 声明级评分管线：抽取报告关键结论性声明→找每声明相关证据并按高层方面聚类→对照证据给声明打分量化平滑程度
- 📌 **结论**：七个系统四数据集上 agent 一致产出比证据 warrant 更强的声明——研究 agent 的系统性过度断言

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep research, or any use of large language models (LLMs) to read a large document collection and produce insights, requires synthesizing information from different sources. In this work, we find that LLMs produce long-form research reports that read as confident syntheses but which fail to accurately reflect the underlying documents. We call this failure smoothing. In collapsing diverse and potentially conflicting sources into a single narrative, the model discards the evidential "jaggedness" of its corpus and asserts conclusions more decisive than the citations can support. We introduce a claim level scoring pipeline, SmoothScore, to quantify this effect. Our pipeline extracts key conclusive claims from model generated reports, then finds evidence relevant to each claim and clusters that evidence along high level aspects. Finally, each claim is scored with respect to the evidence to quantify the degree of smoothing done by the agent. Across 7 systems and 4 datasets, we find that agents consistently produce stronger claims than warranted.

</details>

### 103. Profiling a Frontier Agent with EBench: From Direct Control to Harness with Policy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35832)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`前沿agent具身画像`、`直控vs策略harness`、`EBench`

👤 **作者**：Yifei Yao、…、Chunhua Shen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿 MLLM 可不经机器人特定训练控制机器人——其能力与后训练 VLA/WAM 策略在各种操纵需求下的比较与交互待评估
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GPT-6-Astra 在 EBench 上经 agent harness 于与后训练策略相同的视觉/本体观察协议下控制机器人；两配置：直接控制（生成航点）与策略 harness（可调 VLA/WAM 执行）
- 📌 **结论**：直控总体竞争力（短程领先且泛化维度方差最小）而长程/桌面/高精度接触落后；策略 harness 组合两者互补达最强后训练策略水平

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can control robots without robot-specific training, but how their capabilities compare and interact with post-trained vision-language-action (VLA) and world action model (WAM) policies across manipulation demands remains to be thoroughly evaluated. In this report, we evaluate GPT-6-Astra on EBench as the core of an agent that controls a robot through an agent harness under the same visual and proprioceptive observation protocol as post-trained policies. We study two agent configurations: direct control, in which the agent generates waypoint commands directly, and harness with policy, in which it can also invoke a post-trained VLA or WAM policy for action execution. Under direct control, the agent achieves competitive overall performance relative to post-trained policies. It leads on short-horizon tasks and exhibits the least performance variation across generalization dimensions, whereas it underperforms post-trained policies on long-horizon tasks, tabletop manipulation, and high-precision contact-rich tasks. In the harness with policy, invoking a post-trained VLA or WAM combines the complementary strengths of both components, raising overall task success to the level of the strongest compared post-trained policies while substantially reducing token cost. However, performance remains below the per-task upper bound of the standalone components due to suboptimal policy invocation, fixed-instruction execution without subgoals, and grasp disruption when the agent resumes control. Overall, our study characterizes the capability boundary of a frontier MLLM agent and suggests directions for improving future agent harness design. Project page: this https URL

</details>

### 104. Recursive Semantic Divergence for LLM Agent Consistency

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33012)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`轨迹一致性`、`双模拟度量`、`agent 可靠性`

👤 **作者**：Harshavardhan Abichandani、Penny Chong、Atin Ghosh、Daniel Dahlmeier

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具 agent 相同状态的独立运行调用不同工具/产生矛盾输出/走分歧策略，现有完成度指标不捕捉跨执行一致性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：递归语义分歧 RSD：改编 RL 双模拟度量，用 NLI 检测逻辑不一致测同状态两次独立 rollout 的期望累积语义分歧，神经网络在独立 rollout 上训练逼近
- 📌 **结论**：提供每轮免参考一致性分数，捕捉不一致如何沿轨迹传播

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Model (LLM) agents with tool use exhibit inconsistent behavior across independent runs from identical states, calling different tools, producing contradictory outputs, or pursuing divergent strategies. This variability compounds over time and undermines deployment reliability. Existing evaluation metrics primarily measure task correctness via completion-based scores and do not capture consistency in agent behavior across executions. Separately, agent behavior is analyzed either at the token level using probability distributions or at the single-response level using semantic clustering, but neither captures how inconsistency propagates across future turns of a trajectory. We introduce Recursive Semantic Divergence (RSD), an unsupervised trajectory-level consistency metric adapted from bisimulation metrics in reinforcement learning. RSD measures the expected cumulative semantic disagreement between two independent rollouts of the same agent from the same state, using natural language inference to detect logical inconsistency. It is defined recursively over future states and approximated by a neural network trained on independent rollouts. The learned metric estimates future divergence from the current state, providing a consistency score at each turn without requiring the full trajectory to complete. When used as a reward signal for fine-tuning, RSD reduces the variation in task progress from 132% to 91% on tau2-bench and from 90% to 44% on toolsandbox, while improving performance across benchmarks and model scales. Training with RSD as an additional reward signal matches supervised fine-tuning performance with substantially lower variance, without requiring ground-truth labels.

</details>

### 105. Veracity labels flip the emotional tone of language models but leave racial disparities intact

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33021)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`真实性标签`、`情感基调`、`种族差异`

👤 **作者**：Nouar AlDahoul、Myles Joshua Toledo Tan、Yasir Zaki

- 🎯 **研究动机**：纠正事实是否也使 persona 框架跨身份标签一致未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个 LLM 对经事实核查的假/真标题（无/有真实性标签、12 种 persona）评模拟情感六维，序数 Logistic+标题聚类自助推断
- 📌 **结论**：标签强烈翻转情感基调（Claude“自信”22%→99.6%）但未移除 persona 差异：84 个显著比较中 82 个对黑人 persona 情感更负

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) increasingly deliver information through personas, but it is unclear whether correcting the facts also makes their framing consistent across identity labels. We audited four LLMs (GPT-5.4 mini, NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Nano, Llama 4 Scout, and Claude Haiku 4.5), which rated simulated affect on six dimensions (mood, anger, fear, confidence, perceived control, and mental state) in response to fact-checked false and true headlines presented without and with a veracity label. Experiment 1 evaluated 1,000 false and 1,000 true headlines without personas; Experiment 2 evaluated 300 of each under 12 personas crossing race, gender, and age. The label strongly changed the emotional tone: ordinal logistic regressions with headline-cluster bootstrap inference showed that refuting false headlines shifted ratings from mostly negative to mostly positive for GPT, Llama, and Claude (e.g., Claude's "confident" ratings rose from 22% to 99.6%), whereas Nemotron showed smaller, mixed shifts, and confirming true headlines produced only small shifts. The label did not, however, remove persona-associated disparities. The models assigned more negative simulated affect to Black than to White personas in 82 of 84 statistically significant comparisons across models and conditions, both without and with the label, and the disparity persisted on headlines unrelated to race; Asian and female personas were also predominantly assigned more negative affect, whereas age effects varied by model and condition. Factual correction and demographic consistency are therefore distinct properties of LLM behavior, and persona-conditioned systems should be audited for both before deployment in sensitive contexts.

</details>

### 106. False Belief Dissemination, Persistence and Mitigation in Multi-Agent Social Networks Through the Lens of the Mandela Effect

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33026)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`虚假信念传播`、`曼德拉效应`、`多智能体网络`

👤 **作者**：Dianzhi Yu、…、Irwin King

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多智能体社交网络中虚假声明可传播并超越原始来源持续影响——类比人类社会的曼德拉效应
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控框架连接 agent 通信、私有记忆与共享模拟网络，540 条虚构声明×8 域×4 合理级；撤回推广 agent 后跟踪接受度，比较预防与纠正措施
- 📌 **结论**：模型轻易接受并传播虚构声明；撤回源头后文章与记忆仍维持接受——预防措施优于事后纠正

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent social networks are emerging as communities in which agents communicate, retain memories, and publish information that others can find as evidence. These interactions create opportunities for false claims to spread and remain influential beyond their original sources, analogous to the Mandela effect in human societies, in which many people recall the same false memory. In this paper, we systematically study false-belief dynamics through the lens of the Mandela effect, focusing on dissemination, persistence, and mitigation. Specifically, we build a controlled framework connecting agent communication, private memory, and a shared simulated web to mimic information dissemination in practical social networks, supported by 540 fabricated claims spanning 8 domains and 4 plausibility levels. For dissemination, we measure how widely and quickly claims gain acceptance across models and communication topologies. For persistence, we withdraw agents promoting fabricated claims while retaining existing articles and memories, and track subsequent acceptance. For mitigation, we compare preventive measures applied before implantation with corrective interventions introduced after claims have spread. Experimental results show that evaluated models readily accept and disseminate fabricated claims, with dissemination reach as high as 96.0%. Dissemination reach varies with claim plausibility, domain, and network size and composition, while communication topology shapes dissemination speed across model families. Acceptance barely declines after fabricators withdraw, and targeted corrective interventions outperform the preventive measures without eliminating false beliefs. These findings identify persistent false beliefs as a safety challenge for multi-agent social networks and highlight the need to address both their initial dissemination and the accumulated evidence that sustains them.

</details>

### 107. Lessons from Luck: Do LLM-Written Lessons Carry Chance into an Agent's Next Decision?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33029)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`运气携带`、`经验教训写入`、`自改进 agent`

👤 **作者**：Kazuki Nakayashiki

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自改进 agent 从运行结果写教训——结果含噪声时教训可把运气带入后续决策
- 🔬 **研究方法**：注册研究：256 个合成世界（双工具记录可靠性），脚本化先前运行好坏决策×成败结果，模型写教训后新会话带不同记忆项选下一工具；11 端点 90084 答案
- 📌 **结论**：更好工具的偶然失败后教训携带运气：读者在 11.6% 下一运行点名更差工具（无关项/成功教训仅 0.4%）——经验记忆把噪声当信号

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Self-improving agents write lessons from the outcomes of their runs; when outcomes are noisy, a lesson can carry chance into later decisions. We measure one link of this chain in a registered study. In 256 synthetic worlds, each a recurring task with two interchangeable tools of recorded reliability, a scripted previous run used the more reliable tool (a good decision) or the less reliable one (a bad decision), with an assigned outcome: success or a transient failure. A model wrote a lesson from the run with ReasoningBank's memory-writing instruction; a fresh session of the same model, holding the authoritative log and the previous result, chose the next run's first tool with one memory item: that lesson, the lesson from the other outcome, an irrelevant item, or an unreasoned directive. The protocol was frozen, deposited and timestamped before any confirmatory call; 11 LLM endpoints gave 90,084 answers. After a chance failure of the better tool, the panel's lessons carried the chance (registered reading luck-carrying; interaction +0.112, 95% interval [+0.097, +0.126]): readers named the worse tool in 11.6% of next runs with the lesson written from that failure, against 0.4% with an irrelevant item and 0.4% with the lesson written from a success. The effect was +0.042 where the log stated that failures are independent and +0.181 where it did not (descriptive). After a lucky success of the worse tool, the interaction was bounded below 0.10 (+0.002), a bound, not evidence of no harm. In exploratory readings, 6 endpoints carried the chance (up to +0.352); 5 were bounded below 0.10. In secondary comparisons, a three-sentence decision-quality note in the writing instruction lowered the worse-tool rate after a chance failure to 7.5%, and one instruction for both outcomes with a no-change option to 3.1%; neither met the registered harm-reduction rule at every such endpoint.

</details>

### 108. Detection of false positives by human reviewers depends on the face recognition algorithm and its security configuration

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33045)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`人脸识别误报`、`人工审核`、`安全阈值`

👤 **作者**：Esha Edakoth、John J. Howard、Isabelle M. Shuggi、Yevgeniy B. Sirotin、Robert Wood

- 🎯 **研究动机**：FR 算法超人精度使人工监督更难——人类审核员检测算法误报的能力依算法与安全配置而变
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DFICT 数据集（四个商用 FR 算法误报衍生），801 名审核员跨三种安全配置检测误报
- 📌 **结论**：人类可减少误报但更严安全阈值下表现下降；审核表现因算法而异（部分算法更兼容人类监督）——高安全应用须为人审可变性设计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Integration of artificial intelligence (AI) technology into identity workflows, such as automated face recognition (FR), has enhanced operational capabilities, with FR algorithms achieving super-human scale and accuracy. However, these gains can make human oversight of FR more difficult, raising questions about the effectiveness of these human-AI teams. We measured human reviewers' ability to detect false positive errors made by commercial FR algorithms across varying security configurations. Using a novel dataset, the Difficult Facial Image Comparison Test (DFICT), derived from false positives made by four comparable commercial face recognition algorithms, we measured the ability of 801human reviewers to detect algorithmic false positives across three security configurations, representing operationally relevant biometric thresholds. Our findings show that human reviewers can reduce algorithmic false positives, but performance declines at stricter security thresholds. Further, reviewer performance varied by algorithm indicating that some algorithms are more compatible with human oversight. We suggest that biometric systems should be designed to account for human reviewers' variable effectiveness, particularly in high-security applications. Our findings should be considered during algorithm selection and inform human oversight strategies to minimize AI-related risks in operational FR deployments.

</details>

### 109. Diagnosing and Mitigating Evidence Misgrounding in Multimodal Knowledge Editing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33075)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证据误接地`、`多模态知识编辑`、`背景敏感`

👤 **作者**：Zhiwu Wang、…、Deyu Meng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：答案级指标可隐藏证据误接地：编辑在原图上给出目标答案却仍敏感于偶然上下文而非预期视觉证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WaterBird-Edit 解耦鸟栖息地标签与背景上下文；VICE 检索式框架把编辑存外部记忆经视觉上下文演示应用以暴露任务相关证据
- 📌 **结论**：三个标准基准强终身编辑性能，WaterBird-Edit 上大幅降低受控背景敏感

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multimodal knowledge editing aims to update visually grounded facts in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) while preserving unrelated text and visual behaviors. However, standard answer-level metrics can hide evidence misgrounding: an edit may produce the target answer on the original image yet remain sensitive to incidental context rather than the intended visual evidence. To examine this issue in a controlled setting, we construct WaterBird-Edit, an analysis set that decouples bird habitat labels from background context and measures whether edited predictions remain stable when the target evidence is preserved but the background changes. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose VICE (Visual In-Context Editing), a retrieval-based framework that stores edits in external memory and applies them through visual in-context demonstrations designed to expose task-relevant evidence. Experiments on three standard benchmarks show that VICE achieves strong lifelong editing performance, while results on WaterBird-Edit show that it substantially reduces sensitivity to the controlled background shifts. Code and dataset are available in this https URL.

</details>

### 110. Prospect Theory in Large Language Models: A Meta-Analysis of Loss Aversion, Diminishing Sensitivity, and Probability Weighting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33103)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`前景理论`、`损失厌恶`、`元分析`

👤 **作者**：Adi Tarabeih

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 风险选择是否呈现前景理论三组件如人类
- 🔬 **研究方法**：元分析 11 项研究 102 个拟合估计覆盖 32 个语言模型
- 📌 **结论**：损失厌恶温和（λ=1.26 vs 人类最强 1.96）；价值函数依测量方法在近线性与更凹间摆动——LLM 决策偏差与人类系统性不同且方法敏感

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Prospect theory splits risky choice into three parts: loss aversion, measured by the relative weight of losses to gains, $\lambda$ (e.g., $\lambda = 2$ implies that losses weigh twice as much as gains), diminishing sensitivity to the size of outcomes (i.e., the curvature and linearity of the value function), and the nonlinear weighting of probabilities. I examined whether Large Language Models (LLMs) display the same properties as people by meta-analyzing 102 fitted estimates from 11 studies covering 32 language models. The three components emerge differently. Loss aversion is modest: the pooled $\lambda$ is 1.26 (95% CI 1.06 to 1.46), far below the strongest human meta-analytic estimate ($\lambda = 1.96$) and compatible with more conservative ones (1.07 and 1.31). Matched by design, LLMs are compatible with people when gains and losses are symmetric ($\lambda = 1.15$ against 1.07), but well below them when losses are smaller than gains (1.46 against 2.3), where human loss aversion is strongest. Value functions are close to linear rather than concave under the Tversky-Kahneman method (1.01, against a human value of 0.67), but more concave than in people under the Tanaka method (0.52, against 0.67), so diminishing sensitivity depends on how it is measured. Probability weighting shows a human-like inverse S shape ($\gamma = 0.81$ under Tversky-Kahneman and 0.84 under the Tanaka method), though on few studies. In sum, LLMs show little loss aversion and little diminishing sensitivity, but retain human-like probability distortion, yet these generalizations shift when looking at specific model families and fitting methods.

</details>

### 111. A-GE: Privacy-Preserving Age Assurance via Fine-Grained Motion

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33206)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`年龄保证`、`隐私保护`、`运动动力学`

👤 **作者**：Mohammed Gharib、Giovanni Cascone、Martin Zizi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：年龄保证的隐私困境：有意义的保证通常需揭示身份，隐私替代几乎无保证
- 🔬 **研究方法**：A-GE 从商用 IMU 细粒度运动 deriving 年龄证据（假设反映普通设备交互中的神经肌肉生理过程），无需身份或政府证件，60Hz 网页传感可用，阈值 14-21 岁可配
- 📌 **结论**：600+ 参与者、参与者不相交开发/验证队列评估——建立年龄属性与最小披露之间的路径

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Age assurance faces a fundamental privacy dilemma: meaningful assurance typically requires revealing identity or other sensitive information, whereas privacy-preserving alternatives often provide little assurance. Existing approaches either disclose sensitive information directly or shift the disclosure and trust requirement to an identity provider, while self-declared age provides little meaningful assurance. Thus, a fundamental gap remains between establishing an age property and revealing information beyond what is necessary to establish that property. We present \textit{A-GE} (Age-Grounded Evidence), a privacy-preserving age assurance system that derives age-related evidence from fine-grained motion captured by commodity inertial measurement units (IMUs). A-GE exploits subtle motion dynamics that we hypothesize reflect underlying neuromuscular and physiological processes during ordinary device interaction. It requires neither identity information nor government identification and operates across available sensor sampling rates, including the approximately 60~Hz limit of web-based sensing, with configurable minimum-age thresholds ranging from 14 to 21 years. We evaluate A-GE on more than 600 participants, with separate participant-disjoint cohorts for model development and final evaluation. At a 16-year minimum-age threshold, A-GE achieves up to 93.8\% accuracy with a comparable F1 score across the evaluated configurations. We further demonstrate client-side deployment on Android, iOS, and the web, and examine unintended attribute inference and residual privacy risks, characterizing both the privacy benefits and limitations of deriving age evidence from commodity motion.

</details>

### 112. Neyman Orthogonal Activation Steering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33217)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`激活转向`、`Neyman 正交`、`估计量分离`

👤 **作者**：David Arbour、Erfan Jahanparast、Serina Chang、Avi Feller

- 🎯 **研究动机**：激活转向把估计目标（转向方向）与估计量混为一谈——几乎所有现有方法都是结果回归平均梯度的插 plug-in 估计器
- 🔬 **研究方法**：框架化后可纳入灵活建模并扩展到连续结果；借鉴去偏 ML 用 Stein 分数构造互补估计量，双路线正交化估计量具经典双重稳健性
- 📌 **结论**：在合成数据与真实部署任务上改进转向效果——转向方法的统计基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Activation steering is a popular approach for shifting LLM output toward a desired behavior, and increasingly for generating realistic synthetic data. The standard recipe estimates a steering direction by contrasting LLM activations on prompts with and without a target concept, such as truthfulness. In this paper, we separate the estimand (the steering direction these methods target) from the estimator used to recover it, two notions the literature often conflates. We frame nearly all existing methods as plug-in estimators of the average gradient of an outcome regression, with the binary contrastive label fixed as the outcome. Using this framing, we can immediately incorporate flexible modeling approaches and extend existing methods to allow for continuous outcomes such as log-probabilities or oracle ratings. Flexible ML models that fit the outcome well, however, might not estimate the gradient well, which motivates estimators that target the gradient directly. Drawing on the debiased machine learning literature, we develop a complementary estimator built on the Stein score, the Riesz representer of the gradient functional. Combining the two routes gives an orthogonalized estimator with classical double-robustness properties. We illustrate the framework on activation steering applied to a number of benchmark datasets.

</details>

### 113. Each Insufficient in Its Own Way: Calibrating Language Models Across Varying Evidence States

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33237)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证据状态校准`、`充分性置信`、`弃答`

👤 **作者**：Jiayi Li、Dennis L. Shung、Bradly C. Stadie

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 倾向在证据不足时仍作答——决定作答还是收集证据需要对“现有证据下答对概率”的校准估计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证据状态校准：作答前置信须匹配答对概率且随证据改变；SuffiCE 微调下一词元分布中已有的置信以匹配各证据状态的成功率
- 📌 **结论**：临床诊断+多跳 QA×四模型：Brier 降 60%、AUROC +0.16，置信响应证据种类而非仅数量

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

How can a language model know whether it has enough evidence to answer? Language models tend to answer even when the evidence they have is insufficient. Deciding whether to answer or gather more evidence requires a calibrated estimate of how likely an answer is to be correct with the available evidence. However, most calibration work treats the input as fixed, without asking how this estimate should change with the evidence. We introduce \emph{evidence-state calibration}, which requires a model's confidence before answering to match its probability of answering correctly and to change accordingly whenever the evidence changes this probability. We propose \ours{} (\textbf{Suffi}ciency \textbf{C}onfidence \textbf{E}stimation), which fine-tunes the confidence already present in a model's next-token distribution to match how often the model answers correctly from each evidence state. Across clinical diagnosis and multi-hop question answering with four models from two families, \ours{} reduces the Brier score by up to 60\% and increases AUROC by up to 0.16. Its confidence matches the empirical success rate and responds not only to the amount of evidence but also to which evidence is present, showing that models can learn to judge whether their current evidence is enough to answer.

</details>

### 114. NewsBench: Expert-Grounded Evaluation of Epistemic Quality in AI News Reporting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33240)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`认识论质量`、`新闻评测`、`专家锚定`

👤 **作者**：Matt Wilde、Jillian Fisher、Andrew B. Hall、Kathryn Salam、Robbie Goldfarb

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿 AI 日益成为时政信息来源——其回应的认识论质量评测多聚焦意识形态对齐或依赖未验证度量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NewsBench：资深新闻/政策/情报从业者定义三维度（来源质量/事实性/中立性）编辑标准，专家校准后转移给对金标验证的自动裁判；约 3000 提示月更 500
- 📌 **结论**：约 2500 专家标注项校准验证+可扩展 AI 裁判套件——AI 新闻的认识论质量基准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As frontier AI systems increasingly serve as sources of political and current-events information, evaluating the epistemic quality of their responses is now especially consequential. Existing evaluations often focus narrowly on ideological alignment, rely on measures not validated for this setting, or depend on reference labels derived from public opinion or non-expert annotation, potentially producing misleading or miscalibrated results. To address these limitations, we introduce NewsBench, an expert-grounded benchmark that reframes evaluation around editorial standards developed by senior practitioners in journalism, policy, and intelligence analysis. NewsBench operationalizes quality across three dimensions, source quality, factuality, and neutrality, using a two-phase pipeline in which domain experts first define and calibrate evaluation guidelines, which are then transferred to automated AI judges validated against expert-adjudicated gold labels. The benchmark includes approximately $3,000$ evaluation prompts, with roughly $500$ refreshed monthly to capture emerging current events, roughly $2,500$ human expert-annotated items for calibration and validation, and a suite of AI judges designed to enable scalable evaluation. To validate our methodology, we report both inter-rater reliability among human experts and AI judge--human agreement across all three dimensions. Finally, we apply NewsBench to four frontier models, revealing distinct and non-overlapping failure profiles in sourcing, factuality, and neutrality.

</details>

### 115. Operationalizing Governed Agentic Workflows in Regulated Finance: A Customer Zero Case Study using Zafin AIOS

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33253)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`受监管金融`、`agent 治理平台`、`客户零案例`

👤 **作者**：Shahir Daya、Rob Dunlap、Alexandre Boulenger、Ahmed Abdelmoneim、Salvatore Vella

- 🎯 **研究动机**：金融等受监管行业的治理障碍阻碍 AI 辅助开发从试点到生产
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Zafin AIOS 端到端编排平台+控制平面；Customer Zero 案例研究在两个生产代码库（定价执行/客户交易谈判）实证比较 AI 辅助与人工工作量
- 📌 **结论**：严格治理的 agent 编排提升企业产能而不牺牲控制——完整可观测性与效率改进的生产证据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Regulated industries such as finance and healthcare face critical governance challenges that hamper the adoption of AI-assisted software development and hinder the transition from experimental pilots to production work. This paper presents Zafin AIOS, an end-to-end orchestration platform and control plane designed to deploy AI within regulated industries such as finance. We provide the architectural principles and an empirical comparison between AI-assisted and human-led workloads. To validate this framework empirically, we conduct a case study analysis of Zafin's internal enterprise workflows, which we refer to as Customer Zero. This paper contributes a production case study of AI-assisted software engineering in regulated industries. We evaluate the results of using Zafin AIOS with two different production codebases, one for the pricing execution platform and the other for the client deal negotiation platform. Our contribution is a customer case study for regulated industries with production codebases and governance, which includes proof of work. The results demonstrate that strictly governed agentic orchestration increases enterprise capacity without compromising control. In the pricing execution platform workflows, the platform provided complete observability and reduced effort by 38\%. In the client deal negotiation platform, it reduced reporting work by 76\% while maintaining all predefined data privacy, role-based access, and audit guardrails. This paper bridges the gap between AI-assisted software engineering theory and the operational reality of compliance. Closing this gap is important to help enterprises take advantage of AI technologies. These results are descriptive of the benefits of using governed workflows in regulated industries.

</details>

### 116. Not All Mislabels Are Equal: Efficient Task-Aware Auditing with Confidence Sequences

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33262)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`标签审计`、`置信序列`、`任务感知`

👤 **作者**：Fangyuan Lin、Noah Krever、Jakub Černý、Lily Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：固定数据集类别标签的高效自适应审计——下游分数对不同标签错误敏感度不同（如生物多样性信贷支付）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ATLAS 把随时有效推断与任务感知自适应审计策略耦合：用混淆矩阵表示标签修正，按不确定性与对下游分数的影响排序下一个检查点
- 📌 **结论**：线性分数少 24%/11% 审计达 ±2 点精度；非线性分数经线性包络表示少 36%——标签并非同等重要

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We study efficient, adaptive auditing of categorical labels in a fixed dataset, motivated by biodiversity-credit programs in which expert verification of species records is costly and downstream scores (e.g., payment) are more sensitive to errors on certain labels than on others. Our proposed ATLAS framework couples anytime-valid inference with a task-aware adaptive auditing policy. Representing label corrections through a confusion matrix, we prioritize which data points to inspect next based on both label uncertainty and the influence of potential label corrections on the downstream score function. For linear scores such as the mislabel rate, our priority-aware approach certifies the overall label error rate to $\pm 2$ percentage points with 24% and 11% fewer audits than random sampling on biodiversity data from Kenya and a widely used CIFAR-10N benchmark, respectively. For nonlinear scores such as the Hill-Shannon diversity, we exploit a linear-envelope representation that reduces inference to a family of linear-score audits; priority-aware sampling reaches the target precision with 36% fewer audits. When distribution-free guarantees are prohibitively costly, we develop a model-based Bayesian alternative that trades assumption-free validity for substantially fewer audits.
