Our biodiversity case study highlights a gap between statistical validity and practical feasibility: generic methods such as Serfling's bound and existing confidence-sequence procedures are rigorous but can require prohibitively many expert reviews in practice. Together, these results show task-aware auditing can significantly reduce the number of expert audits needed while providing finite-sample, anytime-valid guarantees.

</details>

### 117. Scoped Semantic Invariance as a Foundation for LLM Reliability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33267)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`语义不变性`、`一致性热点`、`知识图接地`

👤 **作者**：Rosario Uceda-Sosa、Maria Chang、Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy、Moninder Singh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 对语义等价查询回答不一致——把一致性形式化为查询等价类上的语义不变性，任意保义变换下不可判定
- 🔬 **研究方法**：范围化语义不变性：知识图作显式语义控制结构；MITRE D3FEND+八个 Wikidata 域测热点结构
- 📌 **结论**：不一致结构化而非随机（TREE/STAR/CYCLE 热点稳定跨模型域）；选择性接地改善而泛滥接地回退，热点可迁移到组合推理任务

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLMs often answer semantically equivalent queries inconsistently, even when the underlying facts are known. We formalize consistency as semantic invariance over equivalence classes of queries and show that invariance under arbitrary meaning-preserving transformations is undecidable in general. This motivates scoped semantic invariance, where model behavior is evaluated relative to bounded semantic domains. We operationalize this framework using knowledge graphs (KGs), which act as explicit semantic control structures defining local semantic constraints and grounding contexts. Across the MITRE D3FEND ontology and eight Wikidata- derived domains, we show that inconsistencies are structured rather than random, concentrating in localized hotspot subgraphs organized around recurring TREE, STAR, and CYCLE patterns that remain stable across models and domains. We further show that selective graph grounding improves correctness and cross-model consistency, while indiscriminate grounding introduces regressions. A layered grounding strategy achieves the strongest monotonicity profile, preserving correct knowledge while reducing inconsistency. Finally, we show that hotspot structures discovered from simple consistency probes transfer to more realistic compositional reasoning tasks.

</details>

### 118. The Emergence and Sparsification of a Syntactic Circuit

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33299)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`电路演化`、`主谓一致`、`稀疏化`

👤 **作者**：Qing Yao、Sasha Boguraev、Tiago Pimentel、Kyle Mahowald

- 🎯 **研究动机**：追踪语法行为如何在训练中涌现与重组
- 🔬 **研究方法**：pythia-1b 与 pythia-410m 十个种子上跨训练追踪主谓数一致电路
- 📌 **结论**：一致行为早期习得且初期由弥散神经元处理；行为稳定后机制继续重组（介导头发生转移）；电路随训练收缩，最终出奇稀疏

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

To understand the emergence of grammatical behavior in language models, we track circuits for subject-verb number agreement in pythia-1b and ten seeds of pythia-410m across training. We find that subject-verb agreement is learned early, and is initially handled by a diffuse collection of neurons. However, after agreement behavior has stabilized, the mechanisms responsible for it continue to reorganize. The attention heads which mediate agreement often shift during training: in the case of pythia-1b, behavior is controlled by two heads early in training but by a third (different) head later. Furthermore, the circuit shrinks over training, with pythia-1b's final circuit being strikingly sparse. We take these results to be informative about how linguistic knowledge emerges and is represented in a language model.

</details>

### 119. From Hype to ROI: An Economic Model for LLM Adoption in Information Systems and Security Operations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33352)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM 采用经济学`、`ROI 模型`、`安全运营中心`

👤 **作者**：Pellegrino Casoria、Andrea Araldo、Rosario Patanè、Simon Pietro Romano

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 采用决策大多机会主义——缺系统经济框架评估特定场景是否创造价值
- 🔬 **研究方法**：参数为以权威基准为中心的随机变量，蒙特卡洛跨悲观-乐观假设模拟；首个实例化于 SOC（工作流映射既定标准识别适配任务）
- 📌 **结论**：给出每配置盈利概率与在险价值+敏感度分析，平衡生产力收益与推理/基础设施/错误修复成本

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) promise significant efficiency gains across information-intensive operations by automating repetitive language-based tasks. However, adoption decisions remain largely opportunistic, as organizations struggle to move from pilots to sustainable industrialization. Without systematic economic frameworks, practitioners cannot assess whether LLM investments create value in a given context. This paper develops a general economic model to support informed LLM adoption decisions in operational information systems. Facing high uncertainty, we represent parameters as random variables centered on authoritative benchmarks, and evaluate the model by Monte Carlo simulation across pessimistic-to-optimistic assumptions on LLM efficiency. The ROI distributions show the probability of profitability and the Value at Risk for each configuration, together with a sensitivity analysis of its main drivers. As a first instantiation, we apply the model to Security Operations Centers (SOCs), mapping workflows onto established standards to identify tasks suited to LLM augmentation. The model balances productivity gains, driven by alert volume, analyst effort, and LLM efficiency, against operational costs, including inference, infrastructure, and error remediation, covering both cloud API and self-hosted deployments. Results show that adoption economics are context-dependent, and that automation is an exploitable lever, gated by accuracy, while the cost of errors and the model's price are secondary. Across the analyzed models and prices, most configurations are unprofitable, with profitability limited to high-accuracy, low-uncertainty deployments. The model gives organizations a quantitative basis for evidence-based adoption decisions, by locating the boundary between profit and loss, and the loss risked beyond it.

</details>

### 120. AV-FABLE: Audio-Visual Manipulation Forensics in Long-Form Videos

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33413)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`长视频取证`、`双时间线`、`稀疏篡改定位`

👤 **作者**：Yuesheng Huang、…、Xiu Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长视频中局部 AI 篡改常影响短暂事件而其余真实——视频级分类不足，音视时间取证基本缺位
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AV-FABLE：16650 对纯净-篡改孪生（998 小时×27 编辑配置），分离音频与视觉时间线支持模态特定时间定位；NFSR 分离读出冻结 AV-LLM 特征
- 📌 **结论**：AV-LLM 零样本近随机而冻结特征的监督读出恢复强取证信号——证据编码于表征但未经语言输出暴露

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Localized AI manipulations in long-form videos often affect brief events within otherwise authentic content, making video-level classification insufficient for forensic analysis. Existing audio-visual deepfake benchmarks largely focus on short talking-head clips, while long-form temporal forensics remains predominantly visual. We introduce AV-FABLE, a dual-timeline benchmark for sparse manipulations in open-scene long-form audio-visual videos. AV-FABLE contains 16,650 pristine--manipulated twin pairs (998 hours) created with 27 editing configurations, with separate audio and visual timelines that enable modality-specific temporal localization and expose errors hidden by joint-timeline evaluation. On AV-FABLE, zero-shot prompting of audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) yields near-chance detection and poor temporal localization. In contrast, supervised readouts of their frozen perception features recover strong forensic signals, suggesting manipulation evidence is encoded in these representations but not effectively exposed through language outputs. Motivated by this observation, we propose NFSR(Native Forensics via Separated Readouts), which reads frozen AV-LLM features with separate audio and visual temporal decoders. NFSR achieves the best audio-visual localization performance in-domain and across all three out-of-distribution settings, including transfer to held-out videos from an external long-video forensic benchmark. AV-FABLE, code, and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>

### 121. ConMem: Counterfactual Multi-Scenario Probing for Resolving LLM Memory Misalignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33432)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`记忆错位`、`反事实多场景探查`、`过度保留`

👤 **作者**：Shuning Zhang、Tenghao Ji、Jingruo Chen、Eryue Xu、Bohao Chu、Xin Yi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期记忆常不当存储/保留/检索过度泛化、错误归因或隐私敏感信息——记忆错位
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MemAlign 基准（1079 例多轮会话错位分类）+ConMem：把保留决策重构为前瞻反事实因果推断，合成反事实交互场景审计下游干预效应（任务执行/无关不变性/风险敏感边界）
- 📌 **结论**：75.16% 准确率，缓解过度保留偏差而不牺牲个性化效用

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term memory is critical for autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agents executing multi-session, long-horizon tasks. However, existing memories often inappropriately store, retain, or retrieve over-generalized, misattributed, or privacy-sensitive information, a problem we term memory misalignment. To study this problem, we construct \textbf{MemAlign}, a benchmark comprising 1,079 curated memory cases grounded in synthetic, validated multi-session conversational contexts covering diverse misalignment taxonomies. We then propose ConMem, an algorithm that reframes retention decisions as prospective counterfactual causal inference. \textbf{ConMem} synthesizes counterfactual interaction scenarios to prospectively audit downstream intervention effects across three aspects: relevant task execution, irrelevant invariance checks, and risk-sensitive boundary shifts. Experiments show that ConMem outperforms other methods, achieving 75.16\% accuracy, 64.15\% balanced accuracy, and a Macro-F1 of 65.31\%, effectively mitigating over-retention biases without sacrificing personalization utility.

</details>

### 122. Combinatorial Lock for Trojan-Resilient AI Engines via Hash-Assisted Spectral Filtering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33437)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多触发木马`、`组合锁`、`硬件可实现防御`

👤 **作者**：Soumita Chatterjee、Sudip Ghosh、Tamal Ghosh、Bhargab B. Bhattacharya、Hafizur Rahaman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：单中毒模型可共存异质触发机制（补丁/混合/扭曲/潜域 Spy-Watermark）——假设单一静态攻击的捷径防御失效，多触发补救靠遗忘训练贵且低投毒率失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：组合锁：免训练参照校准，把多触发识别化为二值激活签名上的组合问题，整数与逻辑运算可直接硬件实现；异常门控谱清洗+哈希压缩组合签名+含复合攻击感知专家的检测集成
- 📌 **结论**：免训练、触发无关、硬件可实现的木马韧性 AI 引擎防线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-trigger backdoor attacks, in which heterogeneous trigger mechanisms (e.g., patch, blend, warping, and the latent-domain Spy-Watermark) coexist within a single poisoned model, cannot be addressed by existing shortcut-based defenses that assume a single, static attack mechanism. Current multi-trigger remedies (BU+FT, BU+ANP, and BU+ABL) rely on backdoor unlearning, which incurs high training costs and loses effectiveness at low poisoning ratios. This paper proposes Combinatorial Lock, a training-free, reference-calibrated defense that reduces multi-trigger identification to a combinatorial problem over binary activation signatures. This formulation is the key novelty: it yields a multi-trigger defense that is training-free, trigger-oblivious, and directly implementable in hardware, since its decision rules can be evaluated with integer and logic operations. The framework combines anomaly-gated spectral scrubbing, which removes the malicious subspace only from inputs identified as anomalous, with a hash-compressed combinatorial signature verified by an ensemble of detection experts, including a compound-attack-aware expert for multi-trigger inputs. A joint threshold-shrinkage search identifies an operating point ($\tau$ at the 97.1st percentile, $\gamma = 0.78$) that reduces the attack success rate (ASR) to as low as 0.1% while preserving 97.8% clean accuracy. Evaluation on six models (ResNet18, VGG16, MobileNet, AlexNet, ViT-Small, ViT-Base) shows strong generalization: near-100% ASR drops to single digits across poisoning rates of 1-10% and 1-9 simultaneous triggers, with only gradual degradation in clean accuracy. ASIC and FPGA implementations confirm practicality, adding a consistent 19-20% latency overhead across all evaluated architectures and establishing Combinatorial Lock as a hardware-native approach to multi-trigger backdoor mitigation.

</details>

### 123. Adaptive Transfer of Representational Signatures for LLM Reliability Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33442)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`表征签名迁移`、`可靠性检测`、`少标注`

👤 **作者**：Yongtong Li、Yixuan Florence Wu、Jiawei Zhang、Naichen Shi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基于内部表征的不可靠输出检测器训练需大量域内标注——限制新域适用性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：跨相关域迁移表征签名：统计框架先提取各域低维签名，交叉拟合学域特异预测器的组合权重（自适应源域相关度），最小二乘类似物的渐近分析刻画极限风险
- 📌 **结论**：新域仅 25 标注问题即使幻觉检测 AUROC +6.8%、推理步预测 +8.4%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are reported to be susceptible to reliability issues including hallucinations and reasoning errors. Existing quality detectors can predict unreliable outputs based on internal representations with decent accuracies. However, training these detectors often requires substantial domain-specific labeled data, which limits their applicability to new domains. We propose to address this challenge by transferring representational signatures across related domains. More specifically, we build a statistical framework that first extracts low-dimensional signatures for each domain, then uses cross-fitting to learn composition weights of domain-specific predictors. The weights adapt to each source domain's predictive relevance to the target domain. Our asymptotic analysis of a least-squares analogue characterizes the limiting prediction risk and aggregation weights in terms of domain discrepancies and representation bias. Experiments across multiple hallucination datasets and open-weight LLMs show that, with only 25 labeled questions in the new domain, adaptive transfer improves hallucination-detection AUROC by 6.8%. For reasoning-step prediction, it improves AUROC by 8.4%.

</details>

### 124. Secure and Functional Code Generation with Self-Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33450)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`安全代码生成`、`CWE 检索`、`自评估修复`

👤 **作者**：Md Afif Al Mamun、Gias Uddin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 可生成功能正确但不安全的代码——安全微调贵且不总可得，提示/检索可减漏洞但可移除必需行为
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SecGent 推理时框架：从 CWE 数据库检索弱点上下文提供任务特定安全指导（不依赖易损/补丁代码例），功能+安全测试执行反馈引导双向修复
- 📌 **结论**：四个开源 LLM+Claude Sonnet 5.5×五语言 CWEval 上全部五模型最高联合功能-安全成功

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate functionally correct code that remains insecure. Security-focused fine-tuning can be expensive and is not always available, motivating inference-time approaches. Security-oriented prompting and retrieval can reduce vulnerabilities, but may also remove required behavior or apply defenses incompatible with the task. The challenge is to identify relevant security knowledge and use it to improve security without sacrificing functionality. We present SecGent, an inference-time framework that retrieves weakness context from the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) database and applies it to secure code generation. By connecting task specifications to relevant textual CWE records, SecGent provides task-specific security guidance without relying on retrieved vulnerable or patched code examples. Complementary functional and security tests check the resulting implementation, and their execution feedback guides repairs toward satisfying both objectives. We evaluate SecGent with four open-source LLMs and Claude Sonnet 5.5 across five programming languages on the CWEval benchmark. It achieves the highest joint functional--security success against four inference-time baselines for all five models. Across the open-weight models, mean functional-security@1 (FS@1) improves by 15.5% over RAG, with gains in both functionality and security, and by 19.8% over the strongest prompt optimizer compiled for each model. On Sonnet, FS@1 reaches 92.1%, outperforming the strongest baseline AutoGen by 4.8%. In a separate comparison, SecGent outperforms Claude Code with security-focused prompting by over 6% in FS@1. Feedback-guided repair further improves FS@1 by up to 6.7% over generation without feedback, primarily by recovering functionality while checking security.

</details>

### 125. Small Edits, Shifting Decisions: Benchmarking LLM Accuracy and Reliability on French Input Variants

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33493)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`法语表面变体`、`鲁棒性基准`、`校准迁移`

👤 **作者**：Jiaming Yu、Xiaotian Xie、Jian Ma、Yingjie Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：法语用户日常输入省略撇号/变音符或打字错误——对模型性能与预测可靠性的影响不足理解
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控法语表面变体基准：七任务型×4200 原始-低-高三元组（12600 实例），六模型两家族
- 📌 **结论**：精度随扰动强度降、Brier/NLL 升；更大模型高强精度损失更小但概率质量退化不随规模一致减少；键盘错字损失最大——原输入拟合的校准温度可有效迁移

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

French users may omit apostrophes and diacritics or make keyboard typos in everyday input. The effects of these common variations on language model performance and prediction reliability remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we construct a controlled French surface-variant benchmark covering seven task types, with 4,200 original-low-high triplets (12,600 input instances). Each high-intensity variant retains all low-intensity edits and adds further changes. We evaluate six models from two LLM families. The results show that accuracy decreases and Brier score and negative log-likelihood (NLL) increase with perturbation intensity. Larger models show smaller accuracy losses under high intensity, but increases in Brier score and NLL do not consistently decrease with scale. Keyboard typos cause the largest losses in accuracy and the quality of predicted probabilities at the predefined high-intensity settings. Finally, calibration temperatures fitted on original inputs transfer effectively to surface variants. Combining calibration with input restoration further improves Brier score and NLL over either method alone while preserving restored accuracy. Our benchmark provides a unified framework for jointly evaluating task accuracy and prediction reliability under common French surface variations.

</details>

### 126. Five Architectures, One Shared Space Cross-Model Representational Alignment Without Weight Merging

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33495)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`跨模型表征对齐`、`共享空间`、`免权重合并`

👤 **作者**：Oladimeji Anthonio、Dimeji Olawuyi、Oloruntoba Ajayi、Temilola Aderemi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：合并模型通常靠权重平均（需共同起点）——五个独立训练冻结模型能否仅凭激活关联
- 🔬 **研究方法**：置换检验+独立复制探针+深度扫描：四组织四个 transformer+一个状态空间模型经广义普鲁克分析建无固定参考的共享空间
- 📌 **结论**：末层二十对有序模型 held-out 检索 0.91-0.93（随机 0.03）超成对线性映射；迁移到陌生语法英语但数学逻辑记号失败——跨架构共性表征的边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Combining language models is usually done by averaging weights, which works only when the models share a common starting point. We ask whether five frozen, independently trained models with different internal designs (four transformers from four organizations and one state-space model) can be related through their activations alone, with no weight merging. Using permutation tests, an independent replication probe set, depth sweeps, pooling ablations and confound checks, we find statistically robust correspondence between how the five models organize the same text. It is strongest at each model's final layer and weaker for the state-space model, whose weakest pairing is still 35-43 times chance. We use this to build a shared space with Generalized Procrustes Analysis, in which no model is a fixed reference. At the final layer, mean held-out retrieval accuracy over the twenty ordered model pairs is 0.91 to 0.93 on two probe sets (chance 0.03), beating pairwise linear maps (0.82 to 0.85), but the space is worse than those maps in the middle layers. It transfers to ordinary English of unfamiliar grammatical form and fails on mathematical and logical notation. We then test whether the space supports a router that picks among the five models on gradeable tasks. Across four task types we find no case where routing beats always using the strongest model, and we report this as a negative result. A correctness signal that looked strong turned out to be category membership in disguise. All experiments ran on one laptop without a GPU.

</details>

### 127. Implicit Beliefs as Scalable Process Rewards for Strategic Persuasion

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33527)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`策略说服`、`在线信念引出`、`过程奖励`

👤 **作者**：Minqian Liu、…、Lifu Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多轮策略说服训练面临两障碍：心理接地的被说服者模拟与策略对话的稠密忠实监督
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PersuArena 环境+Probe-RPO：从冻结被说服者 logits 读三个内省探针把内部状态转为稠密过程奖励
- 📌 **结论**：医疗/金融/职场/教育/亲密五域完全接受率 52.5% vs 35.8%——说服动力学的忠实信号（其反面即操纵面）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Training large language models (LLMs) for multi-turn strategic persuasion faces two coupled obstacles: (i) realistic simulation of psychologically grounded persuadees, and (ii) dense, faithful supervision for credit assignment in strategic dialogues. Outcome-only rewards are sparse and uninformative about which conversational moves drove success, while LLM-judge process rewards are expensive and decoupled from the underlying interaction dynamics. We address both challenges with a unified framework. First, we introduce PersuArena, a persuasion simulation environment that produces realistic, non-monotonic trajectories. Second, building directly on this environment, we propose Process Rewards via Online Belief Elicitation for Relative Policy Optimization (Probe-RPO), an online RL framework that converts the persuadee's internal state into dense process rewards. At each turn, three introspective probes are read from the frozen persuadee's logits, yielding a multi-dimensional reward signal that is faithfully aligned with persuasion dynamics. With extensive experiments on PersuArena spanning healthcare, financial advising, workplace management, education, and personal relationships, we demonstrate that our Probe-RPO achieves the full acceptance rate of 52.5%, compared with the 35.8% for the strongest GRPO ablation and 25.8% for the best off-the-shelf LLM. We also conduct comprehensive analyses on the generalization across persuadee models and the persuasion dynamics across conversations. Our work offers a promising paradigm that can provide effective process supervision for strategic dialogue.

</details>

### 128. SkillShift-Bench: Benchmarking Skill Learning under Environment Evolution for Web Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33528)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`技能漂移`、`开放环境变化`、`非平稳执行性`

👤 **作者**：Bo-Yu Chen、Zhi Zhou、Yu-Feng Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：学到的技能在封闭环境评测——真实 Web 部署的调用上下文/感知接地/执行可行性持续偏移
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SkillShift-Bench：三个自托管 Web 系统模拟上下文/感知/执行三类受控偏移，附动态遗憾分析与测试时适配方法
- 📌 **结论**：受控偏移下系统性鲁棒性退化（执行偏移压力最强）；不重学技能库即可改善漂移执行

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Skill learning enables web agents to reuse prior experience for long-horizon interactive tasks. However, learned skills are typically evaluated in closed environments, overlooking the fact that real-world web deployment involves continuous shifts in invocation context, perceptual grounding, and execution feasibility. To this end, we introduce SkillShift-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating skill learning with controlled open-environment shifts. SkillShift-Bench simulates Contextual Shift, Perceptual Shift, and Execution Shift across three self-hosted web systems, and evaluates agents with diagnostics covering task success, interaction cost, skill use, and skill failure. Comprehensive experiments reveal systematic robustness degradation under controlled shifts, characterized by lower success rates, higher interaction costs, and more frequent skill failures, with Execution Shift exerting the strongest pressure. We further formulate shifted skill execution as a nonstationary local executability problem and provide both a dynamic-regret analysis and a practical test-time adaptation method, demonstrating the feasibility of improving learned-skill execution without relearning the skill library. Code, tasks, environments, documentation, and the leaderboard are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 129. FraudSociety: A Generative Agent-Based Society for the Financial-Fraud Economy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33536)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`欺诈经济社会模拟`、`生成式agent社会`、`损失集中`

👤 **作者**：Keyang Chen、…、Hongfeng Chai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：美国 2025 年金融欺诈损失 159 亿美元——理解需联合建模信息与金融动力学，现有模拟器只覆盖一侧
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FraudSociety 首个联合建模二者的 LLM-agent 模拟器：动态社交环境+持久金融账本+Need-Intent-Action 平民决策，欺诈者按交互史与账本自适应
- 📌 **结论**：宏观结果贴近真实统计；微观复现损失集中（前 10% 受害记录占 81.1% 损失）——干预效应可验证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Financial fraud caused over \$15.9 billion in losses in the United States in 2025, according to the Federal Trade Commission~\citep{ftc2026databook}. Understanding such large-scale harm requires jointly modeling informational and financial dynamics. Yet existing simulators typically capture only one side of this interaction, leaving a gap in modeling how fraud unfolds in the real world. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textsc{FraudSociety}, the first LLM-agent-based simulator that jointly models these coupled dynamics. It integrates a dynamic social environment for information exposure and communication, persistent financial ledgers that preserve payment and loss histories, and a Need-Intent-Action architecture for civilian decision making. Fraudsters further adapt their communication and financial actions to individual interaction histories and ledger states, forming a closed feedback loop between the two dynamics. We validate \textsc{FraudSociety} across macro-level outcomes, micro-level behavior, and intervention effects. At the macro level, the simulated fraud outcomes closely match real-world patterns across multiple key statistics. At the micro level, it reproduces strong loss concentration, with the top 10\% of victimization records accounting for 81.1\% of total losses, consistent with Canadian Anti-Fraud Centre statistics~\citep{cafc2025annual}. Counter-fraud experiments further reveal variation across intervention strategies, with the best-performing personalized intervention reducing the repeat-payment rate from 49.4\% to 10.9\%. These results establish \textsc{FraudSociety} as both a high-fidelity simulator and a scalable testbed for evaluating anti-fraud strategies under diverse intervention conditions, thereby advancing research on financial fraud and its prevention.

</details>

### 130. What Remains OOD? OODConceptBench for Open-World Concept Recognition in Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33564)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`OOD 概念识别`、`开放世界基准`、`规模效应`

👤 **作者**：Zhiran Luo、Zhiyong Yang、Zitai Wang、Sicong Li、Qianqian Xu、Qingming Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：零样本泛化日益模糊已知/未知概念边界——现有评测不直接揭示各 VLM 能识别哪些开放世界概念
- 🔬 **研究方法**：OODConceptBench：30752/14285 图像两级层次×560 概念短语×七开放世界类别，37 个 VLM 跨发布年/家族/变体
- 📌 **结论**：更新模型不一定更强开放世界识别；整体识别提升不意味着各类别一致改善——OOD 仍是挑战

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In the era of large-scale pretrained models, zero-shot generalization has increasingly blurred the boundary between known and unknown concepts. However, current evaluations do not directly reveal which open-world concepts different vision-language models can recognize. To study this problem, we introduce OODConceptBench, a hierarchical benchmark for open-world concept recognition on image-concept pairs. The full benchmark set (L1) contains 30,752 images, and the harder subset (L2) contains 14,285 images. The benchmark covers 560 concept phrases across seven open-world categories. We evaluate 37 vision-language models across release year, model family, and variant. We obtain three main findings. First, target-concept recognition is lower on L2 than on L1 across all 37 evaluated models. Second, later-released models do not necessarily achieve stronger open-world concept recognition. Third, an increase in overall recognition does not imply consistent improvement across all benchmark categories. Together, these results show that OOD remains a challenge even in the era of large-scale vision-language pretraining.

</details>

### 131. WorldFailBench: Do World Models Understand Policy Failures?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33565)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`世界模型失效保真`、`失效原因/阶段/边界`、`策略评测`

👤 **作者**：Zheng Zhang、…、Dacheng Tao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：世界模型做策略评测只看成败结果——是否忠实模拟策略如何失败未被确立，误判会使策略看似可部署
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WorldFailBench 三任务：失效原因（复现可观测错误）/失效阶段（同阶段集中）/失效边界（受控场景变化下成败转换条件）
- 📌 **结论**：操纵任务的失效保真系统评测——世界模型作为策略评估器的边界

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Robot policy evaluation informs deployment decisions and policy improvement, but physical testing and simulator preparation require substantial effort. World models offer a complementary route through closed-loop interaction in generated environments. Recent benchmarks have demonstrated their potential as policy evaluators through outcome-based evaluation (i.e., assessing task success or failure). However, such evaluation does not establish whether world models faithfully simulate how policies fail. Misrepresenting failures can lead to incorrect diagnoses of policy weaknesses and make policies appear ready for deployment even when small scene changes cause them to fail. We therefore ask: do world models faithfully simulate how and at which stages policies fail, and under what conditions they transition from success to failure? To answer this question, we introduce WorldFailBench with three evaluation tasks. Failure reason tests whether models reproduce the observable errors policies make. Failure stage assesses whether failures concentrate at the same stages of full-task execution. Failure boundary examines whether models reproduce the conditions under which policies transition from success to failure under controlled scene changes in simulation. The benchmark covers manipulation tasks in the LIBERO and RoboTwin simulation environments and real-world manipulation tasks performed by the G1 humanoid robot. Experiments across seven world models reveal substantial gaps in their understanding of policy failures, providing guidance for developing more reliable world models for policy evaluation.

</details>

### 132. Counterfactual Model Testing Under Distribution Shift

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33594)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`分布偏移下测试`、`反事实模型测试`、`数据完整性`

👤 **作者**：Joran Leest、Ilias Gerostathopoulos、Claudia Raibulet、Patricia Lago

- 🎯 **研究动机**：部署模型随输入分布偏移退化——群体变化与管线数据完整性问题经不同机制产生，检测到偏移不说明对模型的影响
- 🔬 **研究方法**：在线模型测试公式化：需在已知标签的受控基线上复现偏移，故须知道哪个机制变了；方法复现两种机制变化估计当前性能与修复收益
- 📌 **结论**：真实数据集模拟故障实验显示分离两种来源普遍改进两个估计——监控与修复排序的支持

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deployed machine learning (ML) models can degrade as the distribution of their input data shifts. Such shifts can result from changes in the population or from data integrity issues in the pipeline that materializes model features, two sources that operate through different mechanisms. Because labels are often absent or delayed, it is common practice to track shifts in the data instead, but a detected shift does not indicate how it affects the model. We formulate this problem as online model testing under distribution shift. Testing requires reproducing the shift on a controlled baseline with known labels. Since the same shift can come from either source, doing so requires knowing which mechanism changed. We propose a methodology that reproduces observed changes in both mechanisms to estimate current performance and the potential gain from repairing observed data integrity issues. Experiments on real-world datasets with simulated faults show that separating the two sources generally improves both estimates, supporting this formulation. A churn prediction case illustrates how these tests can support monitoring and repair prioritization

</details>

### 133. Data Governance for Generative and Agentic AI : Technology, Law, Economics, and Security

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33605)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`数据治理`、`生成式/agent式AI`、`跨学科框架`

👤 **作者**：Narayana Darapaneni、Padmaja K、Gnana Chaithra D

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对检索-生成-推断-记忆-行动系统（RAG 到自主 MAS）的统一治理框架缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十一章八附录整合技术/法律/经济三透镜覆盖全生命周期：数据质量与谱系、全球数据保护法与 IP、数字信任与零信任、生成管线治理、agent 治理、选举与公共领域、六法域监管与 2030 治理控制平面设计
- 📌 **结论**：锚定印度法并保持跨法域比较视角的治理专著

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

This book develops a unified governance framework for systems that retrieve, generate, infer, remember and act on information from large language models and retrieval augmented pipelines to autonomous multi agent systems. Eleven chapters and eight appendices integrate three disciplinary lenses-technology, law and economics-across the full lifecycle: data quality, lineage and provenance; global data protection law and intellectual property; the economics of data governance; digital trust, cybersecurity and zero-trust architecture; ethics and responsible AI; governing generative AI pipelines; governing agentic AI and multi-agent systems; data governance in elections and the public sphere; AI regulation across six jurisdictions; and the design of a governance control plane for 2030. Each chapter is anchored in Indian law and institutions while maintaining a comparative, cross jurisdictional perspective.

</details>

### 134. Strategic Calibration in LLM Multi-Agent Systems: Audited Pivot Scoring for Truthful Elicitation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33728)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`策略校准`、`审计关键评分`、`真实引出`

👤 **作者**：Jinhao Zhou、Zhaohan Zhang、Xijian Xu、Jun Wu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 中报告置信决定下游注意力/算力/决策影响分配——报告变成策略性的：即使信念校准良好也可能夸大
- 🔬 **研究方法**：APSM 机制：抵消平台分配收益+逆倾向加权稀疏审计+Clarke 关键外部性收费+严格适当评分；声明假设下真实报告是唯一期望效用最优响应
- 📌 **结论**：实验显示 LLM agent 在 Top-K 激励下膨胀置信，CPS/APSM 都降低扭曲——报告层激励问题

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems, reported confidence increasingly determines the allocation of scarce downstream attention, computation, and decision influence. This makes reporting strategic: an agent may overstate the relevance of its contribution even when its underlying belief is well calibrated. Existing calibration methods largely assume passive reporting and therefore do not address strategic incentives created when confidence determines downstream allocation. We formulate strategic calibration as a reporting-layer incentive problem and propose the Audited Pivot Scoring Mechanism (APSM). APSM offsets platform-controlled allocation benefits, uses inverse-propensity-weighted sparse audits, charges the Clarke pivot externality, and applies a strictly proper score. Under the stated model assumptions, truthful reporting is the unique expected-utility best response. We also characterize approximate execution and the audit-variance trade-off. Calculator-free experiments show that LLM agents inflate confidence under Top-$K$ incentives. With a shared agent-local utility interface, LLM agents reduce distortion under both Compensated Proper Scoring (CPS) and APSM. CPS achieves higher exact allocation match, whereas APSM yields lower Brier loss and numerically fewer cutoff crossings. Experiments further show that APSM enlarges the utility margin against allocation-changing deviations by $1.93\times$ and improves robustness under approximate execution. These results support incentive-aware elicitation as a distinct layer for reliable multi-agent coordination.

</details>

### 135. Trust Mechanisms for Offline Generative AI at the Edge: A Connectivity-Regime Framework and an Empirical Study on a Raspberry Pi 5

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33732)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`离线边缘AI信任`、`连接状态框架`、`知识包`

👤 **作者**：Georgios Feretzakis、Alexandros Papaspyridis

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式 AI 进入 IoT 而部署多假设云连接——间歇/受限/无连接状态的信任机制缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：C0-C4 连接状态框架+推理时信任机制分类+签名版本化知识包作断连更新通道；树莓派 5 全本地 RAG 网络隔离评测（三 Qwen2.5 尺寸×三上下文×300 SQuAD2 题）
- 📌 **结论**：检索分门控检测知识缺口 AUROC 0.97 但题内不可答仅 0.68；确定性输出验证器把畸形输出转显式弃答零损失——离线边缘的实用信任栈

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative AI and large language models (LLMs) are moving into the Internet of Things, yet most deployments assume cloud connectivity, and many IoT settings have intermittent, restricted, or no connectivity. We propose a connectivity-regime framework (C0-C4), a taxonomy of inference-time trust mechanisms, and signed, versioned knowledge packs as the update channel for disconnected regimes, and evaluate them on a Raspberry Pi 5 running fully local retrieval-augmented generation under network isolation, with three Qwen2.5 model sizes, three context sizes, and 300 SQuAD 2.0 questions. An empirically calibrated retrieval-score gate detects knowledge gaps almost perfectly (AUROC 0.97) but in-topic unanswerable questions poorly (0.68). A deterministic output validator converts malformed outputs into explicit abstentions with no observed loss of correct answers, while the NLI-based verifier we tested adds little; for the 7B model, 96% of substantive answers to unanswerable-labelled questions match normalized spans of the retrieved passages. A 3B model achieves the same gold-answer containment rate as a 7B model at less than half the latency and estimated energy, although the 7B model far more often exceeds a token-F1 threshold of 0.5; five retrieved passages lower correctness from 75% to 52% compared with three. Verifying a signed pack takes about 4.5 ms versus about 29 min for embedding its passages on the device; signature and hash checks alone accept six of nine tested update attacks that layered verification rejects. Yet a validly signed poisoned pack passes every pack check: on 30 previously correct questions, correct answers drop to zero and 14 return the injected answer. None of the mechanisms we evaluated establishes the truth of the evidence. We release code and data.

</details>

### 136. Enhancing LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection via Representation-Aware Logical Ensemble

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33756)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`漏洞检测`、`多表示集成`、`逻辑集成`

👤 **作者**：Francesco Crescenzo Grasso、Pietro Liguori、Roberto Natella、Domenico Cotroneo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 漏洞检测多依赖单一代码表示——隐含假设各类漏洞 uniformly 适配
- 🔬 **研究方法**：系统研究原始源码/AST/CFG 多表示下的 LLM 检测：不同表示互补，特定视图对特定漏洞类更有效；RALE 显式逻辑规则+多数投票集成
- 📌 **结论**：单表示最高 73% F1，集成达 78% 且准确/精确/召回全面胜出

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automated vulnerability detection in C source code is a long-standing challenge, as traditional static analysis tools often trade precision for scalability and struggle with complex vulnerability patterns. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown promising results; yet, most approaches rely on a single code representation, implicitly assuming uniform suitability across vulnerability types. In this work, we systematically study LLM-based vulnerability detection under multiple code representations, including raw source code, Abstract Syntax Trees (AST), and Control Flow Graphs (CFG). Through extensive empirical analysis, we show that different representations exhibit complementary strengths, with specific views being more effective for particular classes of vulnerabilities. Building on these findings, we propose a Representation-Aware Logical Ensemble (RALE) that integrates LLMs trained on different code representations through explicit logical rules and majority voting. While single-representation models achieve up to 73% F1-score, our integrated approach reaches 78% F1-score, with commensurate gains in accuracy, precision, and recall, consistently outperforming individual models. These results highlight the importance of explicitly leveraging complementary code representations when combining LLMs for effective vulnerability detection.

</details>

### 137. Local Entropy Fluctuations for Black-Box LLM-Generated Text Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33767)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`黑箱AI文本检测`、`熵涨落`、`峰值显著率`

👤 **作者**：Xinpeng Wang、Bruce Rushing、Zhizhen Chen、Zhe Zeng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：双模型与重采样检测器在指令条件文本的黑箱设定（源模型未知）灾难性失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：追溯失效到重采样方法提示条件差与双模型跨模型差异缩减；据人类写作平稳信息流 vs 机器文本尖锐局部熵涨落提出 RelPPR 度量熵轨迹结构对比
- 📌 **结论**：多样模型家族数据集上 SOTA 黑箱检测且多种实际情形下最可靠

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Zero-shot detection of machine-generated text is vital for mitigating the risks of large language models (LLMs) misuse. While the state-of-the-art dual-model detectors and resampling-based detectors outperform single-model statistical detectors on unprompted continuations, they suffer a catastrophic breakdown on instruction-conditioned text in the black-box setting, where the source model is unknown. We analyze this failure and trace it to poor prompt conditioning for resampling-based methods and diminished cross-model discrepancy for dual-model detectors. Motivated by recent studies that human writing maintains a steady, uniform information flow, whereas machine-generated text exhibits sharp, localized entropy fluctuations, we propose a new metric, the Relative Peak Prominence Rate (RelPPR) to capture this structural contrast along the entropy trajectory and further leverage it as a signal for LLM-generated text detection. Extensive evaluations across diverse model families and datasets demonstrate that RelPPR achieves state-of-the-art black-box detection performance and remains the most reliable detector under various practical situations. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 138. The Hitchhiker's Guide to System 1 Models: Fast Decision-Making Beyond LLMS

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33775)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`System 1 模型`、`决策基础设施`、`指南`

👤 **作者**：Haggai Roitman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 自回归逐词元推理对路由/门控/评分/分类等亚秒级常规决策不必要地慢、贵且校准差
- 🔬 **研究方法**：实践者导向综述：五家族分类（编码器分类器/奖励模型/非自回归决策引擎/对比决策/提取模型）+适当评分规则理论与 RLCD+IIA 违反/序数偏差/少样本重校准现象+agent 工作流集成
- 📌 **结论**：System 1 决策模型作为 agent 基础设施的全面参考

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models excel at reasoning and generation but their autoregressive, token-by-token inference makes them unnecessarily slow, expensive, and poorly calibrated for the many routine decisions (routing, gating, scoring, classification) that modern AI systems require at sub-second latency. A growing class of models addresses this gap: System 1 decision models, architectures that map an input directly to a typed, structured decision in a single forward pass. This guide provides a comprehensive, practitioner-oriented treatment of the field. We propose a five-family taxonomy covering encoder-based classifiers, reward models, non-autoregressive decision engines, contrastive decision models, and extraction models, and we provide in-depth coverage of each family's architecture, training methodology, and deployment trade-offs. A central theme is calibration: we develop the theory of proper scoring rules, analyze Reinforcement Learning for Calibrated Decisions (RLCD), and examine recently discovered phenomena including IIA violations, ordinal-scale bias, and few-shot recalibration under distribution shift. On the integration side, we describe how System 1 models serve as decision infrastructure within agentic workflows and System 1 + System 2 cascades that route between fast and slow models based on calibrated confidence. The guide covers 18 application domains, maps the rapidly growing open-source ecosystem (over 70 model reproductions in weeks), provides runnable code throughout, and includes a practitioner's checklist, an open-source model zoo, and 22 self-assessment questions with detailed answers. Spanning 190 references, it is intended as a unified entry point for researchers and engineers working at the intersection of fast decision-making, calibration, and production AI systems.

</details>

### 139. IntentDelta: A Human-Constructed Benchmark for Evolving User Intent

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33806)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`意图演化基准`、`多轮约束变更`、`人构造`

👤 **作者**：Jieyu Zhou、Zijian Wang、Yun Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 须适应用户在复杂任务中修订需求与优先级——人构造的演化意图基准缺失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：IntentDelta：638 轨迹 3766 轮（WebShop/TravelPlanner），每轮配用户话语+累积意图状态+人参照动作，交叉验证覆盖可行性与偏好驱动变更
- 📌 **结论**：十一 agent 意图恢复均值 33.1%/44.7%，动作分 31.6%/41.0%——难保留早期需求、难分软偏好与硬约束；正确恢复不保证执行成功

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents must adapt as users revise their requirements and priorities during complex tasks. We introduce IntentDelta, a human-constructed multi-turn benchmark with 638 trajectories and 3,766 turns across WebShop and TravelPlanner. Each turn pairs a user utterance with a cumulative intention state and human reference actions. The trajectories are cross-verified and cover feasibility- and preference-driven changes in constraints and priorities. We evaluate eleven agents on intention recovery and action quality. Average Intention Recovery is 33.1\% in TravelPlanner and 44.7\% in WebShop, while average Action Score is 31.6% and 41.0%, respectively. Agents struggle to preserve earlier requirements and distinguish soft preferences from must-have constraints. Correct intention recovery also does not guarantee successful execution, highlighting challenges in translating evolving user intentions into actions.

</details>

### 140. Task Format as a Factor in Adaptation: A Controlled Comparison of LoRA and Full Fine-Tuning for Prompt-Safety Classification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33813)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LoRA vs 全参`、`任务格式`、`安全分类鲁棒性`

👤 **作者**：John Alderete、Srirakshith Betageri、Jennifer Flynn、Alex Greenhalgh、Josh Rubin、Yoshita Varma Annam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LoRA 与全参微调比较常混淆变量——任务格式很少被研究
- 🔬 **研究方法**：匹配 400M 编码器-解码器对×三种任务格式×每格至多 15 种子的预注册分析（提示安全分类）
- 📌 **结论**：同搜索学习率下编码器差距闭合；给 LoRA 加写理由 +4.0 点但等长空句不行；域内超 8B 提示模型但对抗越狱落后 8-23 点、把 27-66% 良性但危险样貌提示误标（8B 仅 2-3%）——适配体制不如训练目标重要

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Fine-tuning a language model for classification involves many design decisions. Comparisons of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with full fine-tuning are common but often confounded, and task format is rarely studied. We compare both regimes on prompt-safety classification with a matched 400M encoder-decoder pair (Ettin) across three task formats, with up to 15 seeds per cell and pre-specified analyses. At the pre-specified learning rates, full fine-tuning leads LoRA by 2-3 pp in every architecture and format trained on the label alone. On the encoder, the gap closes once both rates are chosen by the same search. Training LoRA to write a reason after its verdict adds 4.0 pp and closes the gap, but a content-free sentence of equal length does not. Trained models score above a prompted 8B in domain but fall behind it on two of three out-of-distribution corpora, trailing by 8-23 pp on adversarial jailbreaks and flagging 27-66% of benign but harmful-looking prompts as unsafe, against the 8B's 2-3%. Together, the results suggest that the adaptation regime matters less than how each model is trained, its learning rate, and training target, and that in-domain accuracy alone is an incomplete guide to robustness.

</details>

### 141. Memory or a Clock? Auditing a Memory Advantage in XLand-MiniGrid with Seeds, Maps and Evaluation Noise Apart

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33816)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`评测噪声审计`、`记忆优势解构`、`时钟信息`

👤 **作者**：Rodrigo Reveron-Fonseca

- 🎯 **研究动机**：部分可观测任务上 LSTM 超 MLP 25 点看似“记忆优势”——多少是噪声、多少真是记忆
- 🔬 **研究方法**：60 次运行×3 次重评分离训练种子/地图/评测随机性；密封新地图面板上给 MLP 时间步
- 📌 **结论**：“任务习得”二元判定 76% 是评测噪声；给时间步后无记忆 MLP 与 LSTM 差 3.3 点（线性探针可从 LSTM 状态读出时间步）——表观记忆优势大部分是知道时间的优势

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

On a partially observable XLand-MiniGrid task, an LSTM agent beats a memory-free MLP by 25 percentage points. The gap looks like a clear case of memory. We ask two questions about it. First, how much of a measured gap is noise? Separating training seed, map and evaluation randomness in 60 runs, each re-evaluated three times, we find that about ten paired seeds detect a 10-point gap in final success. The binary "task acquired" verdict, a common summary of learning curves, is 76% evaluation noise and would need about a hundred seeds for the same gap. Second, is the gap memory? On a sealed panel of new maps, opened once, a memory-free MLP of the LSTM's size comes within 3.3 points of the LSTM when it is given the time step, and gains no measurable benefit from its width without it. With the time step, an MLP a quarter of the LSTM's size, and the LSTM with its state erased at every step, also come within about 5 points. A linear probe reads the time step from the trained LSTM's state. On this task, most of the apparent memory advantage is an advantage of knowing the time, which matters because episodes are cut at a fixed limit and the reward shrinks with time. We recommend giving memory-free baselines the time step, and we release the protocol, the event-driven tooling and every checkpoint.

</details>

### 142. EdgeDeID: A 0.6B Clinical De-identifier Trained Without Real Patient Data

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33839)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`临床去标识`、`0.6B 边缘模型`、`合成数据训练`

👤 **作者**：Sina Daneshgar、Raya Namazi、Amirali Khosravi、Pouria Rouzrokh、Bardia Khosravi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：用托管 LLM 去标识临床笔记恰好泄露该步骤要保护的信息
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EdgeDeID 0.6B 本地运行零真实患者文本：三教师写合成注记+单标注器统一规范+类型化 XML 标识符输出；验证器适配器分离完整/不完整输出
- 📌 **结论**：域内 0.994 micro-F1（最强现成 0.857）且 99% 注记零漏标识；量化后 CPU 615MB；MIMIC 护理注记掉到 0.48（电报体+低标识密度），三条手写示例恢复 0.08

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

De-identifying clinical notes with a hosted language model discloses the information the step is meant to protect. We present EdgeDeID, a 0.6B-parameter decoder-only de-identifier that runs locally and is trained without real patient text: three LLM teachers write synthetic notes, a single labeler re-annotates them into one convention, and the model learns to emit a typed XML schema of the identifiers it finds. On a 100-note synthetic in-domain benchmark with manually verified annotations it reaches 0.994 micro-F1 and leaves no missed identifier in 99% of notes, against 0.857 and 12% for the strongest of eight off-the-shelf systems. Quantized, it runs on CPU in 615 MB. Of three additions to supervised training, only a verifier adapter helps: it separates complete from incomplete outputs of other systems at 0.94 AUROC, whereas reinforcement learning with a recall-weighted reward and a rule-based post-processing layer leave in-domain results unchanged. On MIMIC ICU nursing notes F1 falls to 0.48. The errors follow two properties our synthetic notes lack, telegraphic style and low identifier density, and three hand-written in-context examples recover 0.08 F1 without annotated target data.

</details>

### 143. Intervention-Certified Amplification: A Validity Gate for $σ$-Weighted Post-Training

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33882)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`干预认证放大`、`σ加权后训练`、`有效性门`

👤 **作者**：Leiyue Chen、Meng Wei、Xuefeng Hu、Wei Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：混合后训练用训练侧信号设每样本 SFT/RL 平衡——该信号不说明被强调行为在目标任务评测设定下有用
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DIAA 展示：锚点全部移除 Python 围栏后 MATH-500 上 29% 提示发出未执行 TIR（GRPO 三倍）；干预认证放大 ICA 用配对 rollout 目标证据门控 σ
- 📌 **结论**：冻结/在线 ICA 把 TIR 降到 13.8%/11.2%、准确率 47.5%→48.7%/49.3% 并迁移到 GSM8K——后训练混合权重需要目标侧有效性证据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Hybrid post-training often mixes supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), using training-side signals to set each sample's balance. Such signals characterize the anchor data and policy, but not whether the emphasized behavior helps in the target task and evaluation setting. This mismatch can reactivate and amplify pretrained behavior even after its literal form is removed from supervision. We demonstrate this with DIAA (Dual-Implicit Adversarial Amplification), a per-sample $\sigma$-weighted SFT/RL hybrid. With all literal Python fences removed from its anchor, DIAA emits unexecuted Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) on $29%$ of MATH-500 prompts, three times vanilla GRPO. Paired interventions show $10.6$ percentage points of accuracy recovery when TIR is suppressed and comparable contributions from SFT imitation and $\sigma$-amplification; a student trained on a $0%$-TIR teacher anchor emits it at $45.8%$. Intervention-Certified Amplification (ICA) gates $\sigma$ with paired-rollout target evidence. Frozen and online ICA reduce TIR to $13.8%$ and $11.2%$, halve residual drag, improve accuracy from $47.5%$ to $48.7%$ and $49.3%$, and transfer to held-out GSM8K without retuning.

</details>

### 144. When Personas Become Harmful: Guiding LLMs with Global Citizenship Values Alignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33899)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`persona 危害`、`全球公民价值`、`可控对齐`

👤 **作者**：Siqi Shen、Lajanugen Logeswaran、Moontae Lee、Honglak Lee、Rada Mihalcea

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对多元人口用户保持安全同时尊重价值 persona：persona 条件请求常非显式恶意，对抗对齐可能漏掉 persona 使用中产生的伤害
- 🔬 **研究方法**：11k 争议话题×Schwartz 价值 persona 经 agent 管线迭代精炼；UNESCO 全球公民原则作区分有效表达与有害行为的最小共同 ground；可控对齐模型推理时平衡 persona 遵循与安全
- 📌 **结论**：对齐商用 LLM 有害回应成功率 47%+；可控模型在多数样本上更安全且更贴 persona

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLMs are increasingly being used by users from diverse demographics with pluralistic values and distinct backgrounds. Maintaining safety while respecting value-laden personas is uniquely challenging because persona-conditioned requests are often not overtly malicious, unlike those in existing safety benchmarks. Alignment methods aimed at adversarial requests therefore may miss the harm that emerges from persona use, leaving models to either refuse legitimate value expression or blindly follow unsafe personas. We construct a dataset of 11k controversial topics paired with value-based personas mapping to value dimensions from the Schwartz Value Survey. Through an agentic pipeline that iteratively refines the topic and persona, we surface and collect harmful responses from well-aligned commercial LLMs with a more than 47% success rate. We use UNESCO's global citizenship principles as a minimal common ground for distinguishing valid value expressions from harmful behaviors, and introduce a controllable alignment model that lets users balance persona adherence and safety at inference time. The resulting model is safer and more persona-adherent than baselines on most samples.

</details>

### 145. Sybil-Resilient Multi-Drone Exploration of Indoor and Confined Spaces

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33906)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`女巫攻击`、`多无人机探索`、`免基础设施防御`

👤 **作者**：Shyaam Mahesh、Aditya B Prakash、Rubal Sagwal、Avinash Gautam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：无人机队共同探索时信任每个收到的身份——被攻陷无人机制造假身份认领前沿并伪造扫描，使真机留下未探索区与不存在的墙
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五种攻击者（含封层攻击者）×五级仅用自身传感器与地图的防御（收图审计/身体在场检查/见过才信/隔离未见身份/一体一身份绑定）；70 张七类地图仿真
- 📌 **结论**：无防御时一台被攻陷机平均留 25% 地图未探索（办公室 45%）；封层攻击者把三层楼探索切到 82.3%——传感器内生防御层

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When a team of drones explores a building together, each drone tells the others which frontier it is going to and shares the map it has built. This avoids duplicated effort, but it also means that the team trusts every identity from which it receives messages. In a Sybil attack, one compromised drone creates fake identities, and these fake teammates claim frontiers and send fabricated scans, so the real drones leave parts of the building unexplored and end up with walls in their maps that do not exist. We study five such attackers, including one that seals off the openings between floors, and five levels of defence that need no security infrastructure, only the drones' own sensors and maps: an audit of received map data against what a drone sees itself, a check that a body is present where an identity claims to be, trust only for identities whose body has been seen, a quarantine for identities that no drone has observed, and a rule that binds each observed body to one identity. Tests in simulation cover 70 maps of seven kinds, from offices and tunnels to three-storey buildings and caves. Without a defence, one compromised drone leaves on average up to 25% of a map unexplored (45% in offices), and the floor-sealing attacker cuts the explored part of a three-storey building to 82.3%, or 34.6% on the worst map. The quarantine brings exploration back to the level of an unattacked team in every setting, and in most settings it also brings the false data back to that level. The exceptions are two colluding attackers, fakes placed next to real drones, three of the ten caves and a team of 16 drones, and binding bodies to identities fixes the last three. Over 2,350 missions, an honest drone was revoked by a teammate only twice.

</details>

### 146. Human-Aware Agentic Driving: Rethinking Driver State, Trust, and Intervention

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33915)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agentic 驾驶`、`驾驶员状态`、`信任与干预`

👤 **作者**：Lu Feng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 驾驶把目标推理/记忆/工具/自适应交互带入自动驾驶——车辆成为塑造其观察的人类行为的主动参与者
- 🔬 **研究方法**：审视注意力/接管预测/信任感知规划/自适应警告的既有发现在灵活交互下的适用性，区分仍适用/改变/未解三类
- 📌 **结论**：三大研究优先级：干预效应确立、交互与驾驶策略联合设计、重复使用与系统变化下保持有用适应

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic driving brings goal-directed reasoning, memory, tools, and adaptive interaction into automated vehicles. A vehicle can clarify a request, explain a maneuver, or prepare a driver to take control, then adapt its next action to the response. These capabilities make the vehicle an active participant in shaping the human behavior it observes. We examine how agentic interaction affects driver-state inference, trust and reliance, and human intervention in conditional automation. Established findings on attention, takeover prediction, trust-aware planning, and adaptive warnings provide useful foundations, but their applicability depends on how observations are elicited and how assistance changes the driving task. We distinguish what remains applicable, what changes under flexible interaction, and what remains unresolved across these three areas. The analysis identifies three research priorities: establishing the effects of interventions, jointly designing interaction and driving policies, and maintaining useful adaptation across repeated use and system changes. Progress should be measured through driver comprehension, appropriate reliance, and the ability to perform the required driving action in time.

</details>

### 147. CVE-to-ATT&CK Mapping via Evidence-Constrained Ranking

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33924)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CVE映射ATT&CK`、`证据约束排序`、`长尾标注`

👤 **作者**：Kai Wei、Yuwen Cui、Kehan Shen、Guangjing Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：CVE 到 ATT&CK 技术的映射把漏洞信息翻译为对手行为假设——证据碎片化、技术语义相似且标注长尾
- 🔬 **研究方法**：多证据约束排序 TRACE：结构+语义证据构建有界高覆盖候选池，再以累积证据+文本交互+频率感知评分重排
- 📌 **结论**：候选构造把 201 技术空间减约 60% 且为 95.11-100% CVE 保留至少一个真值技术

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Mapping CVEs to MITRE ATT&CK techniques helps defenders translate vulnerability information into hypotheses about adversary behavior. Automating this mapping is challenging because relevant evidence is fragmented across heterogeneous security sources. In addition, ATT&CK techniques can be semantically similar, and existing annotations cover a highly long-tailed subset of the technique space. Existing supervised, retrieval-based, and LLM-based approaches therefore face limitations from closed-set supervision, semantic ambiguity, or unconstrained prediction. We formulate CVE-to-ATT&CK mapping as multi-evidence constrained ranking. Our framework TRACE first integrates complementary structural and semantic evidence from multiple sources to construct a bounded, high-coverage candidate pool over the complete ATT&CK technique space. It then reranks the retained candidates by combining their accumulated evidence with CVE--technique textual interactions and frequency-aware scoring. We evaluate the framework across six public datasets with different annotation processes and vulnerability distributions. Candidate construction reduces the 201-technique ATT&CK search space by approximately 60% while retaining at least one ground-truth technique for 95.11%-100% of CVEs. These results show that combining heterogeneous security evidence with constrained ranking provides a more controlled and generalizable bridge from vulnerability information to adversary behavior.

</details>

### 148. Why Does Structured Output Fail? An Empirical Study of Prompt-Enforced Pydantic Schemas Across Nine LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33949)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`结构化输出失效`、`Pydantic 模式`、`失效分类法`

👤 **作者**：Amit Verma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：管线可靠性常被单一有效率概括——掩盖为何失败与何种干预有效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：细粒度 schema 一致性失效分类法+确定性分类器，3997 次生成尝试×九个 OpenAI/Anthropic 配置
- 📌 **结论**：相似有效率可掩盖不同失效模式；Markdown 围栏 JSON 最常见——681 次围栏失败中仅 48.0% 去围栏后通过验证，其余藏字段长度违规

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Pipelines that require structured outputs from hosted language models often specify a Pydantic schema in the prompt, validate the response, and retry when validation fails. Although native structured-output modes provide an alternative, application-side validation remains important for enforcing custom constraints. Yet pipeline reliability is often summarized by a single validity rate, which obscures why outputs fail and which interventions may help. We introduce a fine-grained taxonomy of schema-conformance failures and a deterministic classifier that assigns failure categories from model responses and Pydantic validation errors. We apply this framework to 3,997 generation attempts across nine OpenAI and Anthropic model/API configurations, using questions from HotpotQA and FinanceBench. Our results show that similar validity rates can mask different failure patterns, and that dominant failure categories vary with schema constraints and task. Markdown-fenced JSON is the most common failure: among 681 fenced failures, only 48.0% pass validation after fence removal, while the remainder conceal additional violations, predominantly field-length violations. In an exploratory prompt ablation, variants that suppress fences appear to shift failures toward length violations, but the shift is consistent with exposing violations that were already present, illustrating how aggregate validity rates can obscure changes in failure composition. We derive four engineering practices for validator, schema, retry and evaluation design, three supported by our data and one, on retry feedback, proposed but not tested, and release the classifier, completions, and annotations.

</details>

### 149. When Does GEPA Merge Help? A Mechanism-Level Diagnostic

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34027)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`GEPA 合并诊断`、`提示优化器稳定性`、`配置敏感`

👤 **作者**：Chong Zhao、Ruyi Yang、Changming Liu、Alice Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：GEPA 的系统感知合并在模型间不稳定（升 4.1-mini 降 Qwen3-8B）且原因未解释
- 🔬 **研究方法**：五数据集×两模型九配置扫描合并时机与算子；测提示级特征能否预测合并接受
- 📌 **结论**：适当配置下合并可两模型都改善——不稳定源于配置敏感而非算子缺陷；行为探针高风险任务弃答避免大失败

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

GEPA is among the strongest reflective prompt optimizers for language-model programs, but its system-aware merge operator is unstable across models: merge improves gpt-4.1-mini on average while degrading Qwen3-8B, an asymmetry noted but left unexplained by the GEPA paper. We investigate this instability across five datasets and two models. Our nine-configuration sweep over merge timing and merge operator shows that merge can improve both models when configured appropriately. This suggests that merge instability is better explained by configuration sensitivity than by an inherent flaw in the merge operator. We then test whether prompt-level features predict GEPA's merge-accept decisions and find that they do not, ruling out static-feature gating. As a proof of concept, a behavior-based probe built from these diagnostics avoids large merge failures in our multi-seed runs by abstaining on high-risk tasks, although it does not consistently outperform NoMerge. Code is available as GEPA-MergeProbe at this https URL .

</details>

### 150. Memory Tax: Dissecting the Net Value of Agent Memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34073)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`记忆税`、`净价值解剖`、`RAG基线`

👤 **作者**：Xuan He、…、ChengXiang Zhai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期 agent 广用记忆——相对原始历史直接检索的净价值不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：多基准多骨干大规模评测：许多专用记忆系统不及朴素 RAG（负净价值）；把净价值解剖为记忆收益与记忆税（组织访问方式变化的双刃偏差）
- 📌 **结论**：聚焦被忽视的记忆税（删除/遮蔽证据）：从孤立表现转向净价值的记忆设计与评测透镜

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Memory is increasingly used in long-horizon agents, yet the net value of building memory over direct retrieval from raw history remains largely unclear. Through a large-scale evaluation across multiple benchmarks and model backbones, we find many dedicated memory systems underperform naive RAG in many cases, indicating a negative net value. Taking RAG as a reference, we dissect the net value of memory into the gains and losses over direct retrieval, termed memory benefit and memory tax. These gains and losses arise because memory process changes how the raw information is organized and accessed. We view such changes as the double-edged deviations from the raw history. They are originally designed and introduced to benefit the task, for example by reorganizing facts or distilling preferences, but some may also remove or obscure evidence, giving rise to memory tax. Since memory benefit reflects the expected gains of its design, we then focus more on the overlooked memory tax, study how it arises from the system and evaluation side, and generalize the concept to more application scenarios of memory. We hope the notion of memory tax can provide a new lens and shift more attention from standalone performance to the net value of memory, informing more reliable memory designs and evaluations. We will open-source the code and results to the community. Project Page: this https URL

</details>

### 151. Securing Agent-Generated Code with Its Own Development History

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34098)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent代码安全审查`、`开发历史证据`、`符号级追踪`

👤 **作者**：Prateek Kumar Rajput、Shahzeb Khan、El-Hacen Diallo、Yewei Song、Jacques Klein、Tegawendé F. Bissyandé

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 的 PR 可通过测试套件却缺守卫或验证步——agent 变更附带人类提交没有的开发历史
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TRACER 用轨迹中每处编辑+agent 声明假设作安全审查证据：符号级追踪、把记录理由当待验声明、修复须评估+功能验证、接受修复附可执行检查
- 📌 **结论**：SusVibes 上符号追踪把脆弱符号放进 94.6% agent 补丁审查范围（主扫描器警告仅 13.4%）；含理由配置产出最多已验证安全修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Pull requests written by coding agents can pass a project's test suite while omitting a guard or a validation step. To find the missing condition, a reviewer currently relies on static analysis of the final code. An agent-generated change, however, is accompanied by a development history that a human commit does not provide. The agent's trajectory records each edit together with the agent's stated assumptions, and structural differencing shows how each changed function evolved. We present TRACER, a workflow that uses this development history as evidence for security review. TRACER tracks changes at the symbol level, treats the agent's recorded rationale as claims to be verified against the current code, accepts a repair only after assessment and functional validation, and attaches to each accepted repair an executable check that later changes must satisfy. On the SusVibes benchmark, symbol-level tracking places the vulnerable symbol in the review scope for 94.6% of agent patches, compared with 13.4% for the primary scanner warning. With two investigator models, the configuration that includes the agent's rationale yields the largest number of verified security repairs while preserving functionality, and adding AST history to it does not increase that number. A funnel analysis identifies where the remaining candidates are lost. In a study of structural-biology software, the combination of repair memory and a boundary check allows a subsequent feature request to succeed in nine of ten tasks without reintroducing the repaired vulnerability, whereas functional feedback alone succeeds in four and reintroduces the vulnerability in three.

</details>

### 152. Can LLMs Trust Appropriately? Evaluating Calibrated Reliance on Imperfect Partners

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34107)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`校准信任`、`不完美伙伴`、`证据敏感`

👤 **作者**：Mengdi Chu、Jintao Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 日益与人类及同伴 agent 协作——其贡献可不完整/不一致/不正确，需把伙伴证据转化为适当依赖策略
- 🔬 **研究方法**：324 场景×23 协作类：三序贯决策+显式转移动态/反馈发射/成本表，精确解析评估对剩余交互期望后果的选择；七模型+36 受控历史探针
- 📌 **结论**：最强模型近最优，其余病态：过度采购保障、买零信息价值验证、优先即时节省而放弃合作学习——校准信任是可解析评估的能力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models increasingly collaborate with humans and peer AI agents whose contributions may be incomplete, inconsistent, or incorrect. Effective collaboration requires calibrated trust, whereby decisions to rely on, verify, or assist a partner adapt rationally as empirical evidence accumulates. We investigate whether models translate partner evidence into appropriate reliance policies. We introduce a benchmark of 324 scenarios across 23 collaboration categories. Each scenario involves three sequential decisions with explicit transition dynamics, feedback emissions, and cost schedules, permitting exact analytical evaluation of choices against their expected consequences over the remaining interaction. Evaluating seven models alongside 36 controlled-history probes, we dissociate evidence sensitivity from normatively appropriate action. While the strongest frontier model approaches near-optimal decisions, other models exhibit recurring pathologies: procuring excessive safeguards, purchasing verification tests with zero expected value of information, prioritizing immediate savings over cooperative learning, and misallocating assistance to suboptimal channels. Supplying explicit posterior distributions resolves certain errors while introducing new failure modes. Calibrated trust in autonomous agents critically demands coupling empirical evidence with forward-looking decision utility.

</details>

### 153. Execution Logs Sway VLM Judges of Robot Progress

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34151)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`日志误导VLM裁判`、`机器人进度评判`、`一行翻转`

👤 **作者**：Weiyan Zhao、Sunny Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 现在给机器人视频打分——帧旁的文本可改变裁决
- 🔬 **研究方法**：机器人失进度的片段上加一行“正在取得进展”的日志；跨 Gemini/Qwen/GPT 裁判比较与中性行的差异
- 📌 **结论**：单行日志比中性行多翻转 10-35 点正确裁决；一行“日志可能有错”警告消除大部分效应——机器人评测的文本侧信道

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language models now grade robot videos, and text placed beside the frames can change their verdicts. On clips where the robot is losing progress, we add one log line saying the robot is making progress. Across Gemini, Qwen and GPT judges, this flips correct verdicts to progress 10 to 35 percentage points more often than a neutral line does. A one-line warning that the log may be wrong removes most of this effect. Selected fake "task complete" claims flip half of Qwen's correct verdicts, against a tenth for unrelated log lines. They have no detectable effect on Gemini.

</details>

### 154. Higher Reasoning Effort Improves Reference Verifiability and Changes Reasoning Summaries in GPT-5.5

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34212)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`推理力度`、`引用可验证性`、`GPT-5.5`

👤 **作者**：Melika Mobini、Andres Algaba、Vincent Ginis

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 用于科学生产含文献推荐——推理力度是否改善生成引用的可验证性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GPT-5.5 为 1000 篇论文生成学术引用，配对低/高努力设置；生成引用匹配外部书目记录，推理摘要按四预定义特征评分
- 📌 **结论**：高努力使解析器验证率 78.3%→84.8% 且推理摘要更长、四特征得分更高——但特征分数与验证率无一致增长

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are increasingly being used in different stages of scientific knowledge production, including the suggestion of references. These models can operate at different levels of reasoning effort and provide summaries of their reasoning processes. This raises two questions: Does increasing reasoning effort improve the verifiability of generated references, and do the resulting reasoning summaries reveal how the model's behavior changes with greater effort? We examined summaries from GPT-5.5 as it generated scholarly references for 1,000 papers under paired low- and high-effort settings. Generated references were matched to external bibliographic records, and reasoning summaries were scored for four predefined features and explored for additional features discovered by GPT-5.5. High effort increased the mean paper-level resolver-verified reference rate from 78.3% to 84.8%, and produced longer reasoning summaries. All four predefined features received higher scores under high effort. The discovered features changed less uniformly: some appeared equally often in both conditions, whereas others were more common under high effort. Within each effort condition, neither feature set showed a consistent increase in verification rates with higher scores.

</details>

### 155. $Δτ$-Bench: Pushing Agents Toward a Moving Capability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34238)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`移动能力前沿`、`工具生态演化`、`技能维护`

👤 **作者**：Shuofei Qiao、…、Emine Yilmaz

- 🎯 **研究动机**：大型工具生态结构性演化：熟悉工具消失被替换、更高效替代在重叠功能与噪声文档中涌现——agent 能否主动发现高效路径并及时修订积累的程序知识
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Δτ-Bench：能力保持变换生成六个连续生态状态×42 域 1588 个验证多轮任务；Steward 生态管理者联合管理工具与程序知识（增量技能维护+动态供给）
- 📌 **结论**：最强模型/harness 仅 42.3% 任务成功，现有技能学习方法的初始增益不能一致转化为演化下的适应

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In this paper, we introduce $\Delta\tau$-Bench, an executable agent benchmark that models the structural evolution of large tool ecosystems. In $\Delta\tau$-Bench, independently evolving services continually reshape how tasks can be accomplished: familiar tools may disappear or be replaced, while more efficient alternatives emerge amid overlapping functionality and noisy documentation. Against this moving capability frontier, we evaluate whether agents can proactively discover efficient tool-use paths and promptly revise their accumulated procedural knowledge, bringing together demands that existing benchmarks fail to examine. $\Delta\tau$-Bench comprises 1,588 validated multi-turn tasks across 42 domains and six successive ecosystem states generated through capability-preserving transformations. Our evaluation shows that even the strongest evaluated model or harness achieves only 42.3% task success, while initial gains from existing skill-learning methods can not consistently translate into better adaptation as the ecosystem evolves. These findings motivate Steward, an ecosystem manager that jointly manages tools and procedural knowledge through incremental skill maintenance and dynamic provisioning during task execution. Experiments show that Steward sustains higher task success across ecosystem changes with lower skill-maintenance overhead, while improving both recovery from disrupted workflows and adoption of more efficient alternatives.

</details>

### 156. A Russian, a Brazilian, and a German Walk into a Bar, Ask the Same Question-- And Get Three Different Answers: Profiling LLM Perspective Diversity on Contested Topics

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34278)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`跨语言立场`、`对冲vs承诺`、`视角多样性`

👤 **作者**：Wiem Ben Rim、Yao Lu、Pontus Stenetorp、Emine Yilmaz

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多数主观查询基准英语中心——LLM 视角如何跨语言变化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PerspectivesQA 人工验证 18 语言全球争议话题+ROOTS 框架刻画语言/视角/框架多样性；八个开源与专有模型
- 📌 **结论**：LLM 在英语中对冲而在低资源语言中承诺立场；更大模型保持更一致跨语言世界观而小模型更易被左右；对表面输入变化高敏感

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models(LLMs) have shown strong performance on tasks with verifiable answers, but many real-world queries involve subjectivity, disagreement, and conflicting evidence. Most existing benchmarks for such queries remain English-centric, overlooking the vast diversity of perspectives held by users worldwide. To systematically study how LLM perspectives vary across languages, we curate PerspectivesQA, a manually verified multilingual dataset of globally contested topics covering 18 languages, and propose ROOTS, a framework that profiles linguistic, perspective, and framing diversity. We evaluate a diverse set of eight models covering both open-source and proprietary families at varying scales, establishing that LLMs hedge their answers in English but commit to positions in lower-resource languages. Larger models maintain a more consistent cross-lingual worldview, while smaller models are more easily swayed. All models also exhibit high sensitivity to minor surface-level input variations, producing notable stance even in larger models.

</details>

### 157. Repairing Broken Access Control in Web Applications with LLMs: An Empirical Benchmark Study

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34290)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：` Broken Access Control`、`LLM修复基准`、`脚手架`

👤 **作者**：Mingrui Liu、…、Yuling Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：失效访问控制是最严重 web 漏洞类之一而自动修复基本未探——BAC 是逻辑漏洞需理解访问模型与业务语义
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BAC-Bench：六个广泛使用 web 应用×四语言的 46 个真实 BAC 漏洞，每个带沙箱运行时+PoC 验证+功能测试；四维修复策略框架（位置/覆盖/机制/内容）
- 📌 **结论**：八基础 LLM 平均修 37.68%，两个最小脚手架（应用反馈/导航定位）提到 66.03%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Broken access control (BAC) ranks among the most severe web-application vulnerability classes, yet its automated repair remains largely unexplored. Unlike memory-safety vulnerabilities, BAC is a logic vulnerability whose repair demands a deep understanding of an application's access-control model and business semantics, which traditional automated vulnerability repair techniques handle poorly. Large language models (LLMs) open a promising avenue for this problem, but no standard benchmark exists for studying how well they repair BAC vulnerabilities. We construct BAC-Bench, the first benchmark for BAC repair, consisting of 46 real-world BAC vulnerabilities drawn from six widely used web applications spanning four programming languages. Each vulnerability is equipped with a sandboxed runtime environment, a PoC validation program and a functional test program. We further propose a BAC repair-strategy framework that characterizes any BAC patch along four dimensions, Position, Coverage, Mechanism, and Content. Evaluating eight base LLMs and two commercial LLM agents on BAC-Bench, we find that single-turn base LLMs repair 37.68% of the vulnerabilities on average, while two minimal scaffolds, Apply-Feedback and Navigate-and-Locate, raise this average to 66.03%, with the best base LLM reaching 75.36%, close to the 78.99% of the best agent. Notably, some LLM-generated patches surpass the official patches, and one incidentally closed a zero-day vulnerability that had lain dormant for three years. Our findings delineate the current capability boundary of LLM-based BAC repair and offer guidance toward its practical adoption.

</details>

### 158. Developer Task Delegation to LLMs and the Trust Factor: A Mixed Methods Study

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34317)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`开发者委托`、`信任因素`、`混合方法`

👤 **作者**：Samuel Ferino、Rashina Hoda、John Grundy、Christoph Treude、Hashini Gunatilake

- 🎯 **研究动机**：开发者把什么任务委托给 LLM、委托到什么程度、为什么——尚不清楚
- 🔬 **研究方法**：22 访谈+160 从业者调查：全权/部分/自托/回避四模式，任务难度/风险/动机/信任与委托的关联
- 📌 **结论**：信任是与 LLM 任务委托显著关联的因素——人机协作的可操作含义

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With the rise of Large Language Models (LLMs), software developers are presented with a powerful new approach: delegating software development tasks to LLMs. Existing studies have explored task delegation for a subset of tasks and factors. Critically, what tasks developers delegate to LLMs, to what extent, and why they delegate them, is not yet well understood. We explore these questions through 22 interviews and a survey with 160 software practitioners. Developers reported employing four modes of task delegation to LLMs: full delegation, partial delegation, self-delegation, and task avoidance. Drawing on our qualitative findings, we discuss the rationales behind these choices and explore how task difficulty, task risk, developer motivation, and trust appear to be associated with software development task delegation to LLMs. Our statistical analysis reveals that trust is a significant factor associated with task delegation to LLMs. Finally, we share actionable implications for human-AI collaboration in software research, and for software practitioners to make the most of their newly found powers of delegation.

</details>

### 159. AndroidFlux: Evaluating and Reward Modeling Failure Recovery Capabilities in Mobile Use Multimodal Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34384)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`失败恢复评测`、`检查点回放`、`恢复奖励模型`

👤 **作者**：Gyubeum Lim、Yemo Koo、Ghunwoo Jang、Zsolt Kira

- 🎯 **研究动机**：移动 agent 从干净初始态的任务成功评不了从中间错误恢复的能力——环境动态变化要求从意外状态稳健完成
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AndroidFlux 基于 AndroidWorld 把记录轨迹恢复到首个可恢复错误前/中/后的受控检查点使不同 agent 从同设备状态继续；附恢复动作偏好奖励模型基准与离线 RL 数据
- 📌 **结论**：恢复表现与干净启动成功仅部分相关；显式推理只帮 GUI 域 RL 训练过的模型

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>
