Mobile-use multimodal agents are typically evaluated by task success from a clean initial state, which does not isolate their ability to recover from intermediate errors. Because mobile environments change dynamically, a general mobile-use agent must complete tasks robustly even from unexpected states and in unseen environments. This requires knowing when and where an agent makes errors, and whether it can recover from errors and subsequent states. To evaluate this capability, we introduce AndroidFlux, a failure-recovery benchmark built on AndroidWorld. AndroidFlux restores recorded trajectories to controlled checkpoints before, at, and after the first recoverable error, so that different agents continue from the same device state and history. On these checkpoints, a complementary reward-modeling benchmark evaluates recovery-action preferences using downstream rollout outcomes. We further release an offline reinforcement learning (RL) dataset that improves both error recovery and overall task success. Our evaluations show that recovery performance only partially correlates with clean-start success, and that explicit reasoning helps only models trained with GUI-domain specific RL.

</details>

### 160. SymLift: Neural Semantic Lifting for Compile-Free Vulnerability Detection in Context-Incomplete Code Fragments

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34431)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`免编译漏洞检测`、`神经语义提升`、`Datalog证据`

👤 **作者**：Xiarun Chen、…、Tsong Yueh Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：缺项目上下文使构建依赖分析器无法构造程序关系，纯神经预测不建立可审计推导
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SymLift：微调 LLM 以受限 LLVM 风格 IR 重建漏洞相关控制流/数据流/内存/资源关系（不重建完整上下文不直接预测标签）；确定性解析器验证 IR 映射为规范化证据事实，显式 Datalog 规则派生带支撑事实与推导轨迹的发现
- 📌 **结论**：函数级片段使 Clang 编译率 100%→12.00% 而 SymLift 分析完成率仅降 1.17 点；提升器控制流 IoU 96.85%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Missing project context can prevent build-dependent analyzers from constructing program relations required for vulnerability detection, while neural predictions alone do not establish auditable derivations. We present SymLift, a compile-free framework centered on Vulnerability-Oriented Neural Semantic Lifting for context-incomplete code fragments. A fine-tuned large language model reconstructs vulnerability-relevant control-flow, data-flow, memory, and resource relations in a constrained LLVM-like intermediate representation (IR), without reconstructing the full project context or directly predicting vulnerability labels. A deterministic parser validates the IR and maps its supported operations and relations into normalized evidence facts. Explicit Datalog rules derive findings with supporting facts and derivation traces, ensuring deterministic reasoning for fixed facts and rules. We evaluate SymLift on FormAI, Juliet, and MegaVul. Reducing complete Juliet programs to function-level fragments lowers Clang compilation success from 100% to 12.00%, whereas SymLift's analysis completion rate decreases by only 1.17 percentage points. After task-specific fine-tuning, the lifter achieves a control-flow intersection over union (IoU) of 96.85% on FormAI. SymLift-Qwen-7B achieves a Target-F1 of 94.41% on FormAI and, across six vulnerability families on MegaVul, a micro-averaged precision of 98.24% and a micro-averaged Target-F1 of 91.66%. These results support accurate and auditable detection in the evaluated context-incomplete settings, even with partial program relations.

</details>

### 161. From Explaining to Enabling: Actionable Transparency in Algorithmic Insurance Pricing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34459)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可行动透明`、`保险定价解释`、`共形筛选`

👤 **作者**：Agathe Fernandes Machado、Fei Huang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：ML 定价的决定须向投保人解释——有用解释必须有效（按模型真降保费）、可行动（只改可控特征）、可靠（稀疏区域仍达目标）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三公理形式化+共形预测约束筛掉模型太不确定的推荐从而界不可靠率；四种反事实解释技术应用于 57832 条佛州洪水险真实理赔+98058 合成车联网保单
- 📌 **结论**：从解释到可行动推荐的转化框架——个体层面的可问责定价

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine learning (ML) systems increasingly make consequential decisions about individuals, including credit, hiring, and insurance. Research on ML-driven insurance pricing has asked how the pricing mechanism itself can be made accountable. Yet it remains unclear how an individual policyholder's algorithmically determined premium should be explained to them. We argue that a useful explanation must be valid, actionable, and reliable. A valid recommendation lowers the premium according to the model. An actionable recommendation changes only characteristics the policyholder can control. A reliable recommendation has an actual outcome that is likely to meet the target, even in sparsely observed regions of the feature space where the model is uncertain. We formalize these three requirements as axioms and develop a method that turns an algorithmic premium into recommendations that meet them. The method meets the third requirement with a Conformal Prediction constraint, which screens out recommendations that the model is too uncertain about and thereby bounds the rate of unreliable recommendations. We apply four Counterfactual Explanation techniques to 57,832 real Florida flood-insurance claims and 98,058 synthetic driver-telematics policies. We adapt these techniques for insurance applications, so that their recommendations change only controllable characteristics and resemble policyholders observed in the data. In our illustrations, the constrained recommendations for high-risk policyholders are both more reliable and associated with larger predicted risk reductions than the unconstrained ones. These results suggest that insurers can give policyholders explanations that are both actionable and trustworthy. The work contributes to research on algorithmic accountability and offers implications for insurers and regulators.

</details>

### 162. What Effort Level Should I Choose? On the Economics of Reasoning Effort in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34491)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`推理力度经济学`、`信任品`、`努力反常`

👤 **作者**：Kaiser Sun、Yongchan Kwon、Zain Hasan、Shang Zhu、Kaitlyn Zhou、Federico Bianchi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推理力度是信任品：用户看到结果却无法评估所付算力的边际价值
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个基准上测力度档的反事实效应（单次推理+长程 agent 任务），专有与开源模型
- 📌 **结论**：更高力度可总体提准但常在低力度已解的任务上失败；全员可解的易任务上最高力度成本达最低的 42 倍；力度还改变解题方式（更高力度更多自验证与搜索）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative models increasingly expose a reasoning effort setting that lets users scale inference-time compute, trading higher cost for potentially improved performance. Users must choose an effort level before knowing whether extra reasoning will help, and even a successful run does not reveal whether the effort purchased was necessary. Reasoning effort is therefore a credence good: buyers observe the outcome but cannot assess the marginal value of the computation they paid for. In this work, we measure the counterfactual effect of effort levels on both proprietary and open-weight models on four benchmarks, spanning single-call reasoning and long-horizon agentic tasks. Effort, it turns out, does not consistently preserve tasks that lower effort solves, can overspend, and can change trajectory behavior. First, we find that although higher effort can raise accuracy in aggregate, it frequently fails on tasks that lower effort solved. Second, on the subset of easy tasks that every effort level solves, higher effort is generally more costly, with the highest effort level costing up to 42x as much as the lowest. Lastly, effort changes not only how many tokens are used, but also how agents solve tasks: at higher effort, agents verify their own work more heavily and more often search for solutions than work through problems directly, both of which lengthen trajectories without necessarily altering the outcome. These findings show that naively choosing the highest level is not only cost-inefficient but also offers no guarantee of the best instance-level performance. A simple alternative, starting at the lowest effort and escalating only after a verified failure, matches or improves on fixed top effort across all models and benchmarks at lower cost for most proprietary models.

</details>

### 163. The Price of Scale in Proactive Memory Recall

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34507)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`主动记忆召回`、`多重检验`、`共形门`

👤 **作者**：Yichuan Chen、Xin Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长命助手须每轮决定记忆库中是否有值得打断的内容——该决策是难度随库容 N 增长的多重检验问题
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LongMemEval/LoCoMo 流式重放：5% 误警调的阈值在 N=2048 时达 87-100% 误警；证明门误警率 α 的每项假阳率约 -ln(1-α)/N 并把命中率钉在单比较 ROC 左端；线索交换共形门免标有限样本误警保证
- 📌 **结论**：阈值须随 ln N / √ln N 增长，逐线索归一化在匹配误警下约翻倍命中——主动召回的规模代价

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A long-lived assistant that brings up memories without being asked must decide at every turn whether anything in its store is worth an interruption. We show that this decision is a multiple-testing problem whose difficulty grows with the store size $N$. On streaming replays of LongMemEval and LoCoMo, a similarity threshold tuned to 5% false alarms at $N=8$ reaches 87-100% at $N=2048$, across embedding models and for explicit and implicit cues; LLM judges show the same growth from a lower base. We prove that a gate with false-alarm rate $\alpha$ operates at a per-item false-positive rate of about $-\ln(1-\alpha)/N$, which pins its hit rate to the far left of the single-comparison ROC curve and, under regular variation, makes it decay as a power of $N$ with an explicit constant. The resulting prediction matches the hit rate of a per-$N$ oracle threshold to within one percentage point. The analysis explains why lexical and semantic channels need thresholds growing like $\ln N$ and $\sqrt{\ln N}$, and why per-cue normalisation roughly doubles the hit rate at matched false alarms. A cue-swap conformal gate, which compares the current cue with the user's other cues on the same store, needs no labels and has a finite-sample false-alarm guarantee under exchangeability; on LongMemEval it holds false alarms near 5% at the hit rate of a label-tuned threshold. Three annotators (Fleiss' $\kappa=0.63$) find that about nine in ten false alarms are genuine interruptions. Code and data: this https URL

</details>

### 164. VTC-AUDIT: An Interventional Reliability Audit for Visual-Token Compression under Paired Image Shifts

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34545)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`视觉词元压缩审计`、`配对图像偏移`、`增量风险`

👤 **作者**：Isaac

- 🎯 **研究动机**：视觉词元压缩通常以干净精度与效率评——不揭示压缩在未压缩模型保持正确的图像偏移下引入的失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VTC-AUDIT 干预式审计：只在全模型两图都对且压缩模型干净图对的配对上估计增量风险；四臂交叉“哪图选保留位置/哪图供特征”；三开源 8-9B 模型×四任务×八偏移×两免训练压缩器=70.8 万推理记录
- 📌 **结论**：偏移图上重选位置使失败率在全部六设置上升（DivPrune +3.04-9.60 点、ApET +2.05-4.20 点，95% 区间全为正）——压缩可靠性的干预审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Visual-token compression is usually evaluated by clean accuracy and efficiency, but these averages do not reveal failures that compression introduces under an image shift where the uncompressed model stays correct. We introduce VTC-AUDIT, which estimates this incremental risk only on pairs where the full model is correct on both images and the compressed model is correct on the clean image. The four arms then cross two binary choices: which image selects the retained token positions, and which image supplies the visual features. We audit three open 8-9B vision-language models on four tasks and eight pre-specified shifts. We evaluate two training-free compressors---hard-pruning DivPrune and prune-and-merge ApET---at a matched 25% retained-position budget, producing 708,000 inference records. Reselecting positions on the shifted image, rather than reusing the clean-image positions, raises the failure rate in all six model-compressor settings. The increase is 3.04-9.60 percentage points for DivPrune and 2.05-4.20 percentage points for ApET, with every 95% interval above zero. Repeating the audit on a separately resampled second manifest reproduces all six increases. In a pooled monitor audit across the four benchmarks, support Jaccard instability fails the practical AUROC criterion of 0.65 in all six settings, whereas clean compressed-model uncertainty clears it. Thus, support turnover is a weak ranking signal under the audited benchmark mixture, while clean compressed-model uncertainty is substantially more informative. These results suggest that future visual-token compression evaluations should report conditional reliability alongside clean accuracy and efficiency.

</details>

### 165. Does the Outfit Change the Price? Context Halo in Vision-Language Model Valuation of an Identical Low-Cost Necklace

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34591)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`上下文光环`、`VLM估值`、`着装效应`

👤 **作者**：Brianne Lee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：同一低价项链以四种方式拍照——VLM 估值是否随着装与场景变化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：1164 次独立与顺序 API 会话（4604 行）：正式装木墙/派对/便装回收场/平铺无人，六前沿模型估价
- 📌 **结论**：正式照估值是平铺的 1.9-3.3 倍（全 p<0.01）；纯文本描述同样产生 2.2-3.9 比率（不需像素）；平铺本身已是实付价 7.8-18.7 倍——部分“乘数”是基线高估而非上下文效应

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A chunky gold-tone chain necklace bought online for 2.43 USD was photographed four ways: worn with formal attire against a wood-panelled wall, worn at a party, worn with casual clothing in a recycling yard, and laid flat with no person present. We asked six frontier language models to estimate its price in 1,164 independent and sequential API sessions (4,604 logged rows) and compared estimates across settings. Among the four models that answered image prompts, the same necklace was priced 1.9 to 3.3 times higher in the formal photograph than in the flat-lay (all p < 0.01). Against the yard photograph, the ratio ranged from 1.23 (Grok 4.5, not significant) to 3.57 (Kimi K3), with Claude Fable 5 at 3.32. A text-only version of the task, in which the setting is described in words, produced formal-to-yard ratios of 2.2 to 3.9 in all six models, so the effect does not require pixels. Flat-lay estimates themselves were 7.8 to 18.7 times the receipt price, so part of any headline "outfit multiplier" computed against the receipt is baseline overestimate, not a context effect. A single instruction to ignore clothing and setting significantly reduced the effect in one model (ratio of ratios 0.77, 95% CI 0.65 to 0.91) and not detectably in the others; telling the model the photo came from a company website or a personal account changed nothing significant. Asked afterward whether a different outfit would have changed their number, Claude Fable 5 said yes in 24 of 24 sessions, GPT-5.6 in 69%, and GPT-4o in 18%, while GPT-4o also refused 79% of image estimation prompts. The study isolates a mechanism with one object and one wearer at about 10 sessions per model and condition; we outline the replication needed next, with multiple objects and a human benchmark. Data, code, and raw outputs are released.

</details>

### 166. A Battery for AI Propensity Measurement: Rubrics, Synthetic Item Banks, and Annotations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34614)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`倾向测量`、`量表与题库`、`心理测量`

👤 **作者**：Peter Romero、…、José Hernández-Orallo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：影响 AI 系统与人类行为的通用倾向需要可复用的测量工具
- 🔬 **研究方法**：28 个倾向电池：专家写量表+合成数据集+标注需求区间，合成数据集设计为只对单一倾向敏感以便快速画像
- 📌 **结论**：心理测量学构造锚定的 AI 倾向测量基础设施

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We release a battery of 28 propensities for AI research, with expert-written rubrics, synthetic datasets, and annotated demand intervals, built with the method of Romero-Alvarado et al. (2026). We selected a wide collection of generic propensities that may influence the behavior of AI systems and humans. They are inspired by psychometric, psychological, and behavioral research, so that experimental results can be understood in the context of familiar constructs previously used also for human behavior research. The rubrics are useful for annotating existing benchmarks whose items may be relevant or not for one or more propensities. For easier profiling, however, the synthetic datasets are defined in such a way that they should be sensitive to one propensity only, allowing for a quick prototyping of propensity profiles using the battery of datasets. We also outline directions for future propensity research.

</details>

### 167. Thinking-in-Bets at Test Time Reduces Sycophantic Revisions in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34654)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`下注式思考`、`谄媚修订`、`声明清单`

👤 **作者**：Nghi D. Q. Bui

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 常在用户挑战时放弃正确工作，但简单叫它坚持又会忽视真错——读投诉后倾向打折自己先前的检查以同意用户
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Thinking-in-Bets 免训练：agent 在读用户反馈前写简短私有记录（主要声明+支撑检查+置信）；被挑战时查阅声明清单保住已验证工作同时调查不确定部分；五个 Gemini 模型×八基准
- 📌 **结论**：批评前记录声明几乎消除补丁评审中的假翻转并保护正确报告草稿，而读投诉后再写记录几乎无增益——时序是关键

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language model agents often abandon correct work when a user challenges it, yet simply telling them to stand firm makes them ignore real mistakes. Once an agent reads a complaint, it tends to discount its own earlier checks in order to agree with the user. We introduce Thinking-in-Bets, a training-free method in which an agent writes a short private record of its main claims, the checks supporting them, and its confidence before reading user feedback. When challenged, the agent consults this Claim Checklist to keep verified work intact while investigating uncertain or newly challenged parts. We evaluate five Gemini models (3.5 Flash-Lite, 3.5 Flash, 3.6 Flash, 3.8 Flash, and 4.0 Argon) across eight benchmarks: SWE-Together, SWE-Interact, and DeepSWE for software engineering, Agents' Last Exam for professional reports, Humanity's Last Exam and SycEval for academic reasoning, Vals Finance for financial analysis, and Terminal-Bench for command-line tasks. Recording claims before criticism nearly eliminates false reversals in patch review and protects passing report drafts under mistaken pushback, whereas writing the same record after reading the complaint recovers very little of the gain. In multi-turn coding, pre-reply claim records substantially reduce unnecessary file edits after mistaken complaints, and gating the record so it is written only when the agent is uncertain lowers token overhead while fixing more real bugs than the baseline. Even in non-interactive tasks without user feedback, listing the key claims in a draft and re-checking the weakest one before submission improves both answer accuracy and confidence estimates across all tested models.

</details>

### 168. Benchmarking Obstacle-Conditioned Safety in Household Robot Navigation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34674)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`障碍条件安全`、`语义-运动接地`、`家庭导航`

👤 **作者**：Hyunwoong Bae、Jisu Han、Jiyeon Choi、Dohoon Kim、Taesup Moon

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全家庭导航须按障碍物是什么与多近来调节间隙与速度而无过度保守——现有评测不测语义安全区分是否反映到连续运动
- 🔬 **研究方法**：OSMA-Bench：仅障碍类型不同的空间匹配场景集+语义敏感指数 SSI（间隙/速度/平滑是否跟随障碍谨慎排序）；VLM 与 VLA 策略评测
- 📌 **结论**：两者都不一致地把运动适配到障碍安全（即使显式安全提示）；能按安全相关性区分障碍却不反映到运动参数——语义到运动的接地缺口，VLM 策略加显式参数推断步可改善

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safe household navigation requires robots to regulate clearance and speed according to what an obstacle is and how close it is, without unnecessary conservatism. Yet existing evaluations do not test whether semantic safety distinctions are reflected in continuous motion. To that end, we introduce OSMA-Bench, an Obstacle-Conditioned Safe Motion Adaptation Benchmark built on spatially matched scenario sets that differ only in obstacle type. We also propose the \emph{Semantic Sensitivity Index} (SSI), which measures whether clearance and distance-dependent speed and smoothness follow the caution ordering of obstacles. Evaluating VLM-based and vision-language-action (VLA) policies on the benchmark, we find that neither consistently adapts its motion to obstacle safety, even with explicit safety prompts. Our analysis shows that the policies can correctly distinguish obstacles by safety relevance but fail to reflect this distinction in the motion parameters or actions they produce. For VLM-based policies, which recognize the obstacle and then generate a navigation program, adding an explicit parameter-inference step between these two stages improves adaptation. These findings expose a gap in \emph{semantic-to-motion grounding}, highlighting the need to translate semantic caution into appropriately safe motion.

</details>

### 169. LADDER: Diagnosing Domain-Level Self-Assessment Under Noisy Feedback

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34737)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`域级自评`、`噪声反馈`、`能力自知`

👤 **作者**：Zhenshuai Yin、…、Xiaoying Tang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：知道何时可靠对人机协作至关重要——交互反馈不总完整或正确，可误导模型对自身能力的理解
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LADDER 研究模型在不可靠反馈下的自我评估
- 📌 **结论**：模型能准确复述给定性能信息却仍难从交互经验形成可信自评；表面准确的评估可掩盖能力差异且不预测未来任务表现——自评应以观测任务表现及其预测价值来评

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Knowing when they are reliable is essential for language models to support human-AI collaboration. Yet interaction feedback is not always complete or correct, and may mislead models' understanding of their own capabilities. We introduce LADDER to study how language models assess themselves under unreliable feedback. Our findings show that models can accurately report supplied performance information while still struggling to form trustworthy self-assessments from interaction experience. Apparently accurate assessments can obscure capability differences and do not necessarily predict future task performance. These findings suggest that trustworthy self-assessment should be evaluated against observed task performance and its predictive value. Our study provides empirical evidence of the limits of model self-knowledge and a basis for evaluating capability awareness in reliable human-AI collaboration.

</details>

### 170. Mechanistic Insights into LoRA: Layer Sparsity for Adaptive Fine-Tuning via Path Patching

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34756)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LoRA机制`、`层稀疏性`、`路径修补`

👤 **作者**：Fei Zuo、Yizhou Huang、Kezhi Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：标准全层 LoRA 无视组件的异质贡献——LoRA 如何影响模型内部缺乏系统机制分析
- 🔬 **研究方法**：系统机制可解释性分析：LoRA 适配效应高度非均匀（组件与层级稀疏，重要层高度任务依赖）；Adaptive-LoRA 校准驱动经冻结基座零消融自动识别任务特定层
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen2.5-1.5B×11 推理任务上只用 30% 层超全层 LoRA 且适配器参数减 3.5 倍——机制洞见到实用效率的桥

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models. Standard all-layer LoRA applies adaptation throughout the transformer, despite heterogeneous contributions from its components. In this work, we conduct a systematic mechanistic interpretability analysis to understand how LoRA affects model internals. We observe that LoRA's adaptation effect is highly non-uniform, with significant component-wise and layer-wise sparsity. Most critically, we find that important layers are highly task-dependent, with limited overlap across tasks. Motivated by these insights, we propose Adaptive-LoRA, a calibration-driven method that automatically identifies task-specific layers via frozen-base zero-ablation, enabling efficient layer selection without exhaustive search. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5-1.5B across 11 diverse reasoning tasks demonstrate that Adaptive-LoRA outperforms full-layer LoRA while using only 30% of layers, achieving a $3.5\times$ reduction in adapter parameters. This work bridges mechanistic interpretability and practical efficiency, providing both fundamental insights and a principled approach for adaptive fine-tuning.

</details>

### 171. The Physical Cost of Context: Quality-Constrained Resource Accounting for Coding Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34763)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`上下文物理成本`、`质量门控`、`前缀缓存`

👤 **作者**：Zhongming Yu、…、Jishen Zhao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 每次调用重发增长上下文，上下文策略通常只按节省提示词元评判——实际执行工作量还依赖调用数与前缀复用（更短提示若重写缓存历史反而更贵）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：质量门控协议把执行预填拆为逻辑提示减复用前缀并测 KV 驻留/时间/能耗；450 个 SWE-rebench V2 定位案例+900 格研究+100 个 SWE-bench Verified
- 📌 **结论**：Eager 预载省调用 35.7% 与能耗代理 33.2%；启用前缀缓存把其未缓存预填砍 67.7%；Mask-5 删历史省 33.3% 提示量却使未缓存预填涨 608.5%、能耗代理 +125.6% 且未过质量门——token 节省不等于物理节省

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents resend a growing context on every model call, and context policies are usually judged by the prompt tokens they save. Executed work also depends on call count and prefix reuse: a caching runtime prefills only the tokens after the longest cached prefix. A shorter prompt can therefore cost more if it rewrites cached history. Our quality-gated protocol splits executed prefill into logical prompt minus reused prefix and measures KV residency, time, and energy. On 450 localization cases derived from SWE-rebench V2, preloading ten retrieved blocks (Eager) and adding anchored history compaction (Compact) both pass a five-point non-inferiority gate. In a separate 900-cell study, Eager cuts model calls by 35.7% and the GPU-energy proxy by 33.2% against on-demand search (Grep); Compact saves nothing further. Enabling prefix caching leaves Eager's prompt volume similar (+8.3%; its 95% CI spans zero) but cuts its uncached prefill by 67.7%. Deleting history can turn a token saving into extra work. With mini-SWE-agent on 100 SWE-bench Verified instances, keeping only the last five observations (Mask-5) cuts Eager's prompt volume by 33.3% but loses 135.9k reusable tokens per task. Uncached prefill rises by 608.5%, the energy proxy by 125.6%, and the policy misses the quality gate. Policies thus save physical work by shortening the trajectory and can multiply it by editing reusable history.

</details>

### 172. The Right Fact, the Wrong Decision: SciValidityBench for Scientific Agent Memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34767)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`科学使用有效性`、`agent记忆基准`、`决策oracle`

👤 **作者**：Yuexi Pan、Yongchao Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多数 agent 记忆基准把长期记忆当检索——科学 agent 面临更严问题：正确记住的事实用于决策仍可能无效（先定停止规则的确认 vs 从20个检查结果中挑一个的探索）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SciValidityBench：64 个 TaskSpec 跨五有效性别族×六科学域，实验历史+科学状态定义+程序化决策 oracle+受控干预与自动评分；SciStateForge 编译器-阅读器把分布式历史变为显式决策治理状态
- 📌 **结论**：编译科学状态比通用事件图改进有效决策 9.2-18.8 点——检索正确≠使用有效

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Most agent-memory benchmarks frame long-term memory as retrieval: can an agent recover the relevant record from a long and evolving history? Scientific agents face a stricter problem: a correctly remembered fact may still be invalid to use. The same reported test result can support confirmation when the outcome and stopping rule were fixed in advance, yet only exploration when the outcome was selected from twenty inspected results. We call this scientific use validity: whether remembered evidence is eligible to influence a decision under the state governing its interpretation. We introduce SciValidityBench, an executable benchmark factory with 64 TaskSpecs spanning five validity modules and six scientific domains. TaskSpecs couple generated experimental histories with scientific-state definitions, programmatic decision oracles, controlled interventions, and automatic scoring. The SciStateForge compiler-reader framework turns distributed histories into explicit decision-governing state. Counterfactual pairs and targeted corruption-repair controls then test whether models use those dependencies and localize failures. Across four model families, compiled scientific state improves valid decision-making by 9.2-18.8 percentage points over a generic Event Graph. Retrieval alone does not reproduce this cross-family gain, while content-identical flat and typed views show that the improvement comes from exposing governing state rather than a particular graph serialization. SciValidityBench thus shifts memory evaluation from finding the right fact to deciding whether that fact should control the decision.

</details>

### 173. Arya Forge: Auditing Validation and Release Approval for Financial Instruction Data

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34778)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`金融指令数据审计`、`发布审批`、`清单绑定`

👤 **作者**：Kushagra Bhatnagar、Deekshith Marla

- 🎯 **研究动机**：金融指令数据管线须区分内容有效与发布特定数据集的许可
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Arya Forge：类型化记录+当前内容检查+绑定数据集清单的审批覆盖；10 万合成指令记录完整性审计+240 场景 2400 处理行注入故障测试
- 📌 **结论**：金融与发布记录检查拒全部注入数值错误与政策保留同时保 220/240 干净对照——严格生产者契约与有用表示保留的权衡

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Financial instruction-data pipelines must distinguish content validity from permission to release a particular dataset. Arya Forge combines typed records, current-content checks, and approval coverage bound to a dataset manifest. We evaluate its release boundaries in a financial-data systems case study. An integrity audit covers 100,000 synthetic instruction records; a separate arithmetic implementation agrees with all 15,000 numeric records across twelve formula families. In 240 scenarios with 2,400 treatment rows, adding financial and release-record checks to a short-instruction filtering recipe rejects all injected numeric faults and policy holds while retaining 220 of 240 clean controls. The twenty lost controls comprise every tested cash-conversion-cycle example. Nineteen persisted-release cases show that eleven selected negative cases are blocked, four equivalent representations are rejected, and changes to external evidence after binding are outside ordinary Gold promotion's checks. Local processing measurements and controlled checkpoint resumptions characterize cost and recovery through 100,000 input rows. The results expose a tradeoff between strict producer-contract enforcement and useful-representation retention, and identify the protected inputs required for manifest-bound approval. These findings apply to the tested implementation and workloads; they do not establish general financial correctness, authenticated approval, expert-validated quality, or downstream model improvement.

</details>

### 174. Natural Jammers: When Non-Robust Features Become Antagonistic

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34785)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自然干扰`、`非鲁棒特征`、`频谱-空间锁定`

👤 **作者**：Jiaming Zhang、Fuyao Zhang、Pengjun Xie、Wei Yang Bryan Lim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：非鲁棒特征通常被视为标准模型在干净数据上的机会捷径——它们也可以是对抗性的：主动误导标准模型处理干净未改图像
- 🔬 **研究方法**：以标准模型与鲁棒模型的分歧为透镜识别 Jammed Set（鲁棒胜标准）；频谱-空间锁定的因果证据+几何位移下的替代悖论
- 📌 **结论**：干扰只在高频分量与特定空间结构精确对齐时出现，破坏锁定可恢复预测；单干扰脆弱但总体干扰率在变换下重分布而非消失

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep neural networks are known to rely on non-robust features that align poorly with human perception. Prior work has largely framed these features as opportunistic shortcuts that help standard models on clean data. We show instead that non-robust cues can be antagonistic: they can act as Natural Jammers that actively mislead standard models on clean, unmodified images. Using disagreement between a standard model and robust models as an analysis lens, we identify a Jammed Set on which robust models outperform standard models. We provide causal evidence for Spectral-spatial Locking: jamming arises only when high-frequency components are precisely aligned with specific spatial structures. Disrupting this lock through spectral filtering or small geometric shifts can restore correct predictions. Crucially, we uncover a Substitution Paradox: while individual jammers are fragile to geometric shifts, the aggregate prevalence of jamming remains approximately stable and is redistributed rather than eliminated under such transformations. Our results reframe natural non-robustness as a failure field over the empirical model-data geometry: one that redistributes rather than disappears under small transformations.

</details>

### 175. Position Matters More Than Channel: Talking a Language Model Out of a Correct Answer

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34809)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`建议位置效应`、`说服因素分解`、`权威归因`

👤 **作者**：Xinyi Ni

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型在系统提示注释/其他 agent 消息/自己先前回答的伴随下答题——把模型劝离正确答案需要什么（位置/作者/已有内容三因素被早期研究混变）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Qwen3.5-27B 四选 MMLU-Pro：各比较固定其余只变一项
- 📌 **结论**：位置远比通道重要且方向相反（裸声明在临答前最有害，带伪理由的声明在问题前最有害——把几乎不错条目错误率 1%→67%）；权威归因与断言语气各提错误 10-27 点；先放正确解答可保护

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language models now answer questions alongside other claims: a note in the system prompt, a message from another agent, their own earlier answer. We ask what it takes to talk a model out of an answer it gets right on its own, and separate three factors that earlier studies vary together: where the advice sits in the prompt, who is said to have written it, and what the model has already written. On Qwen3.5-27B and four-option MMLU-Pro items, with the rest of the advice held fixed in each comparison, we find three things. First, position matters far more than the API channel, in opposite directions for a bare claim and an argument: a bare "the answer is X" does most harm just before the model answers, while the same claim with a false rationale does most harm before the question, where it raises error on items the model otherwise almost never misses from 1% to 67%; moving the claim between the system message and the user message at the same position changes error by a few points. Second, attributing the note to an authority rather than to another participant, and phrasing it assertively, each raise error by 10-27 points, while the two authorities we tested differ by under two. Third, replaying a correct worked answer before the advice protects it whether the text is shown as the model's own or as someone else's, and the same replay keeps wrong answers from being corrected by a bare correct note. The position result replicates under an independently written prompt. Code, items and all result tables are released at this https URL.

</details>

### 176. Silent and Costly: Characterizing and Detecting Prompt-Caching Mis-Integration in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34817)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`提示缓存误集成`、`静默失败`、`静态+LLM检查`

👤 **作者**：Ying Zhang、…、Leo Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：提示缓存降本降延迟——但异构供应商 API 与持续演化的缓存语义使正确集成不易
- 🔬 **研究方法**：首个实证研究：十个流行开源 agent 系统的 120 个提示缓存相关 PR；发现请求侧误配置与读侧用量误计且多数静默失败；CACHELINT 把误集成建模为调用点+正确集成前提条件，CodeQL 定位+供应商能力 oracle 或 LLM 守卫检查 discharged
- 📌 **结论**：识别 120 个标注误集成精度 0.71/召回 0.78——成本与正确性的静默杀手

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) services process massive volumes of user requests, resulting in substantial inference costs. To reduce both cost and latency, providers have introduced prompt caching. However, correctly integrating prompt caching is non-trivial due to heterogeneous provider-specific APIs and continuously evolving caching semantics. To understand prompt-caching mis-integration in downstream applications, we conduct the first empirical study of 120 prompt-caching-related pull requests from 10 popular open-source agent systems. Our findings show that prompt-caching mis-integrations arise in both request-side misconfiguration and read-side usage miscount. More importantly, most mis-integrations fail silently without causing observable runtime failures. Motivated by these findings, we design CACHELINT, which models a prompt-caching mis-integration as an invocation site together with the preconditions required for correct cache integration. CACHELINT locates caching invocation sites with CodeQL and discharges each precondition either against a dated provider capability oracle or with an LLM guard check. CACHELINT achieves 0.71 precision and 0.78 recall in identifying the 120 labeled mis-integrations across the 10 studied agent systems. Our evaluation further demonstrates the effectiveness of CACHELINT in real-world settings, identifying 32 mis-integrations across 571 agent projects with 0.86 precision. These findings have received 21 developer confirmations and led to 18 upstream fixes, including one in Anthropic's official SDK.

</details>

### 177. Collective Emergent Alignment: Measuring and Amplifying Interaction-Induced Reasoning Alignment in Multi-Agent LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34836)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`交互诱发对齐`、`参考蕴含矩阵`、`事实级通信`

👤 **作者**：Boxuan Wang、Zhuoyun Li、Sarvapali D. Ramchurn、Xiaowei Huang、Yi Dong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MAS 经交互涌现行为——交互是否也产生推理对齐的涌现 largely 未探
- 🔬 **研究方法**：REMAP 框架量化交互特异性对齐增益（语义覆盖+交互增益）；事实级多 agent 通信以紧凑缺口感知选择性路由替换全推理轨迹交换
- 📌 **结论**：跨家族两多跳基准广泛正交互增益且与同伴信息恢复关联；紧凑事实交换在对齐与任务上双升且降通信开销——能力不对称的信息转移

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems can exhibit emergent behaviors through interaction, yet whether a similar phenomenon exists for reasoning alignment remains largely unexplored. We study whether agent interaction produces additional reference-grounded reasoning alignment beyond matched individual self-refinement, and introduce REMAP (Reference-Entailment-Matrix for Agentic Process), a framework that quantifies this effect through Semantic Coverage and interaction-specific alignment gains. Across diverse model families and two multi-hop reasoning benchmarks, we observe broadly positive interaction gains, particularly on reasoning content that depends on integrating evidence distributed across agents. These gains are consistently associated with independently measured peer-information recovery, while further analysis reveals composition-dependent heterogeneity effects and asymmetric information transfer across agent capability levels. Motivated by these findings, we further introduce a fact-level multi-agent communication framework that replaces full reasoning-trace exchange with compact, gap-aware, and selectively routed information. Our experiments show that compact fact exchange improves reasoning alignment and task performance while reducing communication overhead, whereas more selective communication exposes a trade-off between acquiring distributed information, retaining local content, and communication efficiency. Overall, our results show that collective emergent alignment can be quantified, linked to information-transfer patterns, and used to guide more effective multi-agent communication.

</details>

### 178. Recovering Black-Box Point Cloud Models from Pose Shifts via Derivative-Free Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34850)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`免梯度恢复`、`姿态偏移`、`置信代理`

👤 **作者**：Seonghwan Park、Min-Gyu Park

- 🎯 **研究动机**：点云模型经仅分数黑盒接口部署——训练分布外的测试时旋转大幅降低性能而等变重训/梯度适配在此访问模型不可行
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CanDO 测试时规范化：无导数优化搜索最大化模型置信的输入变换；识别恢复三条件（训练增广差距/准确对齐的置信代理/多峰目标全局搜索）
- 📌 **结论**：方位失配恢复 76% oracle 差距、任意 SO(3) 51%——冻结仅分数模型的部署后自愈

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Point cloud models are increasingly deployed through score-only black-box interfaces. Under this access model, test-time rotations outside the training distribution can substantially degrade performance. Although existing mitigations such as equivariant retraining and gradient-based adaptation are effective, they are infeasible without access to model weights or gradients. To address this limitation, we propose CanDO, a test-time canonicalization method that recovers lost performance using only forward queries. Specifically, CanDO searches for an input transformation that maximizes model confidence and uses derivative-free optimization to perform this search without gradients. We further identify three conditions for successful recovery: a training augmentation gap, an accuracy-aligned confidence surrogate, and global search over a multi-modal objective. Across multiple architectures and benchmarks, CanDO recovers 76% of the oracle gap under azimuthal misorientation and 51% under arbitrary SO(3) rotations for object part segmentation. These results show that frozen score-only point cloud models can recover from rotation-induced distribution shifts through post-deployment canonicalization alone.

</details>

### 179. Adaptive Retrieval, Selective Answers: Evaluating Conformal Factuality in Agentic RAG

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34872)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`共形事实性`、`agentic RAG`、`覆盖-可用权衡`

👤 **作者**：Muhammad Aimal Rehman、Chi-Kuang Yeh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：共形事实性已在单级与多跳 RAG 研究——agentic RAG（自适应追加检索或提前终止）下的行为未明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：检索相关评分+生成后共形过滤，六模型-数据集配置×100 种子划分×五共形目标级
- 📌 **结论**：95% 目标下非空响应覆盖仅 17.23-60.17%、非空输出全支撑率 89.14-95.56%——可靠性-信息量的权衡随 agent 终止策略变化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve factual grounding without guaranteeing that every generated claim is supported. Previous work has studied claim-level conformal factuality in single-stage RAG and, more recently, multi-hop RAG. We examine its behavior in agentic RAG, where a language-model policy adaptively issues follow-up retrieval queries or terminates retrieval before response generation. We retain the retrieval-based relevance-scoring formulation and post-generation conformal filtering and evaluate six model-dataset configurations: GPT-6 Luna and Llama 3.1 8B on HotpotQA, Natural Questions, and TriviaQA. Each configuration uses 60 fixed questions, 100 seeded 30/30 calibration-test partitions, five conformal target levels, and a no-conformal-prediction baseline. Across the six configurations, higher target levels are accompanied by higher observed full-support rates and reduced answer availability. At the 95% target, non-empty response coverage ranges from 17.23% to 60.17%, claim retention from 10.72% to 43.16%, and full support among non-empty outputs from 89.14% to 95.56%. We formulate the adaptive retrieval trajectory explicitly and characterize the reliability-informativeness trade-off when conformal factuality is applied after agent termination.

</details>

### 180. NumTrace: Auditing the Number Provenance of Financial Analyst Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34887)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`数字来源审计`、`金融agent`、`右数错账`

👤 **作者**：Tianlei Zhu、Matt White、Xue Liu、Xiao-Yang Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：金融 agent 的数值声明可匹配申报中的数字却指错实体/概念/期间/单位/范围——参照答案检查只判错不解释为何
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NumTrace：540 问×2163 份 SEC EDGAR 文档，按会计规则在冻结快照上建参照答案；每个数值声明三独立裁决（值/绑定/支撑）
- 📌 **结论**：保留独立值与绑定裁决检测“右数错账”RNWA：三 agent 失败声明中 67.5-81.8% 是 RNWA——值检查通过的来源错绑

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A numerical claim generated by a financial analyst agent can match a number in a company filing but refer to the wrong entity, concept, period, unit or scope. A check based on reference answers can mark such an answer as wrong but does not explain why it fails. In this paper, we present NumTrace, a new benchmark that audits the provenance of each numerical claim made by financial analyst agents: its source and financial semantics. NumTrace currently has 540 questions over 2,163 SEC EDGAR documents. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first numerical benchmark about who holds a company's shares (Form 13F) and what its executives are owed (Item 402(i)). On a frozen snapshot of these documents, we build the reference answers according to accounting rules. NumTrace then gives each claim three separate verdicts: value, binding and support. By retaining separate value and binding verdicts, NumTrace detects right number, wrong account (RNWA) errors: a claim has an RNWA error when it passes the value check but fails the binding check. The RNWA rate quantifies the gap between value-only and joint value-and-binding pass rates. Finally, we audit three agents, Claude Opus 5.5, GPT-6 Astra and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash. RNWA errors make up 67.5-81.8% of each agent's failing claims. In particular, on 13F co-filing group questions, about 91% of claims pass the value check, but fewer than half pass the binding check.

</details>

### 181. Checkpoint Reload Explains Most of the Measured State Gap in Two State Discovery Reproductions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34891)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`检查点重载`、`状态重建差距`、`复现审计`

👤 **作者**：Weijia Han、Lisha Qu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：训练中维护逐序列潜态的时序模型在推理时须从零重启重建状态——重建态与训练态是否一致
- 🔬 **研究方法**：State Discovery in World Machine 两次复现运行的配对检查点分析：解码态 MSE 高于发布值 27-36%
- 📌 **结论**：用写表的最后纪元权重（而非最佳验证检查点）做 rollout 消除 90-91% 的逐维 Wasserstein 距离——测量差距大部分是检查点重载而非方法

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Temporal models that maintain per-sequence latent states during training must reconstruct those states when the stored table is unavailable at inference. This creates a basic consistency question: do the reconstructed states agree with those formed during training? We study this question for State Discovery in World Machine, which updates stored states in parallel each epoch but performs inference by sequential rollout from an all-zero start. On Toy1D, we analyze two paired-checkpoint runs whose decoded-state MSE is 27 to 36 percent above the published value. We compare the stored table with rollouts under the best-validation checkpoint reloaded after training and the last-epoch weights that wrote the table. Matching the rollout to the table-writing weights removes 90 to 91 percent of the mean per-dimension Wasserstein-1 distance. Causal distillation and frozen SD updates do not satisfy their pre-specified criteria under either checkpoint. Overall, checkpoint reload accounts for most of the measured gap in these runs.

</details>

### 182. What Outcome RL Buys: Finishing, Reliability, Rarely Reach

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34894)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`结果RL分解`、`完成/可靠/可达`、`pass@k`

👤 **作者**：Shan Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：结果 RL 升 pass@1 不升大 k 的 pass@k——每个 pass@1 都在词元预算下评分，增益是行为改变还是新知识
- 🔬 **研究方法**：固定词元预算上把 20 个已发布 RL 模型（1.5-32B）对基座的 pass@1 变化分解为三部分：完成（预算内答完）/可靠（答完且对）/可达（RL 解了基座八次全错的题减去反之）
- 📌 **结论**：基座常撞预算时变化 82% 是完成（给更多词元基座自行闭合大半）；基座词元够时增益是可靠性；可达至多 +2.1 点且 144 道仅 RL 解的物理题重采样后仅 3.5% 站得住——RL 买到完成与可靠很少买到新能力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Outcome RL raises pass@1 but not pass@k at large k (Yue et al., 2025). But every pass@1 is scored under a token budget. Every recipe also trains under one. On physics and math problems at fixed token budgets, we compare the pass@1 of 20 released RL models (1.5B to 32B) with that of the thinking models they started from. We ask whether each pass@1 gain is changed behaviour or new knowledge. We divide each change in pass@1 into three parts. Finishing measures answering before the budget runs out. Reliability measures being right once finished. Reach measures problems RL solves that its base answered wrongly in all eight tries, minus problems the base solves that RL answered wrongly in all eight. We find four things. (1) Where the base often hits the token budget before answering, most of the change, gain or loss, is finishing (82% at 4k and 8k). Given more tokens, the base closes most of the finishing gap itself. (2) Where the base has enough tokens, the gain is reliability: the RL model is graded right more often, even on problems both finish. (3) By our definition, RL rarely solves what its base cannot. Reach adds at most 2.1 percentage points on any of our four physics and math sets. We checked 144 physics problems that only the RL model solved. Of these, 3.5% hold up when resampled or read. In a random sample, nine of ten new RL steps a human confirmed also appear in the base's samples. (4) In a small-scale RL run on an 8B model, the gain on new problems is finishing, with reliability flat. Supplying each problem's physical setup was not shown to improve on plain RL.

</details>

### 183. PhysEvolve: Verifier-grounded Self-evolution for Physical Reasoning with Executable Worlds

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34921)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`验证器接地自进化`、`可执行世界`、`反事实分支`

👤 **作者**：Jinyuan Hu、Yilei Jiang、Chen Tang、Minghong Cai、Xinran Liu、Xiangyu Yue

- 🎯 **研究动机**：物理推理受限于接地的反事实训练数据稀缺（观测视频只记录发生的事实轨迹）——自进化范式需能验证模型提议环境与干预结果的外部真值源
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PhysEvolve：共享 VLM 提议可执行世界程序与证据模式，确定性物理引擎冻结有效世界、仿真匹配基线与身体消融反事实分支、合成离散答案绑定视觉任务；精确验证器反馈经 GRPO 更新策略与自适应课程
- 📌 **结论**：从 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 出发的验证器接地物理推理自进化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Physical reasoning underpins how embodied systems understand and act in the physical world, yet vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable on questions about dynamics and physical causality. Progress is critically constrained by the scarcity of grounded counterfactual training data, as observational videos capture only the factual trajectory that occurred. The self-evolving paradigm offers a route beyond fixed datasets by enabling models to construct their own training problems. Extending this paradigm to physics, however, requires an external source of truth that can both validate model-proposed environments and verify alternative physical outcomes under intervention. We introduce \physevolve{}, a verifier-grounded self-evolving framework that trains VLMs for causal physical reasoning from model-proposed executable worlds and simulation-authored tasks. At each round, a shared VLM proposes executable world programs and evidence schemas; a deterministic physics engine freezes each valid world, simulates matched baseline and body-elided counterfactual branches, and synthesizes discrete, answer-bound visual tasks. Exact verifier feedback from task solving updates both the shared policy via GRPO and an adaptive curriculum over physical intents. Starting from Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, \physevolve{} achieves targeted gains across PhysBench, CausalPhys, and ContPhy without human intervention data, improving PhysBench Throwing by $8.8$ points and ContPhy Counterfactual Removal and Goal-Pit by $7.4$ and $24.4$ points. These gains concentrate on interaction-sensitive and altered-world questions, demonstrating zero-shot transfer of causal principles to unseen complex dynamics (cloth, fluid, soft bodies) and real-world video.

</details>

### 184. Attack logics, not outputs: Concept-based Property Attacks for Shortcut Elimination in Neural Networks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34923)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`概念级属性攻击`、`逻辑不一致`、`捷径消除`

👤 **作者**：Raik Dankworth、Felix Mächtle、Thomas Eisenbarth、Gesina Schwalbe

- 🎯 **研究动机**：当前对抗攻击只证伪最终输出类（翻 stop_sign 标签）——鲁棒性分析应聚焦概念级推理的逻辑不一致
- 🔬 **研究方法**：概念属性形式化（人可读概念上的约束如 red∧octagonal⇒stop_sign）并以其证伪为原则化攻击；C-XAI 技术实现并证明概念属性攻击期望搜索空间更小
- 📌 **结论**：攻击逻辑而非输出——概念级证伪为局部鲁棒性保证与对抗训练提供更细粒度基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deep neural networks (NNs) for computer vision are vulnerable to adversarial attacks, such that minuscule malicious changes to inputs may induce unintuitive outputs. One key approach to verify and mitigate such robustness issues is to falsify expected output behavior, which enables local robustness guarantees and adversarial training. Due to the black-box nature of NNs, current attacks only falsify a class of the final output, such as flipping from $\text{\texttt{stop_sign}}$ to $\neg \text{\texttt{stop_sign}}$. In this work we argue that robustness analysis should focus more generally on logical inconsistencies in concept-level reasoning rather than solely on output misclassification. We formalize this perspective through concept-based properties, i.e., constraints over human-interpretable concepts such as $\text{\texttt{red}} \land \text{\texttt{octogonal}} \implies \text{\texttt{stop_sign}}$ and study the falsification of such properties as a principled form of adversarial attack. Furthermore, an implementation of concept-based properties on already trained NNs is proposed using techniques from concept-based explainable artificial intelligence (C-XAI). For that we formally prove that attacks on concept-based properties are expected to have a reduced search space compared to simple class falsification, whilst arguably be more aligned with intuitive robustness targets. The practical effectiveness of our approach for logically informed adversarial training is shown in an empirical study across multiple models and datasets, including both vision and a security-relevant code processing task. Our results demonstrate that concept-level falsification generalizes classical adversarial attacks: Retraining on our novel attacks maintains robustness gains as compared to classical adversarial retraining whilst better eliminating semantic biases.

</details>

### 185. How Do Coding-Agent Harnesses Self-Evolve? An Empirical Study of Retrospective Adaptation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34938)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness自进化实证`、`回顾性适应`、`变更有效性`

👤 **作者**：Christoph Treude、Sebastian Baltes

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 可基于早前任务经验改编 harness——这些改编是否改善后续任务行为不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：研究 RETRO 技能：agent 评审完成会话并提 harness 变更；Claude Code×30 个 SWE-bench 问题，定性编码 125 个提案、实施 24 个、12 个变更在有/无变更的新会话上评估
- 📌 **结论**：七次目标问题出现中两次消除目标动作、一次减少、四次无减少且部分变更给无关议题加活——harness 自进化需要确立变更是否有效的机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Coding agents can adapt their harnesses, the software, configuration, and instructions used to run them, based on experience from earlier tasks, but whether these adaptations improve their behavior on later tasks remains unclear. We study \retro, a skill that asks an agent to review a completed session and propose harness changes, and ask what changes it proposes and whether implemented changes reduce repeated work on later tasks. We ran Claude Code on 30 SWE-bench Verified issues, qualitatively coded the 125 proposals from \retro, implemented 24, and evaluated 12 harness changes on later issues from the same repositories in fresh sessions with and without each change. Most proposals referred to an event in the session, but 18 of 43 lint-related proposals recommended a practice regardless of what had happened. Among the seven evaluations in which sessions without the change encountered the targeted problem, two changes eliminated the targeted action, one reduced its occurrence, four showed no reduction, and some changes added work on unrelated issues. Harness self-evolution requires more than generating and implementing changes: it needs ways to establish whether a change helps on later tasks and to revise or remove changes that do not.

</details>

### 186. Intellectual Property Protection of Hyperdimensional Computing Classifiers: Black-Box Ownership Verification via Model Watermarking

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34942)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`超维计算水印`、`所有权验证`、`HDC`

👤 **作者**：Hamed Poursiami、…、Maryam Parsa

- 🎯 **研究动机**：超维计算为资源受限系统提供高效学习推理——其模型所有权验证未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：首个 HDC 模型水印系统研究：后门嵌入适配三种原型学习机制分类器；四基准+神经网络参照，检查水印可学性、准确率代价与修改/复制下的存活
- 📌 **结论**：错误驱动 AdaptHD/OnlineHD 在 1% 触发暴露下验证且准确损失 ≤0.58 点；提取产生无水印代理而覆盖写入在 HDC 中比 NN 更易侵蚀水印

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Hyperdimensional computing (HDC) offers efficient learning and inference for resource-constrained systems, yet ownership verification of its models remains underexplored. We present the first systematic investigation of model watermarking in HDC by adapting backdoor-based embedding to three classifiers with different prototype-learning mechanisms. Across four benchmarks, with conventional neural networks as references, we examine whether watermarks can be learned without compromising task accuracy and whether they survive subsequent model modification and replication. The error-driven classifiers, AdaptHD and OnlineHD, support verification at a nominal trigger exposure of 1% with at most 0.58 percentage points of mean accuracy loss, whereas single-pass Vanilla HDC requires greater exposure and can incur substantial accuracy costs. The error-driven models retain verification under native continued training, but extraction produces surrogates that reproduce much of the task behavior without verifiable watermarks. Watermark overwriting readily embeds attacker watermarks into HDC prototypes and erodes the owner's watermark more strongly than in the neural network references, although the strongest erosion coincides with large accuracy losses. We further deploy watermarked AdaptHD on an FPGA and stress its execution through undervolting and overclocking. Verification survives substantial computation errors, whereas more severe stress either causes verification failure together with accuracy collapse or results in inference timeouts. These findings establish the feasibility of watermarking in HDC while identifying limitations in retention under extraction and sensitivity to overwriting.

</details>

### 187. When Pointing Becomes Profiling: Social Bias and Refusal in Text-Steerable Visual Encoders

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35017)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`文本可转向视觉编码器`、`人口画像`、`无法拒绝`

👤 **作者**：Seyedarmin Azizi、Parsa Razmara、Sajjad Ghiasvand、Ardalan Aryashad、Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：文本可转向视觉编码器按 NL 提示指向匹配图像区域——审计 SteerViT 发现其转向界面把语言驱动指向变成人口画像
- 🔬 **研究方法**：反事实人脸马赛克与真实多人照片：位置基率之上的选择偏斜、最后两个交叉注意力层的因果定位（仅给这两层提示即复现种族效应）
- 📌 **结论**：“这个人”超基率选男人、犯罪词选黑人超职业词、“四女中的男人”75.6% 选黑人女性、“猴子”选黑人；模型无法拒绝——patch softmax 必须把质量放某处，四个其他接地模型无犯罪路由且种族路由更弱

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Text-steerable visual encoders condition a frozen Vision Transformer on a natural-language prompt and point at the matching image region, producing prompt-conditioned heatmaps and features. We audit the released SteerViT model and find that its steering interface turns language-driven pointing into demographic profiling. On counterfactual face mosaics and real multi-person photographs, "the person" selects a man more often than the base rate, crime words select Black people more often than occupation words do, "the man" among four women selects the Black woman 75.6% of the time on SocialCounterfactuals, and "the monkey" selects Black people. None of them is in the backbone's unsteered saliency, and the crime association is not in the frozen text encoder; the race effects grow with steering strength and enter through the last two cross-attention layers: giving the prompt to those layers alone reproduces them. Four other grounding models, CLIPSeg, Grounding-DINO, SAM 3, and Qwen2.5-VL, show no crime routing and weaker race routing. The model cannot refuse. Its patch softmax must place its mass somewhere, and its heatmap separates a groundable request ("the police officer") from one that is absent ("the pizza") or that would require a stereotyped demographic inference ("the criminal") only at chance (AUROC 0.52). We introduce SafeSteer, which adds a learned abstention output and counterfactual-invariance training on the same 23M steering parameters. SafeSteer separates should-point from should-abstain prompts at AUROC 0.997, generalizes to held-out sensitive and absent words (0.998; at a 0.5 threshold it refuses 99.8% of trained and 62% of held-out sensitive words) with no over-refusal of visible people on the benchmark, and removes most race routing while keeping localization and retrieval within 1 point of the released model. Code, benchmarks, and the curated mosaic sets will be released.

</details>

### 188. Synthetic Child-Adult Interactions Are Not Developmentally Plausible

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35029)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`合成儿童对话`、`发展合理性`、`真实数据微调`

👤 **作者**：Bastian Bunzeck、Francesca Padovani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：合成数据已成 LM 训练常态并渗入发展合理性语言建模研究——LM 生成的儿童-成人交互是否真像自然交互
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TinyDialogues 与自然对话的广泛语言学度量对比；真实对话创建数据指令微调的试点研究
- 📌 **结论**：合成数据词法级匹配但大幅低估口语交互的杂乱碎片性；小模型经适度参数高效微调可逼近自然数据统计倾向

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Synthetic data has become a common resource for training language models, and this trend has spilled over into research on developmentally plausible language modeling. However, it remains unclear whether LM-generated child-adult interactions actually resemble natural ones. We compare the synthetic TinyDialogues dataset to natural dialogue across a broad range of linguistic measures. While synthetic data matches natural data on the lexical level, it drastically underestimates the messy, fragmented nature of spoken interaction. To mitigate this, we propose instruction-tuning with data created from real dialogues, and show in a pilot study that a small model approximates the statistical tendencies of natural data after moderate parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>

### 189. Risk-Aware Output Control Should be the Focus of Machine Unlearning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35052)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`输出控制遗忘`、`统计保证`、`立场`

👤 **作者**：Yahya Alkhatib、Yuyang Xiao、Wee Peng Tay

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器遗忘多瞄准参数空间不可区分性（尽量接近从头重训）——与实用及用户关切错位
- 🔬 **研究方法**：论证 MU 应优先统计保证的输出空间控制：支撑严格鲁棒目标、扩展到现代部署、使保证可解释；实证与近期理论佐证
- 📌 **结论**：带统计保证的输出控制是操作上有意义的遗忘概念——遗忘目标的重定位

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Well-documented failure modes of machine learning (ML) systems, including bias, toxicity, hallucination, and leakage of sensitive information, has driven the rise of machine unlearning (MU). Most existing work, however, targets parameter-space indistinguishability, seeking unlearned models that are as close as possible to models retrained from scratch on retained data. In this paper, we argue that this objective is misaligned with practical and user-relevant concerns: MU should prioritize statistically guaranteed output-space control rather than parameter-space similarity. We contend that output control with statistical guarantees is the operationally meaningful notion of unlearning, as it supports rigorous and robust objectives, scales to modern deployment settings, and enables interpretable guarantees. We substantiate this position with empirical evidence and with recent theoretical results that, although not originally formulated in this framing, are consistent with it.

</details>

### 190. Reinforcement Learning Done Right for Explicit Confidence Targets

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35059)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`显式置信目标`、`RL弃答`、`奖励信号保持`

👤 **作者**：Zefeng He、Yichen He、Yuan Lin、Yang Yang、Yu Cheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：训练按置信弃答的 RL 多用固定错误惩罚——推理时无法适配不同置信要求；显式目标 p 下单模型学不出区分（区分 p 的奖励信号在优化中丢失且 p→1 时惩罚无界）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RLEC：保持区分置信目标所需学习信号+稳定奖励族（期望答案条件不变）+重采样
- 📌 **结论**：单模型跨不同置信目标达标——按用户指定精度的自适应弃答

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) can produce plausible but incorrect answers when they are uncertain, which can mislead users and undermine model reliability. Training models to abstain with reinforcement learning is a natural approach, but most existing methods use a fixed error penalty and therefore cannot adapt their behavior to different confidence requirements at inference time. We study \emph{explicit confidence targets}, where a user specifies a target \(p\) in the prompt and the model directly chooses whether to answer or abstain, with the goal of achieving an accuracy of at least \(p\) among answered questions. However, learning to respond to different confidence targets with a single model has proven difficult, and prior attempts have failed to distinguish different targets. We show that this failure comes from two problems: the reward signal that distinguishes different values of $p$ is often lost during optimization, while the original error penalty grows without bound as $p$ approaches one. We propose RLEC (\textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning with \textbf{E}xplicit \textbf{C}onfidence Targets), which preserves the learning signal needed to distinguish confidence targets, introduces a stable reward family that keeps the desired answer condition unchanged, and uses reward scheduling to balance effective learning and training stability. Together, these designs enable the first successful training of a single LLM for explicit confidence target control. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and model scales show that RLEC largely preserves general capabilities and achieves competitive calibration performance. RLEC also supports fine-grained target control without an external decision system or threshold calibration after training. By making explicit confidence targets part of the trained policy, RLEC opens a new and more direct path toward behavioral calibration.

</details>

### 191. Beyond Single-Attribute Auditing: A Configurable Multi-Attribute Benchmark for Intersectional Fairness in LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35069)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`交叉公平审计`、`多属性基准`、`非可加交互`

👤 **作者**：Zheng Zhang、…、Baosheng Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：公平基准一次审一个敏感属性——单属性范式漏掉多属性非可加交互产生边际评测不可见的交叉伤害
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ComFair：47.9 万受控双/多属性实例并与任务等价源配对；四任务型（识别/选择/续写/对话）统一审计偏移、新增属性引入新错或掩盖旧错、模型排名稳定性；十个 LLM
- 📌 **结论**：判别任务在聚合变化不大时也暴露双向转变，生成任务偏移更大且方向不对称，排名随属性组合变化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated for fairness, yet most existing benchmarks audit one sensitive attribute at a time. This single-attribute paradigm can miss intersectional harms, where multiple attributes interact non-additively and produce failures that are invisible under marginal evaluation. We introduce ComFair, a configurable benchmark that converts existing fairness data into 479,066 controlled Dual- and Multi-attribute instances and pairs every augmented instance with its task-equivalent source. Across Recognition, Selection, Continuation, and Conversation, this pairing supports a unified audit of performance or bias-score shifts, how often added attributes introduce new errors or mask existing ones, and model-ranking stability. Experiments on ten LLMs show task- and subgroup-dependent sensitivity: discriminative tasks expose both transition directions even when aggregate changes are modest, generative tasks exhibit larger and directionally asymmetric shifts, and rankings vary with attribute composition. ComFair therefore provides a scalable controlled sensitivity audit---rather than a model of the natural joint distribution of identities---for testing the robustness of fairness-benchmark conclusions beyond single-attribute settings.

</details>

### 192. Uncertainty Evaluation to Support Safety of the Intended Functionality Analysis for Determining the Applicability of Performance Targets Across Triggering Conditions in ML-Based LiDAR Object Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35092)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`SOTIF`、`触发条件迁移`、`性能目标适用性`

👤 **作者**：Milin Patel、Rolf Jung

- 🎯 **研究动机**：ISO 21448 的预期功能安全分析要求 ML 检测器在每个触发条件下满足性能目标——一条件下选的阈值只对该分布有统计保证
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把阈值迁移变成触发条件：以声明错误概率判定目标在该处适用/不适用/未定，下界计数适用结论所需独立单元；PointPillars 集成于 KITTI/nuScenes 按记录序列计数
- 📌 **结论**：按序列计数两数据集对任何目标限都不够独立单元；按帧计数则在远距/雨/跨检测器迁移得到预设限下的不适用结论——SOTIF 的不确定性评估框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

For safety of the intended functionality (SOTIF) analysis according to ISO 21448, a machine learning object detector for light detection and ranging (LiDAR) point clouds can meet a performance target, a limit on the mean per-frame share of wrong detections among accepted detections, by rejecting detections above an uncertainty threshold. A threshold selected on data of one condition carries a statistical guarantee only for data distributed like its selection data, whereas SOTIF analysis covers every triggering condition. This paper presents an uncertainty evaluation in which the threshold is transferred into a triggering condition, a test decides at a stated error probability whether the target is applicable there, not applicable or not determined, and a lower bound counts the independent units an applicable outcome requires. For PointPillars ensembles on KITTI and nuScenes, counted in recorded sequences, as the dependence between frames requires, neither dataset held enough independent units for any target limit, and no transfer had a not applicable outcome. Counted in frames, the same data gave not applicable outcomes at limits fixed in advance after transfers into far distance, into rain and between two separately trained detectors, thresholds that met the target limit after a transfer rejected most correct detections, and in rain the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve did not decrease. A performance target met under one condition therefore counts as evidence under a triggering condition only after a test there, reported with its share of accepted correct detections and its number of independent recorded sequences.

</details>

### 193. CalibraRL: Confidence-Calibrated Reinforcement Learning for Mitigating Hallucinations in RAG

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35142)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`置信校准RL`、`幻觉缓解`、`Beta后验弃答奖励`

👤 **作者**：Qianchi Zhang、Hainan Zhang、Liang Pang、Hongwei Zheng、Zhiming Zheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：固定结果奖励不区分不同置信的错误也不考虑查询间可靠性差异——惩罚不足过度自信错误且不抑制不必要弃答
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CalibraRL：非弃答响应结合正确性效用与 Brier 惩罚（置信-正确一致）；弃答用组内非弃答正确性的 Beta 后验估条件可靠性
- 📌 **结论**：复用训练 rollout 无需额外“模型是否知道”标注，三 QA 数据集上更优的幻觉-弃答权衡

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) often produce plausible but incorrect answers, motivating reinforcement learning methods that reward correct answers, penalize incorrect answers, and allow abstention. However, fixed outcome-based rewards do not distinguish incorrect responses with different confidence levels or account for differences in answering reliability across queries. Consequently, such rewards may inadequately penalize overconfident errors and fail to discourage unnecessary abstention. We propose CalibraRL, a reinforcement learning framework that incorporates response confidence and query-level answering evidence into reward design. For non-abstaining responses, CalibraRL combines correctness-based utility with a Brier-based penalty that encourages agreement between confidence and correctness. For abstentions, it estimates conditional answering reliability using a Beta posterior derived from the correctness of non-abstaining responses within each sampled group. The abstention reward increases with the posterior probability that the model's accuracy on non-abstaining responses is below a predefined threshold. CalibraRL reuses training rollouts without requiring additional annotations indicating whether the model knows the answer to each question. Experiments on three QA datasets demonstrate that CalibraRL improves answer precision and reduces hallucinations compared with strong baselines. Further analysis shows that CalibraRL reduces mean expected calibration error (ECE) by 30.8\% across two backbone models and three datasets, highlighting its potential to improve the reliability of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in web search.

</details>

### 194. Temporal Slice Learning for AI-Generated Video Detection with 400$\times$ Fewer FLOPs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35148)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI视频检测`、`时间切片`、`400倍省算`

👤 **作者**：Wei Shang、Zhiyuan Li、Shengfeng He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成视频检测依赖微弱且空间局部的时间不一致而大部分内容冗余——重骨干建模全视频成本高难扩展
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把视频重构为紧凑时间切片表示：信息性空间行列随时间聚合为切片域输入，高低频切片暴露互补时间伪影（不稳定细节与异常运动平滑）
- 📌 **结论**：轻量时间切片一致性网络以 400 倍少 FLOP 保持检测力——表示视角的 AIGC 视频检测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent advances in generative video models have improved visual realism, making detection increasingly dependent on subtle temporal inconsistencies. However, these signals are weak and spatially localized, while most of the video content is redundant. This creates a fundamental mismatch: existing detectors rely on heavy backbones to model full videos, which leads to high computational cost and limited scalability. In this work, we rethink AI-generated video detection from a representation perspective. Instead of modeling entire videos, we restructure them into compact temporal slice representations that isolate informative temporal evolution. We introduce a lightweight framework using frequency-aware temporal slice consistency learning, where informative spatial rows and columns are aggregated over time to form slice-domain inputs. This reparameterization suppresses redundant appearance while preserving discriminative temporal dynamics, enabling efficient and targeted detection. By constructing slices from both high- and low-frequency regions, our method exposes complementary temporal artifacts, including unstable details and abnormal motion smoothness. We instantiate this idea in a Temporal Slice Consistency Network, which integrates task-driven slice localization, hierarchical positional encoding, and a lightweight Transformer to model cross-slice and cross-frequency dependencies. We further introduce AI-Artist, a new benchmark with artist-curated videos from recent high-fidelity generators, including Seedance and Kling. Experiments on GenVideo and AI-Artist show that our method achieves strong performance while requiring over 400$\times$ fewer model-forward FLOPs and 3.6$\times$ faster model inference than the strong video-based baseline ReStraV (2.9$\times$ faster end to end, including preprocessing), enabling scalable and practical deployment.

</details>

### 195. LongSearchEval: Evaluating Long-Horizon Web Search Capabilities of Large Language Models via a Trajectory-Aware Agent Framework with Failure-Node Localization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35150)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`长程搜索评测`、`轨迹感知`、`失败节点定位`

👤 **作者**：Jialiang Liu、Shusheng Li、Wenjun Tan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长程 Web 搜索评测多靠模拟或事后重建轨迹而非保留的真实交互痕迹——行为评估保真度与细粒度错误定位受限
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LONGSEARCHEVAL：统一执行协议+来源受限结果验证+回顾性失败节点定位；16 模型×100 任务=1600 保留运行与 6545 自动标记失败节点（累计 1037+318 小时运行时）
- 📌 **结论**：一阶段无金答案对照 agent 引用网页验证答案组，二阶段把失败节点链接到错误答案组——证据获取-验证-使用的全链路评估

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-horizon web search requires LLM-based agents to integrate information across web pages through extended interactions under task-specific constraints. Evaluating this capability is important not only for measuring final answer quality, but also for assessing whether an agent acquires, verifies, and uses evidence reliably over extended interactions. Existing evaluations, however, mainly rely on simulated trajectories or on post-hoc trajectories reconstructed from final answers, rather than on retained real interaction traces, which limits the fidelity of behavioral assessment and weakens support for fine-grained error localization. To address this gap, we introduce LONGSEARCHEVAL, a trajectory-aware agent evaluation framework that combines a unified agent execution protocol with source constrained outcome verification and retrospective failure-node localization. Stage 1 verifies answer groups against agent-cited webpages without human-authored gold answers; Stage 2 audits normalized rounds and links flagged failure nodes to incorrect answer groups. We evaluate 16 models from eight families on 100 tasks, yielding 1,600 retained runs and 6,545 automatically flagged failure nodes. Trajectory collection and evaluation required 1,037 and 318 hours of cumulative runtime, respectively. Human validation yields 94.50% agreement on supplied answer-group labels and 98.11% feature-correspondence precision for audited round-group links. Together, outcome scores and linked trajectory evidence support controlled model comparison and retrospective inspection of answer-related content.

</details>

### 196. SoK: Timestamps Are Not a Time Model: Rethinking Temporal Reasoning in Host-Based Intrusion Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35151)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`主机入侵检测`、`时间模型SoK`、`证据假设`

👤 **作者**：Rene Heinzl、Markus Nissl

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基于主机的入侵检测器按时间戳排序事件/开窗/排攻击阶段——每个结论都依赖时钟/交付/实体身份/证据完整性假设而大多未声明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：借时序数据库/流处理/运行时验证蒸馏时间模型五组件+证据假设清单+四级保证；系统化十五个检测调查族并审十一 KRR 形式化对九攻击模式
- 📌 **结论**：早期告警相关器声明时钟交付假设、取证测时钟误差、当前基于 provenance 的检测器不声明任何时钟界——时间戳不是时间模型

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Host-based intrusion detectors order events, window audit streams and sequence attack stages by their timestamps. Every conclusion they draw therefore rests on assumptions about clocks, delivery, entity identity and the integrity of the evidence, and most of these assumptions go unstated. This Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) makes them explicit. Drawing on temporal databases, stream processing and runtime verification, we distill five components of a time model, together with a checklist of evidence assumptions and four levels of guarantee, and walk through a running campaign with them. A documented search yields fifteen families of detection and investigation. For papers selected by screening role and period, plus named systems and landmarks, we check the stated assumptions in the full texts, and we assess eleven temporal knowledge representation and reasoning (KRR) formalisms and a learned baseline on nine attack patterns. Some early alert correlators stated clock and delivery assumptions, and forensics measures clock error. Current provenance-based detectors state no clock bound. Four audited papers describe input-ordering conditions or conventions, but none specifies conditions for conclusion validity across a stated class of delivery failures. In the selected provenance-based systems, campaign composition uses system-specific procedures or objectives, learned state, causal graph queries or language models, and beyond CEP-based query systems, we found no temporal rule language or stream reasoner from KRR applied to host audit data. In 28 of 38 audited provenance-era detector papers, capture or stored evidence is trusted or its manipulation is placed out of scope, and none says which conclusions survive deleted, delayed or forged records. Five open challenges follow from these gaps.

</details>

### 197. Trainable Boundary-Keeping Where No Formal Answer Exists: A Controlled Knock-In Study of Reward Training in Evidence-Sufficiency Judgment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35226)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`边界保持训练`、`证据充分性判断`、`无形式答案域`

👤 **作者**：YaJie Yin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推理调优与奖励训练模型常被假设验证更可靠——在无可判定正确性奖励的证据充分性判断域做受控敲入检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预注册同基座对照：边界保持配方（SFT 后 GRPO）vs 自身基线 vs 打乱配对安慰剂 vs 通用推理模型，无评估邀请的动作压力协议；承重结果由两臂盲模型评议会评定
- 📌 **结论**：训练臂把自发正确拒绝从 25% 提到 100%（安慰剂崩溃至 5%），281 个良构声明自动误拒仅 1.1%——边界特异而非一揽子拒绝：无形式答案域的可训练边界保持

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reasoning-tuned and reward-trained models are often assumed to verify more reliably because they reason longer or receive correctness signals. We test this in evidence-sufficiency judgment: deciding whether available information actually supports a causal claim, a domain with no decidable correctness reward. Using a pre-registered, same-base, controlled knock-in design, we compare a boundary-keeping recipe (SFT then GRPO) with its own baseline, a scrambled-pairing placebo, and a general-purpose reasoning model under an action-pressure protocol with no evaluation invitation. All load-bearing outcomes are rated by two separate model-rating sessions masked to arm labels; automatic judges only trigger. The trained arm raises spontaneous correct refusal from 25% to 100% (40 per arm; Wilson 95% CI lower bound 91.2%, non-overlapping with the baseline interval [14.2%, 40.2%]), while the scrambled placebo collapses (5% spontaneous / 7.5% invited correct; 91.1% false refusal). The effect is boundary-specific rather than blanket refusal: automatic false refusal on 281 well-formed claims is 1.1% (Wilson upper bound 3.09%), and a targeted 18-item arm-(c) recheck (3 flagged plus 15 accepted) yields 0/18 and 1/18 false refusals in two sessions. The dissociation is the main result: final boundary-keeping is trainable without an inference-time verifier, while observed active numeric/calculation checking in the H3 assay remains near zero; SFT saturation makes the GRPO increment not separately identifiable. A pre-registered mechanism adjudication favors reward internalization of final-action status (H2, best-supported, not proven). The scope is a controlled 14B same-family study: we do not claim proof, cross-family generality, or independent human-expert validation; the rating sessions are model sessions, not human reviewers.

</details>

### 198. Identities, Not Pairs: Distribution-Free False-Accept Control for Embedding-Based Verification

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35277)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`嵌入验证假接受`、`身份交换性`、`分布自由控制`

👤 **作者**：Salim Khazem

- 🎯 **研究动机**：嵌入验证常从 m 身份的全部冒名对校准假接受阈值——但这些对共享身份（hub 身份对人群分数高时校准失效）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：刻画最坏极限并提 COVE：把试判对排入加入探针与参照后的全部 N 对，身份交换性下边际 FAR ≤⌊αN⌋/N
- 📌 **结论**：六基准八冻结编码器上实际 FAR 与精确界一致，两有效基线都不接受处 COVE 保留采样 oracle 真接受率中位 100%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Embedding-based verification often calibrates a false-accept threshold from all impostor pairs among $m$ identities, although these pairs share identities. We characterize a worst-case limit: when hub identities score highly against a fraction $\rho$ of the population, a calibration-only threshold with marginal false-accept rate (FAR) at most $\alpha$ under every identity-exchangeable law reaches the hub score on hub-free calibration sets with probability at most $(m+2)\alpha/(2\rho)$. Uniformly accepting near that score at levels well below $\rho/m$ therefore requires trial information. We propose COVE, which ranks the trial pair among all $N=\binom{m+2}{2}$ pairs formed by augmenting the calibration units with the probe and reference.
