Under identity exchangeability, its marginal FAR is at most $\lfloor\alpha N\rfloor/N$, with first possible acceptance at $1/N=O(1/m^2)$. The rule also retains this guarantee with a fixed symmetric cohort-normalized score using an independent cohort. On 6 benchmarks and 8 frozen encoders, realised FAR was consistent with the exact bound. Where both valid calibration-only baselines accepted nothing, COVE retained a median $100\%$ of the sampled oracle's true-accept rate over reliable comparisons. In contrast, an empirical quantile over dependent pairs reached 2.87$\,\alpha$ FAR, and a binomial upper confidence bound over pairs missed its nominal 95\% conditional-FAR target in up to 55\% of calibration draws. Symmetric CSLS raised COVE's true-accept rate by up to 4.3-fold in the tested settings. The code is available in this https URL

</details>

### 199. Did the Model Use That? Counterfactual Training Boosts Self-Attribution in Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35295)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自归因`、`反事实训练`、`外部信息影响`

👤 **作者**：Zikai Zhou、Jiacheng Sang、Yilin Xu、Yufei Jin、Monica S. Lam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可解释可问责的 LM 须揭示外部信息如何影响其答案——模型是否自归因（当且仅当回应受所供信息影响时表明）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CREDIT 用反事实标签直接监督终答自归因（无需人工标注）；十一个前沿模型大多不归因有影响的信息或归因无影响的信息
- 📌 **结论**：把致谢训练进推理不解决问题（致谢到不了终答）；CREDIT 大幅改善四个开源模型的自归因并在与用户建议一致的答案中区分被建议改变与本来就会给——可迁移

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A fundamental challenge for explainable and accountable language models is revealing how external information influences their answers. This paper examines whether models are self-attributive: that is, whether they indicate when -- and only when -- their responses to questions are influenced by information provided alongside the question. Furthermore, we explore how self-attribution can be improved through counterfactual training, which explicitly teaches models to distinguish between answers that do and do not depend on supplied information. We found that most of the eleven frontier models rarely attribute influential information in their answers, and the rest attribute non-influential information. Training acknowledgment into reasoning does not fix this: the acknowledgment does not reach the final answer. We introduce CREDIT, which directly supervises final-answer self-attribution with counterfactual labels, without the need for human annotation. CREDIT substantially improves attribution in four open models, including two distilled reasoning models, and outperforms reasoning-level fine-tuning and prompting baselines. Among answers that agree with a user's suggestion, CREDIT distinguishes those changed by the suggestion from those the model would have given anyway. Gains transfer partly to unseen information types and prompts containing sources that support conflicting answers.

</details>

### 200. RSIV-Harness: A Recursive Self-Improvement Harness Framework for Open-Domain Conversational Text-to-Visualization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35316)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RSI harness`、`开放域文本可视化`、`锚点检查`

👤 **作者**：Jiawei Lu、Chen Zhang、Jinwei Lu、Yuanwei Song、Yuanfeng Song、Raymond Chi-Wing Wong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：开放域对话式文本转可视化需求模糊且多轮演化而静态 harness 不随复现失败改变
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RSIV-Harness：冻结 LM 权重，RSI 从 agent 自身失败轨迹修订外围 harness；四层框架经锚点与记忆检查评估修订，通过则精炼否则恢复最佳已验证 harness；ODAC-Vis 640 任务
- 📌 **结论**：灵活自改进与稳定性的平衡——RSI 应用于可视化 harness

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Users often request charts without knowing which database or table holds the relevant data. Such requests may be ambiguous, and users may ask follow-up questions to deepen their analysis or iteratively refine existing charts. A useful assistant must therefore discover relevant data sources among heterogeneous databases, ask clarifying questions when needed, and track evolving user intent while preserving constraints established in earlier turns. Yet recent text-to-visualization agents typically rely on static agent harnesses that are predefined and remain unchanged despite recurring failures across tasks. To address this limitation, we introduce RSIV-Harness, which keeps the language model weights fixed and applies Recursive Self-Improvement to revise the surrounding harness from the agent's own failure traces. To balance flexible self-improvement with stability, we introduce a four-layer framework that evaluates proposed harness revisions through anchor and memory checks, refining those that pass and restoring the best validated harness otherwise. We further introduce ODAC-Vis, a benchmark with 640 tasks for Open-Domain, Ambiguous, and Conversational text-to-visualization that jointly evaluates open-domain data discovery, ambiguity resolution, multi-turn visualization refinement, and executable chart generation. Experiments show that a single round of RSIV-Harness improves all six evaluation metrics relative to the seed harness, with particularly large gains in executability (+28.02 points) and chart structure (+34.01 points), demonstrating the ability to learn from task-solving experience and turn recurring failures across tasks into substantial performance improvements.

</details>

### 201. Constrained Decoding: Harmful or Beneficial? Revisiting Its Impact on LLM Performance

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35328)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`约束解码`、`三类约束分解`、`因果消融`

👤 **作者**：Ngoc Trinh Hung Nguyen、Alonso Silva、Armen Aghasaryan、Mehwish Alam

- 🎯 **研究动机**：约束解码常被假设天然损害推理——经三类约束（格式/有效性/推理）分解重检
- 🔬 **研究方法**：理论证明恰当约束可通过消除无效路径保持或改善表现；四家族十一开源模型多样基准的因果消融
- 📌 **结论**：表观退化主要来自非等价提示与输出格式而非解码器级掩码；有效性约束提升可靠性与可提取性，推理约束在强制有益轨迹时可提准

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Constrained decoding guarantees that Large Language Models (LLMs) produce outputs conforming to predefined formats, but is often assumed to inherently degrade reasoning. We examine this assumption through a taxonomy of three constraint types: \emph{format constraints}, which enforce output structure; \emph{validity constraints}, which mask invalid answer; and \emph{reasoning constraints}, which restrict reasoning trajectories. We theoretically show that appropriate constraints can preserve or improve performance by eliminating invalid paths. Across four model families and eleven open-source models, causal ablations on diverse benchmarks using state-of-the-art constrained decoding strategies reveal that apparent performance degradation largely stems from non-equivalent prompting and output formats rather than from decoder-level masking. Meanwhile, validity constraints improve reliability and extractability by eliminating invalid answers, while reasoning constraints can improve reasoning and, consequently, accuracy when they enforce beneficial trajectories. These findings show that the impact of constrained decoding depends on the type of constraint and the output space it removes. Our code is available.

</details>

### 202. Knowing When to Escalate: Disagreement Patterns for Uncertainty-Aware Content Moderation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35335)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`升级决策`、`分歧结构`、`价值多元`

👤 **作者**：Michał Wawer、Jarosław A. Chudziak

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agentic 内容审核聚合方法把 agent 间分歧当可靠性问题用共识/投票消除——对价值负载任务该框架不当（分歧可反映不可约价值多元）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RTDA 推理轨迹分歧分析：嵌入视角差异化 agent 的理由并按理由相似×结论一致四态分类，聚合分歧（理由相似结论不同）作为价值负载冲突签名
- 📌 **结论**：1000 条测仇恨语料上该结构信号比仅幅度/仅输出基线更好预测人类标注者分歧——升级决策应基于分歧结构而非幅度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic content-moderation systems increasingly rely on collective judgments from LLM agents, while aggregation methods commonly treat inter-agent disagreement as a reliability problem to be removed through consensus or voting. This framing is inappropriate for value-laden tasks, where disagreement may reflect irreducible value pluralism - a distinction standard uncertainty decompositions cannot recover, since they operate on output distributions and discard the reasoning behind each judgment. We propose Reasoning Trace Divergence Analysis (RTDA), which embeds model-generated justifications from perspective-differentiated agents and classifies each case by justification similarity and conclusion agreement. The resulting four-state taxonomy isolates convergent disagreement, where agents reasoning similarly but concluding differently, as a candidate signature of value-laden conflict. On 1,000 items from the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, this structural signal predicts human annotator disagreement better than magnitude-only and output-only baselines. We contribute a design principle: condition human escalation on the structure of disagreement, not its magnitude alone, with RTDA serving as a post-processing layer for systems that already generate reasoning traces.

</details>

### 203. NeSySpec: Neuro-Symbolic Technical Intelligence for Auditing Engineering Specifications with an Application to Aircraft

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35403)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`工程规范审计`、`神经符号`、`Logic Tensor Network`

👤 **作者**：Pier Mattia Basciano、Gianvito Antenori、Cristiano Andolfi、Giovanni Farina、Salvatore Monteleone

- 🎯 **研究动机**：审计已声明工程规范需知它遵守/违反哪些物理约束、哪个字段被改、如何纠正
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NeSySpec：Logic Tensor Network 上物理关系为可微分级谓词（容差从真实记录学习），同一管线诊断违约+溯因定位改动字段+最小变更纠正+图像证据关联+证据缺失处弃答；107 个机型（16 测试机 9 家族 held-out）
- 📌 **结论**：单一管线整合全部审计输出（无学习/规则/求解器/神经符号/LM 比较者做到），测试机宏 F1 领先

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Engineers who audit a declared specification of an engineered system need to know which physical constraints it respects, which it violates, which field was altered and how to correct it. NeSySpec is an integrated and interpretable neuro-symbolic audit framework, built on Logic Tensor Networks, that answers these questions in one pipeline. Its axioms encode the physical relations of the domain as differentiable graded predicates whose tolerances are learned from a fleet of real records; from the same degrees, it diagnoses the violated constraint, identifies the altered field by abduction, proposes a minimum-change correction and ties image evidence to the parts it describes, abstaining exactly where evidence is missing, and returns all of it in one verification report. We validate NeSySpec on 107 aircraft types, with 16 test aircraft of 9 families held out of training, on specification records paired with ground photographs and satellite imagery and on synthetic errors: generated corruptions, edits labeled from the edit alone and deliberate falsifications. NeSySpec combines all the assessed audit outputs in one pipeline, which no learned, rule-based, solver-based, neuro-symbolic or language-model comparator does; on the test aircraft none is ahead of it in the macro-F1 of its constraint verdicts, in naming the violated constraint and the altered field or in the repair error, and it is competitive in record and image-pair detection. Its diagnosis names the violated constraint in 94.2% of corrupted test records, against 79.5% for classical Fellegi-Holt diagnosis, and on edits labeled from the edit alone, without retraining, abduction finds the altered field in 59.3% of records, against 30.6% for minimum-cardinality localization with random tie- breaking. Only the knowledge base, the visual predicates, the corruption generator and the data are specific to the domain.

</details>

### 204. The Geometry of Emotion Representations Across Scale, Depth, and Instruction Tuning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35411)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`情绪几何`、`规模与深度`、`指令微调`

👤 **作者**：Adam Hollowell

- 🎯 **研究动机**：情绪表征的几何结构如何随规模/深度/指令微调变化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Gemma 2 家族六模型（2B/9B/27B×基座/指令）经确立的提取管线比较：线性映射跨模型迁移 held-out 情绪、主成分方差份额与效价相关、情绪向量投影词表的深度剖析
- 📌 **结论**：全部六格保持核心情绪几何；规模把方差摊到更多方向而不削弱与效价的联系（所有模型主成分-效价相关 0.71-0.78）——跨规模深度一致的情绪几何

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We measure the emotion geometry of six LLMs spanning Google's Gemma 2 family. Using an extraction pipeline established by Sofroniew et al. (2026) on Claude Sonnet 4.5 and replicated by Hollowell (2026) on Gemma 2 27B base, we compare emotion geometry across base and instruction-tuned variants of Gemma 2 2B, 9B, and 27B. First, all six model cells exhibit the core emotion geometry. A linear map fit between two cells places held-out emotions nearest their own vectors in the target model. Across all fifteen model pairs, fit in one direction each, this failed exactly once, and the median rank was 1 in every pair. Linear maps refit on cleaned embedding-table vectors recover every held-out emotion at rank 1 in all thirty ordered pairs. Second, scale spreads the geometry's variance across more directions without weakening its tie to valence. The leading component's share of variance falls from 31.6 percent (2B) to 29.8 (9B) to 26.7 (27B) in the base models and from 31.9 (2B) to 29.2 (9B) to 27.3 (27B) in the tuned ones, while its correlation with human valence ratings stays between .71 and .78 in all six models. Third, we project each emotion vector onto the output vocabulary and ask at what depth the vector's own emotion word enters the top 10,000 of the 256,000 tokens. In the 2B and 9B models the median emotion word is there from layer 0, while in the 27B models it begins near chance, stays outside the top 10,000 for half the network, and enters at layer 23 in one step. Fourth, tuning leaves every registered analysis-layer measure within its floor. At every size, valence correlation rises and arousal correlation falls by amounts within their floors, the latter against prediction. PC2's variance share also falls, with a pooled decrease significant after correction. Mid-late layer stability exceeds its floor and traces to base 27B's final layer. Code, data, and audit reports are released.

</details>

### 205. Model-Aware Generation of Safety-Critical Scenes for Robust End-to-End Driving via Distilled Planner Surrogates

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35448)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`安全关键场景生成`、`规划器代理攻击`、`质量多样性搜索`

👤 **作者**：Khaled M. Seyam、Julian Wiederer、Samar Fathalah、Markus Braun、Bin Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：端到端驾驶模型在标准基准表现好而安全关键场景频繁碰撞——真实驾驶日志中安全关键交互稀少导致训练不充分
- 🔬 **研究方法**：模型感知框架：从规划头蒸馏可微代理在 BEV 级攻击特定规划器（昂贵渲染器出攻击环）；每规划器提取 50 场景（攻击+安全逃生轨迹对作微调标签），质量多样性搜索全自动生成
- 📌 **结论**：NeuroNCAP 失效分析显示 DiffusionDrive 95.9% 碰撞是规划失败（检测可靠）——微调使两规划器闭环碰撞率约减半

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

End-to-end driving models perform well on standard benchmarks but collide frequently in safety-critical scenarios. We present a failure-mode analysis on NeuroNCAP which shows that 95.9% of DiffusionDrive's collisions are planning failures despite reliable detection, pinpointing the bottleneck in planning rather than perception. We attribute this to a fundamental data imbalance, as safety-critical interactions are rare in real driving logs, so planners are hardly ever trained on them. We close this gap with a novel model-aware framework that generates safety-critical training scenes against a specific planner. Each planner is attacked at the bird's-eye-view (BEV) level through a differentiable surrogate distilled from its own planning head. This keeps the expensive video renderer out of the attack loop. We extract 50 scenes per planner, each pairing an attack with a safe escape trajectory that serves as the fine-tuning label. A fully automated quality-diversity search generates both the attacks and the escapes, so that their variety is not specified by hand. Only these scenes are rendered to fine-tune the planner, which reduces the computational cost significantly. Fine-tuning on these scenes roughly halves the closed-loop collision rate of two planners on NeuroNCAP (SparseDrive 28.0% to 12.2%, DiffusionDrive 37.5% to 19.2%) while largely preserving normal driving. A cross-planner transfer experiment shows that the gain comes from targeting the specific planner. Each planner improves most on the scenes generated against itself, and DiffusionDrive fine-tuned on SparseDrive's scenes retains only 6.7 of its 18.3-point collision-rate reduction.

</details>

### 206. EvoBench: An Evolving, Diagnostic Benchmark Framework for Environmental Modeling

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35464)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`演化基准`、`环境建模`、`非平稳失效`

👤 **作者**：Yiming Sun、…、Xiaowei Jia

- 🎯 **研究动机**：环境 ML 模型在固定基准上以单一平均分比较——分数趋同后基准无法区分且单数字隐藏何时何地失败（非平稳/空间外推/稀缺机制三常见失败）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EvoBench 框架显示失败位置、跨时间跟踪并演化聚焦能力缺口；CAMELS-Evolve（约 9000 美国流域 1985-2024）案例：NWM/HBV/数据驱动模型比较
- 📌 **结论**：无模型在每个测试场景全胜；所有数据驱动模型低估低流——诊断性演化基准设计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine learning models are now widely used to predict environmental variable dynamics in domains like hydrology, climate science, agriculture, and ecology. These models are usually compared on fixed benchmark datasets using a single average accuracy score. As models improve, their scores become very close when averaged over large regions and long time periods, so the benchmark can no longer tell them apart, and a single number hides \emph{where} and \emph{when} a model fails. Three failures are common across environmental domains: models get worse as the climate shifts (non-stationarity), models fail at unmonitored sites (spatial extrapolation), and models miss rare but important regimes such as floods and droughts. We present \textbf{EvoBench}, a benchmark framework that shows where a model fails, tracks failures over time, and evolves to focus on model capability gaps. We apply EvoBench to daily streamflow prediction as a case study (\textbf{CAMELS-Evolve}, about 9{,}000 U.S. basins, 1985--2024) and compare an operational physical model (NWM), a conceptual reference (HBV), and several data-driven models. No model wins on every testing scenarios (e.g., in terms of average accuracy, performance at low flows and unmonitored basins). Also we observe that all data-driven models under-predict floods. Additionally, we show that targeted fix based on the reported failures could mitigate weaknesses the benchmark identified in certain scenarios. EvoBench turns model evaluation from a saturated ranking into a moving map of model limitations.

</details>

### 207. MANTIS: Multi-Axis News Transformations for Instance-Wise Stress Testing and Worst-Case Training in Fake News Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35475)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`假新闻压力测试`、`多轴改写`、`最坏情况训练`

👤 **作者**：Dexin Liu、…、Qi Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：假新闻检测依赖表面写作线索或单风格改写——多样装饰修改下鲁棒性差
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MANTIS-Bench：原文×写作风格/情感表达/传播框架多轴重写视图配对；MANTIS 实例自适应对抗框架做零推理开销的动态最坏情况训练
- 📌 **结论**：联合 ASR 较 SheepDog 降 3.55 点且 Macro-F1 一致超先前单轴防御——从启发式格式平滑到定点事实不变可信度评估

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Automated fake news detection is vital on social media, where fake news is easily rewritten to fool detectors. However, existing approaches typically rely on superficial writing cues or single-style text rewrites, resulting in poor robustness under diverse cosmetic modifications. We construct MANTIS-Bench, a multi-axis presentation-attack benchmark that pairs original news with rewritten views across writing style, emotional expression, and dissemination framing. We further propose MANTIS, an instance-adaptive adversarial framework enabling dynamic worst-case training tailored to each news article with zero inference-time overhead. This reframes fake news defense from heuristic format smoothing into targeted, fact-invariant credibility assessment. Experiments on MANTIS-Bench show that MANTIS reduces the Union Attack Success Rate (UASR) by 3.55 percentage points relative to SheepDog on GossipCop and consistently outperforms prior single-axis defenses on Macro-F1 while maintaining competitive clean accuracy, demonstrating the effectiveness of multi-axis and instance-adaptive worst-case training.

</details>

### 208. Visible Reasoning: User-Facing Decision Transparency for Generative AI Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35483)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可见推理`、`决策日志`、`用户面透明`

👤 **作者**：Sanjay Krishna Anbalagan、…、Umesh Mohan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式 AI 的核心问题从“执行对了吗”变为“能信任它如何得出结论吗”——缺不暴露原始 CoT 的用户面透明机制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Filtered Decision Logs：每回应配简洁决策理由+影响排序的支持数据点（工具输出派生）+相关性指示器；轨迹法把可观测遥测重建为溯源树并以四确定性语义结构信号自适应加权排序证据
- 📌 **结论**：三域（职业/投资/旅行规划各 4-5 工具）演示+形成性研究——用户面决策透明原语

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Across multiple waves of computing, public trust in new technology has followed the emergence of governance mechanisms that made dominant risks legible and manageable. Generative AI shifts this pattern: these systems synthesize across heterogeneous sources, and users increasingly experience them as reasoning systems. The central question changes from "did it perform correctly?" to "can we trust how it arrived at its conclusion?" This paper introduces Filtered Decision Logs (FDLs), a user-facing transparency mechanism that makes decision formation visible without exposing raw chain-of-thought. FDLs pair each response with (i) a concise decision rationale, (ii) influence-ordered supporting data points derived from tool outputs, and (iii) relevance indicators showing which sources most shaped the conclusion. To operationalize evidence selection, we propose a trace-based method that reconstructs observability telemetry into a provenance tree and ranks evidence using four deterministic semantic and structural signals - Final Answer Similarity, Average Relevancy, Persistence, and Structural Proximity - combined via adaptive weighted scoring. We demonstrate FDLs across three domains: career advising (5 tools), investment analysis (4 tools), and travel planning (4 tools). In a formative evaluation with 50 participants (25 students, 25 working professionals in AI development), 92% rated the influence-ranked evidence as helpful for understanding and trusting the system's recommendation. Counterfactual tool-removal experiments confirm that removing the top-ranked source changes the answer more than removing a low-ranked source, validating that the ranking tracks decision dependence. We conclude that evidence-ranked transparency helps users identify misleading signals and missing context, enabling more confident acceptance, correction, or re-run of system decisions.

</details>

### 209. FeatureLoop: Benchmarking Coding Agents under Evolving Requirements

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35488)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`演化需求`、`持久工作区`、`累积检查`

👤 **作者**：Jia Feng、Alyssa Wu、Cuiyun Gao、Yunlong Wang、Songyuanyi Lu、Zhichao Hu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 开发始于粗略请求、用户试用后精炼——最终正确性不捕捉所需的反复引导
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FeatureLoop：61 人审任务×34 仓库×五语言七形态；每任务一目标一持久工作区经演化反馈（增改需求）与完成反馈（报告失败）；累积可执行检查跟踪新行为/修复/保留
- 📌 **结论**：最强模型双反馈下完成 72.1%；观察到的失败阶段仅 15.4% 在两次纠正修订内恢复——低干预可靠开发的障碍识别

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Software development with coding agents often begins with a rough request that users refine after trying the implementation. Final correctness alone does not capture the repeated guidance this process requires. We introduce FeatureLoop, a benchmark of 61 human-reviewed tasks from 34 repositories across five primary programming languages and seven software forms. Each task follows one development goal in a persistent workspace through evolution feedback, which adds or clarifies requirements, and completion feedback, which reports failures of stated requirements. Cumulative executable checks track new behavior, repair, and preservation of earlier functionality. We evaluate 12 frontier models and find that the strongest completes 72.1% of tasks with both feedback types. Across models, however, only 15.4% of observed failed stages recover within two corrective revisions. Further analysis reveals incomplete repairs, regressions, and model-specific differences in feedback use and token efficiency, identifying obstacles to reliable, low-intervention development.

</details>

### 210. The Hidden Costs of Forgetting: A Study of Trajectory Deletion in Software Engineering Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35496)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`轨迹删除`、`延迟删除`、`缓存感知定价`

👤 **作者**：Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz、Islem Bouzenia、Michael Pradel、Leon Moonen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：SE agent 每轮重发整条轨迹催生在线轨迹缩减——先前随机删 75% 词元可保持效果，但为何有效/agent 如何补偿/是否真降成本不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：42 个轨迹缩减配置实证（范围/粒度/量/时机）×9400 次运行×两 SOTA agent on SWE-bench Verified
- 📌 **结论**：删至 100% 推理文本仍可保持效果但必须延迟（立即删显著退化）；agent 常重取被删内容增轮次与 token；缓存感知定价下 42 评估零降成本——删除打破前缀缓存

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Software engineering agents resend their entire trajectory to an LLM at every turn, motivating online trajectory reduction. Prior work shows that randomly deleting 75% of tokens in old turns may preserve agent effectiveness, but it remains unclear why, how agents compensate for missing content, and whether it actually reduces costs. This paper presents an empirical study of 42 trajectory-reduction configurations, focusing on effectiveness, agent behavior, and cost. We systematically explore deleting content from old turns by varying the scope, granularity, amount, and timing of deletion across 9,400 runs with two state-of-the-art agents on SWE-bench Verified. We find deleting large amounts of trajectory content (e.g., up to 100% of reasoning text) can preserve overall effectiveness. Importantly, such deletion must be applied with a delay, as immediate deletion tends to significantly degrade effectiveness. Agents often compensate for deleted content by re-fetching it, increasing both the number of turns taken and the input/output tokens. Whether trajectory reduction reduces costs depends on the pricing model: Under a flat pricing model, estimated costs fall in 26 of 42 evaluations. Surprisingly, under a caching-aware pricing model, zero evaluations yield lower costs, since reducing the trajectory breaks the cached prefix billed at a lower rate. Our findings have three implications: First, token counts alone are misleading; trajectory reduction techniques must consider overall costs under a realistic pricing model. Second, analyzing trajectories is crucial to understand agent behavior, as end-to-end effectiveness hides differences such as excessive re-fetching. Finally, these results highlight the need for future work on memory management to reduce costs without sacrificing effectiveness.

</details>

### 211. Test-Time Confidence Adaptation under RL-Induced Trajectory Shift

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35500)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RL轨迹漂移`、`置信自适应`、`稳定性迁移`

👤 **作者**：Zhenzhen Ren、…、Ke Han

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基于轨迹的置信估计在 agentic RL 后大幅退化（RL 诱发推理轨迹漂移）——但更稳定轨迹更可能正确的可靠性结构跨基座与 RL 模型保持
- 🔬 **研究方法**：StaR-TTA：标记基座轨迹上联合学正确性监督与稳定性派生可靠性监督；测试时用无标注 RL 轨迹的稳定性诱导可靠性排序免标适配置信分数
- 📌 **结论**：免标后 RL 置信适配——漂移分布下的可靠信号迁移

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Trajectory-based confidence estimation has shown promising performance for large language models (LLMs) by leveraging rich signals from their reasoning processes. However, we find that such confidence estimators can degrade substantially after agentic reinforcement learning (Agentic RL) as it induces substantial shifts in the reasoning trajectories. More importantly, despite these shifts, we uncover a reliability structure that is largely preserved across Base and RL models: $\textbf{more}$ $\textbf{stable}$ $\textbf{trajectories}$ $\textbf{remain}$ $\textbf{more}$ $\textbf{likely}$ $\textbf{to}$ $\textbf{produce}$ $\textbf{correct}$ $\textbf{answers}$. This preserved relationship provides a transferable, label-free reliability signal for post-RL confidence adaptation. Building on this finding, we propose $\textbf{StaR-TTA}$, a stability-guided framework for label-free test-time confidence adaptation. StaR-TTA jointly learns from correctness supervision and stability-derived reliability supervision on labeled Base trajectories, establishing the connection between trajectory stability and answer reliability. At test time, it reuses stability-induced reliability rankings from unlabeled RL trajectories to adapt confidence scores to the shifted trajectory distribution, without requiring target correctness labels. Across four QA benchmarks, StaR-TTA achieves an average AUROC of \(0.721\), with consistent improvements across unseen datasets, model families and scales, and different Agentic RL algorithms.

</details>

### 212. CANS: Capability-Preserving Neuron Steering for Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35530)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`能力保持转向`、`闭式解`、`神经元级缩放`

👤 **作者**：Yucheng Wu、Chengyi Xie、Chenpeng Wang、Liangming Pan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：激活转向免训练动态控制目标行为但可损害通用能力且需领域特定设计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CANS：双代理（目标词元 log 概/通用输入输出 KL）局部近似得闭式解，轻量免迭代免推理时额外计算；共享数据构造与干预程序跨域适配
- 📌 **结论**：安全/毒性/幻觉控制上与 SFT/CAA/神经元基线相当或更好且更好保持 MMLU/BBH/IFEval

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Activation steering offers training-free and dynamic control of target behaviors in large language models, but existing methods can impair general capabilities and require domain-specific intervention designs. We introduce Capability-Preserving Neuron Steering (CANS), a unified framework that balances target-behavior control with capability preservation. To make target-behavior improvement and general-capability preservation tractable, we introduce two proxies: log-probabilities of target tokens in model generated responses, and output-distribution KL divergence on general-purpose inputs. Combining the local approximation of the two proxies yields a closed-form solution, enabling lightweight steering without iterative model training or additional inference-time computation. The resulting neuron-wise scaling coefficients achieve effective steering while limiting general-capability loss. A shared data-construction and intervention procedure supports adaptation across domains using sampled responses with target-behavior filtering and target-span annotations. Across safety, toxicity, and hallucination control on Qwen and Llama models, CANS achieves comparable or better steering while better preserving MMLU, BBH, and IFEval performance than SFT, CAA, and neuron-level baselines. On Qwen3-4B, CANS reduces HarmBench attack success from $15.50\%$ to $1.75\%$ with a $1.11$-point decrease in average capability. These results support CANS as a practical approach to behavioral control across domains.

</details>

### 213. Copying As Defense: Protecting Images from Editing By Eliciting Model Copying

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35566)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`复制即防御`、`注意力集中`、`DiT编辑防护`

👤 **作者**：Salma Abdel Magid、Sanghyuk Chun、Eric Slyman、Kushal Kafle、Olga Russakovsky

- 🎯 **研究动机**：DiT 图像编辑器可把单照变成有说服力的操纵图（含有害非自愿编辑）——现有防护让输出少注意参考图，但模型仍可按文本与自生成词元生成弱接地新图
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CaD 相反策略：鼓励模型复制参考——早期块集中目标词元注意力于参考图即可使编辑行为坍塌（输出变成有损拷贝而非执行变换）
- 📌 **结论**：注意行归一化使增参考注意即抑制其余——主动防护的注意力机制反转

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Image editors built on diffusion transformers (DiTs), such as Flux Kontext, make it possible to turn a single photograph into a convincing manipulated image, including harmful and non-consensual edits. Proactive defenses aim to prevent such manipulations by adding an imperceptible perturbation before an image is shared. Existing defenses for DiT-based in-context editors make the output attend less to the protected reference image. But this leaves the model free to follow the edit instruction using the text and its own generated tokens, often producing a new, weakly grounded image rather than preventing the edit. We find that the opposite strategy is more effective: instead of leading the model to ignore the reference, we encourage it to copy it. Through controlled attention interventions, we show that concentrating target-token attention on the reference image in only a few early blocks is sufficient to collapse the editing behavior, causing the model to reproduce its input, often as a lossy copy, rather than execute the requested transformation. Based on this insight, we introduce Copying-as-Defense (CaD), which maximizes per-token target-to-reference attention mass in early transformer blocks. Since attention rows are normalized, increasing reference attention suppresses the attention allocated to the text and generated canvas. Restricting the objective to early blocks also lets us truncate the backpropagation path, keeping optimization cheap. Across editors and benchmarks, CaD reduces average edit success from 81.4% on unprotected images to 24.0%, a 70.6% relative reduction, and by a further 44.3% relative to DeContext, the strongest baseline (43.0%). CaD is also roughly 1.8-2.0x faster per optimization step than comparable methods.

</details>

### 214. Linear Probes Transfer from Base to Distilled Language Models despite Rotation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35586)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`探针迁移`、`蒸馏后继`、`旋转下AUC稳定`

👤 **作者**：Marios Tsatsos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：线性探针广泛用于检测有害请求——模型换后继时审计者想复用而非重训
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个基座（Qwen2.5-Math-7B/Llama-3.1-8B/Qwen2.5-14B/32B）XSTest 训练探针不变式应用到 DeepSeek-R1 蒸馏与指令后继
- 📌 **结论**：后继把不安全/安全方向旋转 17-35° 而 XSTest AUC 仅变 -0.011~+0.027；但探针标记 29-42% 听起来有害实则良性的提示——部分读到的是伤害表象而非伤害本身

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Linear probes, classifiers that read a language model's internal activations, are widely used to detect harmful requests. They are cheap to train and run, and an auditor who has trained one would like to reuse it when the model is replaced by a successor rather than retrain it for every release. We test how well probes can be reused. For four base models (Qwen2.5-Math-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-14B, Qwen2.5-32B) we train a probe on the base model with the XSTest prompt set and apply it, unchanged, to two successors of that base: the DeepSeek-R1 distilled reasoning model and the developer's instruction-tuned chat model. We find that the successors rotate the direction separating unsafe from safe prompts by 17$^\circ$ to 35$^\circ$, yet on XSTest the transferred probe's AUC changes by only $-0.011$ to $+0.027$, and on an independent OR-Bench evaluation, never used for training, by $-0.015$ to $+0.053$. On XSTest, whose safe prompts deliberately contain alarming words, the probes beat a classifier that sees only the prompt text by 0.15 to 0.20 AUC. On the OR-Bench prompts that margin shrinks to 0.03 to 0.20, and the probes flag 29\% to 42\% of prompts that sound harmful but are benign, so part of what they read is the appearance of harm rather than harm itself. A probe trained on a base model can thus be reused, in these families, after distillation and after instruction tuning; how much it reads beyond textual cues depends on the test set.

</details>

### 215. Adversary Brains: Hypothesis-Based Logical Signatures for Threat Actor Detection with LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35624)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`对手大脑`、`假设逻辑签名`、`威胁行为者检测`

👤 **作者**：Gadi Evron

- 🎯 **研究动机**：IoC 匹配文件、Sigma 匹配日志行——威胁行为者的活动模式需要持续可检验的演化逻辑签名
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Adversary Brain：竞争假设分析（ACH）从数个假设提升到数千并操作化为持续检测；LLM 把散文报告变 brain、判匹配、驱动保持最新的循环；部分模式可匹配新值（如新 C2 域）
- 📌 **结论**：未知未知变已知未知再到已知——LLM 时代的威胁情报机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

An adversary brain is an LLM-based model of a threat actor's behavior, held as an evolving logical signature: a hypothesis with enough structure that data can test it continuously, without a person re-interpreting it each time. Rather than any single observation, such as an IoC (Indicator of Compromise), it is the arrangement that becomes a detection, or intelligence, mechanism. Where YARA matches a file and Sigma matches a log line, an adversary brain matches the activity pattern of a campaign or threat actor. This is based on an intelligence analysis technique called Analysis of Competing Hypotheses (ACH), which the design lifts from the limitation of a few hypotheses at a time, to thousands, and then also operationalizes it for continuous detection purposes. Language models make the concept, and scale, practical: they turn prose reports into brains, judge matches, and drive the loop that keeps brains current. A partial pattern in a brain can also match a new value, such as a fresh C2 (command and control) domain name, assisting with the discovery of new indicators, and turning unknown unknowns into known unknowns, and potentially into knowns. The author would like to thank Ryan Moon for the collaboration, and Sounil Yu, Chris Inglis, Thomas Roccia, Marius Benthin, Israel Grimberg, Michal Kamensky, Justin Borland, Brandon Levene, John Hultquist, and John Bambenek for their feedback and assistance.

</details>

### 216. Cheap AI-Assisted Reverse Engineering and What It Will Do

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35633)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI辅助逆向`、`法律张力`、`难度过滤器失效`

👤 **作者**：Ilan Mandel、Madiha Zahrah Choksi、Joey Castillo、Sebastian Benthall、James Grimmelmann

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式 AI 使逆向工程大幅变快变易——IP 与技术法假设 RE 昂贵耗时需专长并以难度为过滤器区分许可 RE 与窃取，该假设正在失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：描述 AI 改变 RE 的方式、映射到既有法律（商业秘密/版权/反规避/计算机滥用/合同）并勾勒法律系统可选回应与权衡
- 📌 **结论**：供应商与逆向者之间的斗争将挑战广泛法律领域——AI 消费级化的法律分析

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative AI is putting the law of reverse engineering (RE) under severe strain. Existing IP and technology laws assume that RE is expensive and time-consuming, and that it requires specialized expertise. They generally allow it, but they rely on its difficulty as a filter to separate (permissible) RE from (impermissible) copying and theft. Generative AI is changing this equation by making \RE much faster, cheaper, and easier. AI systems can identify hardware components, extract firmware, decompile object code, and clone entire apps. The resulting struggle between technology vendors and reverse engineers will challenge a wide range of laws, including trade secret, copyright, anti-circumvention, computer misuse, and contract. In this article, we describe the ways in which AI is changing \RE, map these new practices onto existing laws, and sketch ways that the legal system could respond and their associated trade-offs.

</details>

### 217. Printed and Scanned: Exposing the Fragility of Single-Image Morphing Attack Detectors under Print-Scan Degradation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35682)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`人脸变形检测`、`打印-扫描退化`、`跨设备鲁棒`

👤 **作者**：Diogo J. Paulo、Hugo Proença、João C. Neves

- 🎯 **研究动机**：S-MAD 基准只在数字域——真实护照签发管线仍要求打印重扫申请人照片，该步引入半调/重采样/色彩空间/传感器噪声可掩盖高频伪影
- 🔬 **研究方法**：打印重扫 SYN-MAD22 全部真伪与变形图（300/600 DPI）评八个 SOTA S-MAD 模型（仅数字训练）；第二台扫描仪重复采集测跨设备鲁棒
- 📌 **结论**：数字域训练的检测器经打印扫描后大幅退化——数字基准高估真实管线检测力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Face morphing attacks, in which the biometric traits of two or more identities are blended into a single face image, pose a severe threat to identity document issuance and automated border control. Consequently, a large body of work has proposed Single-image Morphing Attack Detection (S-MAD) algorithms and benchmarked them on datasets such as SYN-MAD22. However, the majority of these benchmarks operate exclusively in the digital domain, which contrasts with the real-world passport issuance pipeline in some countries, which still requires an applicant-supplied photograph to be printed and subsequently re-scanned before it is embedded in the identity document or fed into an automated verification system. This print-scan step introduces halftoning, resampling, color-space, and sensor noise that are absent from digital pipelines, and may mask the high-frequency artifacts that S-MAD models rely on. In this work, we introduce a print-scan version of the SYN-MAD22 dataset, obtained by printing and re-scanning all bona fide and morphed images at 300 and 600 DPI, and we use it to evaluate eight state-of-the-art S-MAD models trained exclusively on digital data. We additionally repeat the acquisition with a second scanner to assess cross-device robustness. Our results show that detectors that achieve near state-of-the-art performance in the digital domain suffer a more than threefold increase in average equal error rate, with the same qualitative degradation observed across both scanning devices, exposing a critical, previously under-reported gap between digital-benchmark performance and robustness to print-scan degradation.

</details>

### 218. Not One Belief: Answer Payloads, Persistence, and Dissociated Confidence Signals in Conversational Question Answering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35694)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`答案载荷`、`持续错误修复`、`置信信号解离`

👤 **作者**：Duc-Vinh Tran

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对话 QA 应在用户反驳时纠错并标示可信答案——把追问拆为言语行为（重问/同意/挑战）与载荷（答案串，对或错）
- 🔬 **研究方法**：同一转录上测四置信信号（言语化/长度归一似然/冻结隐态探针/P(True)）；81 个 Qwen2.5-7B 持续错误的修复实验
- 📌 **结论**：答案只在用户提供答案串时改变；冻结探针随答案持续性而非真实性上升（与真值解离）；无答案串的修复率全 <0.15 而金答案修 75-95%、含答案句修 67-76%——证据门控修复

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Conversational QA systems should fix errors when users push back and signal which answers to trust. We split each follow-up into its speech act (re-ask, agree, or challenge) and its payload (an answer string, correct or not), and measure four confidence signals on the same transcripts: verbalized confidence, length-normalized likelihood, a frozen hidden-state probe, and P(True). Answers change when the user supplies an answer string and almost never under the speech act alone: in every retained full-size cell, the correct answer converts at least four times as many wrong answers as re-asking or agreement. The four signals disagree. On Qwen2.5-7B the frozen probe rises with answer persistence rather than truth; this holds on held-out questions, with Llama-3-8B (cleaned answers) as support, but not on four other models. Among 81 Qwen2.5-7B errors that survive one re-ask, re-asking, a fresh chat, or abstain repair almost none, while a gold answer repairs 75-95%. In two preregistered replications, every repair rate without an answer string stays below 0.15; a larger post-hoc rerun excludes 0.15 except for the empty elicit. With BM25 TriviaQA evidence, sentences that contain the answer repair 67-76% of persistent errors versus at most 12% when they do not. An end-to-end contain-gate cuts harm on TriviaQA, but a deployable gate that does not know the gold answer repairs few errors.

</details>

### 219. MiRL: Error-Targeted Reinforcement Learning for Correcting Terminal Agent Misbehaviors

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35697)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`错误定向RL`、`可泛化失范`、`终端agent`

👤 **作者**：Runchu Tian、Zaid Khan、Zun Wang、Justin Chih-Yao Chen、Mohit Bansal

- 🎯 **研究动机**：错误定向学习（识别弱点-训练修复-检验）应用于终端 agent——现有错误定向方法面向单轮，失败分析用于评测调试而非训练
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MiRL：先画像策略错误找可泛化失范（跨环境复发的错误步骤类型），再从失范发生步起 rollout，仅当通过测试且避免失范才奖励
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen3.5-4B 上八个失范占错误步骤 52.1%；训练后未见前缀上失范平均减 43-57%，TB Lite 51.8% vs TMax 47.4% 且只用 35% rollout

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Error-targeted learning gives a legible way to improve a model: identify specific weaknesses of the model, train the model to fix these weaknesses, and check whether the weaknesses are fixed. We study how to apply error-targeted learning to terminal agents, which solve long-horizon tasks specified in natural language by interacting with a command shell. Existing error-targeted methods are designed for single-turn tasks, and existing analyses of agent failures are used to evaluate and debug agents rather than to train them. We propose Misbehavior-driven RL (MiRL), which first profiles the errors of a policy to find generalizable misbehaviors: kinds of wrong steps that recur across many environments. MiRL then trains the policy with RL, starting rollouts at the steps where these misbehaviors occur and rewarding a rollout only if it passes the tests and avoids the misbehavior. On Qwen3.5-4B, eight misbehaviors account for 52.1% of wrong steps. After training with MiRL, the policy repeats the measured misbehaviors 43% to 57% less often on average on unseen prefixes. MiRL outperforms TMax-4B on Terminal-Bench Lite (51.8% vs. 47.4%) and matches it on Terminal-Bench 2.1 (21.3% vs. 20.6%), while using 35% of its rollouts. These results show that error-targeted learning can be applied to realistic, long-horizon terminal tasks.

</details>

### 220. From Self-Confidence to Statistical Accuracy: Scaling Human-in-the-Loop Evaluation with Direct and LLM-Assisted Auditing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35698)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`HITL评测缩放`、`置信分层采样`、`裁判校正估计`

👤 **作者**：Harine Choi、…、Yang Zhou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型自置信常失校准但仍可用于高效评测；LLM 裁判可扩展但收益依校准质量与推理成本
- 🔬 **研究方法**：统一统计框架：直接审计（置信分层采样恢复总体精度，免校准置信+有限样本不确定性）；裁判辅助（灵敏度/假阳率建模得误差校正估计器）；两条路线经人标与总成本效率边界比较
- 📌 **结论**：MMLU-Pro/MMMU-Pro 上失校准自置信仍支撑统计有效分层审计；高不均衡置信分布下比例分配大幅降估计误差

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

For large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs), model-reported self-confidence is often miscalibrated, yet it may still be useful for efficient evaluation. At the same time, LLM-as-a-judge offers a scalable alternative to direct human auditing, but its practical benefit depends on calibration quality and inference cost. This paper develops a unified statistical framework for both evaluation routes under alternative sampling strategies. For direct auditing, confidence-stratified sampling is used to recover population accuracy from partially verified outputs without requiring calibrated confidence. Finite-sample uncertainty is characterized, and the analysis shows how highly uneven confidence strata affect audit efficiency and label allocation. For judge-assisted evaluation, the judge is modeled through sensitivity and false-positive rate, yielding an error-corrected estimator with calibration uncertainty. The two routes are then compared through human-label and total-cost efficiency boundaries. Experiments on MMLU-Pro and MMMU-Pro show that miscalibrated self-confidence can still support statistically valid stratified auditing. Under highly imbalanced confidence distributions, proportional allocation substantially reduces estimation error relative to equal-per-stratum allocation. For judge-assisted evaluation, only a small subset of target--verifier pairs satisfies the idealized human-label efficiency condition, and automated inference costs can further limit potential savings. These findings clarify the statistical and economic conditions under which confidence-guided auditing and automated verification are useful.

</details>

### 221. A Category-Selective Encryption Framework for Privacy Protection of UAV Images

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35729)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`类别选择性加密`、`无人机图像`、`可逆加密`

👤 **作者**：Ting Yan、Minghui Shang、Haolang Sun、Xu Wang、Yamin Han、Mingze He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：无人机航拍常拍下行人与车辆等敏感对象——类无关显著性检测无差别混淆所有显著区造成不必要信息损失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：类别选择性加密：80 类检测器识别场景后只加密用户指定敏感类别；双方案（TPE 自适应像素化+联合训练 U-Net 恢复；XOR 流密码参数无关精确可逆）
- 📌 **结论**：语义级选择性保护非敏感对象与背景供下游分析——隐私-分析平衡的 UAV 框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Imagery captured by unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) systems increasingly raises privacy concerns, as UAV aerial surveillance routinely captures sensitive objects such as pedestrians and vehicles alongside ordinary scene content. Existing privacy-preserving approaches typically employ class-agnostic saliency detection to indiscriminately obfuscate all visually salient regions, leading to unnecessary information loss and hindering downstream scene analysis. This paper proposes a category-selective encryption framework for privacy-preserving UAV surveillance. The core advance over prior class-agnostic approaches is semantic-level selectivity: the system first recognizes what is present in the scene via an 80-class detector, then encrypts only user-specified sensitive categories while leaving non-sensitive objects and background intact for downstream analytics. The framework supports two complementary encryption schemes: Thumbnail-Preserving Encryption (TPE), which applies adaptive pixelation with a learnable blend parameter and recovers content through a jointly trained Enhanced U-Net, and XOR stream cipher encryption, which offers parameter-free, mathematically exact reversibility. The architectural design, composite loss formulation, and joint training methodology are presented in detail, establishing a foundation for practical privacy-preserving UAV surveillance that balances protection strength and scene utility.

</details>

### 222. Free to Look, Free to Reason: Simple LLM Harnesses Outdiagnose Hand-Crafted Root Cause Analysis Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35767)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`简单harness胜出`、`RCA agent复盘`、`处方化调用代价`

👤 **作者**：Tingzhu Bi、Xun Zhang、Jinglin Li、Ping Wang、Meng Ma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：微服务失效根因分析的 LLM agent 多为手工设计（标准程序/固定阶段/角色 agent）且各评各的数据——强模型自由调查是否胜过这些设计未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三个不规定模型调用的简单 harness（证据包单调用/SQL agent/Codex 编码 agent）对比五个已发表 RCA agent 与统计因果方法：RCAtlas 90 个验证案例×六模型×三推理力度
- 📌 **结论**：简单 harness 全面最准（PR@1 65-77% vs 已发表 agent 16-53% vs 非 LLM 6-39%）；已发表 agent 把十分之一到全部模型调用固化在程序里并跳过决定性证据——处方化设计的代价

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Root cause analysis (RCA) of microservice failures has attracted many LLM agents whose investigation is hand-crafted, through standard operating procedures, fixed stages and role agents. Each is evaluated on its own data against few baselines, so it is unknown whether this design beats letting a capable model investigate on its own. We compare three simple harnesses that prescribe none of the model's calls (one call over an evidence package, a SQL agent and the Codex coding agent) with five published LLM RCA agents and with statistical and causal methods on RCAtlas, 90 validated cases from eight public datasets, across six models and three reasoning efforts. The simple harnesses are the most accurate (PR@1 65-77% on three mid-tier models), ahead of the published agents (16-53%) and the non-LLM methods (6-39%), on every model on which both families were run. The published agents fix from a tenth to all of their model calls in the program, and a case study shows how these prescriptions skip the decisive evidence. A stronger model mainly improves reliability, a multi-turn harness mainly improves aptitude, and more reasoning effort changes accuracy by a few points at most while multiplying the time by up to seven. Among runs more accurate than the best non-LLM method, every Pareto-optimal run in cost or time uses a simple harness.

</details>

### 223. Atomic Security Concerns: Towards a Foundational Language for Security Knowledge Representation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35782)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`原子安全关切`、`安全知识表示`、`组合语义`

👤 **作者**：Hanane Houmani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：网络安全治理/风险/合规/架构概念经异构抽象与词汇表示——关系难一致表达且推理依赖专家
- 🔬 **研究方法**：引入 Security Concerns 与 Atomic Security Concerns（ASCs）假设：安全知识可用有限基础关切集表示，如化学原子般组合成概念
- 📌 **结论**：ASC 作安全语言的词汇表与概念组合语义层——独立于领域/性质/粒度的共同抽象

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Cybersecurity governance, risk management, compliance, and security architecture rely on numerous concepts, including security properties, domains, controls, risks, implementation measures, capabilities, and compliance requirements. Although these concepts are widely used, they are often represented through heterogeneous abstractions and vocabularies, making relationships among them difficult to express consistently and leaving much of the associated reasoning dependent on expert knowledge. This paper introduces the concepts of Security Concerns and Atomic Security Concerns (ASCs) and explores the hypothesis that cybersecurity knowledge may be represented through a finite set of foundational concerns. Inspired by the role of atoms in chemistry, the proposed approach views cybersecurity concepts as compositions of more fundamental concerns rather than as independent abstractions. Hence, Atomic Security Concerns could be viewed as a common abstraction layer for the representation of cybersecurity concepts, independently of their domain, nature, or level of granularity. Building upon this perspective, the paper explores how Atomic Security Concerns may provide the vocabulary of a cybersecurity language and how cybersecurity concepts may be represented as concern sets supporting explicit relationships among concepts. The contribution of this work is intentionally conceptual: it introduces a framework and research hypothesis for investigating the foundations of a common language for cybersecurity knowledge representation, reasoning, and future formalization.

</details>

### 224. Selective Tie Learning: Mitigating Spurious Correlations in Preference Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35787)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`平局学习`、`伪相关缓解`、`影响最优选择`

👤 **作者**：Christian Moya、Purav Matlia、Elliott Thornley、Guang Lin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：偏好优化可学伪相关——用平局（等偏好响应）增广可减伪依赖但平局的矫正效果不同，选哪些很重要
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对数线性策略推导每个平局对伪依赖的影响并证明取最大预测削减为同尺寸子集一阶最优；隐式奖励边际幅度作代理的条件刻画；广义 Gauss-Newton 曲率近似迁移到神经与语言模型
- 📌 **结论**：跨对数线性/神经/LLM，选择规则以更少平局达与均匀选择相当的伪依赖削减

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Preference optimization can learn spurious correlations, and augmenting the preference data with ties (equally preferred responses) can reduce spurious reliance. However, ties differ in their corrective effect, making their selection for augmentation important. For log-linear policies, we derive each tie's influence on spurious reliance and show that taking the largest predicted reductions is first-order optimal among subsets of equal size. Computing these influences requires knowledge of the spurious features and the curvature of the training objective, which is often unavailable or expensive to estimate. To this end, we explore using the magnitude of a tie's implicit reward margin and characterize the conditions under which it is a useful proxy for predicted influence. To test the theory and its qualitative transfer to neural models, we estimate influence with a counterfactual proxy for spurious reliance and use a generalized Gauss--Newton approximation to curvature for language models. Across log-linear policies, neural models, and large language models, our selection rules consistently achieve comparable reductions in spurious reliance with fewer ties than uniform selection.

</details>

### 225. Does VGGT Know What It Does Not Know?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35806)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`3D重建失败自知`、`冻结特征探针`、`跨域迁移`

👤 **作者**：Adityan Thirumani Srinivasan、David Nordström

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VGGT 等前馈 3D 重建对相机位姿不报不确定性——错误相对位姿与正确无异：冻结模型是否知道何时错
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HardMatch（VGGT 35% 失败的 1000 个困难像对）上冻结特征线性探针预测位姿失败
- 📌 **结论**：ROC-AUC 93.5%（点图置信 79.7%）；知识不在单图编码器而在融合两视图的层中段（π³ 同样）——VGGT 对自己失败知道的比报告的多

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models such as VGGT regress cameras and geometry in a single forward pass, but they report no uncertainty for the camera poses they predict: an incorrect relative pose looks no different from a correct one. We ask whether the frozen model nonetheless knows when its pose is wrong. On HardMatch, a benchmark of 1,000 deliberately difficult image pairs on which VGGT fails on 35% of pairs, a linear probe on VGGT's frozen features predicts pose failure on held-out categories with ROC-AUC 93.5%, compared with 79.7% for VGGT's own point-map confidence and 77.7% for a classifier trained on all of VGGT's outputs. The probe catches failures VGGT is confident about, lets a downstream system abstain on the pairs it distrusts, and transfers without retraining to rotation failures on PhotoTourism and ScanNet-1500, beyond what co-visibility explains. This knowledge is absent from the per-image encoder: it is computed partway through the layers that fuse the two views, and the same holds for a second model, $\pi^3$. In essence, we find that VGGT knows more about its own failures than it reports.

</details>

### 226. Reva: Real-Time Vulnerability Detection through Contextual Probing of Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35829)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`实时漏洞检测`、`上下文探针`、`单前向`

👤 **作者**：Amine Lbath、Massih-Reza Amini

- 🎯 **研究动机**：编码 agent 生成代码快于审查——需每新函数的实时第一道防线；agentic 检测器一次裁决需分钟级而直接提示又不准
- 🔬 **研究方法**：REVA：把 agentic 检测器背后的安全上下文与代码理解压进 LLM 单次前向——预计算模块威胁模型+轻量静态分析发现+调用图信息，训练探针读内部表征给出可校准的实时裁决
- 📌 **结论**：agent 级准确率与近实时速度的结合——安全上下文探针化

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A vulnerability costs more to fix the later it is discovered. As coding agents generate code faster than developers can review it, software needs a first line of defense screening each new function in real time. LLM agents have recently become capable of finding real-world vulnerabilities by working as a security expert would: they run analysis tools, explore the surrounding code, test hypotheses and weigh the evidence before forming their own judgement. This reasoning, however, spans many model calls, so that each verdict takes minutes of compute, far too long for real-time screening. Faster detectors, which fine-tune deep learning models or prompt an LLM for a direct verdict, are less accurate: they judge a function without the context an agent gathers, and an LLM's generated verdict is unreliable even when given some of it. We present REVA (REal-time Vulnerability Analysis), an AI vulnerability detector that brings the security context and code understanding components behind agentic detectors into a single forward pass of an LLM. For each new function, REVA gives the LLM a pre-computed threat model of its module, lightweight static analysis findings and call-graph information. REVA leverages a trained probe to read the LLM's internal representations of the new function in this context. On PrimeVul's held-out test set, REVA on GLM-5.3-Flash reaches 60.0% accuracy in about one second per function. This matches VulTriage (59.2%), the state-of-the-art agentic detector, run on the same LLM, while being over 250 times faster and less than a tenth as costly. On the same prompt, the probe is 14% to 17% more accurate than the LLM's own direct verdict. Across three open LLMs, REVA is at least as accurate as VulTriage, and about 10% more accurate on two of them. An LLM's internal representations thus encode much of what agentic detectors obtain through reasoning.

</details>

### 227. Patient-specific rubric-based evaluation of large language models across longitudinal cancer care

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35847)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`纵向癌症护理评测`、`阶段感知量表`、`决策遗漏`

👤 **作者**：Wei-Wei Zhang、…、Ying Sun

- 🎯 **研究动机**：专科医学 LLM 评测多聚焦孤立临床接触——临床责任沿纵向护理路径演化未被跟踪
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NPC-RubricBench：405 名患者两中心的纵向记录，941 个患者-阶段单元跨六个顺序护理阶段与 17688 条患者特定原子标准（严格限于决策时临床证据）；30 个通用与医疗 LLM
- 📌 **结论**：强诊断表现不传导到下游护理；主导失效模式随阶段反转（解剖分期显式决策错误 48.2% vs 初诊与随访决策遗漏 33.4%/30.4%）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) show promise in specialty medicine, yet existing evaluations largely focus on isolated clinical encounters rather than tracking how clinical responsibilities evolve across a longitudinal care pathway. Here we introduce NPC-RubricBench, a phase-aware benchmark for evaluating multistage clinical reasoning across the cancer care continuum, using nasopharyngeal carcinoma as a defined disease context. Curated from longitudinal records of 405 patients across two cancer centers, the benchmark comprises 941 patient-phase units across six sequential care phases and 17,688 patient-specific atomic criteria spanning five evaluation dimensions, with each assessment strictly restricted to decision-time clinical evidence. Evaluating 30 general-purpose and medical LLMs revealed that strong diagnostic performance does not carry over to downstream care. We evaluated 30 general-purpose and medical LLMs, showing that high diagnostic performance did not translate into improved downstream care. Instead, the dominant failure modes reversed across phases. Explicit decision errors dominated anatomical staging at 48.2%, whereas decision omissions dominated initial work-up and post-treatment follow-up at 33.4% and 30.4%, respectively. In the evaluated model set, medically adapted models showed higher omission rates than general-purpose models. Moreover, varying query granularity exposes a critical specification trade-off: broad open-ended requests degrade Decision Quality across all 30 models, while more detailed, checklist-like prompts raise Decision Quality but reduce Evidence Faithfulness and Safety Adherence. Furthermore, NPC-RubricBench demonstrates that static diagnostic benchmarks are insufficient to ensure consistent clinical reliability over time, highlighting the importance of phase-adaptive safeguards in multistage medical AI.

</details>

### 228. Auto-Research Arena: Benchmarking and Advancing Auto-Research in Language Modeling

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35866)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`自动研究竞技场`、`可容许性vs改进`、`九目标`

👤 **作者**：Xi Chen、…、Hanwen Jiang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主研究系统越强——基准须既评表现又支撑有意义的研究进展
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Auto-Research Arena：把可容许性（模型质量与资源约束决定结果是否合格）与改进（选定目标测可容许结果间进展）分离；语言模型预训练中实例化九目标（模型/数据/训练/推理效率）×七研究 harness×两基础模型
- 📌 **结论**：数个复杂方法被简单顺序搜索基线击败——当前 auto-research 系统余量巨大；发现的复用在新搜索中以同数据 FLOPs 得更低训练损失

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As autonomous research systems become more capable, benchmarks must both evaluate their performance and support meaningful research progress. We introduce Auto-Research Arena, a multi-target benchmark for evaluating autonomous research systems and discovering ideas in language modeling. Arena supports diverse research objectives within a shared experimental environment by separating admissibility from improvement: model quality and resource constraints determine whether a result qualifies, while the selected target measures progress among admissible results. We instantiate Arena in language model pretraining with nine targets spanning model, data, training, and inference efficiency, and evaluate seven research harnesses using two foundation models. Arena clearly differentiates existing harnesses across research objectives, with several sophisticated methods outperformed by our simple sequential search baseline, indicating substantial headroom in current auto-research systems. Beyond evaluation, Arena reveals a substantially broader frontier of model and system ideas aligned with active research directions. Reusing these discoveries in a new quality-driven search yields lower training loss under matched data and FLOPs and lower held-out BPB at the same model size. Together, Arena provides a common framework for benchmarking autonomous research and expanding its scope in language modeling. The code is available at this https URL

</details>
